### [1] [A Review Of Robotic World Models For Dynamic Environments Based On Factor And Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08800)
*Marco Giberna,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Jose Luis Sanchez Lopez,Holger Voos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这篇综述系统梳理了机器人与图结构世界模型（因子图与场景图及其混合模型）应对动态环境的方法，围绕表示、构建更新与下游任务利用三方面展开，并总结了趋势与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人环境往往是动态的，而早期的因子图与场景图世界模型多以静态环境为简化假设，这给纯静态的世界表示带来严重挑战，因此有必要系统审视如何在图结构世界模型中处理动态性。

Method: 以三个维度组织论述：(I) 适合的动态表示；(II) 构建与更新这些表示的流程；(III) 它们在下游任务中的利用。综述涵盖基于因子图或场景图的方法，并特别关注将二者结合为混合模型的新方法，分析不同类型动态的建模方式并归纳常见架构模式。

Result: 归纳并分类了动态图世界模型的常见架构模式，明确了因子图偏重不确定性感知的几何估计、场景图偏重结构化语义抽象的互补特性，以及混合模型在融合几何与语义方面的作用。

Conclusion: 指出若干新兴趋势与开放挑战：从学习式感知到表示层的不确定性传播、最小感知条件下动态实体运动与尺度的可观测性、可扩展的终身维护，以及缺乏能将世界模型质量与动态下下游任务性能挂钩的数据集和评测协议。

Abstract: Models based on graphs have emerged in robotics as a powerful foundation for internal world representations, where factor and scene graphs are among the most prominent model types found in the related literature and in successful robotic solutions. Initially, many of these models were assuming static environments as a simplification. Herein, factor graphs mainly provide uncertainty-aware geometric estimations while scene graphs enable a structured semantic abstraction. However, real-world robotic environments are often dynamic, posing severe challenges for purely static world representations. Therefore, this review presents a comprehensive view on how dynamic aspects of real-world environments can be addressed in such graph-based world models. We organize our assessments around three main aspects: (I) suitable representations, (II) pipelines to construct and update the representations, and (III) their exploitation for downstream tasks. We review approaches that are either based on factor or scene graphs, but put special emphasis on novel approaches that combine both types to form hybrid models. We mainly analyze how different types of dynamics can be modeled herein, and categorize common architectural patterns. Finally, emerging trends and open challenges are identified, including uncertainty propagation from learned perception through the representation layers, the observability of dynamic-entity motion and scale under minimal sensing, scalable lifelong maintenance, and the lack of datasets and evaluation protocols that ground world-model quality in downstream task performance under dynamics.

</details>


### [2] [SAFE: Unified Slip and Fracture Detection with Low-Cost Acoustic Sensing in Robotic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08802)
*Zerun Wang,Vivek Kamat,Shekhar Bhansali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SAFE：基于两个被动PVDF声学传感器与电机本体感知的低成本通用感知方法，结合自适应抓取控制器，实现实时滑动/断裂检测与失效感知抓取控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在抓取易碎物体时需实时理解抓取状态（滑动、断裂）以便恰当响应，尤其在材料属性未知的情况下；现有方法多依赖视觉或先验材料知识，成本高且泛化性差。

Method: 在柔性Fin Ray夹爪上安装两个被动PVDF声学传感器，结合电机本体感知信号，构建79维特征向量，使用统一的HistGradientBoosting分类器输出正常/滑动/断裂三态；在该检测层之上构建自适应抓取控制器，运行于Jetson Orin Nano（104 Hz）。

Result: 留一抓取交叉验证下Alert-F1达0.884，滑动与断裂几乎无混淆，消融实验证明声学感知不可或缺；46次闭环机器人试验成功率达91.3%，而固定力策略在不匹配条件下降至0%；对训练未见新物体成功率达82.4%。

Conclusion: SAFE无需视觉与材料先验即可实现鲁棒、失效感知的抓取控制，无需物体特定标定，具备低成本、通用性与实时性。

Abstract: Manipulating fragile objects remains challenging as robots must understand the state of what they grasp, such as slip or fracture, to respond appropriately, especially when material properties are unknown. In this paper, we present SAFE: a low-cost, general-purpose sensing approach that detects both slip and fracture in real time using two passive polyvinylidene fluoride (PVDF) acoustic sensors and motor proprioception, without relying on vision or prior material knowledge. The sensors are mounted on a compliant Fin Ray gripper, and a unified HistGradientBoosting classifier reports the state (normal, slip, or fracture) from a 79-dimensional feature vector. Under leave-one-grasp-out cross-validation, SAFE achieves an Alert-F1 of 0.884 with near-zero slip-fracture confusion, and ablations confirm that acoustic sensing is indispensable. An adaptive grasp controller built on this detection layer runs at 104 Hz on a Jetson Orin Nano, achieving 91.3% success across 46 closed-loop robot trials spanning diverse object categories, while each fixed-force strategy drops to 0% on object conditions that mismatch its preset. It further reaches 82.4% success on novel objects unseen during training, demonstrating robust, failure-aware grasp control without object-specific calibration.

</details>


### [3] [Context-Aware Adaptive Pesticide Spraying for Agricultural Robots under Changing Weather and Terrain Using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08807)
*Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于视觉语言模型(VLM)的上下文感知自适应喷洒框架，结合作物类型、农药类型和天气数据进行空间推理与自适应决策，并用MPPI轨迹跟踪控制器实现精准导航与喷洒，在作物行检测精度上提升至少30%，并能灵活调整喷洒量与行进速度、减少农药飘移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准喷洒对提升施用效率与化学分布均匀性至关重要。喷洒性能受温度、风速等环境条件、农药类型以及机器人对作物与目标喷洒位置的感知能力等多因素影响。现有方法多侧重作物检测并依赖预设喷洒参数，而人工操作者会综合环境条件、区域作物特征与农药类型动态调整喷洒策略，因此需要具备上下文感知能力的自适应喷洒方案。

Method: 提出基于视觉语言模型(VLM)的上下文感知自适应喷洒框架，使机器人能够进行空间推理，并融合作物类型、农药类型与天气数据等多源信息，做出自适应且优化的喷洒决策；随后采用基于模型预测路径积分(MPPI)控制的轨迹跟踪控制器，保证精准导航与在作物位置的准确喷洒。

Result: 对比结果表明，所提方法在作物行检测精度上至少提升30%；在两个环境下开展的实验评估进一步证明，机器人能够灵活调整喷洒量与行进速度，同时减少农药飘移。

Conclusion: 该框架通过VLM驱动的多源上下文融合实现了自适应喷洒决策，配合MPPI控制器提升导航与喷洒精度，验证了在检测精度、喷洒参数灵活调节及减少农药飘移方面的有效性，为精准农业中替代人工经验式喷洒策略提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Precision pesticide spraying is essential for optimizing application efficiency and ensuring uniform chemical distribution. Spraying performance is influenced by multiple factors, including environmental conditions such as temperature and wind speed, pesticide type, and the robot's capability to accurately perceive crops and target spray locations. Existing approaches predominantly emphasize crop detection and rely on predefined spraying parameters, whereas human operators dynamically adjust their spraying strategies by considering environmental conditions, region-specific crop characteristics, and the type of pesticide being applied. In this study, we propose a context-aware adaptive spraying framework based on Vision-Language Models (VLMs), which enables robots to leverage spatial reasoning and integrate information from multiple sources, including crop type, pesticide type, and weather data, to make adaptive and optimized spraying decisions. Subsequently, a trajectory-tracking controller based on Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is employed to ensure precise navigation and accurate spraying at crop locations. The comparative results demonstrate that the proposed method improves accuracy by at least 30% in detecting crop rows. In addition, the experimental evaluations conducted in two environments further demonstrate the robot's ability to flexibly adjust spraying volume and travel speed, while reducing pesticide drift.

</details>


### [4] [Taming an End-to-End Autonomous Driving Policy for Urban Navigation of Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08812)
*Joochan Kim,Chanuk Yang,Tackgeun You,Ziran Wang,Hwasup Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Go2-DrivoR，将端到端自动驾驶轨迹规划框架DrivoR适配到四足机器人城市导航，通过目标token实现局部坐标系子目标条件下的轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将成熟的自动驾驶端到端轨迹规划框架拓展至四足机器人城市导航，实现短时域、目标条件下的局部规划，且无需重新设计核心解码器。

Method: 以局部坐标系子目标通过目标token对轨迹生成进行条件化；适配以车辆为中心的评分公式；重新定义面向人行道导航的可行驶区域合规项；将原有的自车进度项重构为目标条件下的自车进度项。

Result: 仅使用TartanGround仿真数据训练，在未见过的仿真环境中提升了航点条件下的规划性能，并零样本迁移至真实世界的开环轨迹预测。

Conclusion: 目标条件化的框架适配可有效将自动驾驶规划器迁移至四足机器人导航，具备跨仿真环境的泛化能力与向真实场景零样本迁移的潜力。

Abstract: We present Go2-DrivoR, a goal-conditioned adaptation of the end-to-end autonomous driving trajectory planning framework DrivoR for urban navigation with quadrupedal robots. By conditioning trajectory generation on a local-frame subgoal through a goal token and adapting the vehicle-centric scoring formulation, the method extends DrivoR to short-horizon goal-conditioned local planning without redesigning its core decoders. Specifically, we redefine drivable-area compliance for sidewalk-oriented navigation and reformulate the original ego progress term as goal-conditioned ego progress. Trained exclusively on TartanGround simulation data, Go2-DrivoR improves waypoint-conditioned planning performance on unseen simulation environments and transfers zero-shot to open-loop real-world trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [5] [Autonomous Droplet Navigation via Model-Based Reinforcement Learning: Zero-Shot Transfer and Emergent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08852)
*Rajneesh Anand,Mayuresh V. Kothare*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个基于模型强化学习的机器人平台，实现开放无约束表面上液滴的闭环自主导航，无需仿真或解析模型，50–150个物理回合即可训练完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动实验室（SDLs）虽已通过闭环自动化变革化学与材料发现，但针对柔软、可变形物质的物理操控基础设施仍超出当前机器人平台的能力范围。开放表面上的自主液滴输运是典型难题：接触角滞后、毛细钉扎与表面非均匀性导致动力学部分可观测，对经典基于模型的控制器构成重大挑战。

Method: 构建双轴倾斜板平台，板面覆有薄硅油膜以驱动液滴，顶置相机提供实时反馈；采用基于模型的强化学习训练控制策略，不使用仿真或解析模型，依据几何复杂度仅需50至150个物理回合完成训练。

Result: 平台展示三项对SDL领域有价值的能力：零样本迁移至未见几何构型且表现稳健；液滴粘滞时自主发现振荡去钉扎策略以释放液滴；完整训练流程在90分钟内完成。

Conclusion: 该工作将强化学习操控从刚性微机器人拓展至可变形软物质体系，为下一代自驱动实验室提供了新的自动化操控能力。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) are transforming chemical and materials discovery through closed-loop automation, yet automated infrastructure for physical manipulation of soft, deformable matter remains beyond current robotic platforms. A critical instance is autonomous droplet transport on an open surface, where contact-angle hysteresis, capillary pinning, and surface heterogeneity produce partially observable dynamics that pose significant challenges for classical model-based controllers. We introduce the first robotic platform for closed-loop autonomous liquid droplet navigation on an open, unconfined surface using model-based reinforcement learning. A two-axis tilting board coated with a thin silicone oil film drives the droplet, while an overhead camera provides real-time feedback. A learned policy was trained on just 50 to 150 physical episodes depending on geometric complexity, without simulation or analytical models. Beyond performance alone, the platform demonstrates three capabilities of interest to the SDL community: it robustly transfers zero-shot to unseen geometries; it autonomously discovers an oscillatory depinning strategy to free the droplet when it sticks; and it completes its full training pipeline in under 90 minutes. These results extend reinforcement-learning manipulation from rigid microrobots to deformable soft-matter systems for next-generation SDLs.

</details>


### [6] [CIRRA: Dual-Level Continual Instruction Reconciliation with Ongoing Execution for Embodied Robot Agents in Interactive Household Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08862)
*Ci Zhang,Enfu Nan,Arman Akbari,Lin Zhao,Li Wang,Chen Wang,Weiwei Chen,Yanzhi Wang,Geng Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出持续指令协调（CIRRA）框架，通过语义推理与结构约束双层级整合，使家庭机器人在执行任务时能无缝融入新指令，并发布CHIRP基准验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人需在执行进行中任务的同时接纳新用户指令，但现有智能体常重新生成或大幅修改剩余任务序列，导致计划歧义、逻辑不一致和冗余执行，因此需要一种保持结构连续性的指令协调方法。

Method: CIRRA采用双层级框架：结合基于大语言模型的语义推理与规则约束的结构整合。首先将新指令落地为唯一可执行技能，并解决欠指定的动作与执行位置；随后保留进行中子任务序列作为执行主干，通过将新子任务插入位置匹配的片段生成整合候选；语义推理器仅评估被修改片段以识别依赖与冲突，并选择逻辑最连贯的局部整合方案。

Result: 在CHIRP基准上，CIRRA达到74.2%的决策一致率，比最强重规划基线高出30个百分点，且每个正确的融合决策都产生位置正确、无冲突的调度。在Unitree G1人形机器人上，CIRRA在每次试验中都能在正确时机中断进行中技能，并在所有指标上显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 结构保持式的指令协调能维持与进行中执行的对齐，缓解歧义和不一致，并通过复用共享子任务减少冗余执行，验证了CIRRA框架在文本基准与真实人形机器人上的有效性。

Abstract: Household robots must accommodate new user instructions while executing ongoing tasks. Existing agents often regenerate or extensively revise the remaining task sequence, introducing plan ambiguity, logical inconsistency, and redundant execution. We formulate continual instruction reconciliation and propose CIRRA (Continual Instruction Reconciliation for Robot Agents), a dual-level framework combining LLM-based semantic reasoning with rule-constrained structural integration. CIRRA first grounds incoming instructions to unique executable skills and resolves underspecified actions and execution locations. It then preserves the ongoing subtask sequence as an execution backbone and generates integration candidates by inserting incoming subtasks into location-matched segments. The semantic reasoner evaluates only modified segments to identify dependencies and conflicts and select the most logically coherent local integration. This structure-preserving process maintains alignment with ongoing execution, mitigates ambiguity and inconsistency, and reuses shared subtasks to reduce redundant execution. We also introduce CHIRP (Continual Household Instruction Reconciliation and Planning), a text-based benchmark of 120 episodes across eight household environments and six categories of everyday activities. On CHIRP, CIRRA achieves 74.2% decision agreement, exceeding the strongest replanning baseline by 30 percentage points; every correct fusion decision yields a correctly placed, conflict-free schedule. On a Unitree G1 humanoid, CIRRA interrupts ongoing skills at the correct moment in every trial and significantly outperforms all baselines on every metric.

</details>


### [7] [Trajectory Planning without Trajectory Data: A Manifold-Guided Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08863)
*Silong Yong,Anji Liu,Cunxi Dai,Carl Busart,Guanya Shi,Yilun Du,Katia Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Ariadne，通过学习状态空间流形并利用其几何结构进行轨迹规划，仅需状态观测即可泛化到未见起止约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹规划方法依赖生成模型与专家轨迹，存在监督成本高、随序列长度扩展性差、难以泛化到新约束（如新颖起止对）等问题。

Method: 学习状态空间底层流形，并在所学流形上构建满足约束的轨迹；训练仅需状态观测，无需轨迹监督。

Result: 在Maze2D与机器人运动规划基准上，Ariadne能从纯状态监督构建可行路径并泛化到未见起止组合；在高维双臂规划中与轨迹监督方法及经典规划器保持竞争力，且训练无需轨迹数据。

Conclusion: 利用状态空间流形几何进行规划可规避轨迹监督瓶颈，实现无需轨迹数据的可泛化轨迹规划。

Abstract: A common way for trajectory planning is to leverage generative models trained on large collections of expert trajectories. At inference time, the model generates executable trajectories by conditioning on task goal constraints. However, trajectory-based methods rely on costly supervision, scale poorly with sequence length, and often generalize poorly to unseen constraints such as novel start-goal pairs. We propose an alternative to learn the underlying state-space manifold and use the geometry of the manifold for trajectory planning. This approach requires only state observations and enables generalization to unseen constraints by con- structing trajectories on the learned manifold of the state space. Experiments on Maze2D and robotic motion-planning benchmarks show that Ariadne constructs feasible paths from state-only supervision and generalizes to unseen start-goal combinations. On high-dimensional dual-arm planning, it remains competitive with trajectory-supervised and classical planners, while requiring no trajectory data for training.

</details>


### [8] [From Wearable Interfaces to Dexterous Policies: Contact Shifts and Tactile Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08870)
*Ruitong Tian,Fang Xu,Noah B. Wilson,Xianyao Li,Eric Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 可穿戴外骨骼接口的几何设计会影响触觉记录内容与演示质量，进而显著影响所训练策略的性能，因此该硬件应被视为机器人学习数据生成过程的一部分加以评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴遥操作接口让操作者用自身手部动作直接与任务物体交互来采集灵巧演示，降低了对目标机器人的依赖，但也使采集硬件成为生成演示的物理过程的一部分。接口几何可能同时影响任务执行方式与可记录的触觉观测，但此前缺乏对"接口设计—触觉数据—策略性能"这一链条的系统研究。

Method: 对比同一 DexUMI 系列外骨骼的两个版本：二者共享相同的机器人指令定义、映射流程与触觉模块类型，仅手部几何不同（基线版 vs 改进版）。在四个任务上（开盖扭转、鸡蛋盒开启、USB 插入、焊接工具拾放）开展用户评估与触觉记录分析，并分别以二值聚合输入和空间+力输入训练匹配策略，比较不同接口演示所训练策略的成功率。

Result: 改进版接口降低了报告的身体负荷、提升了部分设备评分，并在基线版被机械性阻挡的精细抓取中实现了触觉接入；记录的接触变化具有任务依赖性——开盖扭转主要表现为接触位置变化，鸡蛋盒开启主要表现为接触发生与否的变化。在开盖扭转与鸡蛋盒开启任务上，基于改进版演示的策略成功率高于基线版，且存在显著的接口汇总效应；在全部四个任务上，空间+力输入均优于二值聚合输入。

Conclusion: 可穿戴采集硬件是机器人学习数据生成过程的组成部分。对此类接口的评估不应仅停留在操作者体验层面，还应考察其所使能记录的触觉交互以及其演示所支撑的策略性能。

Abstract: Unlike conventional teleoperation, wearable interfaces allow operators to collect dexterous demonstrations through their own hand motions while directly interacting with task objects. This direct interaction reduces dependence on the target robot during collection, but it also makes the collection hardware part of the physical process that generates each demonstration. Interface geometry can influence both how a task is performed and what tactile observations are recorded for learning. We study two versions of a DexUMI-family exoskeleton that share the same robot command definition, mapping procedure, and tactile module type but differ in hand-side geometry. The revised interface reduces reported physical demand, improves selected device ratings, enables tactile access in a precision grasp that is mechanically blocked by the baseline, and produces task-dependent changes in recorded contact. Lid twisting primarily exhibits a change in contact location, whereas egg carton opening primarily exhibits a change in contact occurrence. We then train matched policies using either a binary aggregate input or a spatial-plus-force input. On lid twisting and egg carton opening, policies trained on revised-interface demonstrations achieve higher success than those trained on baseline demonstrations, with a significant pooled interface effect. Across these and two additional tasks, USB insertion and soldering tool pick and place, the spatial-plus-force input likewise outperforms binary aggregation. These results show that wearable collection hardware is part of the data-generation process for robot learning. Such interfaces should therefore be evaluated not only through operator experience, but also through the tactile interactions they make recordable and the policy performance their demonstrations support.

</details>


### [9] [Toward Evidence-Driven Human-Agent-Robot Teaming for Earth-Independent Anomaly Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08933)
*Ignacio G Lopez-Francos,Alexis Gallagher,Samira Shalal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向深空人类-智能体-机器人协同的证据驱动异常分诊架构，将AI智能体定位为有界、可审查服务之上的状态化协调者，通过硬件在环原型验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深空乘组无法依赖地面实时支持应对紧急异常事件。初始警报往往不能确定根本原因，而具有判别力的证据可能存在于乘组观察中，或位于不安全、代价高昂、无法供乘组检查的位置。因此需要一种地球独立的异常分诊机制来协调人类、AI与机器人获取证据。

Method: 提出证据驱动的人类-智能体-机器人团队架构，将Agentic AI视为针对有界、可探查服务的状态化协调者，而非完全自主的载具控制器。系统包含：分诊状态管理器（维护假设、证据出处、不确定性、操作上下文与工具状态）；面向乘组的具身智能体（引出观察并解释评估变化）；移动机器人（获取针对性、局部化证据）。采用类型化接口将对话与编排同监控、机器人指令、上下文检索及安全关键控制相分离。

Result: 通过两个场景进行说明：一是基于真实ISS氨气误报的载人深空任务，其中疑似污染限制了乘组进入；二是载人月球基地的电源接口异常，通过机器人检查将局部连接器故障与远程遥测中模糊的其他原因区分开。并在使用Reachy Mini与Innate MARS移动机器人的硬件在环集成原型中进行了演练。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是一种权限有界的闭环证据环路架构，并在硬件在环集成原型中加以验证，为深空独立异常分诊提供了可行的人机协同方案。

Abstract: Deep-space crews cannot rely on real-time ground support for urgent off-nominal events. Initial alerts may underdetermine cause, while discriminating evidence may reside in crew observations or at locations that are unsafe, costly, or unavailable for crew inspection. We present an evidence-driven architecture for human-agent-robot teaming in Earth-independent anomaly triage. Agentic AI is treated as a stateful coordinator over bounded, inspectable services rather than as a fully autonomous vehicle controller. A triage state manager maintains hypotheses, evidence provenance, uncertainty, operational context, and tool status; a crew-facing embodied agent elicits observations and explains assessment changes; and a mobile robot acquires targeted, localized evidence. Typed interfaces separate dialogue and orchestration from monitoring, robot command, context retrieval, and safety-critical control. Two scenarios illustrate the architecture: a crewed deep-space mission based on an actual ISS ammonia false alarm, where suspected contamination restricts crew access, and a power-interface anomaly at a crewed lunar base, where robotic inspection distinguishes a local connector fault from other causes ambiguous in remote telemetry. Our main contribution is an authority-bounded closed evidence-loop architecture, exercised in a hardware-in-the-loop integration prototype using Reachy Mini and an Innate MARS mobile robot.

</details>


### [10] [HULK: Learning Whole-Body Forceful Loco-Manipulation for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08970)
*An Dang,Arturo Flores Alvarez,Yu-Ming Chen,Conor Mc Gartoll,Helen Sun,Aaron Ames,Nima Fazeli,Manikantan Nambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HULK全身控制框架，用MPC引导强化学习并蒸馏双教师策略，实现人形机器人负重搬运的强力全身操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型重物会改变人形机器人质心并在上半身施加持续负载，导致平衡与指令跟踪困难，现有方法难以应对这类强力全身交互任务。

Method: 以MPC引导强化学习并引入负载动力学预测，训练两个教师：一个在腕部受力下跟踪手臂运动，另一个将大型物体抱于身前进行移动；训练时用捕获点控制屏障函数（CBF）增强腕力教师以改善负载平衡；最后将两个教师蒸馏为单一策略。

Result: 在仿真中，带屏障函数的教师在每臂10 kg负载下取得最低的前向与侧向速度跟踪误差，并将聚合DCM偏移幅值较仅用MPC引导的强化学习降低35.7%；腕力教师可承受高达130 N的躯干推力扰动；并在Unitree G1上完成评估。

Conclusion: HULK通过MPC引导强化学习、CBF增强与双教师蒸馏，有效提升人形机器人重载全身操作中的平衡与指令跟踪能力，并具备真机可迁移性。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation of large, heavy objects demands forceful interaction across the entire body. However, such payloads shift a humanoid's center of mass and impose sustained loads across the upper body, challenging balance and command tracking. We present HULK, a whole-body control framework for forceful loco-manipulation. Using model predictive control (MPC) to guide reinforcement learning with predictions of the loaded dynamics, we train two teachers: one tracks arm motions under wrist forces, and the other locomotes while holding large objects against the body. A capture-point control barrier function augments the wrist-force teacher during training to improve balance under load. We distill both teachers into a single policy. Evaluation spans simulation and the Unitree G1. In simulation, the teacher with the barrier function achieves the lowest forward and lateral velocity tracking errors at 10 kg per arm among evaluated controllers and reduces aggregate divergent component of motion (DCM) excursion magnitude by 35.7% relative to MPC-guided reinforcement learning alone. Our wrist-force teacher withstands torso push disturbances of up to 130 N.

</details>


### [11] [PhysEvo: Astra Can Act, Let It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08995)
*Wenqing Tian,Zeyu Zhang,Zhaocheng Liu,Fengwei Liu,Qiang Liu,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysEvo：围绕单一冻结模型进行物理递归自我改进的框架，通过任务智能体执行与元智能体诊断修订工具技能，实现无需权重更新的具身操作能力持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Astra等模型虽具备行动能力，但可靠操作依赖其观察与控制世界的系统；现有方法往往依赖模型权重更新或单独训练的动作策略，难以在冻结模型下实现持续、可复用的自我改进。

Method: 提出PhysEvo框架：由一个执行机器人任务的任务智能体和一个利用轨迹诊断失败、修订工具与技能并检验修正的元智能体组成；元智能体还能改进自身诊断工具，使保留的修订既支持后续行动也支持后续自我改进。全过程不更新模型权重，也不单独训练动作策略。

Result: 在42项RoboDojo任务上，保留的任务特定部署版本在留出布局评估中取得五维平均分68.14/100、成功率62.00%，对比最强公开参考RoboDawn一次性Astra智能体的47.17%；在8项对直接Astra具有挑战的操作任务上成功率55.00%，对比1.25%。将仿真进化得到的harness部署到AgileX PiPER并继续技能修订，在5项真实任务25次试验中取得平均分90.60/100、成功率84.00%。

Conclusion: PhysEvo把行动后果转化为对冻结模型“如何行动与如何改进”的持久、可检验的改变，无需模型权重更新即可实现物理递归自我改进，并在仿真与真实平台上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Astra can act, yet reliable manipulation depends on the system through which it observes and controls the world. We introduce PhysEvo, a framework for physical recursive self-improvement (RSI) around a single frozen model. A task agent executes robot tasks; a meta-agent uses the resulting trajectories to diagnose failures, revise tools and skills, and test corrections. The meta-agent can also improve its own diagnostic tools, so retained revisions support both later action and later self-improvement. This process develops joint-level control, evidence-seeking observation, and reusable manipulation skills without model-weight updates or a separately trained action policy. Across 42 RoboDojo tasks, held-out-layout evaluation of retained task-specific deployment versions yields a five-dimension average score of 68.14/100 and 62.00% success, compared with 47.17% for RoboDawn's one-shot Astra agent, the strongest published reference in our comparison. On eight manipulation tasks challenging direct Astra, PhysEvo achieves 55.00% success, compared with 1.25% for the direct-Astra reference. Deploying the simulation-evolved harness on AgileX PiPER and continuing skill revision yields 90.60/100 average score and 84.00% success across 25 trials on five real-world tasks. PhysEvo turns the consequences of action into persistent, testable changes to how a frozen model acts and improves.

</details>


### [12] [MimicX: Policy-in-the-Loop Supervision Refinement for Video-Driven Humanoid Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09055)
*Shuaijun Liu,Chenglong Zhang,Xuhao Liu,Feiyang You,Yifan Liao,Shuyang Hao,Chaozhe Zhang,Chengyu Wu,Zhen Sun,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MimicX 是一个策略在环框架，利用执行反馈优化视频驱动的人形动作跟踪：定位困难过渡与受影响身体区域，联合调整跟踪目标与重置课程，并通过重复 rollout 验证筛选改进。四项核心任务上平均降低 25.7% 身体跟踪误差，执行时域提升 255.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频为人形学习提供了丰富的运动目标，但视觉上合理的参考在基于物理的执行中仍会产生持续性失败。这些失败揭示了训练监督应当调整的位置，而现有方法缺乏利用执行反馈来修正视频驱动跟踪的机制。

Method: 提出 MimicX，一个 policy-in-the-loop 框架。从重建并重定向的运动出发，定位困难过渡和受影响身体区域，联合自适应调整跟踪目标与用于策略延续的重置课程（reset curriculum）；通过重复 rollout 验证，在跟踪约束（tracking guards）下选择以执行为优先的改进。此外，MimicX-HLoop 通过异构执行加速反馈。

Result: 在四项核心视频任务上，MimicX 相对 Fixed Reference 基线持续提升跟踪精度与 Robust Execution Horizon。任务平均结果：身体跟踪误差降低 25.7%，执行时域增加 255.6%。额外的视频、运动参考与碰撞场景研究评估了核心任务之外的表现。

Conclusion: MimicX 将策略失败转化为可操作的监督信号，用于决定应优化什么以及保留哪种优化，从而实现更稳健的视频驱动人形动作跟踪。

Abstract: Human videos provide rich motion targets for humanoid learning, yet visually plausible references can still produce persistent failures under physics-based execution. These failures reveal where training supervision should change. We present MimicX, a policy-in-the-loop framework that uses execution feedback to refine video-driven humanoid motion tracking. Starting from reconstructed and retargeted motion, MimicX localizes difficult transitions and affected body regions, then jointly adapts tracking objectives and the reset curriculum for policy continuation. Repeated rollout verification selects execution-priority improvements subject to tracking guards. Across four core video tasks, MimicX consistently improves tracking accuracy and Robust Execution Horizon relative to the Fixed Reference baseline. Task-averaged results show a 25.7% reduction in body-tracking error and a 255.6% increase in execution horizon. Additional video, motion-reference, and collision-scene studies evaluate the method beyond the core tasks, while MimicX-HLoop accelerates feedback through heterogeneous execution. Overall, MimicX turns policy failure into actionable supervision for deciding what to refine and which refinement to retain.

</details>


### [13] [Workhorse: Learning Robust Whole-Body Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from Human Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09117)
*Songbo Hu,Qiayuan Liao,Yufeng Chi,Kevin Zakka,Yakun Sophia Shao,Pieter Abbeel,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Workhorse 通过无机器人的人类演示数据，分别训练视觉规划器与强化学习全身跟踪器，并互相注入对方部署误差进行数据增强，在真实 Unitree G1 与仿真中实现接触丰富的人形全身操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人难以仅凭第一视角 RGB 和本体感觉规划接触丰富的全身操作；且依赖机器人遥操作或重定向（retargeting）获取数据成本高、泛化差。作者希望直接从无机器人的人类演示中学习此类操作，避免重定向带来的误差与复杂性。

Method: 采用双策略解耦框架：视觉规划器预测五连杆目标（躯干、双腕、双脚位姿），强化学习全身跟踪器在机器人上跟随这些目标。两策略分别在相同的录制人类姿态上独立训练，无需重定向。关键方法是相互误差模仿的数据增强：各自训练时加入对另一个策略在部署时会产生误差的模仿，以弥合规划与跟踪之间的分布偏差。

Result: 在真实 Unitree G1 上完成用双手和踢腿分拣箱子、接住抛来的箱子、推倒并爬上行李箱。分拣任务中展示了被人推或拿走箱子后的恢复能力。仿真复现房间中，分拣在 77% 回合完成，在 40 N·s 推力下为 64%；用相同演示重训两策略后，另一台仿真人形在无推力下分拣成功率达 83%。

Conclusion: 直接从人类演示训练规划器与跟踪器、并通过互相误差数据增强，可使人形机器人实现接触丰富的全身操作，并具备一定的抗扰动恢复能力与跨机器人可复现性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots still struggle to plan contact-rich whole-body manipulation from egocentric RGB and proprioception. Workhorse learns such manipulation from robot-free human demonstrations. A visual planner predicts five-link targets: the poses of the torso, both wrists, and both feet. A reinforcement-learning whole-body tracker follows them on the robot. Both policies train separately on the same recorded human poses, without retargeting. We augment the training data of each policy to imitate the errors that the other makes at deployment. On a real Unitree G1, Workhorse sorts boxes with its hands and a kick, catches a thrown box, and topples and climbs a suitcase. During box sorting, we show recoveries after a person pushes the robot or takes the box away. In a simulated copy of the demonstration room, the system completes box sorting in 77% of episodes, and in 64% under 40 N.s pushes. With both policies retrained from the same demonstrations, a simulated second humanoid completes box sorting in 83% of episodes without pushes.

</details>


### [14] [FlashNeRD: Performance-First Contact-Rich Neural Robot Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09130)
*Mohammadmehdi Ataei,Mohammad Amin Nabian,Jie Xu,Miles Macklin,Yashraj Narang,Eric Heiden,Octi Zhang,Yunrong Guo,Ruben D'Sa,Oliver Hennigh,Gavriel State,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlashNeRD 通过并行流式架构、任意位置接触编码器和双向耦合，解决了 NeRD 的三大局限，实现更快更准的机器人动力学学习与规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeRD 将碰撞检测保持为解析方法、用学习模型替换机器人数值动力学，但与所学习的模拟器相比加速有限、仅在预定义点接受接触、且与被操作物体无双向耦合，这三点限制其应用。

Method: 提出 FlashNeRD：采用并行流式架构使每次预测更快更准；设计可接受任意位置接触的编码器；实现与解析求解器模拟物体的双向耦合。

Result: 在五种机器人上动力学更快更准、策略学习更快、推理时规划更快。三种机器人上模型比优化 NeRD 快至多 55 倍且长程滚动更准；PPO 训练 ANYmal 运动策略仅 34 秒，比解析模拟器快 3.8 倍、比优化 NeRD 快 2.7 倍；DIAL-MPC 下机器人攀爬全部六个测试平台（固定接触 NeRD 仅一个），规划时间约为解析模拟器一半；用 FlashNeRD 训练的立方体重定向策略完成量在模拟器训练策略目标数的 2% 以内。

Conclusion: FlashNeRD 通过并行流式架构、通用接触编码与双向耦合，显著提升学习动力学模型的精度与速度，并有效加速策略学习与采样规划。

Abstract: Compared with analytical physics, learned dynamics models promise robot simulation that is faster, inherently differentiable, and easily adaptable to real data. Neural Robot Dynamics (NeRD) pursues this by keeping collision detection analytical and replacing a simulator's numerical dynamics for the robot with a learned model. Three limitations remain. NeRD offers little speedup over the simulator it learned from, accepts contact only at predefined points, and has no two-way coupling with objects it manipulates. FlashNeRD removes all three with a parallel streaming architecture that makes each prediction faster and more accurate, an encoder that accepts contacts wherever they occur, and two-way coupling with objects simulated by analytical solvers. Experiments across five robots show faster and more accurate dynamics, faster policy learning, and faster inference-time planning. Across three robots, FlashNeRD's dynamics model is up to $55\times$ faster than an optimized NeRD and more accurate over long rollouts. This speedup extends to policy learning, where PPO trains an ANYmal locomotion policy in 34 seconds, $3.8\times$ faster than the analytical simulator and $2.7\times$ faster than an optimized NeRD. With DIAL-MPC, a sampling-based MPC method, the robot climbs all six test platforms where fixed-contact NeRD manages one, at approximately half the analytical simulator's planning time. Cube-reorientation policies trained with FlashNeRD complete within 2% of the simulator-trained policy's target count.

</details>


### [15] [World Models Dream of Success: Diagnosing and Repairing Failure Insensitivity in Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09134)
*Jiuyi Xu,Xiao Hu,Meida Chen,Peng Gao,Yang Ye,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出CureWM，通过构造并执行验证的反事实失败数据，对已发布的世界模型检查点进行事后微调，显著降低其对失败动作的乐观预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人世界模型需区分成功与失败动作，但现有已发布检查点对动作变化不敏感，且在真实失败上仍给出"成功"预测；尽管已有工作将失败纳入训练，仍不清楚何种数据能在不改变架构和目标的前提下修复已发布模型。

Method: CureWM从成功演示出发，在严重度网格上构造替代动作，并在仿真或硬件上执行以验证结果，随后将失败样本、存活成功样本与常规演示一起微调已发布模型，从而在共享初始情境下形成受控的动作对比。

Result: 在484个LIBERO失败反事实上，微调官方数据后乐观率由80%降至四个CureWM模型的30–43%（均值38%）；真实机械臂两项评估中，被潜距离诊断判为"像成功"的失败预测由90%降至33%；在失败数、成功回放数据和训练预算匹配下，反事实失败的成败价值差为0.124，而新采集的同策略失败仅为0.014。

Conclusion: 执行验证的反事实回放可用于事后修复世界模型，且降低乐观偏差必须与成败判别能力联合评估。代码已开源。

Abstract: Robot world models support policy evaluation, planning, and synthetic data generation, but these applications require predictions that distinguish successful actions from failures. Across four released checkpoints from two architecture families, we observe weak sensitivity to action changes and success-like predictions on verified failures. Although recent work incorporates failures into model training, which data can repair released checkpoints without changing their architecture or training objective still remains underexplored. To this end, we introduce CureWM, which constructs alternative actions from successful demonstrations across a severity grid, verifies their outcomes through execution in simulation or on hardware, and fine-tunes released models on the resulting failures and surviving successes alongside nominal demonstrations. This construction provides controlled action contrasts from shared starting contexts. On 484 held-out LIBERO failure counterfactuals, optimism falls from 80\% after fine-tuning on the official data to 30--43\% across four independently fine-tuned CureWM models (38\% mean). In two separate evaluations on a physical robot arm, failure predictions scored as success-like by a latent-distance diagnostic decrease from 90\% after fine-tuning on successful demonstrations alone to 33\% with CureWM. With failure counts per task, successful replay data, and training budget matched, counterfactual failures yield a success--failure value gap of 0.124, compared with 0.014 for freshly collected on-policy failures. These findings support execution-verified counterfactual replay for post-hoc repair and show why reduced optimism must be evaluated alongside success--failure discrimination. Code is available at https://github.com/jiuyixu25/CureWM.

</details>


### [16] [A Unified Kinematic Representation Enables Reusable Biological Joint Moment Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09150)
*Jinwoo Hwang,Ilseung Park,Changseob Song,Vu Phan,Eni Halilaj,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究以关节运动学作为中间表征，解耦硬件特定传感与生物关节力矩估计，仅用开源生物力学数据训练估计器，并在髋、膝外骨骼及IMU等多种平台上验证了跨平台可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的生理状态估计（尤其是生物关节力矩）广泛用于外骨骼控制，但现有估计器通常与特定设备的传感器配置耦合，限制了控制器迁移以及开源生物力学数据集的使用。

Method: 提出以关节运动学作为中间表征，将硬件特定传感与下游生物关节力矩估计解耦；仅使用开源生物力学数据训练一个基于关节角度与角速度的关节力矩估计器，并在髋外骨骼、膝外骨骼及IMU传感套件采集的运动学数据上评估，涵盖平地行走、上坡与下坡行走。

Result: 使用髋外骨骼运动学时RMSE为0.17 Nm/kg、R²为0.79；使用膝外骨骼运动学时RMSE为0.19 Nm/kg、R²为0.62；使用IMU导出的双侧髋、膝、踝关节运动学时RMSE为0.15 Nm/kg、R²为0.85。

Conclusion: 关节运动学使仅在开源数据上训练的估计器能够跨不同可穿戴平台运行。该框架有望减少目标设备的数据采集需求，并支持面向外骨骼控制与可穿戴生物力学的可迁移生物关节力矩估计。

Abstract: Objective: Data-driven models that estimate physiological states, particularly biological joint moments, are widely used in exoskeleton control. However, these estimators are often coupled to device-specific sensor configurations, limiting controller transfer and the use of open-source biomechanics datasets. Methods: We proposed joint kinematics as an intermediate representation that decouples hardware-specific sensing from downstream biological joint moment estimation. A joint-moment estimator using joint angles and angular velocities was trained exclusively on open-source biomechanics data and evaluated using kinematics from a hip exoskeleton, knee exoskeleton, and inertial measurement unit (IMU) sensor suite during level-ground, ramp-ascent, and ramp-descent walking. Results: The estimator achieved an root mean square error (RMSE) of 0.17 Nm/kg and coefficient of determination (R2) of 0.79 using hip exoskeleton kinematics, 0.19 Nm/kg and 0.62 using knee exoskeleton kinematics, and 0.15 Nm/kg and 0.85 using kinematics derived from IMUs across bilateral hip, knee, and ankle joints. Conclusion: Joint kinematics enabled an estimator trained only on open-source data to operate across distinct wearable platforms. Significance: This framework may reduce target-device data collection and support transferable biological joint moment estimation for exoskeleton control and wearable biomechanics.

</details>


### [17] [geodex: A Library for Motion Planning on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09165)
*Phone Thiha Kyaw,Ben Wei,Sepehr Samavi,Miguel Angel Rogel Garcia,Jonathan Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 geodex，一个开源 C++20 库（含 Python 绑定），在统一采样规划接口下将流形、黎曼度量、retraction 与采样器解耦为可互换组件，支持在配置相关的黎曼度量下进行避障运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器人配置空间内在几何（曲率及配置相关代价）规划的轨迹，比在平坦环境度量下规划的轨迹更短、更节能、更自然。然而现有流形优化库虽提供丰富几何原语，却不支持避障规划；通用运动规划库虽支持多种状态空间与自定义距离函数，却未将配置相关黎曼度量作为驱动距离、插值与测地线的几何结构。

Method: geodex 将流形、其黎曼度量、retraction 与采样器设计为独立且可互换的组件，并通过单一采样式运动规划接口暴露。同一规划器无需修改即可运行于 R^n、T^n、S^n、矩阵李群（SO(2)、SE(2)、SO(3)、SE(3)）、其乘积空间以及多关节机器人配置空间，且各空间均可配备用户自定义黎曼度量。

Result: 同一规划器在上述多种标准空间与关节配置空间上无需改动即可运行；库公开提供，并附带文档、测试与可复现的基准测试套件。

Conclusion: geodex 填补了流形优化库与通用运动规划库之间的空白，为配置相关黎曼度量下的避障规划提供了模块化、可扩展且可复现的开源工具。

Abstract: Planning motions that respect the intrinsic geometry of a robot's configuration space, including its curvature and a configuration-dependent notion of cost, yields shorter, lower-energy, and more natural trajectories than planning under the ambient flat metric. Existing libraries for optimization on manifolds provide rich geometric primitives but do not plan around obstacles. While general-purpose motion planning libraries support many state spaces and custom distance functions, they do not yet treat a configuration-dependent Riemannian metric as the geometry that drives distance, interpolation, and geodesics. We present geodex, an open-source C++20 library with Python bindings. The library exposes the manifold, its Riemannian metric, the retraction, and the sampler as independent, interchangeable components through a single sampling-based motion planning interface. The same planner runs unchanged on canonical spaces $\mathbb{R}^n$, $\mathbb{T}^n$, $\mathbb{S}^n$, matrix Lie groups such as $SO(2)$, $SE(2)$, $SO(3)$, and $SE(3)$, products of these spaces, and articulated-robot configuration spaces, each equipped with a user-defined Riemannian metric. We make geodex publicly available with documentation, tests, and a reproducible benchmark suite.

</details>


### [18] [Beyond Reconstruction: What Matters in Action Tokenization for Robot Policies?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09170)
*Haoran Chen,Jingtian Ji,Samuel Wheeler,Kaylene Caswell Stocking,Matthew Walter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ProAct，一种将动作分词器训练目标从纯重建扩展为兼顾可预测性与鲁棒性的方法，显著提升下游策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归动作 token 策略依赖动作分词器将离散 token 序列解码为连续精确控制动作。现有分词器多通过重建目标学习 token-动作映射，但作者分析发现：精确重建只是下游机器人策略成功的必要条件之一。策略还需能为新观测预测正确 token，且未见过的 token 预测仍能解码为合理动作。这两点属于分词器训练的下游属性，无法被单一重建目标直接激励。

Method: 提出 ProAct（Predictable and Robust Action Tokenization）：在重建目标基础上策略性地增广训练信号，以提升下游可预测性与鲁棒性。该方法对策略无关（policy-agnostic），仅使用动作数据集训练。

Result: 在 Robomimic、LIBERO、RoboTwin 三个基准及多种分词器架构上，ProAct 平均提升 rollout 成功率 11.3 个百分点；迁移至视觉-语言-动作策略平均增益 21.8 个百分点，真实机器人操作平均增益 36.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 有效的动作分词应被设计为一种策略接口，在保真度、可预测性与鲁棒性之间取得平衡，而非仅视为重建问题。

Abstract: Autoregressive action-token policies such as vision-language-action models require action tokenizers to translate discrete token sequences into precise control actions in continuous space. Many action tokenizers learn the mapping between tokens and actions via a reconstruction objective. However, as we show through extensive analysis, sufficiently accurate action reconstruction is only one part of what makes a downstream robot policy successful. It is also critical that the policy is able to predict the right tokens for new observations, and that unseen policy token predictions still decode into reasonable actions. These properties are downstream of tokenizer training and are not directly incentivized by a reconstruction objective alone. In this work, we introduce Predictable and Robust Action Tokenization (ProAct), a tokenizer training method that strategically augments reconstruction with the goal of improving downstream predictability and robustness. ProAct is policy-agnostic and uses only action datasets for training. Across the Robomimic, LIBERO, and RoboTwin benchmarks and a diverse set of tokenizer architectures, ProAct improves rollout success by an average of 11.3 percentage points. These improvements also translate to vision-language-action policies and real-world robotic manipulation, yielding average gains of 21.8 and 36.7 percentage points, respectively. These results suggest that effective action tokenization should be designed as a policy interface that balances fidelity, predictability, and robustness, rather than as a reconstruction problem alone.

</details>


### [19] [CAP: Codebook-Aligned Prediction for Tokenized Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09178)
*Haoran Chen,Jingtian Ji,Samuel Wheeler,Kaylene Caswell Stocking,Matthew Walter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Codebook-Aligned Prediction (CAP)，直接复用动作 tokenizer 的码本向量作为策略分类原型，从而保留 tokenizer 学到的潜在结构。在四类量化器、三个仿真基准和两个真机任务上，CAP 均优于标准的 token 分类头，且收益并非来自更高的 token 准确率，而是源于潜在结构信息使动作空间的预测误差更良性并改善了策略骨干的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 tokenizer 的策略通常忽视 tokenizer 学到的潜在码本结构：token 化之后，策略把 token 当作互不相关的类别索引，从零学习新的分类器。作者认为这一被丢弃的结构信息其实有价值，应被保留用于下游策略训练。

Method: 提出 Codebook-Aligned Prediction (CAP)：直接将 tokenizer 的码本向量复用为策略的分类原型（class prototypes），而 tokenizer 与策略骨干网络均保持不变。方法在四种量化器家族、三个仿真基准和两个真机任务上进行验证，并与标准 token 分类头对比（tokenizer 及其重建质量固定不变）。

Result: 在四种量化器、三个仿真基准和两个真机任务上，CAP 一致地提升了任务成功率，优于标准 token 分类头。分析表明增益并非由更高的 token 准确率或仅策略头的改变所解释；复用码本为策略提供了关于 tokenizer 学到的跨 token 潜在结构的信息，使 token 预测误差在动作空间中更良性，并改善了策略骨干学到的表示。

Conclusion: 动作 tokenizer 学到的不仅是离散目标，还包含对动作有意义的潜在结构，这些结构在下游策略训练时应当被保留。

Abstract: Action tokenization converts continuous robot actions into discrete symbols that can be modeled autoregressively. However, existing tokenizer-based policies typically ignore the tokenizer's learned latent code structure: after tokenization, the policy treats tokens as unrelated class indices and learns a new classifier from scratch. We show that this discarded structure is valuable. We introduce Codebook-Aligned Prediction (CAP), a method that directly reuses the tokenizer's code vectors as policy class prototypes while leaving the tokenizer and policy backbone otherwise unchanged. Across four quantizer families, three simulation benchmarks, and two real-robot tasks, CAP consistently improves task success over standard token classification heads while holding the tokenizer (and therefore its reconstruction quality) fixed. Our analysis further shows that these gains are not explained by higher token accuracy or changes in the policy head alone. Instead, reusing the tokenizer codebook provides the policy with valuable information about the tokenizer's learned latent structure across tokens, making token prediction errors more benign in action space and improving the representations learned by the policy backbone. These results suggest that action tokenizers learn useful action-aware latent structure beyond discrete targets that should be preserved when training downstream policies.

</details>


### [20] [Embedded Evaluation of Task Admission Coalescing in Decentralized Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09187)
*James Lott,Vahraz Honary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 评估任务准入合并（coalescing）作为控制多机器人任务分配器处理器工作量的机制，兼及目标服务延迟，在CBAA、ACBBA、PI、HIPC上开展AGX Orin、零计算配对及RP2040硬件在环实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态任务场景下分配器通常即时接纳新发布任务，可能对每个新到达任务触发一次分配，造成处理器开销。作者希望以任务准入合并来调控分配器计算负载，同时量化其对目标服务延迟的影响，明确该机制在何种条件下有效。

Method: 以CBAA、ACBBA、PI、HIPC为代表MRTA算法套件；开展三项实验：3000任务AGX Orin（含实测计算延迟）、3000任务零计算配对、96任务Pololu 3pi+ RP2040硬件在环；对比Eager即时接纳与阈值2/4/8任务以及"4任务+10秒等待上界"策略，跨三种任务到达率。

Result: AGX实验中48个条件中的31个实现分配器调用数与处理器工作量下降，节省幅度依分配器而异；稀疏到达下4任务批处理使在线目标平均延迟增加20.08–23.57 s，主要来自准入等待。有界准入在12个分配器-负载条件中的10个降低平均工作量，其中高到达率HIPC降低19.35%而平均延迟仅增1.40 s。RP2040上该权衡更有利：4个匹配的中等到达率HIPC场景中Count b=4使平均工作量降59.85%、服务延迟降39.47%，而对应AGX案例延迟反增27.69%。

Conclusion: 任务准入合并的有效性取决于到达强度、具体分配器行为以及执行平台上计算的相对代价；处理器约束越紧，合并带来的收益越可能超过延迟代价。

Abstract: Multi-robot task allocators in dynamic missions commonly admit newly released tasks immediately, potentially invoking allocation for each new arrival. We evaluate task-admission coalescing as a mechanism for controlling allocator processor work while accounting for target-service latency, using CBAA, ACBBA, PI, and HIPC as a representative MRTA suite. The study comprises a 3,000-mission AGX Orin campaign with measured computation delay, 3,000 paired zero-compute missions, and a 96-mission Pololu 3pi+ RP2040 allocator hardware-in-the-loop campaign. Immediate (Eager) admission is compared with thresholds of two, four, and eight tasks and a four-task policy with a 10-s waiting bound across three arrival rates. Across the AGX experiments, coalescing reduces both allocator calls and processor work in 31 of 48 evaluated conditions, although the magnitude of the saving varies by allocator. The principal cost appears under sparse arrivals, where four-task batching increases online-target mean latency by 20.08-23.57 s, predominantly through admission waiting. Bounded admission reduces mean work in ten of twelve allocator-load conditions, including a 19.35% reduction with a 1.40-s mean latency increase for high-arrival HIPC. RP2040 experiments show that this tradeoff can become more favorable as processor constraints tighten. Across four matched medium-arrival HIPC scenarios, Count b=4 reduces mean RP2040 work by 59.85% and service latency by 39.47%, while the corresponding AGX cases increase latency by 27.69%. These results show that the usefulness of task-admission coalescing depends on arrival intensity, allocator-specific behavior, and the relative cost of computation on the execution platform.

</details>


### [21] [Belief-Space Planning with Planner-Conditioned Estimator Error under Intermittent Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09207)
*Peter M. Donley,Joseph L. Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种信念空间规划方法，预测、传播并惩罚规划器条件化估计误差，在倾转旋翼VTOL舰载着舰仿真中降低终端任务指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分离设计或即插即用的估计器可能获得估计器自身未观测到的状态相关信息，因此即使估计器在其自有信息下最小化均方误差，其误差均值在规划器信息条件下也非零。当未来修正具有间歇性和随机性时，被接受与被拒绝误差均值之间的差异会给修正事件引入额外不确定性，而零均值假设会忽略这些项。

Method: 提出一种信念空间规划方法，沿评估轨迹预测、传播并惩罚规划器条件化的估计误差。采用规划器条件化估计误差模型，将仿射误差动态与保留随机诱导修正不确定性的矩递推相结合。给出在指定工作区间内预测估计误差动态的方法，并将预测误差矩纳入任务加权二次风险目标。

Result: 在倾转旋翼VTOL舰载着舰的滚动时域规划仿真中评估：对命令盲EKF，条件化于规划器信息使误差预测损失相对规划器无感模型降低13.4%，预测能力强烈依赖工作区间与预测时域。在16对闭环试验中，规划器将终端任务指标的median从1.60降至0.99，并能以1.3倍误差预测2s以上的估计误差，而仅用协方差的规划低估达5.7倍。

Conclusion: 利用规划器信息条件化估计误差并进行矩传播，可提升误差预测精度并改善闭环任务性能。

Abstract: Belief-space planners with separately designed or off-the-shelf estimators may have access to state-relevant information the estimator does not observe. Consequently, even an estimator that minimizes mean-squared error under its own information can have a nonzero error mean when conditioned on planner information. When future corrections are intermittent and stochastic, differences between accepted and rejected error means introduce additional uncertainty terms to correction events that zero-mean assumptions ignore. In this paper, we propose a belief-space planning approach that predicts, propagates, and penalizes planner-conditioned estimator error along evaluated trajectories. A planner-conditioned estimator-error model combines affine error dynamics with a moment recursion that preserves stochastically-induced correction uncertainty. We describe a method by which to predict estimator error dynamics within specified operating regimes and incorporate predicted error moments into a task-weighted quadratic risk objective. We evaluate the approach in simulation for a tilt-rotor VTOL landing on a ship deck using receding-horizon planning. We find that conditioning on planner information reduced error-prediction loss for a command-blind EKF by 13.4% relative to a planner-ignorant model, with strongly regime and horizon-dependent forecasting abilities. In a small set of 16 paired closed-loop trials, the planner lowered the median terminal task gauge from 1.60 to 0.99, and predicted estimator error beyond 2s within a factor of 1.3, versus a 5.7-fold underprediction by covariance-only planning.

</details>


### [22] [Fast Planning for Multi-object Multi-target Throwing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09224)
*Zhengming Zhu,Yang Liu,Xiao Gao,Aude Billard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个多物体多目标抛掷（MOMT）统一规划框架，通过离线构建可行集模型+在线快速匹配过滤，在5ms内生成多物体抛掷序列，实现高吞吐量机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抛掷可扩大工作空间并加速物流与仓储流程，但单物体抛掷吞吐量有限。利用拟人手的高自由度实现一次挥动抛掷多个物体（MOMT）可显著提升吞吐量，其核心难点在于快速生成既快又可行的抛掷运动，需在短轨迹时长与短规划时间之间取得复杂权衡。

Method: 分两阶段求解。离线阶段：结合物体倒置飞行动力学与机器人运动学、动力学，构建可行集模型。在线阶段：通过快速解匹配并过滤物体的有效脱离状态与机器人的可行状态，生成可组合成抛掷序列的可行抛掷，耗时小于5ms。在配备多指手的7自由度机械臂上验证。

Result: 仿真中，协调双物体抛掷相比独立单物体规划执行时间最多缩短46%，扩展至三物体时该改进得以保持。真实实验中双物体任务缩短29%，与理论50%的差距归因于抛掷间过渡开销。当目标位置在执行中被随机改变时，系统重新规划并在100ms延迟内成功到达新目标且无需停止机器人。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个MOMT抛掷的统一规划框架，仿真中展示了对多物体的可扩展性，真实世界中验证了双物体任务的可行性，推动了高吞吐量机器人操作的发展。

Abstract: Robot throwing has emerged as a promising technique for improving efficiency in logistics and warehouse automation, by enlarging the workspace and speeding up the process. To significantly increase the throwing system's throughput, we develop strategies for throwing multiple objects in one swipe. Such multi-object multi-target throwing (MOMT) leverages the large degrees of freedom of anthropomorphic hands. The key is to quickly generate fast and feasible throwing motions, which involves a complex trade-off between short trajectory duration and short planning time. We solve this problem in two stages. Offline, we build a model of the feasible set by combining object's inverted flying dynamics and the robot's kinematics and dynamics. Online, we generate feasible throws through fast solution matching and filtering of object's valid detach state and robot's feasible state that can compose sequences of throws in less than 5 ms. We validate the framework on a 7-DoF manipulator equipped with a multi-fingered hand. In simulation, coordinated two-object throwing reduces execution time by up to 46% compared to independent single-object planning, and this improvement is maintained when scaling to three objects. Real-world experiments with two objects confirm a 29% reduction; the remaining gap to the theoretical 50% is attributed to inter-throw transition overhead. When target positions are randomly changed mid-execution, the system re-plans and successfully reaches the new targets within 100 ms latency without stopping the robot. These results establish the first unified planning framework for MOMT throwing -- demonstrating scalability to multiple objects in simulation and real-world feasibility on two-object tasks -- advancing the frontier of high-throughput robotic manipulation. A video summarizing the method and the hardware experiments is available at https://liuyangdh.github.io/momt-video

</details>


### [23] [Co-Evolving Robot Orchestrators and Policies through Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09228)
*Xilun Zhang,Maggie Wang,Erik Bauer,Hong-Xing Yu,Huang Huang,Jiajun Wu,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Robo-COP，让编排器与策略在部署中共同演化，通过自采技能示范、按需微调与验证采纳，将部署变为自我改进飞轮，在仿真与真实任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略在其训练域内有效，但在真实部署中泛化不足。现有 agentic 机器人系统以 VLM 编排器搭配冻结策略，虽能规避策略失败却无法克服失败，策略成为系统瓶颈；而单独微调策略又会使其与原本适配旧行为的编排器脱节。

Method: Robo-COP 在部署过程中让编排器与策略协同演化：从自身执行中筛选（curate）技能示范数据；当这些数据能够解决反复出现的失败时才对策略进行微调；新策略只有在其所训练技能上确有提升后才被采纳。

Result: 在十个 RoboLab 仿真任务上，平均留出集成功率由冻结策略同一框架下的 64.8% 提升至 73.8%，而固定日程微调且无验证仅达 65.8%；在三个真实任务上，留出成功率由 38.3% 提升至 50.0%。

Conclusion: Robo-COP 将部署转化为自我改进飞轮，使机器人在实践中学习，每一轮执行改进都为下一轮学习产生更优数据，从而持续突破冻结策略带来的瓶颈。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies trained on large datasets are capable within their training domains, yet they still fail to generalize to the variety of situations a robot meets in real-world deployment. Agentic robot systems complement the policy with a vision-language model (VLM) orchestrator that learns when to call the policy, how to instruct it, and when to use scripted skills instead. However, because the harness is built around a frozen policy that has limited language steerability, the orchestrator can avoid the policy's failures but never overcome them. The policy becomes the bottleneck of the whole system. Fine-tuning the policy can remove this bottleneck, but updating it alone decouples it from an orchestrator tuned to its old behavior. We propose Robo-COP, in which the orchestrator and policy co-evolve during deployment. Robo-COP curates skill demonstrations from its own executions, fine-tunes the policy when this data can address recurring failures, and adopts each new policy only after it improves the skills it was trained for. Across ten simulated RoboLab tasks, Robo-COP raises mean held-out success from 64.8% to 73.8% over the same harness with a frozen policy, while fine-tuning on a fixed schedule without verification reaches only 65.8%. On three real-world tasks, Robo-COP raises held-out success from 38.3% to 50.0%. Robo-COP turns deployment into a self-improving flywheel in which robots learn by doing, with each improvement in execution producing better data for the next round of learning. Videos and code are available at https://robo-cop.pages.dev/.

</details>


### [24] [RoboRender: Robot-Oriented Video Generation for Visual Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09254)
*Huang Huang,Wensi Ai,Ziyu Chen,Youhui Wang,Zijian Du,Yang Liu,Jiaolong Yang,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboRender 将仿真轨迹转化为照片级真实 RGB 视频用于策略学习，实现零样本真实世界部署，平均成功率 71%，显著优于原始仿真渲染与视觉域随机化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真可大规模低成本生成机器人数据，但受 sim-to-real 视觉差异影响，仿真训练的策略难以迁移到真实世界。现有方法依赖中间表示，会丢失丰富语义信息，或需在部署时增加额外感知模块。

Method: 提出 RoboRender 框架：训练面向机器人的视频生成模型，以仿真深度视频、语言指令和机器人 RGB 掩码视频为条件，在保留仿真几何、机器人运动与动作标签的同时合成真实纹理、背景与干扰物；生成的 RGB 视频与仿真提供的状态和动作配对，用于训练可零样本真实部署的策略。

Result: 在机器人视频测试集上，该视频模型生成质量优于深度条件视频生成基线；在抓取放置、铰接物体操作和移动操作任务中，用 RoboRender 生成数据训练的策略平均成功率达 71%，分别约为原始仿真渲染和常规视觉域随机化的 7.1 倍和 3.6 倍；每条仿真轨迹生成更多视频可提升策略性能，开合任务成功率提升 65 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明生成式视频渲染能有效缓解视觉 sim-to-real 差距，支持零样本策略迁移。项目网站：https://robo-render.github.io/。

Abstract: Simulation enables large-scale, low-cost robot data generation, but policies trained in simulation often fail to transfer to the real world due to the sim-to-real visual discrepancies. Existing approaches often rely on intermediate representations, which can discard rich semantic information or require additional perception modules at deployment. We address this visual sim-to-real gap with RoboRender, a framework that converts simulated trajectories into photorealistic RGB videos for policy learning. RoboRender trains a robot-oriented video generation model conditioned on simulated depth videos, language instructions, and robot RGB mask videos, preserving simulator geometry, robot motion, and action labels while synthesizing realistic textures, backgrounds, and distractors. The generated RGB videos are paired with simulator-provided states and actions to train policies for zero-shot real-world deployment. On robot video test sets, our video model outperforms depth-conditioned video generation baselines in generation quality. In real-world experiments across pick-and-place, articulated-object manipulation, and mobile manipulation tasks, policies trained on RoboRender-generated data achieve a 71% average success rate, outperforming raw simulation renderings and conventional visual domain randomization by approximately 7.1x and 3.6x, respectively. We further show that policy performance improves with more generated videos per simulation trajectory, increasing opening-task success by 65 percentage points. These results demonstrate that generative video rendering mitigates the visual sim-to-real gap for zero-shot policy transfer. Project website: https://robo-render.github.io/.

</details>


### [25] [Evaluating the Transfer of Co-Evolved Communication from 2D to 3D Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09280)
*Fernando Montes-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究考察了两个机器人智能体在离散二维模拟器中共同进化的通信机制，迁移到具有真实物理的三维模拟器后是否能保持其功能角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证在简化2D环境中共同进化出的通信机制，在迁移到更接近真实物理的3D环境后是否仍然保持其功能作用，从而评估仿真环境简化对通信机制有效性的影响。

Method: 将2D模拟器中两个智能体共同进化的通信机制迁移至3D物理模拟器；分析回合时间预算、社会线索以及两个共进化角色之间不对称性的影响；进行8次独立进化运行；并通过控制智能体间的处理顺序排除物理引擎伪影。

Result: 成功率随评估时间预算近似线性增长，在2,000至6,000物理步之间未出现平台期；社会线索在两种模拟器中主要起"拥堵辅助"而非导航引导作用，且在2D中效应更明显；8次独立进化运行显示不对称方向一致但幅度存在差异；控制处理顺序排除了物理引擎伪影。

Conclusion: 基于较短回合的评估可能低估已训练控制器的性能；共进化通信机制在迁移后仍保留部分功能角色，但存在残余性能差距，论文讨论了可能导致该差距的因素。

Abstract: This work examines the transfer of a co-evolved communication mechanism between two robotic agents from a discrete two-dimensional (2D) simulator to a three-dimensional simulator with real physics (3D). The study focuses on whether a communication mechanism co-evolved in a 2D environment retains its functional role after transfer to a 3D physics-based simulator. To support this analysis, the effects of the episode time budget, the social cue, and the asymmetry between the two co-evolved roles were examined. The results indicate that the success rate increased approximately linearly with the evaluated time budgets, with no evidence of a plateau between 2,000 and 6,000 physics steps, suggesting that evaluations based on shorter episodes may underestimate the performance of the trained controllers. In both simulators, the social cue functioned primarily as a jam- assistance mechanism rather than as a navigation guide, although with a more pronounced effect in 2D. Analysis of eight independent evolutionary runs revealed a consistent direction of asymmetry, although its magnitude varied across runs. Controlling the processing order between agents allowed us to rule out an artifact of the physics engine. Finally, the results are discussed in terms of the factors that may contribute to the remaining performance gap observed after transfer.

</details>


### [26] [LACE-CRAFT: Robot Co-Design with Actor Inheritance and Blackboard Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09283)
*Yuhan Wen,Jiawei Wang,Qixuan Zhang,Yusen Qin,Lan Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出LACE-CRAFT机器人协同设计框架：LACE通过继承前任策略状态实现持续学习，CRAFT以多角色协作生成并交叉评审形态-奖励配对方案，并结合生成式扩展将网格转为可编辑articulated模型；在五个locomotion基准上平均得分较D2C高6.4–91.9%，并完成实物原型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人协同设计将形态搜索与策略学习耦合，但对每个新设计从头训练会丢弃已获得的控制经验，导致学习效率低、经验无法复用。

Method: 提出LACE-CRAFT：LACE对比当前机器人上的continued learning与新形态-奖励配对上的策略适应，恢复在位者的完整学习状态，并用其actor参数与观测统计初始化兼容挑战者，由固定任务指标在两条分支及冻结在位者间选择；CRAFT通过共享实验记录与行为回放协调Feedback、Morphology、Reward、Integration四个角色，生成并交叉评审配对方案；生成式扩展将生成网格转换为带关节、执行器接口并可一致更新仿真资产的可编辑articulated模型。

Result: 在五个locomotion基准、三个评估种子上，平均得分较D2C高6.4–91.9%。两种方法均在五轮中训练30个新形态-奖励配对，LACE额外使用四个continuation training单元。五任务消融考察了策略继承与回放反馈。制作的原型演示了室内行走并展示了从几何到硬件的流程。

Conclusion: 继承并复用已有策略与观测统计可有效提升机器人持续协同设计的效率与性能；多角色协作生成与交叉评审、行为回放反馈以及生成式articulated建模共同支撑了从仿真优化到实物原型的完整工作流。

Abstract: Robot co-design couples morphology search with policy learning, yet training every new design from scratch discards acquired control experience. We present LACE-CRAFT, which compares continued learning on the current robot with policy adaptation to new morphology-reward pairs. LACE resumes the incumbent's full learning state and initializes compatible challengers with its actor parameters and observation statistics. A fixed task metric selects among both branches and the frozen incumbent. CRAFT coordinates Feedback, Morphology, Reward, and Integration roles through shared experimental records and behavioral replays to generate and cross-review paired proposals. A generative extension converts generated meshes into editable articulated models with configured joints, actuator interfaces, and consistently updated simulation assets. Across five locomotion benchmarks, mean scores over three evaluation seeds are 6.4-91.9% higher than D2C. Both methods train 30 new morphology-reward pairs over five rounds; LACE additionally uses four continuation training units. Five-task ablations examine policy inheritance and replay-derived feedback. A fabricated prototype demonstrates indoor walking and illustrates the geometry-to-hardware workflow.

</details>


### [27] [Co${}^{2}$Skill: Whole-Body Control via Skill Composition for Long-Horizon Human-Environment Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09291)
*Jeonghwan Kim,Hyeonwoo Kim,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一策略Co²Skill，将全身场景交互与灵巧物体操作整合进同一控制框架，通过预训练运动先验、任务/阶段相关观测掩码、目标条件移动操作课程与跨任务课程，实现顺序任务执行与边交互边操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂非结构化环境中的人类级灵巧性需要全身场景交互与灵巧物体操作的深度融合，但现有基于物理的控制器大多将这两种能力分开处理，缺乏统一的策略表述。

Method: 基于预训练运动先验构建统一策略；采用任务与阶段相关的观测掩码筛选当前交互目标所需信息；设计目标条件移动操作课程，结合部分参考引导保证精度、从多样初始状态探索，并支持超出示范轨迹的目标导向执行；引入跨任务课程，联合训练单项技能与选定任务序列，跨任务边界保持物理状态并在后续场景交互中维持抓取。

Result: 在坐下、站起、攀爬、上下楼梯、目标导向操作以及顺序执行和随机不同条件下进行评测；并在室内环境中展示技能组合，说明其可在同一控制表述内集成。

Conclusion: 统一策略表述可同时支持顺序任务执行与场景交互中的物体操作，验证了场景交互与灵巧操作一体化控制的可行性与通用性。

Abstract: Achieving human-level dexterity in complex, unstructured environments requires the seamless integration of whole-body scene interaction and dexterous object manipulation skills. While existing physics-based controllers generate physically plausible behaviors in each domain, they largely address these two capabilities independently. In this paper, we present Co${}^{2}$Skill that integrates scene interaction and dexterous manipulation through a unified policy formulation. Built on a pretrained motion prior, the policy uses task and phase dependent observation masks to select information relevant to the current interaction goals. We introduce a goal-conditioned loco-manipulation curriculum that combines partial reference guidance for precision with exploration from varied initial states while allowing goal-directed execution beyond the demonstrated trajectories. We further introduce a cross-task curriculum that jointly trains individual skills and selected task sequences, preserving physical states across task boundaries and maintaining grasps during subsequent scene interactions. Together, these support sequential task execution and simultaneous scene interaction with object manipulation. We evaluate sitting, standing, climbing, stair traversal, and goal-directed manipulation, together with sequential execution and with random different conditions. Additionally, we demonstrate skill compositions in indoor environments, illustrating their integration within the same control formulation.

</details>


### [28] [Adaptive Risk-Certified Event-Triggered Replanning for Dynamic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09302)
*Richie R. Suganda,Bin Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CERT-Replan框架，用校准的障碍风险作为早预警信号触发重规划，结合单步安全滤波与视界级风险监控，在非平稳环境下显著降低碰撞率并减少安全滤波干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中障碍预测误差具有不确定性、非平稳性，且可能引发罕见但安全关键的失效。现有控制障碍函数（CBF）安全滤波器只能拒绝当前不安全控制，难以判断有限视界规划模式本身在预测不确定性演化下何时变得不安全。

Method: CERT-Replan在线校准按时视界索引的障碍预测残差，用得到的不确定性半径评估动态障碍安全裕度。单步安全滤波器保护下一个将执行的控制；视界级风险监控器沿当前MPC rollout评估预测障碍违反损失的上尾CVaR，当风险超过分配预算时，拒绝当前规划模式并选择低风险替代方案（如不同速度剖面、走廊或同伦类），而非反复修正同一标称计划。

Result: 在非平稳基准中，相对仅安全滤波基线碰撞减少83.3%，相对简单重规划触发减少77.8%，平均安全滤波干预减少32.4%；结合Trajectron++实现96%无碰撞运行。硬件实验与机载运行时剖析验证了计算可行性。

Conclusion: 以校准障碍风险作为重规划早预警信号、并在视界层面监控CVaR风险，可有效提升动态环境导航安全性并降低安全滤波负担，且具备实际部署的计算可行性。

Abstract: Safe navigation in dynamic environments requires robots to plan under obstacle predictions whose errors are uncertain, non-stationary, and can induce rare but safety-critical failures. Existing control-barrier-function safety filters can reject immediately unsafe controls, but they provide little guidance on when the current finite-horizon planning mode itself is becoming unsafe as prediction uncertainty evolves. We propose Conformal Event-Triggered Risk-Certified Replanning (\emph{CERT-Replan}), a framework that uses calibrated barrier risk as an early-warning signal for replanning. CERT-Replan calibrates horizon-indexed obstacle-prediction residuals online and uses the resulting uncertainty radii to evaluate dynamic-obstacle safety margins. A one-step safety filter protects the next applied control, while a horizon-level risk monitor evaluates the upper-tail CVaR of predicted barrier-violation losses along the current MPC rollout. When this risk exceeds an allocated budget, CERT-Replan rejects the current planning mode and selects a lower-risk alternative, such as a different speed profile, corridor, or homotopy class, rather than repeatedly correcting the same nominal plan. In a non-stationary benchmark, CERT-Replan achieves an \(83.3\%\) collision reduction relative to the safety-filter-only baseline \textcolor{black}{and a \(77.8\%\) reduction relative to simple replanning triggers}, while reducing average safety-filter intervention by \(32.4\%\). \textcolor{black}{With Trajectron++, CERT-Replan achieves \(96\%\) collision-free operation. Hardware experiments and onboard runtime profiling demonstrate computational feasibility.}

</details>


### [29] [Predicted Futures Are Not Enough: Learning Executable Goals for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09309)
*Tzu-Yu Chuang,Ching-Hsiang Chang,Yi-Hsiu Lee,Yi-Ting Chen,Min Sun,YuanFu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出"实体级目标读出"(Entity-Level Goal Readout)接口，将3D轨迹世界模型的可执行终端目标显式输出为SE(3)紧凑目标，并用共享的Pose-Native Executor实现闭环控制，无需重跑世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型能预测操作场景如何向任务目标演化，但这些未来预测并不直接暴露控制所需的紧凑任务变量。仅以未来预测为监督时，终端目标精度并非显式学习目标，即使具备几何恢复能力也难以保证可执行性。因此需要把"预测到执行"的接口作为显式学习组件。

Method: 提出Entity-Level Goal Readout：结合以物体为中心的位姿预测与基于观测深度的平移估计，生成SE(3)空间中的紧凑目标。共享的Pose-Native Executor以该固定目标配合在线物体位姿反馈进行闭环控制，且无需重新运行世界模型。同时设计目标诊断（直接测量终端目标精度）与受控平移扰动实验（刻画目标误差下的执行退化）。

Result: 在五项操作任务上平均成功率达79.69%。在Franka机械臂上零样本部署：标称StackCube 73.33%，含干扰物66.67%，策略训练中未见目标的PickPlate 75.00%。

Conclusion: 结果支持将"预测到执行"接口视为世界模型规划中显式学习的组成部分，而非控制流程中的附带后处理。

Abstract: Generative world models provide rich predictions of how manipulation scenes may evolve toward task objectives, yet those futures do not directly expose the compact task variables required by control. When training supervises future prediction alone, terminal goal accuracy is not an explicit learning objective, even when geometric recovery is available. We present Entity-Level Goal Readout, a learned prediction-to-execution interface that makes the executable terminal goal an explicit output of a 3D trace world model. It combines object-centric pose prediction with translation grounded in observed depth to produce a compact goal in SE(3). A shared Pose-Native Executor consumes this fixed goal with online object-pose feedback for closed-loop control without rerunning the world model. Across five manipulation tasks, the pipeline achieves a mean success rate of 79.69%. Goal diagnostics directly measure terminal goal accuracy, while controlled translation perturbations characterize how execution degrades under goal error. Zero-shot deployment on a Franka arm achieves 73.33% success on nominal StackCube, 66.67% with distractors, and 75.00% on PickPlate with a target unseen during policy training. These results support treating the prediction-to-execution interface as an explicit learned component of world-model planning rather than incidental post-processing in the control pipeline itself. Project page: https://claire0730.github.io/executable-goals/

</details>


### [30] [ClimbLab: MATLAB Simulation Platform for Legged Climbing Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09315)
*Kentaro Uno,Warley F. R. Ribeiro,Yusuke Koizumi,Keigo Haji,Koki Kurihara,William Jones,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源的MATLAB仿真与分析平台，用于腿式爬壁机器人的建模、行走与攀爬仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为腿式爬壁机器人的设计提供通用仿真与分析工具，支持在任意环境中进行多体动力学仿真。

Method: 将任意腿式机器人建模为带浮动基座的铰接多体系统，采用刚体动力学引擎；环境参数可调（倾角、重力、地面刚度），并支持将任意点云作为地形图。

Result: 文中描述了仿真器的结构、计算流程，并给出代表性仿真示例，包括四足机器人贴壁抓握以及在陡坡上攀爬。

Conclusion: 该开源MATLAB平台可用于腿式爬壁机器人的设计与仿真，支持任意环境下的攀爬与行走验证。

Abstract: This paper presents an open-sourced MATLAB simulation and analysis platform dedicated to legged climbing robots. This simulator enables the design of any limbed robotic system as an articulated multi-body with a floating base and simulates it walking and climbing in an arbitrary environment. The main variable environmental parameters are inclination, gravity, and ground stiffness, and any point cloud can be installed as the terrain map. Furthermore, the simulator employs a rigid body dynamics engine. This paper first describes the simulator structure, and the computational flow and next presents the representative simulation examples where quadrupedal robots assumed gripping on the wall or climbing on the steep slope.

</details>


### [31] [MCFR: A Mask-Guided Coarse-to-Fine Regression Framework for Robust Multi-Variant Board-to-Board Connector Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09327)
*Guanghui Shen,Song Wang,Dan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MCFR框架，用于多型号板对板（BTB）连接器的自动化插装，通过掩码引导的由粗到细回归与光度细化提升视觉精度与部署鲁棒性，实机插入成功率达99.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3C制造中BTB连接器自动插装需兼具高视觉精度与强部署鲁棒性。难点有二：一是多型号连接器在形态与外观上差异显著，跨型号泛化困难；二是固定视角检测下训练与部署环境不一致，易引入背景伪相关，导致实际性能下降。

Method: 提出MCFR（Mask-Guided Coarse-to-Fine Regression）框架：引入目标感知的掩码先验以抑制背景干扰，并结合显式的光度细化（photometric refinement），以“由粗到细”的回归方式提升对准精度与鲁棒性。

Result: 在自建多型号数据集、BTB批量插装测试台以及真实手机装配任务上，MCFR均持续优于代表性基线方法，实机平均插装成功率达到99.25%。

Conclusion: MCFR有效缓解了背景伪相关与跨型号泛化问题，验证了其在多型号BTB连接器自动化装配中的有效性与实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Automated insertion of board-to-board (BTB) connectors in 3C manufacturing requires both high visual accuracy and strong deployment robustness. This problem remains challenging because multi-variant connectors exhibit significant morphological and appearance variations, making stable cross-variant generalization difficult, while the mismatch between training and deployment under fixed-view inspection settings induces background spurious correlation and degrades real-world performance. To address these issues, this paper proposes MCFR, a Mask-Guided Coarse-to-Fine Regression framework for multi-variant BTB connector assembly. By introducing an object-aware mask prior and explicit photometric refinement, the proposed method suppresses background interference and improves alignment accuracy and robustness in practical deployment. Experiments on a self-constructed multi-variant dataset, a BTB batch insertion testbed, and a real smartphone assembly task show that MCFR consistently outperforms representative baselines and achieves an average real-world insertion success rate of 99.25%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and practical potential of MCFR for automated assembly of multi-variant BTB connectors.

</details>


### [32] [Learning Unknown Constraints without Unsafe Data via Optimality and Counterfactual Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09350)
*Zhouyu Zhang,Chih-Yuan Chiu,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Counterfactual KKT（CF-KKT）框架，利用学习到的动力学模型与局部最优演示恢复未知约束，无需已知动力学或额外高风险探索，兼顾数据效率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从演示中学习约束的方法分为两类：约束逆最优控制（CIOC）依赖最优性条件（如 KKT 条件），但通常假设动力学已知且约束表示结构化；逆约束强化学习（ICRL）能处理复杂未知约束与未知转移动力学，却常需大量在线探索，期间可能发生不安全的约束违反。因此需要一种兼具 CIOC 数据效率与安全性、以及 ICRL 灵活性的方法。

Method: CF-KKT 首先使用局部学习的可微动力学模型，将 KKT 启发的优化性条件直接施加于演示数据上；其次利用学习到的动力学在演示附近生成提升奖励的反事实行为，揭示在无未知约束时更优的行为，从而提供合成的不可行数据。当约束参数化已知时，同一学习动力学框架可实现基于 CIOC 的直接参数恢复，并刻画其对动力学误设的敏感性。

Result: 在高维机器人控制任务上，该方法学习到神经约束表示，相较最先进的离线 ICRL 基线在安全性和数据效率上均有提升。

Conclusion: CF-KKT 通过结合学习动力学、KKT 启发最优性条件与反事实数据生成，实现了无需已知动力学和额外风险探索的约束学习，融合了 CIOC 的数据效率优势与 ICRL 的灵活性。

Abstract: Learning from demonstrations (LfD) provides a framework for inferring unknown constraints from locally optimal, constraint-satisfying expert behavior. Existing approaches largely fall into two paradigms, constrained inverse optimal control (CIOC) and inverse constrained reinforcement learning (ICRL). CIOC exploits optimality conditions such as the Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) conditions but typically assumes known dynamics and structured constraint representations. Meanwhile, ICRL accommodates complex unknown constraints and unknown transition dynamics but often requires extensive online exploration, during which unsafe constraint violations may occur. In this work, we introduce Counterfactual KKT (CF-KKT), a constraint learning framework that leverages learned dynamics and locally optimal demonstrations to recover unknown constraints without requiring known dynamics or additional risky exploration, thereby combining the data efficiency and safety advantages of CIOC with the flexibility of ICRL. First, we use a locally learned differentiable dynamics model to impose KKT-inspired optimality conditions directly on the demonstrations. Second, we use the learned dynamics to generate reward-improving counterfactual behaviors near the demonstrations, revealing behaviors that would be preferable in the absence of the unknown constraint and thus providing synthetic infeasible data. When the constraint parameterization is known, the same learned-dynamics framework enables direct CIOC-based parameter recovery, and we characterize its sensitivity to dynamics misspecification. Across high-dimensional robotic control tasks, our approach learns neural constraint representations with improved safety and data efficiency relative to state-of-the-art offline ICRL baselines.

</details>


### [33] [COOL: Curiosity-Driven Object Ownership Learning for Personalized Robotic Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09358)
*Samira Huber,Ruben Hammele,Sören Pirk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出COOL框架，使机器人能够从日常观测中自主学习物体所有权关系，并维护长期空间记忆，从而支持个性化服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在日常环境中提供个性化服务时，需理解如"我的背包在哪"这类自然语言指令，并基于物体实例、人物、位置与所有权进行推理执行。其核心难点在于所有权信息很少被显式标注，必须从长期的人-物交互行为证据中推断。

Method: COOL通过自主观测学习物体所有权，并维护环境的长期空间记忆；采用基于智能体的好奇心驱动数据采集策略，引导机器人前往最有价值的位置获取信息并更新过时的观测。

Result: 离线实验、消融研究及真实世界评估表明，COOL能够从真实交互中推断所有权关系，并利用该知识完成以所有权为条件的导航与任务执行。

Conclusion: COOL验证了从长期行为观测中自主学习物体所有权并用于机器人个性化服务的可行性。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly expected to provide personalized services in everyday environments. To do so, they must ground natural-language commands such as "Where is my backpack?" or "Find my bottle" and execute them by reasoning about object instances, people, locations, and ownership. This is challenging because ownership is rarely labeled explicitly and must be inferred from long-term, behavioral evidence of human-object interactions. To address this, we present COOL, a novel robotic framework for autonomously learning object ownership from everyday observations and maintaining a long-term spatial memory of its environment. To keep its memory current, COOL uses an agent-based curiosity-driven data collection strategy that guides the robot toward the most promising locations to gain information and refresh stale observations. Offline experiments, ablation studies, and real-world evaluations show that COOL can infer ownership relations from real-world interactions and use this knowledge for ownership-conditioned navigation and task execution.

</details>


### [34] [Immiscible Diffusion Policy: Preserving Multimodal Robot Actions through Label-Free Noise Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09369)
*Xiao Zhang,Yuxin Chen,Zhixuan Liang,Guojian Zhan,Chenran Li,Chenfeng Xu,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Yiheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 扩散策略常无法恢复多模态动作分布，会塌缩为单一模态；本文提出 Immiscible Diffusion Policy，通过动作-噪声分配保持噪声到动作路径的区分度，无需改动架构或推理，在多项任务中显著提升多模态保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略被期望能恢复多模态动作分布，但作者发现即使保证数据模态平衡和批内精确对称，策略仍常塌缩为单一模态。分析表明，独立采样的动作-噪声配对会增加扩散路径之间的混合与交叉，导致去噪响应被平均化，从而抑制模态特有行为；在动作空间稠密且低维的机器人规划中该问题尤为严重。

Method: 提出 Immiscible Diffusion Policy：一种无标签、训练时的附加方法，通过对动作与噪声进行分配（action-noise assignment）来保持相对独立的噪声到动作路径，且不修改策略架构或推理过程。

Result: 在五个仿真和两个真实世界的人形操作任务上（涵盖状态、RGB 和点云观测），方法在保持较强任务性能的同时显著提升动作模态保持能力：三个双模态任务中非主导模态占比提升 6.0x–14.6x；两个四模态任务中恢复了原始策略 rollout 中完全缺失的示范模态。

Conclusion: Immiscible Diffusion Policy 为在通用机器人学习任务中保持动作多模态性提供了一种简单而稳健的方案。

Abstract: When diffusion policies were first introduced, they were expected to recover multi-modal action distributions. However, we find this expectation does not always hold, as diffusion policies often collapse to a single modality even when we guarantee the balance of dataset modalities and exact within-batch symmetry. Our analysis indicates that independent action-noise pairing contributes to this failure by increasing mixing and crossing among diffusion paths, which can produce averaged denoising responses and suppress modality-specific behavior. This issue is especially severe in robot planning, where action spaces are dense and low-dimensional, significantly increasing such mixing and crossing. To alleviate this problem, we propose Immiscible Diffusion Policy, a label-free training-time add-on to diffusion policy that uses action-noise assignment to preserve relatively distinct noise-to-action routes without modifying the policy architecture or inference procedure. Across five simulated and two real-world humanoid manipulation tasks spanning state, RGB, and point-cloud observations, our method significantly improves the policy's preservation of action modalities while maintaining strong task performance. It increases the proportion of the non-dominant modality by 6.0x-14.6x across three two-modality tasks and recovers demonstrated modalities that are entirely absent from vanilla policy rollouts on both four-modality tasks. These results demonstrate that Immiscible Diffusion Policy provides a simple yet robust approach to preserving action multi-modality in general robot learning tasks.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
