Method: 构建统一的无人机系统框架，融合基于Whisper与LLM的语音识别、基于计算机视觉的果实成熟度分类，以及自适应路径规划；整体系统在Gazebo仿真环境中建模与验证。

Result: 在受控的农业仿真场景下对系统性能进行了评估（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 语音控制的无人机系统在仿真层面具备自动化大规模种植园采摘任务的可行性，为后续实机部署提供基础。

Abstract: In tropical regions, their agricultural sectors remain highly dependent on manual labor for fruit harvesting. On large-scale farms, this dependency often results in significant labor cost and logistic complexities. This project presents the development and simulation of a voice-controlled Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) system designed to automate harvesting tasks in extensive plantations. The proposed system integrates speech recognition using Whisper [1] and LLM, computer vision-based ripeness classification, and adaptive path planning within a unified framework. The entire system is modeled and validated in a Gazebo simulation environment, allowing performance evaluation under controlled agricultural scenarios

</details>


### [36] [TempoBridge: Language-Guided Tempo Control for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09451)
*Yeonseo Lee,Hyosup Shin,Guebin Hwang,Sungho Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TempoBridge：一种轻量框架，利用冻结的VLA表征在任务各阶段依据指令中的节奏线索调节动作，无需额外节奏条件示范或基策略微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型擅长理解“做什么”，但对“怎么做”（如快慢节奏）控制能力有限。

Method: 从上下文VLM表征中提取节奏线索，通过因果阶段路由器与任务进度对齐，并在执行过程中调节标称运动指令；使用冻结VLA表征，无需节奏条件示范与节奏专用基策略微调。

Result: 在LIBERO任务上，规范节奏指令下节奏成功率由52.6%提升至89.7%，同时保持高任务成功率；无节奏线索时性能接近基线，并能泛化到未见节奏表达而无需额外训练；物理机器人实验验证了真实操作中的语言条件节奏调节。

Conclusion: TempoBridge以轻量方式为VLA模型补充节奏控制能力，兼顾任务成功与节奏遵循，并具备泛化性与真实世界适用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are effective at understanding what task to perform, but provide limited control over how it should be executed, such as moving quickly or slowly. We introduce TempoBridge, a lightweight framework that uses frozen VLA representations to modulate actions according to tempo cues in the instruction at each task phase, without additional tempo-conditioned robot demonstrations or tempo-specific base-policy fine-tuning. TempoBridge extracts tempo cues from contextual VLM representations, aligns them with task progress through a causal phase router, and modulates nominal motion commands during execution. Across LIBERO tasks, TempoBridge improves Tempo Success Rate from 52.6% to 89.7% under canonical tempo instructions while retaining high task success. It also preserves near-baseline performance when no tempo cue is present and generalizes to unseen tempo expressions without additional training. Experiments on a physical robot further demonstrate language-conditioned tempo modulation in real-world manipulation.

</details>


### [37] [RobotAPO: Adversarial Physics Preference Optimization for Robotic Manipulation Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09454)
*Kerui Li,Zhe Jing,Chenyi Huang,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Haoming Cui,Huaibo Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出10,000样本偏好数据集AgiBot-PhysPref与对抗式物理偏好优化框架RobotAPO，通过修正生成视频中局部物理违规（如穿透、提前移动），显著提升下游机器人执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作视频常被用作具身智能体的视觉规划，但仅优化视觉合理性无法捕捉真实交互脆弱的物理流形。交互边界处微小物理违规（穿透、物体提前运动）会使下游执行所需时序与位姿失效，而标准监督微调缺乏惩罚此类局部失败的直接压力。

Method: 1）构建AgiBot-PhysPref偏好数据集，严格筛选出条件匹配的物理违规样本，将生成器自身失败分布转化为物理一致性信号；2）提出RobotAPO，在连续流匹配去噪空间中进行对抗式物理偏好优化；3）引入轻量级对抗反事实提议器，学习条件依赖、偏向物理失败的去噪方向，避免策略仅记忆静态失败样本；4）推理时保持纯提示加参考接口，无需外部结构条件。

Result: 在留出的AgiBot条件下，RobotAPO物理一致性较最强受控内部基线提升6.8%硬分数与10.0%软分数；真实机器人回放中，任务成功率相对提升37.4%。

Conclusion: 显式修正生成视频中的局部物理违规可有效提升下游机器人执行能力；将生成器失败分布转化为偏好信号并结合对抗反事实探索，是在无额外结构条件约束下实现物理一致性增强的有效路径。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation videos are increasingly used as visual plans for embodied agents, but optimizing purely for visual plausibility often fails to capture the fragile physical manifold of real-world interactions. Even minor physics-violating errors at the interaction boundary, such as interpenetration or premature object motion, can completely invalidate the inferred timing and pose needed for downstream execution. Because standard supervised fine-tuning lacks the direct pressure to penalize these localized failures, we introduce AgiBot-PhysPref. This rigorously curated 10,000-sample preference dataset isolates condition-matched physics violations, turning the generator's own failure distribution into a foundational signal for physical consistency. Building upon this, we propose RobotAPO, an adversarial physics preference optimization framework operating in the continuous flow-matching denoising space. To prevent the policy from merely memorizing static curated failures, RobotAPO employs a lightweight adversarial counterfactual proposer that learns a condition-dependent, physical-failure-biased direction in denoising space. This encourages the model to explore and better respect the physical interaction boundary, all while maintaining a pure prompt-and-reference inference interface without requiring external structural conditioning. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that explicitly correcting these localized physics violations improves downstream robot execution from generated videos. On held-out AgiBot conditions, RobotAPO outperforms the strongest controlled internal baseline in physical consistency by 6.8% hard score and 10.0% soft score. Crucially, in real-robot replay, it translates these physical-consistency gains into a 37.4% relative improvement in task success over the strongest controlled internal baseline.

</details>


### [38] [TMT: Runtime Backdoor Detection for Vision-Language-Action Policies on Unseen Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09462)
*Zirun Zhou,Jingfeng Zhang,HaoChuan Xu,Xizhe Zhang,Elliott Wen,Jing Sun,Hong Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出运行时后门检测器 TMT（Token Manifold + 潜在 Transition 建模），并用自蒸馏进行策略净化，在三个 VLA 后门攻击、未见任务上取得 SOTA 检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略被植入后门后，可在保持正常任务性能的同时，在触发条件下输出恶意动作。此类激活难以检测：恶意行为由单个看似合理的动作组成，且陌生任务本身也会带来观测与行为的合法变化，导致触发行为与正常差异难以区分。

Method: 1) TMT：仅用良性 rollout 训练的双分支运行时检测器——token manifold 分支评估输入 token 结构，相邻层潜在动态分支评估预测误差；token 分支发现可疑 rollout 后，经潜在偏差确认，再引导后续监测的 transition 选择。2) 净化：自蒸馏，用冻结的后门策略在良性输入上产生的动作，监督学生模型在成对的良性/触发观测上学习，无需独立干净参考策略。3) 评测：改造传统后门检测器，并将异常检测与故障检测方法复用为 VLA 后门检测器作为基线。

Result: 与十种基线进行事后对比，TMT 在三个 VLA 后门攻击下、在未见任务上达到 SOTA 后门检测性能。

Conclusion: TMT 通过 token 流形与潜在 transition 双分支建模，实现运行时 VLA 后门检测，并配合自蒸馏净化，在未知任务上优于十种基线检测方法。

Abstract: Backdoored vision-language-action (VLA) policies can preserve benign task performance while producing malicious actions when a trigger appears. Detecting such activation is difficult because malicious behavior can comprise individually plausible actions, while unfamiliar tasks introduce legitimate changes in observations and behavior. We introduce TMT, a runtime backdoor detector based on Token Manifold and latent Transition modeling. Trained on benign rollouts, its two branches assess input-token structure and prediction errors in adjacent-layer latent dynamics. A suspicious rollout identified by the token manifold branch, once confirmed through latent deviations, guides transition selection for subsequent monitoring. We further explore policy purification through self-distillation: a frozen copy of the backdoored policy provides benign-input actions to supervise a student on paired benign and triggered observations, without requiring a separate clean reference policy. For evaluation, we adapt traditional backdoor detectors and repurpose anomaly and failure detection methods as VLA backdoor detectors. In a post-hoc comparison with ten baselines, TMT achieves state-of-the-art backdoor detection performance on unseen tasks across three VLA backdoor attacks. Our project page is available at https://zzr42.github.io/tmt/.

</details>


### [39] [Precise SE(3) End-Effector Tracking in Whole-Body Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09479)
*Joohwan Seo,Xiaofeng Guo,Jinkun Cao,Roberto Horowitz,Rocky Duan,Guanya Shi,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ResGAC，将几何导纳控制（GAC）与残差强化学习结合的人形全身控制器，实现全身运动中的精确末端位姿跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在全身运动中进行精确末端执行器跟踪面临挑战，主要源于浮动基座振荡、重力、动态耦合以及行走引起的扰动等因素。

Method: ResGAC将几何导纳控制（GAC）与残差强化学习相结合。GAC提供结构化的SE(3)任务空间反馈并生成标称手臂关节位置目标；残差RL在共享的关节位置动作空间中补偿未建模动态并协调行走与平衡。左不变几何表述使同一GAC律可跨不同操作参考系使用，从而支持采用地面固连航向参考系，在保留平面行走的同时去除骨盆的滚转、俯仰和升沉，减少行走过程中参考系引入的末端运动。

Result: 在真实Unitree G1人形机器人上验证。四项站立末端跟踪基准中，ResGAC持续优于包括SONIC在内的代表性基线，取得更低的平移和旋转误差。实验进一步证明，采用所提出的地面固连航向参考系可减少骨盆运动向期望末端位姿的传播。站立轴孔装配任务中ResGAC成功率达90%，而SONIC为50%，并在下肢运动时实现准确的世界坐标系SE(3)末端位姿跟踪。

Conclusion: ResGAC通过几何导纳控制与残差强化学习的结合，有效实现了人形全身运动下的精确末端位姿跟踪，并显著提升了下肢运动时末端跟踪的精度与任务成功率。

Abstract: Precise end-effector tracking during humanoid whole-body motion is challenging due to floating-base oscillations, gravity, dynamic coupling, and locomotion-induced disturbances. We propose ResGAC, a whole-body humanoid controller for precise end-effector pose tracking that combines geometric admittance control (GAC) with residual reinforcement learning. GAC provides structured $\SE$ task-space feedback and generates nominal arm joint-position targets, while residual RL compensates for unmodeled dynamics and coordinates locomotion and balance in the shared joint-position action space. The left-invariant geometric formulation allows the same GAC law to be used across manipulation reference frames. This enables the use of a ground-attached heading frame that preserves planar locomotion while removing pelvis roll, pitch, and heave from the manipulation reference, thereby reducing reference-induced end-effector motion during locomotion. ResGAC is validated on a real Unitree G1 humanoid. Across four standing end-effector tracking benchmarks, ResGAC consistently outperforms representative baselines, including SONIC, achieving lower translational and rotational errors. Real-world experiments further demonstrate reduced propagation of pelvis motion to the desired end-effector pose using the proposed ground-attached heading frame. ResGAC achieves $90\%$ success in a standing peg-in-hole task compared with $50\%$ for SONIC, and accurate world-frame $\SE$ end-effector pose tracking during lower-body motion. Experimental videos are included in the supplementary material and are also available on the project website: https://resgac.github.io/ResGAC-website/.

</details>


### [40] [SiGNgapore - An Interactive Dataset for Sign-based Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09488)
*Nicky Zimmerman,Joel Loo,Zishuo Wang,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向无地图导航的标识中心数据集，在新加坡多种公共空间用手持设备采集，包含RGB、稀疏深度、里程计与IMU数据，提供56个长时导航任务、450余个标识场景、场景图与GPS对齐地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在人类环境中应用的前提是无需预建地图即可导航，而利用人类为人类设计的导航标识（如指示牌）是实现该目标的关键。然而目前缺乏围绕标识进行无地图导航决策的公开数据集。

Method: 使用手持设备在新加坡多处公共空间采集数据，场景以导航标识及其所处的复杂环境为中心；记录RGB、稀疏深度、里程计与IMU多模态数据；提供人类可读格式及ROS 2转换脚本，并附带场馆地图与GPS对齐的场景图。

Conclusion: 该数据集为基于导航标识的无地图自主导航研究提供了真实场景下的多模态数据支持，并讨论了其在相关任务中的潜在应用场景。

Abstract: The future of autonomous robots in human-oriented environments depends on their ability to navigate in the absence of prebuilt maps; exploiting navigational aids, such as navigational signs, designed by humans for humans is critical for enabling autonomy. This manuscript introduces a unique dataset collected using a handheld device, at various public spaces across Singapore, representing environments that humans frequent daily, and focusing on sign-centric decision making for mapless navigation. The dataset includes RGB, sparse depth, odometry and IMU measurements of scenarios centered around navigational signs and the complex environments where they are placed. Additionally, we provide 56 long-horizon navigation missions and over 450 sign-centric scenarios. All data is provided in a human- readable format, as well as utility scripts for conversion to ROS 2. Lastly, we provide venue maps and GPS aligned scene graphs of the test environments. We discuss the potential use cases for this dataset.

</details>


### [41] [Sparse Feature Policy Unlearning Mitigates State Hallucination in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09496)
*Jiho Lee,Jeongeun Park,Heayoun Choi,Taekyung Kim,Eunwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究VLA模型中的"状态幻觉"失效模式，通过稀疏自编码器定位相关稀疏特征，提出SOUL方法选择性遗忘幻觉行为相关知识，显著减少幻觉失效并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型虽在机器人操作中泛化性强，但部署时存在反复出现且不可靠的行为。作者聚焦于"状态幻觉"——VLA持续表现得仿佛一个并未实际达成的机器人-物体状态已然实现——这一反复出现的失效模式，亟需理解其机制并加以修正。

Method: 首先分析发现状态幻觉与对任务相关视觉区域注意力减弱相关；借助稀疏自编码器进行机制解释，揭示幻觉相关稀疏特征在失效发生时被激活。基于此提出SOUL（稀疏特征策略遗忘）：将从幻觉失效中识别出的稀疏特征作为遗忘目标，将成功行为中识别出的稀疏特征作为保留目标，从而选择性遗忘与状态幻觉相关联的策略知识。

Result: 在多种VLA架构及仿真与真实环境中开展实验，结果表明该方法大幅减少幻觉失效，提升任务成功率，同时未显著损害既有操作能力。

Conclusion: 可解释的特征分析为选择性修改机器人策略中的不良知识提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong generalization in robotic manipulation by leveraging rich representations from pretrained vision-language models. However, their deployment in real-world environments remains limited by recurring unreliable behaviors. In this work, we study state hallucination, a recurring failure pattern in which a VLA continues acting as if an unrealized robot-object state had been achieved. Our analyses find that state hallucination coincides with weakened attention to task-relevant visual regions, and a mechanistic interpretation via sparse autoencoders reveals that hallucination-associated sparse features are activated when these failures occur. Based on this analysis, we propose SOUL (Sparse feature pOlicy UnLearning), which selectively unlearns policy knowledge associated with state hallucination behaviors, where sparse features identified from hallucination failures and successful behaviors serve as explicit forgetting and retention targets, respectively. Experiments across VLA architectures in simulated and real-world environments show that our method substantially reduces hallucinated failures and improves task success without substantially compromising the existing manipulation capabilities. These results suggest that interpretable feature analysis provides a practical basis for selectively modifying undesirable knowledge in robot policies.

</details>


### [42] [Not All Uncertainty Matters: Simulation-in-the-Loop Fast-Slow Reasoning for Decision-Critical Autonomous Driving System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09520)
*Jiayi Chen,Shuai Wang,Guangxu Zhu,Derrick Wing Kwan Ng,Chengzhong Xu,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SIGMA——仿真在环的任务导向快慢协作框架，用决策级指标EPG决定云端推理调用时机，在CARLA中减少50%无用云交互并提升导航成功率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型可为自动驾驶提供开放世界感知与推理，但计算开销大、推理延迟高，难以持续在云端使用；因此需要快慢协作（车载模块实时感知控制、云端按需高层推理）。核心难题在于判断云端推理何时应影响时敏驾驶决策。现有方法依赖感知不确定性、启发式触发或资源驱动策略，未评估"解决该不确定性是否真能改善规划"，导致无效云调用。

Method: SIGMA为仿真在环（simulation-in-the-loop）的任务导向快慢协作框架。将规划器嵌入不确定性评估过程，在语义与几何不确定性下采样可行的场景实现，评估其对可行轨迹与规划代价的影响，据此估计"消解不确定性"所能带来的规划代价期望降低量。进一步提出期望规划增益（EPG），作为决策级度量，用于云端调用触发、云引导集成，以及在截止时间与资源约束下的请求优先级排序。

Result: 在CARLA中的实验表明，SIGMA在静态与动态障碍场景下均减少不必要的云交互，同时改善规划、效率与导航成功率。相较固定周期协作，无用云交互减少50%，导航成功率提升6%以上，动态场景中完成时间最多缩短26.2%。

Conclusion: 以决策级规划增益（EPG）而非感知不确定性作为云端调用依据，可有效实现任务导向的快慢协作：在保证甚至提升规划与导航表现的同时大幅降低云端交互与时间开销，验证了"评估不确定性消解是否改善规划"这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (VLMs) provide powerful open-world perception and reasoning for autonomous driving, but their high computational cost and inference latency make continuous cloud-side use impractical. This motivates fast--slow collaboration, where efficient onboard modules handle real-time perception and control while cloud models provide high-level reasoning only when needed. The key challenge is deciding when cloud reasoning should influence time-critical driving decisions. Existing methods often rely on perception uncertainty, heuristic triggers, or resource-driven policies, without assessing whether resolving an uncertainty will improve planning. We propose \textbf{SIGMA}, a simulation-in-the-loop framework for task-oriented fast--slow collaboration. SIGMA embeds the planner into uncertainty assessment and evaluates how plausible scene realizations under semantic and geometric uncertainty affect feasible trajectories and planning cost. Based on these outcomes, it estimates the expected reduction in planning cost from resolving uncertainty. We further introduce expected planning gain (EPG), a decision-level metric for cloud invocation, cloud-guidance integration, and request prioritization under deadline and resource constraints. Experiments in CARLA show that SIGMA reduces unnecessary cloud interactions while improving planning, efficiency, and navigation success in static and dynamic obstacle scenarios. Compared with fixed-period collaboration, SIGMA reduces unnecessary cloud interactions by 50\%, improves navigation success by more than 6\%, and cuts finish time by up to 26.2\% in dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [43] [Contact-Aware Imitation Learning Through Contact Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09533)
*Jiho Hong,Daeun Song,Sanghyun Kim,Mingyo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FACE——一种接触因子化模仿学习框架，将任务意图与环境相关的接触因素分离，通过接触法向估计与在线摩擦估计实现力观测的归一化编码和策略输出的物理解码，从而在不更新策略参数的前提下适应接触条件变化，并在真实机器人上验证了泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务要求机器人在接触条件变化时保持任务意图行为，同时调整其物理实现方式。然而，交互力会随表面几何、姿态和摩擦的微小变化而剧烈波动，导致直接在原始力测量上训练的策略难以迁移到示教条件之外。

Method: FACE框架将交互力表示在归一化的、相对于接触的坐标系中；利用学习到的接触法向估计器和在线摩擦估计器推断局部接触法向与有效摩擦尺度；借助这两个估计器，力观测被编码、策略输出被解码为执行时的物理运动与力指令。

Result: 在真实机器人接触丰富操作任务上，面对未见过的表面属性与几何变化，FACE表现出跨接触条件的鲁棒泛化能力；通过与将先前方法适配到该设定下的变体进行受控对比，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: FACE通过接触因子化的表征与在线估计，实现了在不更新策略参数情况下对当前接触条件的执行适应，同时保持任务意图行为，为接触丰富操作的可泛化模仿学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Generalizable contact-rich manipulation requires robots to preserve intended task behavior while adapting its physical realization to changing contact conditions. However, interaction forces can vary substantially with small changes in surface geometry, orientation, and friction, making policies trained directly on raw force measurements difficult to transfer beyond demonstrated conditions. We introduce FACE, a contact-factorized imitation learning framework that separates intended task behavior from environment-dependent contact factors. Our representation expresses interaction forces in normalized, contact-relative coordinates, while a learned contact-normal estimator and an online friction estimator infer the local contact normal and effective friction scale. Together, these estimators enable force observations to be encoded and policy outputs to be decoded into physical motion and force commands during execution. In this way, FACE adapts execution to current contact conditions while preserving the intended task behavior, without updating the policy parameters. We evaluate FACE on real-robot contact-rich manipulation under unseen variations in surface properties and geometry, demonstrating robust generalization across contact conditions through controlled comparisons with variants that adapt prior approaches to our setting. Videos and additional materials can be found on the project page: https://rcilab.khu.ac.kr/face.

</details>


### [44] [MagCilia: A Compact Magnetociliary Tactile Sensor with 3D Force Sensing for Robotic Contact Perception and Grasping Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09536)
*Yu Feng,Hao Wu,Haotian Guo,Haoming Liu,William Su,Jingxiang Guo,Jiankun Li,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Wen Jung Li,Jianshu Zhou*

TL;DR: 提出紧凑型磁纤毛触觉传感器MagCilia，结合柔性磁纤毛与霍尔传感器实现3D力感知，并用因果历史融合回归（CHFR）重建力，支撑抓取反馈与表面识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取与表面探索需要同时测量法向与切向力，并获取接触表面信息，现有方案通常难以在单一紧凑单元中兼顾3D力感知与表面识别。

Method: 设计柔性磁纤毛结构与霍尔传感器结合的紧凑触觉单元；采用准静态有限元分析研究多向载荷下的结构形变与磁响应；提出CHFR回归方法，将当前磁场测量值与其近期变化量融合，从耦合磁通道重建力；并以校准记录分组的五折交叉验证评估，结合机器人实验与频域特征分类。

Result: Fx、Fy、Fz的RMSE分别为0.40、0.57、0.69 N，R²分别为0.93、0.90、0.92；实现切向力引导的夹爪调整与外部扰动下的多轴载荷监测；基于重建力频域特征，在按采集会话分组的三折交叉验证中六类表面识别准确率达99.39%，在线演示识别全部六种表面。

Conclusion: 单一紧凑触觉单元即可实现3D力重建、抓取反馈与表面识别，验证了磁纤毛方案的多功能集成潜力。

Abstract: Robotic grasping and surface exploration benefit from simultaneous measurement of normal and tangential forces and from surface information obtained through contact. Here, we present a compact magnetociliary tactile sensor (MagCilia) that combines a flexible magnetic-cilia structure with a Hall sensor for 3D force sensing. Quasi-static finite element analysis is used to investigate structural deformation and magnetic responses under multidirectional loading. To reconstruct forces from the coupled magnetic channels, we propose causal history fusion regression (CHFR), which combines current magnetic-field measurements with their recent changes. Five-fold cross-validation grouped by calibration record yields root-mean-square errors of 0.40, 0.57, and 0.69 N for Fx, Fy, and Fz, respectively, with corresponding coefficients of determination of 0.93, 0.90, and 0.92. Robotic experiments demonstrate tangential-force-guided gripper adjustment and multi-axis load monitoring under external perturbations. Frequency-domain features of the reconstructed forces distinguish six surface categories with 99.39% accuracy in three-fold cross-validation grouped by acquisition session. An online robotic demonstration additionally identifies all six tested surfaces. These results demonstrate 3D force reconstruction, grasping feedback, and surface recognition using a single compact tactile unit.

</details>


### [45] [Targeted Modality Dropout for Real-Robot Manipulation Robust to Intermittent Vision Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09566)
*Genki Shikada,Kazuki Osamura,Masaru Ide,Tetsuya Ogata,Kanata Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Targeted Modality Dropout (TMD)，通过注意力估计各模态依赖度并选择性丢弃主导模态，配合依赖分布的熵正则，缓解模仿学习策略对视觉的过度依赖，提升视觉缺失下的执行鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模仿学习策略在训练中易过度依赖某一主导模态（如视觉）；当推理时该模态缺失，策略执行会严重受损。因此需要一种机制抑制模态依赖失衡，提升策略在模态丢失情况下的鲁棒性。

Method: TMD方法包含两部分：(1) 利用注意力机制估计策略对各模态的依赖程度，并针对性地选择性丢弃依赖度最高的主导模态；(2) 在该依赖分布上施加熵正则化，以平衡模态间依赖、避免过度集中。对比方法为不使用熵正则、随机选择丢弃模态的传统dropout。

Result: 在双臂机械臂的真实机器人实验中：视觉缺失时，基线策略成功率大幅下降，而TMD仍能维持任务执行；相比之下，传统随机dropout（无熵正则）即使在视觉未缺失情况下也在许多任务上失败。

Conclusion: 针对主导模态的选择性丢弃结合依赖分布熵正则，能有效缓解多模态模仿学习的模态过依赖问题，在模态丢失时保持鲁棒执行，且优于随机dropout策略。

Abstract: Imitation learning policies that integrate multiple sensory modalities are prone to overreliance on a dominant modality, such as vision, during training, which can disrupt policy execution when that modality is lost at inference time. In this paper, we introduce Targeted Modality Dropout (TMD), in which the dependence on each modality is estimated using attention and the most dominant modality is selectively dropped. This is combined with entropy regularization over the dependence distribution. Through real-robot evaluation using a bimanual manipulator, we show that under vision loss the success rate of the baseline policy drops substantially, whereas TMD sustains task execution. In contrast, a conventional dropout that selects the dropped modality at random, without the entropy regularization, fails on many tasks even without vision loss.

</details>


### [46] [Point It, Strike It: Direction-Conditioned Dynamic Manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09573)
*Yi Yang,Xiang Fei,Lehong Wang,Zilin Dai,Ruogu Li,Jiting Cai,Liyao Chang,Xinyi Yang,Henry Kou,Ruijie Fu,Lu Li,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究单摆绳索打击任务，目标同时指定绳端的3D位置与到达方向，并提出仿真器DeformX2.0、数据生成方法TRACE与真机迁移方法RECAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变形线性物体（DLO）的目标条件动态操作多仅指定绳端到达位置，但许多任务依赖绳端"如何到达"，即到达方向。该问题具有挑战性：绳索动力学难以建模、缺乏示范数据、不同摆动到达同一目标的可靠性不同，且仿真到现实的差距不仅限于绳索本身。

Method: 1）扩展DLO仿真器DeformX，加入GPU加速、稳定的Cosserat杆求解器与横流气动模型，得到DeformX2.0，速度提升超过20000倍；2）提出TRACE（Trace-rooted Adaptive Cross-Entropy），以存储中绳端路径最接近新目标的摆动作为热启动生成打击数据，其代价函数惩罚绳索弯曲与绳端突变运动以偏向可重复摆动；3）用条件流匹配策略在生成数据上训练；4）提出RECAP（Residual Calibration Policy），用少量标定摆动拟合仿真器的绳索与机械臂参数，并在仿真中训练校正策略以调整动作。

Result: 仿真中条件流匹配策略达到92.1%准确率。真机实验中，在三条绳索上，RECAP将位置目标5cm内成功率从72%提升到87%；对同时指定到达方向的目标，10cm与10°内成功率从50%提升到79%。

Conclusion: 通过高速稳定仿真、路径热启动的自适应交叉熵数据生成以及残差标定策略，可在真机上实现兼顾位置与到达方向的单摆绳索打击，显著缩小仿真到现实的差距。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned dynamic manipulation of deformable linear objects has mainly specified goals as positions for a rope tip to reach. Many tasks, however, depend on how the tip arrives. We therefore study single-swing rope striking with goals that specify the tip's 3D position and arrival direction, across the workspace and on different ropes. This is challenging because rope dynamics are hard to model, no demonstrations exist, distinct swings reach the same goal with different reliability, and the sim-to-real gap extends beyond the rope. To address these challenges, we extend the state-of-the-art DLO simulator DeformX with GPU acceleration, a stable Cosserat rod solver, and a cross-flow aerodynamic model, yielding DeformX2.0, which is more than $20{,}000\times$ faster. We then propose TRACE (Trace-rooted Adaptive Cross-Entropy), which generates striking data by warm-starting each new target from the stored swing whose tip path passes closest to it. Its cost penalizes rope bending and abrupt tip motion to favor repeatable swings. A conditional flow-matching policy trained on this data reaches 92.1% accuracy in simulation. Finally, we propose RECAP (Residual Calibration Policy), which fits the simulator's rope and rig parameters to a few calibration swings and adapts actions with a correction policy trained in simulation. On a real robot, across three ropes, RECAP raises success within 5cm from 72% to 87% for position goals, and within 10cm and 10° from 50% to 79% for goals that also specify the arrival direction.

</details>


### [47] [Adaptive Code Generation for Controlling Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09588)
*Justus Flerlage,Thorsten Wittkopp,Alexander Acker,Odej Kao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双AI架构框架，用LLM将高层意图翻译为受形式化机器人库约束的可执行代码，用VLM经蒸馏提供语义接地，并结合环境驱动的重规划触发器与运行时监控的闭环，在动态未知环境中实现稳健的意图级机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知动态环境中，机器人作为复杂自适应系统（CAS）需要从刚性命令库转向意图驱动的自主性，因为自然语言是唯一能表达超出有限指令集能力的复杂目标的媒介。LLM虽可支持自然语言目标描述，但其集成面临三大挑战：不精确意图与可执行动作间的形式化鸿沟、不可预测环境导致的分类学鸿沟、以及时间状态与进度感知的维持难题。

Method: 采用双AI设计：LLM将高层意图转化为可执行程序代码，代码被限制在形式化机器人库内并受可验证语法约束；VLM通过蒸馏过程提供语义接地。框架引入基于几何与语义阈值的环境驱动重规划触发机制，并辅以持续运行时监控与自适应规划闭环。在多个前沿模型上进行了基准测试。

Result: 在多个前沿模型上的基准测试表明，将生成式AI接地于反应式、受约束的闭环中，可在动态且未知的环境中稳健地完成复杂意图。

Conclusion: 本工作提出的架构框架通过生成式AI实现机器人控制，验证了约束化、反应式闭环设计对意图级自主的有效性，为复杂自适应系统中的机器人部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Deploying robots as Complex Adaptive Systems (CAS) in unknown and dynamic environments necessitates a transition from rigid command libraries toward intention-based autonomy, as natural language represents the only medium capable of articulating complex goals beyond the capacity of finite instruction sets. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a path toward natural language goal description, their integration introduces significant challenges: the formalization gap between imprecise intentions and executable actions, the taxonomy gap induced by unpredictable environments, and the challenge of maintaining temporal state and progress awareness. This work introduces an architectural framework that enables robotic control by leveraging generative AI. The system follows a dual-AI design: an LLM translates high-level intentions into executable program code restricted to a formal robotic library and constrained by verifiable syntax, while a Vision-Language Model (VLM) provides semantic grounding via a distillation process. To ensure robustness, the framework incorporates environment-driven replanning triggers based on geometric and semantic thresholds, complemented by continuous runtime monitoring and an adaptive planning loop. Benchmarked across frontier models, our framework architecture demonstrates that grounding generative AI in a reactive, constrained loop enables robust fulfillment of complex intentions in dynamic and unknown environments.

</details>


### [48] [Fast and Robust Teach-and-Repeat Navigation Using MixVPR Visual Place Recognition*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09631)

Abstract: Teach-and-repeat navigation systems employing advanced visual place recognition techniques for localization exhibit key attributes for long-term mobile robot navigation, such as the ability to operate in unstructured and dynamic environments. However, existing solutions based on deep-learning techniques are computationally demanding, limiting their applicability. This work introduces a novel and efficient teach-and-repeat system built on the modern visual place recognition method MixVPR. Real-world testing demonstrated its ability to operate both indoors and outdoors, achieving robustness and navigation precision comparable to other state-of-the-art systems. In addition, its lower hardware requirements make it suitable for a wide range of robotic platforms and practical applications.

</details>


### [49] [Design optimization of tendon-driven robots considering tendon wrapping and shortcut](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09645)
*Yuta Sahara,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种腱驱动手臂设计的多目标优化方法，针对关节力矩与手臂厚度之间的固有矛盾，采用NSGA-II求解，实现力矩最大化与厚度最小化的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动手臂设计中存在关节力矩与手臂厚度之间的固有矛盾，即肌腱缠绕与捷径效应带来的权衡问题。现有设计多依赖传统直觉，缺乏对腱走线、滑轮配置与附着点的系统优化，难以兼顾紧凑性与高力矩输出。

Method: 将设计问题形式化为同时最大化关节力矩、最小化手臂厚度的多目标优化问题，采用NSGA-II算法求解；优化变量包括腱走线、滑轮配置与附着点，并考虑肌腱缠绕与捷径效应。

Result: 得到一组Pareto最优设计，通过策略性捷径实现有效力臂扩展，在紧凑结构下获得较高关节力矩；优化结果呈现超出传统设计直觉的非平凡构型规律。

Conclusion: 该多目标优化方法可为紧凑高力矩腱驱动手臂提供实用设计准则，并为高效机器人系统的工程设计提供新见解。

Abstract: This study proposes a multi-objective optimization method for tendon-driven arm design, addressing the inherent trade-off between joint torque and arm thickness through tendon wrapping and shortcut effects. We formulate the problem to simultaneously maximize torque and minimize thickness, solved using NSGA-II. The algorithm optimizes tendon routing, pulley configurations, and attachment points. Results reveal Pareto-optimal designs with effective moment arm expansion via strategic shortcuts, providing practical guidelines for compact high-torque arms. The optimized configurations demonstrate non-trivial patterns beyond conventional design intuition, offering new insights for engineering efficient robotic systems.

</details>


### [50] [NAViLoss: An Underwater Navigation-Aware Dual-Residual Objective for Physics-Consistent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09690)
*Arup Kumar Sahoo,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出导航感知损失函数NAViLoss，联合惩罚导航状态域速度残差与DVL波束一致性残差，并结合DeepONet构建NAVi-DeepONet模型，实现鲁棒且不确定性感知的水下速度估计，精度提升44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV依赖INS与DVL组合导航，DVL速度估计精度至关重要。现有学习方法虽在退化测量条件下有所改进，但其训练目标多采用常规回归损失，对大残差与损坏观测高度敏感，且未显式考虑传感过程的物理一致性与测量不确定性。

Method: 提出NAViLoss：在导航状态域联合惩罚速度估计残差，在DVL测量域惩罚波束一致性残差；采用有界形式限制大残差影响，并通过自适应机制调节波束几何不确定性。将NAViLoss与DeepONet架构集成，形成NAVi-DeepONet模型进行速度无缝估计。基于多次真实海试采集的约10,000米半合成AUV实验数据评估。

Result: 与传统及基于学习的基线方法相比，速度估计精度提升44%，验证了导航感知与不确定性自适应损失设计在鲁棒学习型水下速度估计中的有效性。

Conclusion: NAViLoss通过融合导航域与测量域的物理一致性与不确定性建模，有效提升了学习型AUV速度估计的鲁棒性与精度，为水下导航提供可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) commonly rely on inertial navigation systems (INS) aided by Doppler velocity logs (DVLs) for reliable underwater navigation. Accurate DVL velocity estimation is therefore essential for successful operation. Recent learning-based methods have demonstrated improved DVL velocity estimation, particularly under degraded measurement conditions. However, their training objectives typically rely on conventional regression losses that are highly sensitive to large residuals and corrupted observations. Additionally, they do not explicitly account for the physical consistency and measurement uncertainty associated with the underlying sensing process. To address these limitations, this paper introduces navigation-aware loss (NAViLoss), a robust and uncertainty-aware objective function for learning-based AUV velocity estimation. NAViLoss jointly penalizes the velocity-estimation residual in the navigation-state domain and the beam-consistency residual in the DVL measurement domain. Its bounded formulation limits the influence of large residuals, while an adaptive mechanism regulates the uncertainty in beam geometry. Furthermore, NAViLoss is integrated with a DeepONet architecture to form a novel NAVi-DeepONet model for seamless estimation of an underwater vehicle's velocity. Lastly, our model is evaluated using approximately 10,000m of semi-synthetic AUV experimental data collected during multiple real-world sea trials. Experimental results demonstrate a 44% improvement in velocity-estimation accuracy compared with conventional and learning-based baselines. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of navigation-aware and uncertainty-adaptive loss design for robust learning-based underwater velocity estimation.

</details>


### [51] [Do Better Visual Representations Always Lead to Better End-to-End Autonomous Driving?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09695)
*Zihao Zhang,Haochen Tian,Tianyu Li,Changhui Jing,Jingliang He,Naisheng Ye,Ziyuan Pu,Zhenjie Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出与规划器无关的视觉表征对齐框架ViRA，系统研究VFM表征何时提升端到端自动驾驶性能，并基于发现开发ViRA-Diffusion，在NAVSIM v2上取得92.3 EPDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型（VFM）凭借强大表征被广泛集成到端到端自动驾驶中，但其表征何时真正提升驾驶性能尚不明确。作者希望厘清VFM引导对规划性能的作用条件与影响因素。

Method: 提出ViRA——一种规划器无关的视觉表征对齐框架，保持规划器架构与推理成本不变；据此开展系统性实证研究，考察不同端到端规划器、不同VFM对齐目标以及辅助感知监督的影响。基于发现设计ViRA-Diffusion：一种不依赖辅助感知监督的扩散式规划器。

Result: 研究发现三点：(1) VFM引导的视觉表征在多种端到端规划器上一致提升驾驶性能，且增益延伸至零样本闭环评估；(2) VFM目标选择会影响规划性能，对预训练VFM编码器的规划器而言，对齐到不同的VFM可带来额外收益；(3) 辅助感知监督可降低对VFM目标选择的敏感性，将五个目标间的EPDMS差值从2.7分缩小至0.5分，并可能补偿效果较弱的VFM目标。ViRA-Diffusion在NAVSIM v2 navtest上达到92.3 EPDMS，比对比中最新方法至少高出1.9分。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在将VFM集成到端到端自动驾驶时，应联合考虑VFM目标选择与规划器监督设计。代码与演示已开源。

Abstract: Visual foundation models (VFMs) are increasingly integrated into end-to-end autonomous driving for their powerful representations, yet it remains unclear when these representations improve driving performance. To investigate this question, we introduce ViRA, a planner-agnostic visual representation alignment framework that keeps the planner architecture and inference cost unchanged. Our study reveals three findings: (1) VFM-guided visual representations consistently improve driving performance across diverse end-to-end planners, with gains extending to zero-shot closed-loop evaluation. (2) The choice of VFM target matters for planning performance, and alignment to a different VFM can further benefit planners with pre-trained VFM encoders. (3) Auxiliary perception supervision reduces sensitivity to VFM target selection, narrowing the EPDMS spread across five targets from 2.7 to 0.5 points and potentially compensating for less effective VFM targets. Guided by these findings, we develop ViRA-Diffusion, a diffusion-based planner trained without auxiliary perception supervision, which achieves 92.3 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 navtest, outperforming recent methods in our comparison by at least 1.9 points. The results motivate jointly considering target selection and planner supervision when integrating VFMs into end-to-end autonomous driving. The results and demo are available at https://github.com/OpenDriveLab/ViRA.

</details>


### [52] [RoboPace: Contact-Aware Time-Optimal Retiming for Action-Chunk Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09696)
*Mimo Shirasaka,Takehiko Ohkawa,Takuya Okubo,Nicola Scianca,Tatsuya Matsushima,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboPace——一种在线重定时层，在不重训策略的前提下保留几何路径、自适应调整执行时序以兼顾接触安全与机器人运动学/动力学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作数据采集正从遥操作转向无需机器人的演示（如UMI或直接人手演示），使VLA策略继承了演示者的时序。但人类时序无法直接迁移到机器人：柔性手可承受快速接触，而机器人因执行器和跟踪限制可能过冲；反之，机器人在自由空间可更快移动。因此需要统一协调执行速度与接触安全的方法。

Method: RoboPace是在线重定时层：保持策略的几何路径不变，仅调整其时序，并遵循目标机器人的运动学与动力学约束；基于预测的接触自适应执行速度，联合考虑接触相关的速度上限和机器人运动约束；无需策略重训练，可实时运行。

Result: 在双臂机器人上的三个接触密集任务中，更快的匀速执行与仅考虑物理极限的重定时基本失败；RoboPace相比慢速匀速执行取得更高整体成功率，并在约一半时间内完成五条指令中的四条，兼具慢速执行的可靠性且无其时间代价。

Conclusion: 通过在路径保持下按接触预测与机器人约束在线重定时，可有效调和执行速度与接触安全，在提升速度的同时保持可靠性。

Abstract: Robot manipulation data collection has been shifting from teleoperation toward robot-free demonstrations, through interfaces such as the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) or directly from human hands. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies trained on such data inherit the demonstrator's timing. Yet human timing does not directly transfer to robots: compliant hands tolerate fast contact, whereas robots may overshoot due to actuator and tracking limitations; conversely, robots can move faster in free space. This motivates a unified approach that reconciles execution speed with contact safety. We present RoboPace, an online retiming layer that preserves the policy's geometric path while adapting its timing, respecting the target robot's kinematic and dynamic constraints. It adapts execution speed based on predicted contact, jointly accounting for contact-dependent speed limits and the robot's motion constraints. The method requires no policy retraining and operates in real time. Across three contact-rich tasks on a dual-arm robot, faster uniform execution and physical-limit-only retiming largely fail. RoboPace instead achieves higher overall success than slow uniform execution while completing four of five commands in approximately half the time, retaining the reliability of slow execution without its time cost.

</details>


### [53] [YUBI-STAG: Contact and Semantic-Rich Alignment for VLAs via Automated Video-Language Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09718)
*Masatoshi Tateno,Takehiko Ohkawa,Yueh-Hua Wu,Hanlong Li,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yoichi Sato,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出YUBI-STAG自动标注框架，为操作演示补充交互语义（动作、接触对象、抓取方式等），并将其蒸馏为可从原始未分割视频直接标注的YUBI-VLM；利用该标注后训练VLA策略，提升了双臂操作性能与细粒度指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人演示通常只有粗粒度任务描述，缺少动作如何执行的信息（哪个夹爪动作、接触哪个物体、如何抓取与移动），导致预训练VLA模型难以通过语言触发细粒度操作能力。

Method: YUBI-STAG结合接触对象分割与视觉语言模型（VLM），标注物体身份、属性与状态、各夹爪动作、双臂协调及空间接地的交互。为解决其对局部序列和多阶段VLM推理的依赖，将其蒸馏为YUBI-VLM，可从原始未分割视频、仅凭腕部视角在少量推理调用中直接恢复动作结构与标注。在YUBI-STAG-Bench上评估时间、语义与空间接地任务，并用标注对VLA策略进行后训练。

Result: YUBI-VLM在更少推理调用和更短运行时间下保留了YUBI-STAG大部分标注精度，并能泛化到未见操作。用这些标注后训练的VLA策略在双臂实验中性能与指令跟随均有提升，可控制原标签中缺失的物体身份、执行夹爪、目标位置与空间关系。

Conclusion: 通过自动丰富的交互语义标注可将预训练VLA与细粒度操作语言对齐，且蒸馏为高效VLM形式可行，为接触感知的细粒度机器人操作提供有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models acquire broad manipulation capabilities via large-scale pretraining, yet eliciting them through language requires fine-grained alignment between instructions and physical interactions. Existing robot demonstrations typically provide only coarse task descriptions, omitting how actions are executed, including which gripper acts, which object is contacted, and how it is grasped and moved. We introduce YUBI-STAG, a framework for Spatio-Temporal Annotation and Grounding that automatically enriches manipulation demonstrations with interaction-rich semantics to align pretrained VLAs with fine-grained manipulation language. Combining contact-object segmentation with vision-language models, YUBI-STAG annotates object identities, attributes and states, per-gripper actions, bimanual coordination, and spatially grounded interactions. To address YUBI-STAG's reliance on localized sequences and multi-stage VLM inference, we distill it into YUBI-VLM. YUBI-VLM directly recovers action structure and annotations from raw, unsegmented video in few inference calls and operates from wrist views alone. We evaluate both frameworks on YUBI-STAG-Bench across temporal, semantic, and spatial grounding tasks. YUBI-VLM retains much of YUBI-STAG's annotation accuracy with fewer inference calls and shorter runtime while generalizing to unseen manipulations. Finally, post-training VLA policies on these annotations aligns them with fine-grained language and contact-aware structure. Bimanual experiments demonstrate improved performance and instruction following, including control over object identity, acting gripper, target location, and spatial relations absent from original labels.

</details>


### [54] [ΔWAM: Distilling Action Tangent Fields into World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09734)
*Ke Wu,Hanwen Huang,Bo Gu,Kaizhao Zhang,Xiangting Meng,Yupeng Zheng,Zijun Xu,Jieru Zhao,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Action Tangent Fields，将世界监督重新表述为"动作如何改变未来动态"的局部泰勒展开，通过 ACWM 在 Residual-VAE 空间中提取动作相关的一阶结构并蒸馏给 WAM，从而在多个基准上提升环境扰动鲁棒性，并将多步 VideoDiT 去噪蒸馏为单步推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）用稠密的未来预测增强稀疏动作监督，但可预测的未来大部分由外观与场景持续性主导，而非动作依赖的动态。作者观察到光学流、运动中心表示、潜在动作等近期 WAM 设计可从同一视角理解：当世界监督中包含更高比例的动作相关变化时，其效率更高。因此需要让世界监督更集中于动作真正改变未来的方向。

Method: 提出 Action Tangent Fields，通过动作如何诱导未来动态变化的局部泰勒展开来重构世界监督。在 Residual-VAE 空间中表示未来动态，使未来潜变量可由当前潜变量及其残差恢复；使用强动作条件世界模型（ACWM）探测动作变化与残差世界变化之间的局部对应关系。将该局部一阶结构蒸馏进 WAM，引导其去噪监督朝向与动作更紧耦合的动态，而不仅仅是从外观可预测的动态。此外将多步 VideoDiT 去噪蒸馏为单步以实现高效推理。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 和 RoboTwin2.0-Plus 上，方法一致提升了对照明、背景、相机、布局及其他环境扰动的鲁棒性。尽管未使用大规模具身预训练，在若干分布偏移下仍比预训练策略具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 有效的 WAM 监督应在保持信息丰富的同时，将预测能力集中于动作改变未来的方向上。实现细节涉及动作条件的局部一阶结构蒸馏与单步去噪推理。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAM) improve robot policies by augmenting sparse action supervision with dense future prediction. However, much of the predictable future is dominated by appearance and scene persistence rather than action-dependent dynamics. We observe that several recent WAM designs, including optical flow, motion-centric representations, and latent actions, can be understood from a common perspective in which world supervision becomes more efficient as it contains a higher proportion of action-relevant variation. Based on this insight, we introduce Action Tangent Fields, which reformulate world supervision through a local Taylor expansion of how actions induce changes in future dynamics. We represent future dynamics in Residual-VAE space, where the future latent remains recoverable from the current latent and its residual, and use a strong action-conditioned world model (ACWM) to probe the local correspondence between action variations and residual-world variations. This local first-order structure is distilled into the WAM to guide its denoising supervision toward dynamics that are more tightly coupled to action, rather than merely predictable from appearance. Across LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin, and RoboTwin2.0-Plus, our method consistently improves robustness to lighting, background, camera, layout, and other environmental perturbations. Despite using no large-scale embodied pretraining, it achieves stronger robustness under several distribution shifts than pretrained policies. We further distill multi-step VideoDiT denoising into a single step for efficient inference. Our results suggest that effective WAM supervision should remain information-rich while concentrating its predictive capacity on the directions along which actions change the future.

</details>


### [55] [AeroEval: Staged Program and Execution Validation for AI-Generated Drone Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09764)
*Kautuk Astu,Naina Rabha,Yogesh Simmhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroEval是一个agent辅助的中间件，用于对AI生成的无人机任务程序进行分阶段验证，在AirSim和Gazebo上显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能从自然语言任务描述生成无人机程序，但语法正确的程序仍可能违背用户意图、环境约束和任务级行为。在信息物理应用中，正确性依赖生成代码、移动感知、环境几何、事件驱动分析和物理执行之间的交互。现有无人机代码生成系统主要依赖提示护栏或仿真结果，故障定位能力有限。

Method: 提出AeroEval，一种agent辅助的中间件，将确定性程序分析与基于上下文的LLM agent相结合，进行分阶段验证：先验证程序语法、平台API使用和任务意图，再基于执行轨迹、任务需求和环境上下文评估实际行为。每一阶段返回结构化故障信息，用于迭代式重新生成。

Result: 在AirSim和Gazebo仿真器上使用20个导航任务和5类分析任务评测：AeroEval将导航成功率从55%提升至95%。在分阶段消融研究中，仅Code和Trajectory验证器的平均运行级成功率分别为44%和56%，而完整AeroEval流水线达到88%；各阶段检测程序结构、API使用、任务意图、避障、高度、覆盖率和事件驱动转换中的互补故障，并引导重新生成加以纠正。在主要分析任务中，AeroEval将聚合运行级成功率从一次性AeroGen的34%提升至88%（在再生预算内）。

Conclusion: 在评测环境中，将程序级验证与基于执行的agent验证相结合，对AI生成的无人机应用具有显著收益。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate drone programs from natural-language mission descriptions, but syntactically valid programs may still violate user intent, environmental constraints, and mission-level behavior. This problem is pronounced in cyber-physical applications, where correctness depends on the interaction among generated code, mobile sensing, environmental geometry, event-driven analytics, and physical execution. Existing drone code-generation systems primarily use prompt guardrails or simulator outcomes and provide limited failure localization. We present AeroEval, an agent-assisted middleware for staged validation of AI-generated drone missions. AeroEval combines deterministic program analysis with context-grounded LLM agents. It first validates program syntax, platform API usage, and mission intent, and then evaluates the realized behavior using execution trajectories, mission requirements, and environmental context. Each stage returns structured failure information for iterative regeneration. In our evaluation using 20 navigation tasks and five analytical mission types over AirSim and Gazebo simulators, AeroEval improves navigation success from 55% to 95%. In a stagewise ablation study, our Code and Trajectory Validators by themselves achieve mean run-level success rates of 44% and 56%, respectively, while the full AeroEval pipeline achieves 88%; the stages detect complementary failures in program structure, API usage, mission intent, obstacle avoidance, altitude, coverage, and event-driven transitions and the guided regeneration corrects for them. Across the main analytics missions, AeroEval increases aggregate run-level success from 34% for one-shot AeroGen to 88% within the regeneration budget. These results demonstrate the benefit of combining program-level and execution-grounded agentic validation for AI-generated drone applications in the evaluated environment.

</details>


### [56] [On-Demand Robotic Assembly via Differentiable Geometric Part Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09777)
*Millicent Schlafly,Fabio Schaub,Diogo Costa Pais,Luca Lelli,Janne Dvorak,Claire Colmont,Sven Marti,Mark D. Fuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套端到端自主流水线，可将自然语言提示直接转化为可被机器人装配的定制木结构，通过生成式AI设计与基于梯度的几何修复，显著提升了设计的可装配性并实现了物理建造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从数字设计到机器人装配通常需要专家花费数月进行手动调参，以调和零件几何形状与机器人约束之间的矛盾。这一瓶颈限制了定制化、小批量产品的快速按需制造，因此需要一种能够自动弥合设计与装配之间鸿沟的方法。

Method: 方法包含两个核心阶段：一是生成式AI智能体，将用户提示转化为初始3D几何，并在视觉保真度与选定的物理约束之间进行平衡；二是基于梯度的修复阶段，通过图注意力网络代理模型进行反向传播，调整构件几何形状以提升可装配性。该修复不仅校正了构件的脱节与重叠问题，还实现了面向硬件特性的差异化修正，直接优化几何以支持机器人拧螺丝操作。

Result: 在60个全新的自然语言输入中，该方法使86.7%的设计能够支持机器人拧螺丝，相比此前工作提升了十倍。研究还选取其中10个结构，使用两台UR5e机器人进行了物理装配演示，验证了实际可装配性。

Conclusion: 该工作为实现按需机器人制造迈出了重要一步，能够支持定制化、低产量产品的快速生产，减少了对专家手动调参的依赖，推动了从设计到物理建造的自动化闭环。

Abstract: Transitioning from a digital design to a robotic assembly process currently requires months of expert manual tuning to reconcile part geometries with robotic constraints. This paper presents an end-to-end, autonomous pipeline for the design and physical construction of bespoke wooden assemblies. A generative AI agent translates user prompts into initial 3D geometries, balancing the visual fidelity of the design with select physical constraints. The assemblability of the design is further improved by a gradient-based repair stage that backpropagates through a graph attention network surrogate to adjust component geometries. In addition to correcting for disjointed and overlapping components, we demonstrate hardware-specific corrections, differentiably optimizing the geometry of components to enable robot screwdriving for 86.7% of 60 novel natural language inputs, significantly outperforming prior work by a factor of ten. For ten of the structures, we physically demonstrate assemblability with two UR5e robots. This work marks a meaningful step toward on-demand robotic manufacturing, enabling the rapid production of customized, low-volume goods.

</details>


### [57] [End-to-End Autonomous Generation of Human Assembly Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09781)
*Faustin Arion von Arx,Millicent Schlafly,Mark D. Fuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将面向装配设计（DfA）原则编码进一个端到端流水线，仅需网格装配体即可自动生成装配工具清单、装配序列与子装配、装配手册及可装配性设计反馈；序列规划通过物理仿真拆卸与DfA成本函数实现，手册/工具标注/反馈依赖多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将CAD设计转化为装配计划目前仍主要依赖人工，工程师需推理几何可行性、工具可达性、稳定性与人因工效；且既有方法多依赖关节元数据、紧固件标注等额外信息，难以自动化与规模化。

Method: 方法仅输入网格装配体，无需关节元数据或紧固件标注。自主处理制造计划的四个部分：装配工具清单、装配序列与子装配、装配手册、可装配性设计反馈。序列规划通过在物理仿真器中系统性地拆卸物体，并施加编码DfA原则的成本函数来求解；手册生成、工具标注与装配反馈主要依托多模态大语言模型。

Result: 与Tian等人“总是先移除最外层零件”的基线相比，DfA感知的序列规划在136个由5至30个零件组成的装配体上，将机器人臂装配时间代理指标衡量的仿真装配时间降低35%；88.6%的装配步骤选对工具。视觉语言模型评判器将生成手册与消融变体对比，识别出哪些页面元素承载读者所需信息。

Conclusion: 该端到端、无需额外标注的DfA装配计划生成方法可输出装配手册或结构化失败报告，代码开源，可供工程师或AI智能体快速加速产品设计的制造计划生成。

Abstract: Turning a CAD design into an assembly plan is still largely done by hand, requiring engineers to reason about geometric feasibility, tool access, stability, and the ergonomics of human assembly. In this work, we encode long-established design for assembly (DfA) principles into a contained, end-to-end approach for generating assembly plans. Our approach takes only a mesh assembly and produces either a step-by-step assembly manual or a structured failure report, requiring no joint metadata, fastener annotations, or additional information. Four major components of a manufacturing plan are addressed autonomously: an assembly tool list, the assembly sequence and subassemblies, an assembly manual, and design feedback for improving assemblability. For determining the sequence plan, we systematically disassemble the object in a physics simulator and apply a cost function that encodes DfA principles. Manual generation, tool labelling, and assembly feedback rely primarily on multimodal large language models. Compared with a baseline that always removes the outermost part first from Tian et al., DfA-aware sequence planning reduces simulated assembly time, measured with a robot-arm assembly-time proxy, by 35% on 136 assemblies of 5 to 30 parts. The correct tool is selected for 88.6% of assembly steps. A vision-language model judge compares the generated manuals against ablated variants, identifying which page elements carry the information a reader needs. The presented approach and open-source code are available for use by engineers or AI agents looking to rapidly accelerate the creation of manufacturing plans for a given product design.

</details>


### [58] [Enhancing Robotic Perception and Adaptability through Sensor Fusion and Origami-Inspired Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09828)
*Namai Chandra,Jaison Jose,Kavi Arya,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种折纸轮紧凑移动机器人，利用车轮驱动同时实现运动与2D LiDAR视角调节，并通过IMU与RGB-D融合提升复杂光照与无纹理场景下的深度覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑移动机器人在载荷与成本受限条件下，需在光照变化和表面纹理缺失环境中恢复场景几何，而单一RGB-D传感器在无纹理走廊与强日照户外区域存在大量无效深度。

Method: 采用折纸启发式车轮实现地形自适应构型；车轮在构型间运动时底盘俯仰带动2D LiDAR扫掠不同仰角，固定轮位可保持选定视角；IMU估计底盘姿态，融合节点将LiDAR回波投影至RGB-D深度流并输入RTAB-Map；复用已有的车轮驱动机构扩展扫描几何，原型成本低于300美元、重量低于2 kg。

Result: 在无纹理室内走廊与日照户外区域分别测试，每种传感器配置各运行3次；平均全帧无效深度比例在室内由21%降至11%，户外由48%降至18%。

Conclusion: 该原型用同一套车轮驱动机构同时实现运动重构与连续可调LiDAR视角，在低成本、轻载荷约束下提升了深度覆盖能力。

Abstract: Compact mobile robots must recover scene geometry under changing lighting and surface texture while working within tight payload and cost limits. We present a compact mobile robot that uses origami-inspired wheels for locomotion and active control of its sensing geometry. As the wheels move between terrain-adaptive configurations, the changing chassis pitch sweeps a 2D LiDAR through intermediate elevations; held wheel positions provide a chosen viewing angle. An IMU accounts for chassis attitude, and a fusion node projects LiDAR returns into the RGB-D depth stream supplied to RTAB-Map. The arrangement uses the wheel actuation already present on a sub-300 USD, sub-2 kg prototype to extend the scanner's viewing geometry. We assess depth fusion in a textureless indoor corridor and an outdoor sunlit area, with three runs per sensor configuration in each setting. Mean full-frame invalid-depth fractions fell from 21% to 11% indoors and from 48% to 18% outdoors. The prototype combines improved depth coverage with a continuously adjustable LiDAR viewpoint using the same actuation that reconfigures its wheels.

</details>


### [59] [Towards Accurate End-Effector Localization for UMI-Style Robotic Manipulation Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09857)
*Junjie Zhang,Deteng Zhang,Zhisong Xu,Bo Sun,Liuyang Li,Yihong Tian,Jie Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出MILD操纵界面定位数据集（真实86序列+Isaac Sim仿真子集）与AprilVINS方法（鱼眼视觉惯性+序列局部AprilTag几何），为UMI式示教采集提供诊断性基准框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人示教学习在近距离操作与相机遮挡下需要准确且时间完整的末端执行器定位。现有SLAM基准侧重导航运动，而操纵数据集侧重策略学习而非定位评测，缺乏面向示教采集的定位基准与统一评测协议。

Method: 构建MILD数据集：真实子集含Insta360 X5与Insight9的86条序列、15类重复桌面任务、标定资产及每次执行的机器人末端参考轨迹；仿真子集MILD-Sim基于Isaac Sim扩展任务覆盖以进行受控操纵回放研究。提出AprilVINS，将鱼眼视觉惯性估计与序列局部AprilTag几何结合，并将先验准入与联合优化状态的受保护导出分离，以支持无需预先勘测基准图标的标记增强示教工作空间。

Result: 在仪表化真实录制上基准测试视觉惯性与标记辅助系统，同名义任务下TCP相对轨迹误差与时间覆盖率差异显著。在Insta360 AprilTag4录制上，采用统一协议与序列特定配置，AprilVINS(full)达到毫米级SE(3)对齐的TCP相对APE RMSE，时间完成率高，且报告误差低于各测试路线在其自身协议下的结果；无标签因子的鱼眼VIO仍停留在厘米级。消融实验将精度与可导出性分离，MILD-Sim回放研究提供任务特定的容差参考以解释误差量级。

Conclusion: MILD与AprilVINS共同构成面向UMI式示教采集的诊断性基准框架。代码、数据集与评测清单将在接收后发布。

Abstract: Robot demonstration learning requires accurate and temporally complete end-effector localization during close-range manipulation and camera occlusion. Existing SLAM benchmarks emphasize navigation motions, whereas manipulation datasets prioritize policy learning over localization evaluation. We introduce MILD, a Manipulation-Interface Localization Dataset with real-world and simulation sequences. The real-world subset provides 86 sensor sequences from Insta360 X5 and Insight9 across 15 repeated tabletop tasks, calibration assets, and a per-execution robot end-effector reference trajectory. The simulation subset, MILD-Sim, extends task coverage in Isaac Sim for controlled manipulation-replay studies. Benchmarking visual-inertial and fiducial-aided systems on instrumented real-world recordings reveals large differences in both TCP-relative trajectory error and temporal coverage, even under the same nominal task. To support marker-augmented teaching workspaces without a pre-surveyed fiducial map, we present AprilVINS, which combines fisheye visual-inertial estimation with sequence-local AprilTag geometry and separates prior admission from guarded export of the jointly optimized state. On Insta360 AprilTag4 recordings, AprilVINS(full) under a unified protocol with sequence-specific profiles reaches millimeter-level SE(3)-aligned TCP-relative APE RMSE with high time completion and lower reported error than the tested routes under their respective protocols, whereas fisheye VIO without tag factors remains at centimeter scale. Ablations separate accuracy from exportability, and a MILD-Sim replay study provides task-specific tolerance references for interpreting those error magnitudes. Together, MILD and AprilVINS provide a diagnostic benchmarking framework for UMI-style demonstration collection. Code, datasets, and evaluation manifests will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [60] [Juno: Taming Predictive Latents for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09940)
*Yuchen Zhu,Chenyi Xu,Yulin Zhang,Gang Xu,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Juno提出统一的动作条件JEPA框架，兼顾控制对齐表征、预测教师与可适应动力学模型，在SimplerEnv和真机任务上显著提升成功率与分布漂移鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA在表征空间预测掩码或未来观测，可为VLA模型提供预测性潜变量，但存在三个失败点：与具体具身控制不匹配、干扰动作学习、分布漂移下教师模型校准失效。

Method: 以单一动作条件JEPA为核心：预训练阶段在具身匹配轨迹上训练，并用动态CLS损失将运动加权patch动力学迁移到紧凑全局状态；策略学习阶段将当前帧JEPA patch融合进VLA感知，采用解耦推理分支与独立变换参数蒸馏未来潜状态用于动作生成；部署阶段在所有观测转移（含失败rollout）上适应世界模型，冻结适应后的教师，并用LoRA适配器与可训练动作头在验证执行上重新对齐策略，无需专家纠正或任务奖励。

Result: 在SimplerEnv上，平均成功率从最强基线Qwen3GR00T的60.9%提升至68.5%，测试时适应进一步达到72.7%；真机上在背景、高度和物体偏移下保持70%–75%成功率，而基础策略降至0%。

Conclusion: 统一的动作条件JEPA可同时作为控制对齐表征骨干、预测教师和可适应动力学模型，有效解决具身不匹配、动作学习干扰与教师失准问题，显著提升VLA策略性能与部署鲁棒性。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) predict masked or future observations in representation space, offering a natural source of predictive latents for vision-language-action (VLA) models. Yet making these latents useful across pretraining, policy learning, and deployment requires addressing three failures: mismatch with embodiment-specific control, interference with action learning, and teacher miscalibration under distribution shifts. We introduce Juno, a unified framework built around one action-conditioned JEPA that serves as a control-aligned representation backbone, a predictive teacher, and an adaptable dynamics model. During pretraining, we train it on embodiment-matched trajectories and use a dynamic CLS loss to transfer motion-weighted patch dynamics to a compact global state. During policy learning, we fuse current-frame JEPA patches into VLA perception and use a decoupled reasoning branch with separate transformation parameters to distill future latent states for action generation. During deployment, we adapt the world model on all observed transitions, including failed rollouts, freeze the adapted teacher, and re-align the policy on verified executions using LoRA adapters and a trainable action head, without expert corrections or task rewards. On SimplerEnv, Juno raises average success from $60.9\%$ to $68.5\%$ over Qwen3GR00T, the strongest baseline, and test-time adaptation further reaches $72.7\%$; on a real robot, it retains $70\%$--$75\%$ success under background, height, and object shifts where the base policy collapses to $0\%$.

</details>


### [61] [Many Ways to Succeed: Diversity-Driven RL Fine-Tuning for VLA Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09943)
*Haoru Li,Jinmei Liu,Zhiyong Wang,Xiaoming Li,Zhenhong Sun,Daoyi Dong,Chunlin Chen,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DRIVE，将成功行为的多样性显式建模为强化学习目标，以提升视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的分布外（OOD）泛化能力，并在多个仿真基准与真实双臂平台上取得一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 微调虽能通过闭环经验改进 VLA 策略，但超出微调分布后泛化仍受限。作者分析发现 RL 会全局收缩行为分布，同时却使成功轨迹更加多样、以更少 rollout 获得成功，并覆盖更多潜在任务有效解空间，这种更广的成功模式覆盖可能为分布偏移提供替代策略，因此值得将其转化为显式优化目标。

Method: 提出 DRIVE（Diversity-driven RL fIne-tuning for VLA gEneralization）：在匹配任务条件下对 rollout 分组，通过时间对齐比较轨迹，并由相对行为多样性导出成功条件下的内在奖励。该设计鼓励覆盖更广的可行解，同时避免奖励多样化的失败或表面的时序差异。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus、ManiSkill3 与 RoboTwin 2.0 上，DRIVE 相比原始 RL 微调在 π0 上平均 OOD 性能提升 5.3 分，在 π0.5 上提升 2.0 分。在双臂 AgileX PiPER-X 平台上，平均 OOD 成功率从 64.1% 提升至 73.3%（+9.2 分），表明增益在物理部署下依然保持。

Conclusion: 成功行为多样性可作为有效的 RL 优化信号，DRIVE 通过成功条件下的多样性内在奖励显著提升 VLA 策略的 OOD 泛化能力，且该改进可迁移至真实机器人部署。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning improves vision-language-action (VLA) policies through closed-loop experience, yet generalization beyond the fine-tuning distribution remains limited. Our analysis reveals a selective reshaping of exploration: RL contracts behavior globally, yet diversifies successful trajectories, elicits success with fewer rollouts, and covers more of the latent task-valid solution space than supervised fine-tuning. Broader successful-mode coverage may provide alternative strategies under distribution shifts. Inspired by this, we introduce DRIVE (Diversity-driven RL fIne-tuning for VLA gEneralization), which turns successful-behavior diversity into an explicit RL objective. DRIVE groups rollouts under matched task conditions, compares their trajectories with temporal alignment, and derives a success-conditioned intrinsic reward from relative behavioral diversity. This design encourages broader coverage of feasible solutions without rewarding diverse failures or superficial timing differences. Across LIBERO-Plus, ManiSkill3, and RoboTwin 2.0, DRIVE improves the average out-of-domain (OOD) performance over vanilla RL fine-tuning by 5.3 points on $π_0$ and 2.0 points on $π_{0.5}$. On a dual-arm AgileX PiPER-X platform, DRIVE further increases average OOD success from 64.1% to 73.3% (+9.2 points), demonstrating gains that persist under physical deployment.

</details>


### [62] [Borrowed Eyes: Markerless Nano-UAV Flight with an Active Quadruped Observer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09967)
*Alejandro Lorite Mora,Dimitrios Arapis,Andrés Faíña*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种四足机器人搭载机械臂相机的视觉定位系统，为纳米无人机提供外部定位，使其在GNSS拒止环境中完成起降轨迹，中位三维定位误差59 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米无人机可进入大型机器人无法抵达的狭小空间，但其载荷能力严重限制可用传感器与算力，在GNSS拒止环境下难以自定位；同时需要解决视觉遮挡导致的跟踪中断问题。

Method: 四足机器人通过分割掩码与深度信息在自身坐标系中估计无人机位置，并经实时无线电链路发送给无人机；开发感知感知非线性模型预测控制器（NMPC），动态调整机身与机械臂姿态以最大化无人机可见性，将观测可靠性作为主要控制目标；无人机仅依赖外部估计执行预定轨迹，短时视觉遮挡时平滑切换至机载惯性飞行。

Result: 纳米无人机从起飞到降落完成预定轨迹，中位三维定位误差为59 mm；系统对短时视觉遮挡具备鲁棒性，视线暂时丢失时可平滑过渡到机载惯性飞行。

Conclusion: 该框架使四足机器人能够卸载纳米无人机的定位负担，实现对两者单独均无法穿越的复杂空间进行巡检。

Abstract: Nano unmanned aerial vehicles (nano-UAVs) can navigate confined spaces that larger robots cannot, but their payload capacity severely limits the sensors and compute available for self-localization in global navigation satellite system (GNSS)-denied environments. We present a vision-based system that localizes a nano-UAV from a quadruped robot with an arm-mounted camera. The quadruped tracks the drone, estimates its position in its own coordinate system using segmentation masks and depth, and transmits that position over a real-time radio link. To ensure continuous tracking, we developed a perception-aware nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (NMPC) that dynamically adjusts the quadruped's body and arm to maximize the drone's visibility, treating observation reliability as a primary control objective. Relying solely on this external estimate, the nano-UAV executed predefined trajectories from takeoff to landing with a median 3D localization error of 59 mm. The system demonstrates robustness against short visual occlusions by smoothly transitioning to onboard inertial flight when line-of-sight is temporarily lost. Ultimately, this framework allows a quadruped to offload the localization burden of a nano-UAV, enabling inspection of complex spaces that neither robot could navigate alone.

</details>


### [63] [Decoding Neural Population Dynamics through Robotic Analog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09977)
*Wenhui Chen,Jiyue Tao,Yitao Cheng,Yutong Shi,Feitian Zhang,Xitong Liang,Ke Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究构建了带人工肌肉、多模态传感器和强化学习神经网络控制器的机器人模拟系统，复现了动物运动皮层中旋转性神经群体动力学，揭示了神经旋转与运动结果的因果联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物证据表明，精确的自主运动源于运动皮层中的旋转性神经群体动力学，但其物理效应尚不清楚，缺乏将神经动力学与运动行为结果直接关联的因果性模型。

Method: 开发了一个生物运动系统的机器人模拟平台，配备人工肌肉、多模态传感器，以及通过强化学习训练的神经网络控制器，从而构建任务驱动、具身化的模型，并与灵长类神经数据进行比对验证。

Result: 该机器人模拟系统展现出精确运动、抗损伤鲁棒性以及与动物相似的神经群体动力学；发现神经旋转产生与到达方向正交的振荡性动作，优化轨迹调整，并经灵长类神经数据证实；同时揭示了传感器与运动冗余下的反直觉神经能量原理，以及运动学习中的"尤里卡时刻"。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了神经动力学如何支撑精确而灵活的运动，架起了生物系统与人工系统之间的桥梁，为未来具备动物般机动能力的智能机器人提供了新思路。

Abstract: Animal evidence shows that precise voluntary movements arise from rotational neural population dynamics in motor cortex, but their physical effects remain unknown. We developed a robotic analog of biological motor systems with artificial muscles, multimodal sensors, and a neural network controller trained via reinforcement learning. The robotic analog exhibited accurate movements, robustness to damage, and neural population dynamics akin to animals. This task-driven, embodied model illuminates the causal link between neural population dynamics and motor outcomes. We discovered that neural rotations generate oscillatory maneuvers orthogonal to the reaching direction, optimizing trajectory adjustments, which is confirmed by primate neural data. The model also revealed counterintuitive neural energy principles under sensor and motor redundancies, and striking Eureka moments during motor learning, bridging biological and artificial systems. These findings provide new perspectives on how neural dynamics contribute to accurate and flexible movement, inspiring future intelligent robots with animal-like mobility.

</details>


### [64] [Tactile Reconstruction of Contact Task Frames and Forces for Hybrid Force/Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10020)
*Antonio Rapuano,Simone Orelli,Barbara Bazzana,Antonio Franchi,Alessandro De Luca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 仅用软光学触觉传感，在线估计接触力与可能时变的任务坐标系：将形变图像映射为压深、两个倾角与3D接触力（含逐样本不确定性），经EKF与本体感知融合，实现仅靠触觉的闭环力调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合力/运动控制需要交互力与任务坐标系（界定力控方向与运动方向）；传统方法依赖力/力矩传感或基于模型的残差，且常需标称环境模型，限制其在未知或时变环境中的适用性。本文旨在仅用软光学触觉传感实现在线估计，避免对力传感器与环境模型的依赖。

Method: 假设局部平面接触且接触力矩可忽略。方法将软光学触觉传感弹性体形变的单张图像映射为可观测量：压深、两个表面-传感器倾角、3D接触力，并为每个量给出逐样本不确定性估计。该映射通过自标注采集流程学习：机械臂施加受控接触，离线用辅助力/力矩传感器提供真值标签。随后将触觉测量与机器人本体感知数据在扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）中融合，持续更新接触任务坐标系与交互力的估计。

Result: 在UR10机械臂上装配DigiTac传感器进行控制实验：在操作者使平板刚性板做线性与角度运动的情况下，实现闭环接触力调节，且触觉是唯一的外感知反馈。

Conclusion: 软光学触觉传感结合自标注学习与EKF融合，可在无专用力传感、无环境模型假设下在线估计接触力与时变任务坐标系，并支撑仅靠触觉的闭环力控制，验证了该方案在混合力/运动控制中的可行性。

Abstract: Hybrid force/motion control requires knowledge of the interaction force and of a task frame defining the force- and motion-controlled directions. These quantities are usually obtained from force/torque sensing or model-based residuals, often assuming also a nominal environment model. This work addresses the online estimation of the contact force and a possibly time-varying task frame using only soft optical tactile sensing, under the assumption of locally planar contact with a negligible contact moment. The proposed method maps a single image of the deformed elastomer of a soft optical tactile sensor to observable contact variables: indentation depth, two surface-to-sensor tilt angles, and 3D contact force, each with a per-sample uncertainty estimate. The mapping is learned through a self-labeling acquisition procedure, in which a manipulator imposes controlled contacts while an auxiliary Force/Torque sensor is used offline to provide ground-truth labels. The tactile measurement is then fused with robot proprioceptive data in an Extended Kalman Filter, producing a continuously updated estimate of the contact task frame and of the interaction force. Control experiments with a DigiTac sensor mounted on a UR10 manipulator demonstrate closed-loop contact force regulation against a flat rigid board in linear and angular motion by a human operator, with touch as the only exteroceptive feedback.

</details>


### [65] [Video-to-Model: Automatic Modeling of Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10039)
*Akshun Sharma,Kimia Forghani,Yancy Diaz-Mercado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从视频自动建模可变形缝合线运动的"视频到模型"框架：用感知模块跟踪线体节点，再用时空CNN估计CBF–CLF–QP结构化模型的有效参数，从而在给定针速度输入下仿真线体运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体（如缝合线）的运动建模通常需要人工调参，过程繁琐且难以泛化。作者希望减少手动参数整定，实现从视频自动构建可变形线体模型。

Method: 1) 采用CBF–CLF–QP数值模型，用少量参数刻画可变形线体的运动；2) 感知模块在视频中定位并跟踪缝合线，输出有序的线体节点序列；3) 使用时空中卷积网络（spatio-temporal CNN）处理观测到的线体运动，估计结构化CBF–CLF–QP模型的有效参数；4) 用估计参数在用户指定的针速度输入下仿真线体运动。

Result: 在未见过的线体构型与运动上进行实验，框架能够从视频可靠地重建线体行为，自动配置结构化模型，并以较低跟踪误差复现预期的线体运动。

Conclusion: 该方法降低了手动调参需求，是迈向基于视频的可变形线性物体自动建模的一步。

Abstract: This paper presents a video-to-model framework for automatically modeling the motion of a deformable suture thread from an input video. We utilize a recently developed CBF--CLF--QP numerical model that simplifies the characterization of deformable string motion through the selection of a small number of parameters. A perception module first localizes and tracks the thread in video, producing an ordered sequence of thread nodes. The observed thread motion is then processed by a spatio-temporal CNN network that estimates the effective parameters of a structured CBF--CLF--QP model. These parameters are used to simulate the thread under a user-defined needle velocity input. Experiments using unseen thread configurations and motion demonstrate that the framework can reliably reconstruct the thread behavior from video, automatically configure the structured model, and reproduce the expected thread motion with low tracking error. The proposed approach reduces the need for manual parameter tuning and provides a step toward automatic video-based modeling of deformable linear objects.

</details>


### [66] [Distributed Motion Planning for Multi-Robot Systems under Topological Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10065)
*Gianpietro Battocletti,Dimitris Boskos,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于MPC的分布式控制器执行辫状拓扑约束，通过缠绕数代理实现解耦跟踪，提升执行速度并降低控制代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人分布式协调是核心难题。拓扑辫可紧凑抽象地编码机器人时空轨迹间的定性关系，但现有执行辫状约束的分布式控制器通常一次只执行一个辫生成元，导致轨迹缓慢且次优。

Method: 以缠绕数（辫的拓扑不变量）为代理，将辫规格转化为连续函数，并在MPC代价函数中加入相应项进行跟踪；由此把全局辫规格解耦为一组成对规格，通过求解局部MPC问题分布式跟踪。为保持全局协调，引入基于共识的进度估计方法，使机器人运动同步趋向目标规格。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中验证了方法有效性，相比现有方法在执行速度和控制代价方面均有改善。

Conclusion: 基于MPC与缠绕数代理的分布式控制器能高效执行辫状拓扑规格，并通过共识进度估计维持全局协调，优于逐生成元执行的传统方法。

Abstract: Efficient and distributed coordination of mobile robots is one of the main challenges in multi-robot systems. Topological constraints, often expressed as topological braids, are a popular tool to encode complex coordination patterns between multiple mobile robots, as they offer a compact and abstract representation of the desired qualitative relation between the space-time trajectories of the robots. However, execution of joint motion plans encoded as braid-based topological constraints via distributed controllers is challenging, with existing approaches, generally based on the execution of one braid generator at a time, producing slow and suboptimal trajectories. We propose a distributed controller based on Model Predictive Control (MPC) to efficiently execute braid-based topological specifications. Rather than directly tracking the braid specification, we propose to use winding numbers, which are topological invariants for braids, as a proxy. This has the twofold benefit of converting braids into a continuous function, which can be easily tracked by an MPC controller through an appropriate term in the cost function, and of decoupling the global braid specification into a set of pairwise specifications, which can be tracked distributedly through the solution of only local MPC problems. To maintain global coordination, we propose a consensus-based progress estimation approach, which allows the robots to synchronize their motion toward the desired specification. We validate the proposed approach in simulation and in real-world experiments, where we demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach and the improvement over existing approaches in terms of execution speed and control effort.

</details>


### [67] [RealtimeWAM: How Fast Can I Run My World Action Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10079)
*Huanan Liu,Ye Li,Kangye Ji,Xiaoyu Chen,Hanyun Cui,Yutian Shen,Yuan Meng,Chenglei Wu,Jingyan Jiang,Bo Li,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RealtimeWAM：一种无需训练的通用框架，通过并行执行与自适应计算协调，实现多种世界动作模型（WAM）的低延迟推理，在RTX 4090上取得8.90×–10.67×加速且成功率几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）融合视觉动态建模与动作生成，但推理延迟高，限制机器人闭环控制的响应能力。FastWAM通过去除测试时显式未来视频生成来加速，但依赖专门架构设计；通用缓存策略虽利用特征冗余，却无法刻画闭环控制中不断变化的计算需求。

Method: 提出RealtimeWAM，一个通用、无需训练的框架，协调并行执行与自适应计算：（1）利用层间依赖，将观测处理与预测重叠；（2）针对并发分支竞争GPU资源的问题，通过选择性复用实现全流程自适应计算——在视觉稳定区域缓存观测特征，复用Transformer残差，同时为小幅预测调整保留额外精化。

Result: 在FastWAM与OpenWAM上，于RoboTwin、LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus评测。RTX 4090上平均推理延迟为24.09 ms和63.09 ms，对应平均加速8.90×和10.67×；平均成功率82.75%和87.41%，分别仅低于原生推理0.02和0.53个百分点。在五项真实任务中，较原生推理平均成功率分别提升17.2和37.2个百分点。

Conclusion: RealtimeWAM以通用、免训练方式显著降低WAM推理延迟，在仿真与真实任务中均保持甚至提升成功率，证明并行执行与自适应计算协同对实时机器人控制的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) combine visual dynamics modeling with action generation, but their high inference latency limits responsive robot control. Recent efforts accelerate inference by removing explicit future-video generation at test time, as in FastWAM, an approach that requires a specially tailored architectural design. More general caching strategies exploit feature redundancy, but redundancy alone does not capture the changing computational demands of closed-loop control. To address these challenges, we present RealtimeWAM, a general, training-free framework that coordinates parallel execution with adaptive computation for low-latency inference across diverse WAM architectures. We exploit layerwise dependencies to overlap observation processing with prediction. However, concurrent branches still compete for GPU resources, limiting the benefit of parallel execution. We therefore adapt computation throughout the pipeline through selective reuse, caching observation features in visually stable regions and reusing Transformer residuals while reserving additional refinement for small predicted adjustments. We evaluate RealtimeWAM on FastWAM and OpenWAM across RoboTwin, LIBERO, and LIBERO-Plus. On an RTX 4090, measured mean inference latencies are 24.09 and 63.09 ms, corresponding to average speedups of 8.90$\times$ and 10.67$\times$. Average success rates are 82.75% and 87.41%, respectively, within 0.02 and 0.53 percentage points of native inference. Across five real-world tasks, RealtimeWAM improves average success rates over native inference by 17.2 and 37.2 percentage points on FastWAM and OpenWAM, respectively.

</details>


### [68] [Lifelong small-object navigation in changing object layouts: a benchmark and method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10125)
*Jiagan Huang,Zikun Zhou,Zijian Ni,Hongpeng Wang,Guangming Lu,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"终身小物体导航于变化布局"(LiSoNav-COL)任务,构建基准LiSoNav-Eval(28个室内场景、45类小物体)并提出IVAM-Nav方法,通过多视角检查与视角锚定记忆提升小物体导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人需在不同物体间反复导航,其中工具、玩具等小物体视觉占位小且频繁被遮挡,还常被人在机器人未察觉时移动。现有任务与基准未围绕"空记忆起步、记忆复用、物体迁址后记忆更新"这些核心假设设计,缺乏对口评测。

Method: 将任务形式化为LiSoNav-COL,要求智能体主动寻找合适视角可靠观测、累积并复用场景知识以高效定位后续目标、并在物体迁址后更新过时记忆,且初始场景记忆为空。提出IVAM-Nav:基于多视角检查(多视角主动观测承载物体的支撑面)与视角锚定记忆(将记忆锚定到观测视角,支持相似视点下的关系式记忆复用与再验证)。

Result: 在LiSoNav-Eval上,IVAM-Nav相较代表性方法表现更优。基准分析表明:物体越小、环境越大、迁址距离越远,任务挑战越大。

Conclusion: 论文定义了面向真实家庭场景的终身小物体导航任务,提供了配套基准与数据集,并通过视角锚定记忆的方法验证了主动观测与记忆复用/更新对该任务的有效性,同时揭示影响难度的关键因素。

Abstract: Household robots need to continually navigate to different objects in the same environment, many of which are small and portable, such as tools and toys. Their small visual footprint and frequent occlusion make reliable observation difficult, and they may be moved by people without the robot observing the changes. We formulate this challenging task as Lifelong Small-object Navigation in Changing Object Layouts (LiSoNav-COL). Agents must seek suitable viewpoints for reliable observation, accumulate and reuse scene knowledge to efficiently locate subsequent targets, and update outdated memory after object relocation. To eliminate the need for prior scene scanning, we also require agents to start navigation with empty scene memory. Although practical, this task still lacks benchmarks designed around its defining assumptions. To bridge this gap, we introduce LiSoNav-Eval, a dedicated benchmark spanning 28 indoor scenes with 45 small-object categories. Its lifelong navigation sequences include both unchanged and relocated targets to evaluate memory reuse and adaptation to object relocation. To address this challenging task, we propose a navigation method based on multi-view Inspection with Viewpoint-Anchored Memory, dubbed IVAM-Nav. IVAM-Nav actively observes supporting surfaces from complementary viewpoints for reliable small-object perception and anchors the resulting memory to their observation viewpoints, supporting relational memory reuse and revalidation under similar viewing conditions. Extensive experiments on LiSoNav-Eval demonstrate favorable performance of IVAM-Nav against representative methods. Benchmark analyses also show that smaller objects, larger environments, and longer relocation distances pose greater challenges. The dataset and code are available here.

</details>


### [69] [Do Vision-Language-Action Models Understand Instructions? A Mechanistic Interpretability Study on Language Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10178)
*Theodor Wulff,Angelo Cangelosi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对π0.5与GR00T N1.7两个视觉-语言-动作模型进行机制可解释性研究，通过激活修补与归因修补考察其对语言指令的依赖程度，发现两模型对抽象改写与虚假物体不敏感，但对空指令与方向性语言高度敏感，且敏感性所在层位与归因修补可靠性存在模型差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型旨在跨环境与任务描述泛化，但其动作生成是否真正依赖语言指令、还是主要依赖视觉线索与表面相关性尚不明确。VLA缺乏显式语言落地模块，只能依赖VLM主干的内在落地能力，而视觉与语言观测空间的变化鲁棒性对实际部署至关重要，因此需要对其语言落地能力做受控的机制层面分析。

Method: 在LIBERO基准上对两个SOTA VLA模型（π0.5与GR00T N1.7）开展受控机制可解释性研究：对动作生成模块的残差流施加激活修补与归因修补；采用五种策略系统性地扰动任务指令——同义词替换、语义缩放、方向性破坏、随机物体替换以及空字符串。

Result: 两模型对抽象改写和引用不存在物体均相对不敏感，但对空任务描述、尤其是方向性语言反应强烈。动作生成中的这种敏感性在两模型集中于不同位置：GR00T N1.7主要在早期周期性交叉注意力层，π0.5则分布在最早层与部分靠后层。对GR00T N1.7而言，方向性扰动产生部分最大因果效应，却几乎不改变内部表征几何，这种解离在π0.5中不明显。归因修补的可靠性具有模型依赖性：对GR00T N1.7与激活修补高度一致，对π0.5则不一致。

Conclusion: VLA模型的语言落地并非均匀依赖指令全部信息，而是选择性地对方向性语义与指令存在性敏感，且敏感层位与因果—表征关系因模型架构而异；归因修补作为代理指标的可靠性需按模型验证，不能一概而论。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models are designed to generalise across environments and task descriptions, raising the question of whether their action generation actually depends on the language instruction, or whether they largely rely on visual cues and superficial correlations. Robustness to variance in the visual and linguistic observation space is critical for real-world deployment, yet VLAs lack explicit grounding modules and instead rely on the intrinsic language grounding capabilities of their Vision-Language model backbones. For this reason, we conduct a controlled mechanistic interpretability study on the language grounding capabilities of two state-of-the-art Vision-Language-Action models, $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.7, by applying activation and attribution patching to the residual stream of the action generation modules. We systematically corrupt the task instruction of input samples of the LIBERO benchmark following five strategies: synonym replacement, semantic scaling, directional corruption, random object substitution, and empty string. Our experiments find that both models are comparatively insensitive to abstract rephrasing and to referencing non-existent objects, but react strongly to empty task descriptions and, especially, to directional language. During action generation, this sensitivity is concentrated in different loci for each model: mainly in the early, periodic cross-attention layers for GR00T N1.7, versus distributed across the earliest and selected later layers for $π_{0.5}$. For GR00T N1.7, directional perturbations drive some of the largest causal effects while leaving the internal representational geometry comparatively unchanged, a dissociation we do not observe clearly for $π_{0.5}$. Finally, the reliability of attribution patching is model-dependent: it closely tracks activation patching for GR00T N1.7 but not for $π_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [70] [Design of a Fully Actuated 4-DOF Robotic Finger With Joint-Specific Hybrid Remote Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10180)
*Hyojae Kang,Hyun-mok Jung,Joonho Lee,Dongil Park,Hyunmin Do,Jongwoo Park,Jeongdo Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全驱动的4自由度机械手指，采用关节专用混合远程驱动架构，并对其传动与运动学进行建模和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现机械手指各关节的完全独立驱动与解耦，在紧凑结构下兼顾驱动力与运动独立性。

Method: MCP关节由两组协调的刚性连杆传动驱动；PIP与DIP关节由闭环丝传动独立驱动，并采用圆形滚动接触关节（RCJ），PIP传动半径大于DIP。基于RCJ丝几何保持关节转动中丝环总长不变，并设计DIP走线使MCP固定时PIP运动不影响其差动驱动。对远端丝传动、MCP连杆与指尖运动学进行解析建模，并用ArUco标记跟踪测量滚珠丝杠位移。

Result: MCP驱动会引起PIP和DIP传动单元可测位移；MCP固定时单独驱动PIP和DIP验证了远端传动间的预期机械解耦。单独驱动MCP、PIP、DIP时指尖峰值力均值分别为21.28 N、9.22 N、5.75 N。还通过不同几何与尺寸物体考察了手指姿态。

Conclusion: 该混合远程驱动架构可实现4自由度手指的全驱动与远端传动解耦，解析模型与实验相符，所提手指具备抓取不同物体的能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a fully actuated 4-DOF robotic finger using a joint-specific hybrid remote-actuation architecture. The metacarpophalangeal (MCP) joint is driven by two coordinated rigid-link transmission sets, whereas the proximal interphalangeal (PIP) and distal interphalangeal (DIP) joints are independently actuated by closed-loop wire transmissions incorporating circular rolling-contact joints (RCJs). A larger transmission radius is used at the PIP joint than at the DIP joint. The RCJ wire geometry maintains the total wire-loop length during joint rotation, and the DIP wire routing is designed so that PIP motion does not affect its differential actuation for a fixed MCP configuration.
