The distal wire transmission, MCP linkage, and fingertip kinematics are analytically modeled. In experiments, the transmission behavior was quantitatively evaluated from the ball-screw displacements measured using ArUco-marker tracking. MCP actuation produced measurable displacements of the PIP and DIP transmission units, whereas isolated PIP and DIP actuation with the MCP fixed supported the intended mechanical decoupling between the distal transmissions. The mean peak fingertip forces under isolated MCP, PIP, and DIP actuation were 21.28 N, 9.22 N, and 5.75 N, respectively. The resulting finger postures were also examined using objects of different geometries and sizes.

</details>


### [71] [Benchmarking Behavioral Steerability in Behavior Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10198)
*Minghe Gao,Zhanxi Yan,Jiahui Liu,Wendong Bu,Xiaoting Chen,Qizhou Wang,Yi Su,Siliang Tang,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Tat-Seng Chua,Juncheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"行为可引导性"概念及首个评测基准RoboSteer，对9个行为基础模型进行大规模实证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为基础模型（BFMs）正从行为生成向通用行为系统演进，其核心问题是：模型能否可靠地按用户意图进行引导？但该能力此前缺乏统一定义与评测手段。

Method: 提出"行为可引导性"概念，定义为BFMs忠实生成满足用户指定意图行为的能力；构建首个基准RoboSteer，将可引导性划分为三级层次——条件引导、约束引导、组合引导，并基于大规模多模态运动语料库建立统一评测框架。

Result: 使用RoboSteer对9个现有BFMs开展了首次大规模行为可引导性实证研究。

Conclusion: 行为可引导性不仅是运动控制能力，更关乎具身系统如何将人类意图转化为有目的的行动；期望RoboSteer推动以"意图实现"为基础的通用具身智能研究。

Abstract: Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) are emerging as a paradigm for translating human intentions into executable humanoid behaviors. As these models evolve beyond behavior generation toward general-purpose behavioral systems, a fundamental question arises: can they be reliably steered according to user intentions? In this paper, we introduce the concept of behavioral steerability, defined as the ability of BFMs to faithfully generate behaviors that satisfy user-specified intentions. To study this capability, we present RoboSteer, the first benchmark for behavioral steerability in BFMs. RoboSteer organizes behavioral steerability into a three-level hierarchy-Conditional Steering, Constraint Steering, and Compositional Steering-and establishes a unified evaluation framework supported by a large-scale multimodal motion corpus. Using RoboSteer, we conduct the first large-scale empirical study of behavioral steerability across 9 existing BFMs. We view behavioral steerability as more than a capability for controlling motion: it concerns how embodied systems translate human intentions into purposeful actions. We hope RoboSteer will advance research on intention realization as a foundation for general-purpose embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [72] [Making Task Abstractions Executable: Control-Aware Layout Repair for a Fixed Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10281)
*Chiyoung Kim,Seungyeon Back,Sumin Shim,Doyoung Heo,Rita Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种任务抽象方法：将整体任务约束编译为可审计的仿射布局约束，仅修复声明连续坐标，保持事件顺序、时序、拓扑与控制器不变；采用最违约行更新获得条件性有限证明与净位移界，并用同编译器二次投影分离表示与优化器。三类任务抽象上两种后端均通过验证并完成各300次配对回放，风险目标扫描还暴露了仅靠布局编辑无法满足的固定事件测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务抽象可指定预期事件，但其空间布局可能使固定智能体与控制器无法完成任务。需要在不改变事件顺序、时序、拓扑及控制器的前提下，通过修复布局坐标来解决该冲突。

Method: 从结构化任务记录出发，将整体任务跟踪、间隙与驱动需求编译为可审计的仿射布局约束；仅修复声明的连续坐标；采用最违约行更新以获得条件性有限证明和净位移界；使用同编译器二次投影将表示与优化器解耦。

Result: 在三项研究者编写的任务抽象上，两种后端均对三种布局完成证明，且每种后端完成全部300次新的配对回放；风险目标扫描进一步揭示出所选证明无法仅通过布局编辑满足的固定事件测试。

Conclusion: 该方法可在保持任务语义约束不变的情况下，将布局修复问题转化为可审计的仿射约束优化，并通过条件性证明与净位移界提供可验证性；但布局编辑存在局限，某些固定事件测试可能需要非布局层面的调整。

Abstract: A task abstraction can specify the intended events while its spatial layout prevents a fixed agent and controller from completing them. Starting from a supplied structured task record, we compile whole-task tracking, clearance, and actuation requirements into auditable affine layout constraints. We repair only declared continuous coordinates, preserving event order, timing, topology, and the controller. A most-violated-row update admits conditional finite-certification and net-displacement bounds; a same-compiler quadratic projection separates the representation from the optimizer. On three researcher-authored task abstractions, both backends certify all three layouts and complete all 300 fresh paired rollouts per backend. A risk-target sweep also exposes fixed event tests that the chosen certificate cannot satisfy through layout edits alone.

</details>


### [73] [Temporal Visuo-Tactile Learning for Dexterous Grasp Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10283)
*Ken Nakahara,Aleksei Buvailik,Prokhor Kotov,Roberto Calandra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究构建了包含200个物体、10,000次抓取试验的多指灵巧手多模态数据集（视觉、本体感觉、四路Digit 360触觉），训练端到端时序多模态模型从抬升前观测预测抬升后稳定性。实验表明引入触觉（尤其是高分辨率动态触觉）可提升稳定性预测，并将预测器作为在线稳定性门控部署于真机，视觉-触觉引导的重新抓取使执行抬升成功率较无触觉门控提升10.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可凭借指尖触觉反馈以近乎完美的成功率抓取日常物体，但机器人抓取研究多依赖视觉与平行夹爪。作者希望系统探究高分辨率、动态触觉传感对灵巧手抓取稳定性预测与模型引导抓取的贡献，探索无需显式接触或力建模、可扩展的数据驱动路径。

Method: 1) 使用配备四个Digit 360触觉传感器的多指灵巧手，在200个物体上采集10,000次抓取试验，同步记录外部视觉、本体感觉与触觉流，形成多模态数据集；2) 基于该数据集训练端到端时序多模态模型，从抬升前抓取观测预测抬升后稳定性；3) 对比不同传感模态与编码骨干网络，并进行受控输入消融实验；4) 将学习到的预测器作为在线稳定性门控部署于真实机器人，用于模型引导的重新抓取。

Result: 消融实验显示，引入触觉、尤其是高分辨率动态触觉，能提升抓取稳定性预测性能；在真机在线门控部署中，视觉-触觉模型引导的重新抓取使实际执行抬升的成功率相比非触觉门控提高10.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 丰富的指尖传感与能捕捉触觉动态的时序模型，可在不显式建模接触或力的情况下支撑多指手的习得式抓取，为从触觉经验走向稳定灵巧操作提供了可扩展的数据驱动路径。数据集已公开。

Abstract: Humans can grasp everyday objects with almost perfect success rates using fingertip tactile feedback, yet much of the robotic grasping literature emphasizes vision-based grasp selection with parallel grippers. In this work, we systematically investigate how high-resolution, dynamic tactile sensing contributes to grasp stability prediction and model-guided grasping in dexterous robotic hands. To this end, we collected a dataset of 10,000 grasp trials across 200 objects using a multi-fingered robotic hand equipped with four Digit 360 tactile sensors, recording external vision, proprioception, and tactile streams throughout each grasp. With this dataset, we trained end-to-end temporal multimodal models to predict post-lift stability from pre-lift grasp observations and compared sensing modalities and encoding backbones. Experimental results and controlled input ablations show that incorporating touch, and particularly high-resolution, dynamic touch, improves grasp stability prediction. Finally, we deployed the learned predictor as an online stability gate on the real robot, where visuo-tactile model-guided regrasping improved the success rate among executed lifts by 10.5 percentage points over a non-tactile gate. These results show how rich fingertip sensing and expressive temporal models that capture the dynamics of touch can support learned grasping with multi-fingered hands without explicit contact or force modeling, providing a scalable data-driven path from tactile experience toward stable dexterous manipulation. The dataset is publicly available at https://lasr-lab.github.io/dexterous-grasp-stability/.

</details>


### [74] [Energy-Efficient Gait Adaptation via Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Quadrupedal Locomotion Across Diverse Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10297)
*Ammar Issa,Anubhav Singh,Anton Tsaritsin,Sergey Kolyubin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层强化学习（HRL）框架，将高频关节级运动执行策略与低频步态自适应策略解耦，后者显式最小化运输成本（CoT）。基于Isaac的三阶段训练实现零样本sim-to-real迁移，在多种速度和地形下降低能耗并保持鲁棒性，并成功部署于Unitree AlienGo四足机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人运动控制中，能效与鲁棒性难以兼顾。端到端RL策略将步态生成、运动执行与能量优化紧耦合，导致对奖励设计高度敏感，难以在不同速度范围和地形条件下同时实现低能耗与鲁棒性能。因此需要一种解耦式框架来分别处理运动执行与能量优化。

Method: 提出分层强化学习（HRL）框架：高频策略负责稳定鲁棒的关节级运动执行，低频策略负责步态自适应并显式最小化运输成本（CoT）。采用基于Isaac的三阶段训练流程，实现零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 与代表性单策略及分层运动基线相比，该方法在大范围指令速度下降低CoT，并在平坦、崎岖不平及倾斜地形上保持鲁棒运动；学习到的分层结构表现出自动速度相关步态自适应，低速踱步（pacing）、高速小跑（trotting）。在Unitree AlienGo四足机器人上实现零样本部署，验证了实用性，同时提升了跟踪精度、鲁棒性与能效。

Conclusion: 该HRL框架通过解耦运动执行与步态能量优化，有效缓解了端到端RL对奖励设计的敏感性，在广泛速度与地形条件下兼顾能效与鲁棒性，并具备零样本实机部署能力。

Abstract: While energy efficiency is a critical objective for legged-robot locomotion control, achieving low energy consumption while maintaining robust performance across different velocity ranges and terrain conditions remains a key challenge. This is particularly true for end-to-end RL policies, where gait generation, motion execution, and energy optimization are tightly coupled, leading to high sensitivity to reward design. In this work, we propose a hierarchical reinforcement learning (HRL) framework that separates a high-frequency policy for stable and robust joint-level motion execution from low-frequency gait adaptation that explicitly minimizes the cost of transport (CoT). The three-stage Isaac-based training procedure enables zero-shot sim-to-real transfer with improved tracking accuracy, robustness, and energy efficiency. The learned hierarchy exhibits automatic speed-dependent gait adaptation, transitioning from pacing at low speeds to trotting at higher speeds. We validate the proposed approach in simulation against representative single-policy and hierarchical locomotion baselines, demonstrating reduced CoT over a broad range of commanded velocities, while maintaining robust locomotion across flat, uneven rough, and inclined terrains. We further demonstrate its practical feasibility through zero-shot deployment on a physical Unitree AlienGo quadruped.

</details>


### [75] [Hall Effect-Based Tactile Force Detection Sensor for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10346)
*Charles DeLorey,Michael Brockdorff,Adam Metcalf,Benjamin Calmé,Pietro Valdastri,Dominic Jones*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可贴附于机器人手术器械夹持面的紧凑型霍尔效应触觉传感器，含三个毫米级感知单元（taxel），可检测法向与剪切力，经标定后在离体猪组织上验证了牵拉与滑移感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人微创手术因医生与器械机械分离而导致触觉反馈丧失，需要小型化的力感知方案以恢复手术中的触觉信息。

Method: 设计紧凑型三taxel传感器：将霍尔效应传感器与磁体嵌入可变形弹性体接触层，可贴附于手术器械夹持面；采用6自由度并联机器人进行标定。

Result: 每个taxel可检测法向和剪切力，平均RMSE为2.133 kPa；在体模和离体猪组织上验证，成功实现牵拉与滑移的触觉感知。

Conclusion: 该工作提出一种小型霍尔效应触觉传感器，可为力敏感的机器人手术应用提供触觉感知能力。

Abstract: Robotic minimally invasive surgery has revolutionized surgical practice. However, as the surgeons are mechanically separated from the surgical tools, loss of tactile feedback occurs. We present a compact force sensor with three, millimeter scale sensing elements (taxels) that can be affixed to the grasping face of a robotic surgical tool. The sensor features a miniaturized design using Hall effect sensors and magnets embedded in a deformable elastomer contact layer. Sensor calibration is achieved with a 6 degree of freedom (DoF) parallel robot. Each taxel can detect applied normal and shear forces with average RMSE of 2.133 kPa. The calibrated sensor was validated on phantom and ex vivo porcine tissue, demonstrating tactile sensing during retraction as well as slip. This work introduces a small-scale Hall effect based tactile sensor, enabling sensing for force-sensitive robotic surgical applications.

</details>


### [76] [MultiFly: A Real-World Multimodal Aerial Dataset with Annotation-Efficient Label Transfer and Cross-Modal Semantic Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10359)
*Markus Gross,Andreas Greiner,Taehyoung Kim,Sivasubiramaniam Subbiah,Tomaž Cotič,Sai Bharadwaj Matha,Conrad Christoph,Oussema Dhaouadi,Simon Zieher,Surya Vijaya Kumar,Gordon Elger,Henri Meeß,Olaf Wysocki,Paul Spannaus,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MultiFly：面向RGB、热成像、LiDAR和雷达四种模态的真实低空无人机语义感知数据集，含17,272个同步样本、15类逐帧标注，仅用115张人工标注RGB图像即可跨模态传播标签，并建立四模态语义分割基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态航空感知缺乏一致标注的真实低空基准数据；逐模态人工标注成本高且不一致。

Method: 构建含四个郊区场景、17,272个同步样本的多模态UAV数据集，提供标定与GNSS-RTK/IMU数据；仅人工标注115张RGB图像，借助共享几何表示将标签传播至全部四种模态。

Result: 额外生成17,157张RGB图像、17,272张热成像、840M LiDAR点、3.4M雷达点的语义标签；迁移标注与留出人工标注平均一致率89.93%，六对模态间平均语义一致性90.94%；建立四模态语义分割基准，揭示密集LiDAR与稀疏雷达的架构行为差异。

Conclusion: MultiFly为多模态空中感知提供可扩展基础，是首个公开的真实低空航空基准，统一了RGB、热成像、LiDAR与雷达的逐帧语义标注。

Abstract: We introduce MultiFly, a real-world, low-altitude UAV dataset for semantic perception across RGB, thermal, LiDAR, and radar modalities. MultiFly provides 17,272 synchronized samples from four suburban scenes with frame-wise annotations for 15 semantic classes, together with calibration and GNSS-RTK/IMU measurements. To avoid costly and inconsistent modality-specific annotation, we propagate labels from only 115 manually annotated RGB images through shared geometric representations to all four modalities. This approach generates semantic labels for 17,157 additional RGB images, 17,272 thermal images, 840M LiDAR points, and 3.4M radar points. Transferred annotations achieve 89.93% average agreement with held-out manual annotations, and 90.94% average semantic consistency across all six modality pairs. We further establish semantic segmentation benchmarks for all four modalities, revealing distinct architectural behavior for dense LiDAR and sparse radar data. Taken together, MultiFly provides a scalable foundation for multimodal aerial perception and, to the best of our knowledge, the first public real-world low-altitude aerial benchmark that combines consistent frame-wise semantic annotations for RGB, thermal, LiDAR, and radar. Data at https://github.com/markus-42/multifly.

</details>


### [77] [Semantic-Aware Predictive Mapping for Exploration and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10382)
*Kenneth J. K. Ong,William W. J. Teo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过在CogniPlan数据集中注入门导致的歧义并对比纯几何模型与语义提示模型，发现语义门线索能显著改善门区域预测占用地图的补全效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性建图可从部分占用观测中估计未观测几何布局，支撑机器人探索与导航。但纯占用表示难以区分几何相似而语义不同的结构，尤其室内门在占用地图中可能表现为墙体般的占用格，却实际暗示可连通的其他房间或走廊。因此本文探究语义门线索是否能改善这类歧义区域的预测几何占用建图。

Method: 修改CogniPlan数据集的一个子集，在保持真实布局不变的前提下，向部分占用地图中注入门导致的歧义。训练两个模型：纯几何对照模型与语义提示模型，后者额外接收一个门通道，二者使用同一修改后数据集训练。评估指标为L1误差、F1分数与IoU，分别在整幅地图与10像素门区域掩码上计算。

Result: 整幅地图性能在两模型间总体相近，但门区域局部结果显示出明显的定性改善：L1误差由0.004342降至0.000025，F1由0.031311升至1.000000，IoU由0.015905升至1.000000。

Conclusion: 语义线索能在仅凭几何观测存在歧义的区域中改善预测性占用补全，表明为预测建图引入语义门信息具有价值。

Abstract: Predictive mapping can support robotic exploration and navigation by estimating unseen geometric layouts from partial occupancy observations. However, occupancy-only representations may fail to distinguish semantically different structures with similar geometry. This is particularly relevant for indoor doors, which may appear as occupied cells like walls but indicate possible connected rooms or corridors beyond the observed region. This work investigates whether semantic door cues improve predictive geometric occupancy mapping around such ambiguous regions. We modify a subset of the CogniPlan dataset by inserting door-induced ambiguities into partial occupancy maps while keeping the ground-truth layouts unchanged. We compare a geometry-only control model with a semantic-cued model trained on the same modified dataset, where the semantic-cued model receives an additional door channel. Evaluation uses L1 error, F1 score, and Intersection over Union (IoU) over both the full map and a 10-pixel door-region mask. Full-map performance remains broadly similar between models, but localized door-region results show a clear qualitative improvement: L1 decreases from 0.004342 to 0.000025, while F1 and IoU improve from 0.031311 and 0.015905 to 1.000000 and 1.000000, respectively. These results suggest that semantic cues can improve predictive occupancy completion in regions where geometric observations alone are ambiguous.

</details>


### [78] [OpenViTac: Learning and Benchmarking Visuo-Tactile Policies in a Unified Sim-and-Real Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10384)
*Yifan Wu,Qin Li,Nan Min,Guojin Zhong,Haoyu Zhao,Zhiyuan Li,Houze Xu,Shengqi Xu,Xingyao Lin,Zijie Diao,Zhaoxiang Liu,Shiguo Lian,Shunlin Lu,Shihao Zhao,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出OpenViTac——一个跨仿真与真实世界的视触觉操作基准，用于评测VLA、WAM、VTLA策略，并基于此构建触觉增强框架OpenVTLA，研究仿真-真实协同训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉反馈为具身智能体提供视觉之外的物理信息，有助于更可靠的真实世界交互。然而，尽管视触觉-语言-动作（VTLA）策略进展迅速，仍缺乏统一的基准来跨仿真与真实世界评测触觉赋能的机器人操作。

Method: OpenViTac将接触密集型操作组织为四个与触觉相关的能力维度，并为VLA、WAM、VTLA策略提供配对的仿真-真实世界设置以实现一致评测。基于该基准，研究不同触觉表征与集成策略对预训练VLA模型性能的影响，并据此提出OpenVTLA触觉增强框架，采用表现最佳的表征与集成策略；同时利用配对设置研究仿真-真实协同训练，分析影响跨域策略学习的因素。

Result: 识别出表现最佳的触觉表征与集成策略，形成OpenVTLA框架；并通过配对基准揭示了仿真-真实协同训练及跨域策略学习的关键影响因素。

Conclusion: OpenViTac为评测与推进视触觉机器人操作提供了统一平台。

Abstract: Tactile feedback provides embodied agents with physical information beyond visual observations, enabling more reliable interaction with the real world. However, despite the rapid progress of vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) policies, there remains a lack of unified benchmarks for evaluating tactile-enabled robot manipulation across simulation and the real world. To address this gap, we introduce OpenViTac, a visuo-tactile manipulation benchmark for evaluating robot policies across simulation and the real world. OpenViTac organizes contact-rich manipulation into four tactile-relevant capability dimensions and provides paired simulation-real-world settings for consistent evaluation of VLA, WAM, and VTLA policies. Building upon this benchmark, we investigate how different tactile representations and integration strategies affect the performance of pretrained VLA models. Correspondingly, we introduce OpenVTLA, a tactile augmentation framework that combines the best-performing representation and integration strategy. Furthermore, we leverage the paired benchmark setting to study sim-real co-training and analyze factors affecting cross-domain policy learning. Together, OpenViTac provides a unified platform for evaluating and advancing visuo-tactile robot manipulation.

</details>


### [79] [NeRFifyMesh: Optimizing Neural Radiance Fields from Textured Meshes for Robotics Scene Building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10387)
*Nillan Nimal,Mahboubeh Asadi,Sajad Saeedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将3D网格模型转换为NeRF表示的流水线，通过采样网格几何与纹理人工生成基于点的真值辐射场，从而无需相机采样或多视图渲染即可训练NeRF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域需要多NeRF模型构建场景，但已有大量3D网格资产难以直接用于NeRF算法开发与测试，亟需将网格资产转换为NeRF表示的转换工具。

Method: 通过采样网格的几何与纹理由人工生成基于点的真值辐射场（ground truth point-based radiance field），据此监督训练NeRF，规避了基于相机采样或渲染多视图图像来训练NeRF的流程。

Result: 大量基准测试表明，该方法渲染质量与基线方法相当；并通过构建统一NeRF场景及利用提取几何进行碰撞仿真，验证了该表示的应用价值。

Conclusion: 该流水线可有效将现有网格资产转换为NeRF表示，为机器人场景表示、仿真与算法快速开发测试提供可行途径。

Abstract: In robotics, scene representation plays a pivotal role in understanding and interacting with the environment. The advent of Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and its variants, as a novel representation, has opened a new frontier of research. In applications such as semantic mapping and simulation, roboticists aim to build scenes using multiple NeRF models, each representing an object. While extensive datasets of 3D mesh models already exist, there is an urgent need to develop tools to convert these assets to NeRF models for rapid algorithm development and testing. This paper presents a new pipeline for converting existing mesh models to NeRF representations by artificially generating a ground truth point-based radiance field through sampling mesh geometry and texture. This approach alleviates the need for camera-based sampling or rendering multi-view images of the original mesh to train the NeRF model. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates that our method yields comparable rendering quality to the baselines. Additionally, the application of this representation is shown by constructing unified NeRF scenes and performing collision simulations with extracted geometry.

</details>


### [80] [RoboQuest: Generalist Physical Agents that Search, Inspect and Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10388)
*Liu Renhang,Navonil Majumder,Tej Deep Pala,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboQuest基准，用于评估具身智能体在目标导向探索中主动获取任务相关信息的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态基础模型虽可作为通用操作智能体，但在陌生环境中，任务相关信息常缺失于观测中（如目标物体位置、未观测属性、陌生工具效果），需要智能体通过物理交互主动获取信息并据此调整行动，而现有基准缺乏对此类探索能力的系统评估。

Method: 构建RoboQuest基准，包含十项移动操作任务，围绕搜索、基于操作的检查、交互式测试三类不确定性设计。在统一的视觉运动接口下评估五个前沿多模态智能体，以及一个在完整回合演示数据上微调的π₀.₅策略。

Result: 最佳智能体仅成功23%的回合，微调策略几乎从未成功。在提供隐藏信息的执行技能单项测试中，智能体能够完成多数所需动作，失败分析将仅少数失败归因于执行层面。

Conclusion: 失败主要源于探索不足：智能体常在观察到完成任务所需证据前就过早停止探索；智能体很少预防或修复探索造成的干扰；且多数模型难以通过试错学习。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal foundation models have made them capable generalist physical agents for a range of manipulation tasks. However, successful operation in an unfamiliar environment may require an agent to seek task-relevant information through interaction when it is absent from the observations: it may need to determine where a relevant object is, inspect an unobserved property, or discover the effect of an unfamiliar tool. We thus introduce RoboQuest, a benchmark for goal-directed embodied exploration, where agents must actively acquire task-relevant information through physical interaction, use the resulting evidence to adapt subsequent actions, and autonomously decide when to commit to task completion. RoboQuest comprises ten mobile manipulation tasks centered on three forms of uncertainty: search, manipulation-based inspection, and interactive testing. We evaluate five frontier multimodal agents through a common visuomotor interface, as well as a $π_{0.5}$ policy fine-tuned on the full-episode demonstrations we release. The best agent succeeds in only 23\% of the episodes, and the fine-tuned policy almost never succeeds. Isolated tests of the execution skills the tasks are built from, with the hidden information supplied, show that the agents can carry out most of the required actions, and our failure analysis attributes only a minority of the failures to execution. Our failure analysis further finds that the agents often stop exploring too early as they make decisions before observing the required evidence for task completion. We also find that agents rarely prevent or repair the disturbances caused by their exploration. Moreover, learning by trial and error remains difficult for most models.

</details>


### [81] [RobotWorld: Benchmarking Multimodal Agents for Robot Use Across Diverse Tasks and Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10409)
*Zhiqin Yang,Chenxin Li,Xiaomeng Hu,Yibin Liu,Weidong Huang,Jiankai Sun,Haitao Li,Zijian Wu,Yuzhi Huang,Fanding Huang,Hanwen Sun,Jiashun Liu,Jingqi Tong,Mingxin Huang,Shaoli Hu,Shijue Huang,Tianyi Bai,Xinyuan Wang,Yunlong Lin,Zhengyang Tang,Zhexin Zhang,Zhuo Chen,Xierui Song,Juntao Dai,Boyuan Chen,Jiaming Ji,Fangneng Zhan,Mengkang Hu,Wei Xue,Yonggang Zhang,Han Hu,Tsung-Yi Ho,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RobotWorld仿真测试平台，考察通用智能体能力向物理世界迁移的程度，揭示能力可迁移性与组合性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体已能编写代码、调用工具并完成复杂数字任务，但其能力在多大程度上可迁移至物理世界尚不明确，缺乏系统评测手段。

Method: 构建RobotWorld仿真测试环境，通过机器人接口将指令与观测转化为物理任务执行；包含84项任务，覆盖操作、移动操作、运动、驾驶与空中控制，并设置显式交互预算与可执行的成功判定；结合任务结果与执行轨迹进行联合分析。

Result: 智能体已能构建图像分割、相机标定、空间估计与动力学计算等复杂感知控制流程，但这些能力无法稳定组合为成功行为：智能体虽到达目标位姿却丢失任务相关物体状态、无法纠正无效动作、恢复过晚，或将未完成任务误判为已完成。不同模型表现存在差异：Astra在空间与受限接触类目标上成功率更高，Opus 5.5在连续平衡与定时交互类目标上更优。

Conclusion: RobotWorld既提供了严格的验证平台，也给出了对剩余能力缺口的实证刻画，为训练与设计更可靠的物理世界智能体确立了具体目标。

Abstract: General-purpose agents increasingly write code, use tools, and complete complex digital tasks, raising the question of how far these capabilities carry into the physical world. To investigate this, we introduce RobotWorld, a challenging simulation testbed for robot use: turning instructions and observations into physical task execution through robot interfaces. Its 84 tasks span manipulation, mobile manipulation, locomotion, driving, and aerial control, with explicit interaction budgets and executable success checks. By analysing task outcomes alongside execution traces, we identify both the capabilities that transfer and the gaps that prevent reliable completion. Furthermore, we find that current agents can construct sophisticated perception and control workflows, including image segmentation, camera calibration, spatial estimation, and dynamics-based computation. These capabilities, however, do not consistently compose into successful behaviour: agents lose task-relevant object states despite reaching commanded poses, fail to correct ineffective actions, recover too late, or mistake unfinished tasks for completion. This uneven transfer also differs across models: Astra succeeds more often on spatial and constrained-contact goals, whereas Opus 5.5 succeeds more often on continuous-balance and timed-interaction goals. By linking these outcomes to execution behaviour, RobotWorld provides both a rigorous proving ground and an empirical account of the remaining capability gaps, thereby establishing concrete targets for training and designing more reliable physical-world agents.

</details>


### [82] [AirGroundVLN: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Goal-Oriented Air-Ground Collaborative Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10421)
*Zhenxuan Zeng,Qingle Wu,Wei Suo,Maojia Wu,Bairong Zhang,Hangzheng Yu,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对空地协同的目标导向视觉语言导航（VLN），提出大规模基准AirGroundVLN与可训练框架AG-CoNAV，通过跨平台时空一致记忆与空地分层规划提升导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标导向VLN需要智能体依据自然语言描述定位并到达目标，且无预设路线。空地协同兼具广域搜索与精细定位的优势，但系统性研究受限于：缺乏大规模多样化基准；空地视角差异大且随导航推进有用观测会消失，难以维持跨平台、跨时间空间一致的情境；空地空间可观测性不对称——地面感知局部精细但范围受限，空中感知广域但局部粗糙，导致单平台规划可靠性不足。

Method: 作者构建基准AirGroundVLN：含10,281个导航片段、955个目标实例，覆盖19个Unreal Engine环境，并提供seen/unseen划分与空中可见性协议以支持系统评估。同时提出可训练参考框架AG-CoNAV，包含两个核心组件：1）Spatiotemporally Anchored Collaborative Memory（SACM），用于在空地观测之间维护并检索空间一致的历史情境；2）Aerial-Guided Regional-to-Local Planning（AGRLP），将区域级空中引导与细粒度地面导航相结合。

Result: 大量实验验证了AG-CoNAV的有效性，并确立AirGroundVLN作为面向未来探索的综合性基准。

Conclusion: 该工作通过基准与框架的双重贡献，推动目标导向空地协同VLN的系统化研究，为后续方法提供了标准化评测平台与可训练参考方案。

Abstract: Goal-oriented Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to locate and reach targets described in natural language without prescribed routes. Air--ground collaboration is valuable for tasks requiring both wide-area search and fine-grained localization. However, systematic study of goal-oriented air--ground collaborative VLN remains limited by the lack of large-scale, diverse benchmarks and two core challenges: 1) substantial differences between aerial and ground views, together with useful observations becoming unavailable as navigation proceeds, make it difficult to maintain spatially consistent context across platforms and over time; and 2) asymmetric spatial observability makes ground perception locally detailed but spatially limited and aerial perception broad but locally coarse, limiting the reliability of single-platform planning. To address these limitations, we introduce AirGroundVLN, a benchmark containing 10,281 navigation episodes and 955 target instances across 19 Unreal Engine environments, with seen/unseen splits and an aerial-visibility protocol for systematic evaluation. Alongside the benchmark, we propose AG-CoNAV, a trainable reference framework comprising two key components: Spatiotemporally Anchored Collaborative Memory (SACM) and Aerial-Guided Regional-to-Local Planning (AGRLP). SACM maintains and retrieves spatially consistent historical context across aerial and ground observations. Meanwhile, AGRLP combines regional aerial guidance with fine-grained ground navigation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of AG-CoNAV and establish AirGroundVLN as a comprehensive benchmark for future exploration.

</details>


### [83] [RFPO: Rectified Flow Policy Optimization for Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10453)
*Ting Huang,Lisiyu Pan,Haoyu Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Siyuan Qian,Yanjun Li,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RFPO是一个面向流策略（flow policy）的优化框架，通过奖励感知在线Reflow与冻结高斯PPO控制器监督，使策略在单步欧拉积分下仍保持接近全步的执行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的策略表达能力强，但迭代ODE积分导致推理开销大。直接减少积分步数会严重降低控制性能，因为按全步执行优化的策略并未被显式约束为在粗粒度数值积分下仍可靠，作者将这种不匹配称为"少步离散化间隙"（few-step discretization gap）。

Method: 提出RFPO框架：①奖励感知在线Reflow，在on-policy学习中校正学生策略引起的传输路径，使策略对粗积分更鲁棒；②冻结的高斯PPO控制器在全步与中间积分预算下提供动作空间补充监督；③部署时仅使用单步欧拉执行的流学生策略。

Result: 在Unitree Go2、Boston Dynamics Spot、Unitree H1、Unitree G1上，单步执行持续保持全步控制性能，单步回报在零初始化与随机初始化下均处于对应64步值的2.4%以内。Unitree Go2上单步执行保留64步回报的98.5%，机载平均推理延迟由4.39 ms降至0.08 ms，实现54.9倍加速；真机实验验证了稳定的单步运动。

Conclusion: RFPO通过在线Reflow与PPO教师监督的结合，有效弥合少步离散化间隙，在多种四足与人形机器人上实现高保真、低延迟的单步流策略部署。

Abstract: Flow-based policies provide an expressive framework for continuous robot control, but their iterative ODE integration incurs substantial inference cost. Naively reducing the integration budget can severely degrade control, since policies optimized under full-step execution are not explicitly constrained to remain reliable under coarse numerical integration. We refer to this mismatch as the few-step discretization gap. To address this problem, we introduce RFPO, a flow-policy optimization framework for reliable few-step execution. Reward-aware online Reflow rectifies student-induced transport paths during on-policy learning, making the resulting policy more robust to coarse integration. A frozen Gaussian PPO controller supplies complementary action-space supervision at full and intermediate integration budgets, while the deployed policy remains a single flow student executed with one Euler step. Across Unitree Go2, Boston Dynamics Spot, Unitree H1, and Unitree G1, RFPO consistently preserves full-step control performance under one-step execution, with one-step returns remaining within 2.4% of their corresponding 64-step values across both zero and random initialization. On Unitree Go2, one-step execution retains 98.5% of the 64-step reward while reducing onboard mean inference latency from 4.39 ms to 0.08 ms, yielding a 54.9x speedup. Real-robot experiments further validate stable one-step locomotion. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/RFPO. Website: https://aigeeksgroup.github.io/RFPO.

</details>


### [84] [FoldBack: Self-Correcting Masked Generative Policy for Long-Horizon Garment Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10462)
*Lipeng Zhuang,Shiyu Fan,Yingdong Ru,Zhuo He,Florent P. Audonnet,Paul Henderson,Gerardo Aragon Camarasa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoldBack是一种面向长时程衣物折叠的自校正掩码生成策略，通过推理期的精炼验证、回滚与重试三项决策，在无需恢复演示和基策略重训练的情况下实现失败交互的检测、撤销与修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有长轨迹策略在抓取失败或滑脱后仍会继续执行，即使衣物尚未达到预期构型，导致误差累积与任务失败。

Method: 围绕三个推理期决策构建恢复机制：何时精炼与验证、如何回滚、在何处及如何重试；将精炼与抓取验证对齐到抓放事件，使机器人回到可重试的预抓取构型并保留成功抓取，选择性重生成失败片段及部分未来动作，同时避开此前失败的抓取位置。

Result: 在六类33件真实衣物上，FoldBack取得75.2%的最终折叠成功率和0.837的最终掩码IoU，较最强基线（45.7%与0.689）显著提升。

Conclusion: FoldBack是首个统一上述决策的可编辑全轨迹策略，可在执行继续前完成失败交互的检测、撤销与修复，且不依赖恢复演示或基策略重训练。

Abstract: We present FoldBack, a self-correcting masked generative policy for long-horizon garment folding. Existing long-trajectory policies may continue after a missed or slipped grasp even when the garment has not reached the intended configuration. We structure FoldBack's recovery mechanisms around three inference-time decisions: when to refine and verify, how to roll back, and where and how to retry. FoldBack aligns refinement and grasp verification with pick-and-place events, returns the robot to a retryable pre-grasp configuration while preserving successful grasps, and selectively regenerates the failed segment and selected future actions while avoiding previous failed grasp locations. To our knowledge, FoldBack is the first editable full-trajectory policy to unify these decisions, enabling failed interactions to be detected, undone, and repaired before execution continues, without recovery demonstrations or base-policy retraining. Across 33 real garments from six categories, FoldBack achieves 75.2% final folding success and 0.837 final-mask IoU, versus 45.7% and 0.689 for the strongest prior baseline.

</details>


### [85] [LLA-MPPI: Rapidly Adaptive Whole-body Control of Legged Robots with GPU-Accelerated Parallel Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10465)
*Sebin Jung,Maitham F. AL-Sunni,Juan Alvarez-Padilla,Zachary Manchester,Changliu Liu,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LLA-MPPI方法，将全身自适应转化为对一组不同物理/结构参数的GPU批量接触仿真器的选择，结合回溯误差选择与前瞻MPPI规划，无需离线训练，在四类仿真任务中达97.5%成功率，并在Unitree Go2上完成负载变化、单腿失能、推箱增重等硬件验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人实时全身控制器通常基于固定标称模型规划，当实际动力学变化时性能下降；自适应方法要么依赖接触动力学不具备的模型结构，要么需针对每种预期工况进行离线训练，泛化与部署成本高。

Method: LLA-MPPI将全身自适应建模为对一组GPU批量接触仿真器的选择：每个仿真器具有不同的物理或结构参数；利用窗口化的预测误差（look-back）选择最能解释近期运动的仿真器；再通过全身MPPI规划器（look-ahead）在所选模型上优化控制。框架无需离线训练，所选假设具有物理可解释性。

Result: 在四个仿真任务中达到97.5%成功率，最强基线为74%，使用真实模型的上限（oracle）为98.5%。硬件验证于Unitree Go2：运行中增加负载仍可行走、一条腿失能后仍可行走、推动箱子至目标同时在线增加箱体质量。

Conclusion: 通过"回溯选择+前瞻规划"的模型选择机制，LLA-MPPI在无需离线训练的前提下实现了对接触动力学变化的在线全身自适应，性能接近使用真实模型的oracle，并具备硬件可行性。

Abstract: Real-time whole-body controllers for legged robots typically plan through a fixed nominal model and degrade when the deployed dynamics change. Adaptive methods typically require a model structure that contact dynamics do not provide, or they need offline training for each anticipated condition. We present Look-back and Look-ahead Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral control (LLA-MPPI). The method converts whole-body adaptation into selection over a bank of GPU-batched contact simulators with different physical or structural parameters. Windowed prediction errors select the simulator that best explains recent motion. A whole-body MPPI planner optimizes controls through the selected model. The framework requires no offline training, and its selected hypotheses are physically interpretable. Across four simulated tasks, it achieves 97.5% success while the strongest baseline reaches 74% and an oracle with the true model reaches 98.5%. Hardware validation on a Unitree Go2 shows the robot walking under a payload added mid-run, walking after one leg is disabled, and pushing a box to its goal while increasing its mass on the fly. Code, videos, and project details are available at: https://lla-control.github.io

</details>


### [86] [CMP-IRRT*: A Perception-Assisted Height-Adaptive Planner for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10470)
*Mingfan Zhao,Wendong Mao,Zhongfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于CMP-IRRT*的感知辅助高度自适应规划框架，使四足机器人可跨越低矮障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D规划流程将障碍建模为二值占据区域，无法区分可跨越的低矮障碍；同时依赖采样搜索，在有限预算下效率低下。

Method: CMP-IRRT*（Channel Mamba PointNet引导的Informed RRT*）：感知模块由校准的顶视RGB观测估计障碍区域，并将深度预测转换为地面相对高度图；规划器执行高度条件碰撞检测（高障碍视为阻塞、低障碍可通行），并用CMP引导采样偏向高潜力区域，同时保留标准自由空间与informed采样回退机制。

Result: 在2D规划基准上相比经典与神经引导基线减少探索节点数与迭代次数；消融实验支持Mamba引导的贡献；在可通行性感知场景中路径长度最多缩短16.3%；Unitree Go2实机演示展示了可执行的绕行与跨越行为。

Conclusion: 感知辅助的高度自适应规划可有效利用低矮障碍的可通行性，提升有限预算下的采样规划效率，并已在真实四足平台上验证可行性；代码已开源。

Abstract: Quadruped robots can traverse low obstacles, but many 2D planning pipelines still model obstacles as binary occupied regions and rely on sampling-based search that can be inefficient under a limited budget. We propose a perception-assisted height-adaptive planning framework based on CMP-IRRT*, a Channel Mamba PointNet-guided Informed RRT* planner. Given a calibrated top-view RGB observation, the perception module estimates obstacle regions and converts depth predictions into a ground-relative height map. The planner then performs height-conditioned collision checking, treating high obstacles as blocked while allowing low obstacles to be traversed, and uses the CMP guide to bias sampling toward promising regions while retaining standard free-space and informed sampling fallbacks. Experiments on 2D planning benchmarks show that CMP-IRRT* reduces explored nodes and iterations compared with classical and neural-guided baselines, and a controlled ablation supports the contribution of the Mamba-based guide. In constructed traversability-aware scenarios, the proposed planner reduces path length by up to 16.3% when low obstacles are traversable, and a Unitree Go2 demonstration further shows executable bypassing and traversal behaviors. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/MingfanZhao/height-adaptive-planner.

</details>


### [87] [Robotic Boomerang Throwing via Model-Based Release Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10472)
*Yang Liu,Colin Jones,Aude Billard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于模型的机器人回旋镖抛掷框架，以释放状态为核心，通过分阶段辨识飞行动力学、按释放自旋可控性筛选参数来设计抛掷动作与回旋镖，在6自由度机械臂上首次实现回旋镖返回飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产生气动升力的物体可让机器人抛掷超越弹道飞行范围，回旋镖是极具挑战的例子：其飞行强烈依赖释放速度、姿态与自旋，而机械臂难以复现人类抛掷的快速动作，因此需要一套面向释放状态的系统化建模与设计方法。

Method: 以释放状态为中心的基于模型框架：分阶段辨识回旋镖飞行动力学，预测设计变化下的飞行特性；依据在不确定接触条件下对释放自旋控制强度与一致性，筛选候选抛掷参数；据此为关节速度受限的6自由度机械臂设计抛掷动作与回旋镖本体。

Result: 首次由机械臂生成返回式回旋镖飞行；演示试验中释放自旋51 rad/s（8.1 rev/s），飞行最远达机器人基座外2.03 m，最终在距基座0.31 m处落地返回；成功释放状态与所测人类抛掷差异显著。

Conclusion: 机器人无需模仿人类抛掷动作也能实现回旋镖返回飞行，验证了以释放状态为中心、分阶段建模与参数筛选的机器人抛掷设计框架的有效性。

Abstract: Throwing objects that generate aerodynamic lift can greatly extend robot throwing beyond ballistic flight. A returning boomerang is a challenging example because its flight depends strongly on the release velocity, attitude, and spin, while robotic manipulators cannot readily reproduce the rapid motions used in human throwing. We present a model-based framework for robotic boomerang throwing centered on the release state. We identify the boomerang flight dynamics in stages to predict how flight changes across design variations. To systematically design the robot throwing motion, we screen candidate parameters according to how strongly and consistently they control release spin under uncertain contact conditions. These models are then used to design the throwing motion and boomerang for a 6-DoF manipulator with limited joint speeds. To our knowledge, this is the first robotic manipulator to generate a returning boomerang flight. In the demonstrated returning trial, the boomerang is released at 51 rad/s (8.1 rev/s), reaches 2.03 m from the robot base, and returns to touch down 0.31 m from the base. The successful release differs significantly from the measured human throws, showing that a robot need not imitate human throwing motion to achieve a returning flight. The project page is available at https://robot-boomerang.github.io

</details>


### [88] [Agentic RSR: Real-to-Sim-to-Real through Scene Reconstruction and Execution-Grounded Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10479)
*Yihan Li,Yating Feng,Shengjiu Sun,Jianing Chen,Hao Ren,Bowen Yang,Weisheng Xu,Qiwei Wu,Hui Cheng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Agentic RSR 通过同一操纵任务串联场景重建、策略开发与真机执行，实现从视频到真机部署的闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人工作区的仿真需保留任务相关交互，且策略须基于真机可获得的观测运行；但现有方法通常将场景重建与策略开发割裂处理，导致仿真与真机脱节。

Method: 提出 Agentic Real-to-Sim-to-Real（Agentic RSR）框架：给定工作区视频、任务描述与已知机器人模型，智能体恢复度量尺度，利用视觉反馈迭代细化场景，并在 MuJoCo 中校验任务相关交互；随后由编码智能体开发可执行策略，从特权物体位姿逐步过渡到视觉观测与随机化仿真。策略单次调用可交织多组观测与动作，智能体依据执行反馈继续、重试或修正方案。共享的任务级接口将策略与累积经验迁移至真机，由新观测与安全检查指导执行。

Result: 在涉及两类机器人的 18 个重建场景中，四视角 Depth MAE 均值为 0.1057 m，Lab ΔE76 均值为 11.04，灰度 SSIM 均值为 0.6990；真机实验中总任务成功率达到仿真成功率的 80%，表明仿真性能在硬件上得到较好保留。

Conclusion: 该框架将重建、策略学习与真机部署统一于同一任务接口，实现了仿真到真机性能的有效迁移，并承诺公开代码与重建场景数据。

Abstract: A simulation of a real robot workspace must preserve task-relevant interactions, while policies developed in it must operate on observations available to the real robot. Yet scene reconstruction and policy development are often treated separately. We present Agentic Real-to-Sim-to-Real (Agentic RSR), a framework that links scene reconstruction, policy development, and real-robot execution through the same manipulation task. Given a workspace video, a task description, and a known robot model, an agent recovers metric scale, iteratively refines the scene using visual feedback, and checks task-relevant interactions in MuJoCo. A coding agent then develops an executable policy, progressing from privileged object poses to visual observations and randomized simulation. The policy can interleave multiple observations and actions within one invocation, while the agent uses execution feedback to continue, retry, or revise its approach. A shared task-level interface carries the policy and accumulated experience to the real robot, where fresh observations and safety checks guide execution. Across 18 reconstructed scenes involving two robots, the mean four-view Depth MAE against reference depth estimates is 0.1057 m, the mean Lab $ΔE_{76}$ is 11.04, and the mean grayscale SSIM is 0.6990. In real-robot experiments, the aggregate task success rate reaches 80% of the simulation task success rate, indicating substantial retention of simulated performance on hardware. Code and reconstructed scene data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [89] [HuMBLE: Human Motion-Driven Behavior Learning for Embodied Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10489)
*Mike Zhang,Dongho Kang,Kevin Bergamin,Nicola Burger,Robin Deits,Jonathan Foster,Bilal Hammoud,Katie Hughes,Francesco Iacobelli,Twan Koolen,M. Eva Mungai,Zach Nobles,Shane Rozen-Levy,Jean Pierre Sleiman,Fangzhou Yu,Yunbo Zhang,Alfred Rizzi,Jessica Hodgins,Scott Kuindersma,Yeuhi Abe,Sylvain Bertrand,Farbod Farshidian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从人类运动数据中学习实时可操控、鲁棒且类人步态的人形机器人运动框架，通过"教师-学生蒸馏 + 多任务微调"在泛化性与仿生性之间取得平衡，并在 Atlas R1/D1 与 Unitree G1 上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两种极端：以指令跟踪和鲁棒性为目标的控制器往往产生机械、生硬的步态；而依赖人类运动数据的控制器在数据分布之外难以泛化。作者希望在同一框架内同时兼顾实时可操控性、鲁棒性和类人仿生步态。

Method: 1）构建自研运动数据集，覆盖多种速度与方向；2）用 RL 训练全身、参考条件（reference-conditioned）策略，再通过教师-学生蒸馏得到仅以本体感知和平面躯干速度转向指令为输入的轻量先验策略；3）用多任务 RL 微调先验策略，联合训练"目标条件任务（跟踪任意指令）"与"参考引导任务（跟踪人类数据作为显式风格正则项）"，以扩展指令覆盖范围与鲁棒性。

Result: 框架在 Boston Dynamics Atlas R1、Atlas D1 和 Unitree G1 三款人形机器人上验证。实验表明在真实室内外环境中进行直接用户控制运动以及作为分层控制栈的运动层均表现鲁棒；与不使用人类数据的 Tabula Rasa RL 策略对比及消融实验表明，该策略轻量可部署，能仅凭转向指令重建协调的全身行为，保留人类步态特征，同时保持鲁棒性与完全可操控性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过蒸馏与多任务微调的结合，成功在人形运动控制中兼顾仿生步态与分布外泛化，产出轻量、可部署、鲁棒且完全可操控的策略，适用于真实场景与分层控制集成。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in humanoid locomotion, controllers optimized for command tracking and robustness tend to produce mechanical gaits, whereas controllers tied to human motion data often fail to generalize to commands outside the data distribution. This work introduces a learning framework that balances these competing objectives to synthesize real-time steerable, robust, and biomimetic locomotion policies from human data. Using an in-house curated locomotion dataset covering diverse speeds and directions, we first learn a natural locomotion prior policy through a teacher-student distillation process. Specifically, we train a full-body reference-conditioned policy with Reinforcement Learning (RL), then distill it into a lightweight prior policy conditioned solely on proprioception and a planar torso-velocity steering command. Next, we fine-tune the prior policy with multi-task RL to expand command coverage and robustness beyond the data distribution, pairing a goal-conditioned task that tracks arbitrary commands with a reference-guided task that tracks the human data as an explicit style regularizer. We validate our framework on three humanoid robots: the Boston Dynamics Atlas R1, Atlas D1, and Unitree G1. Experimental results demonstrate robust performance across real-world scenarios, including direct user-controlled locomotion in indoor and outdoor environments, and integration as the locomotion layer within hierarchical control stacks. Benchmarks against Tabula Rasa RL policies trained without human data and ablation studies confirm that our framework yields a lightweight, deployable policy that reconstructs coordinated whole-body behavior from a steering command, retaining the human gait characteristics while remaining robust and fully steerable.

</details>


### [90] [Factorized Tactile Representation and Control for Sim-to-Real Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10510)
*Siqi Shang,Bianca Aumann,Tye Brady,Joshua Migdal,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出因子化触觉表征与控制框架，将法向力与接触斑块映射为可恢复的有效接触响应，结合Tactile Gated Policy实现跨掩码配置的sim-to-real迁移，在接触定位、力跟踪与真实对抗性轴孔插入任务上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉sim-to-real学习需弥合仿真接触与器件特异性传感器响应之间的差距，同时保留控制所需的信息。现有方法难以独立评估不同触觉表征的效用与迁移可靠性，且响应表征未解耦，导致控制策略难以在多种掩码配置下复用。

Method: 构建因子化触觉表征框架：将有效接触响应分解为接触几何、力分布与时间接触变化三部分，并针对每种表征设计专属编码与随机化；提出Tactile Gated Policy，在控制过程中分别保留各表征，且无需重训练即可适配所有掩码配置。评估涵盖响应重建、空间对齐、力调节及仿真与真实世界的接触丰富对抗性轴孔插入。

Result: 接触定位误差小于1 mm；在未见几何上力跟踪误差为1.69 N；真实世界对抗性轴孔插入任务相比未因子化响应提升35%。不同触觉表征在不同交互任务中各有优势。

Conclusion: 因子化触觉表征可将仿真接触与器件传感器响应解耦并可独立评估，Tactile Gated Policy实现了跨掩码配置的免重训练控制，验证了表征迁移的可靠性与任务依赖性，为触觉sim-to-real提供可分解、可复用的框架。

Abstract: Tactile sim-to-real learning must bridge simulated contact and device-specific sensor responses while preserving information needed for control. We propose a factorized tactile representation and control framework that maps normal force and contact patch to an effective contact response recoverable from sensor readings. The response is separated into contact geometry, force distribution, and temporal contact change, with representation-specific encoding and randomization. A Tactile Gated Policy preserves these representations separately through control and operates over all mask configurations without retraining. We evaluate the approach through response reconstruction, spatial alignment, force regulation, and contact-rich adversarial peg insertion in simulation and the real world, enabling the utility and transfer reliability of different tactile representations to be assessed independently. The approach achieves <1 mm contact localization, 1.69 N force-tracking error on unseen geometries, and a 35% improvement in real-world adversarial peg insertion over the unfactorized response, with different tactile representations benefiting different interactions.

</details>


### [91] [Rephrase Before You Act: Characterizing and Mitigating Language Sensitivity in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10526)
*Mikey Watts,Yuchen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文揭示视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）对指令措辞高度敏感，并提出一种无需修改策略或重训练的"部署期改写规则"方法，通过LLM从少量任务中蒸馏改写规则，在推理前重写指令，显著提升冻结策略在分布外任务上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型建立在VLM之上，却未继承其语言鲁棒性：仅一词之差即可导致成功率剧变（如π_{0.5}在"switch on the stove"与"switch on the hot plate"间从100%跌至2%）。这种敏感性系统性地存在，导致模型在分布外指令上表现大幅下降，因此需要一种无需重训练即可降低该敏感性的方法。

Method: 1) 用统计检验的单编辑波动与oracle短语搜索刻画措辞敏感性；2) 对少量训练任务的多版本措辞打分，用大语言模型将证据蒸馏为10–20条改写规则；3) 部署时依据这些规则对每条输入指令进行一次改写，再交由冻结的策略执行。方法不改动策略、无需逐步验证，对未见任务与指令零样本适用。

Result: 规则使冻结的π_0在12个留出任务（对抗式、VLM生成与人类生成措辞）上相对提升16–27%，增益集中在分布外任务；在π_{0.5}与LIBERO上复现，finetune内成功率由93.6%提升至97.8%。

Conclusion: VLA对指令措辞的敏感性是系统性的，可被显式规则捕获；通过在部署前重写指令即可在不重训练、不改策略的前提下显著提升鲁棒性与分布外泛化能力，且方法可跨模型与基准复现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are strikingly sensitive to instruction phrasing and do not inherit the language robustness of the vision-language models they are built on. A one-word edit can move success by tens of points: $π_{0.5}$ turns on a LIBERO stove 100% of the time for "switch on the stove" and 2% for "switch on the hot plate", and a $π_0$ checkpoint finetuned with rephrase augmentation still shows swings of up to 61 points. We characterize this sensitivity with statistically tested single-edit swings and an oracle phrase search, which shows that phrasing alone nearly closes the 21-point gap between in-distribution and out-of-distribution tasks. We then reduce it without modifying the policy. Because the sensitivity is systematic, it can be expressed as explicit rules: we score many phrasings of a few training tasks, have a large language model distill the evidence into ten to twenty rephrasing rules, and at deployment rewrite each incoming instruction once under these rules. The rules improve the frozen $π_0$ by 16 to 27% relative on twelve held-out tasks across adversarial, VLM-generated, and human-generated phrasings, with gains concentrated on out-of-distribution tasks. The pipeline replicates on $π_{0.5}$ and LIBERO, lifting in-finetune success from 93.6% to 97.8%. The method requires no retraining and no per-step verification, and applies zero-shot to unseen tasks and instructions. Project website: https://sttawm.github.io/rephrase-before-you-act

</details>


### [92] [Long-WAM: Scaling the Context of World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10528)
*Wei Huang,Bohan Zhang,Chenzhi Liu,Isabella Liu,Shuai Yang,Weian Mao,Luozhou Wang,Yicheng Xiao,Weifeng Lin,Qixin Hu,Bryan Chu,Sifei Liu,Linxi Fan,Xiaojuan Qi,Song Han,Yukang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Long-WAM 是一个面向实时机器人控制的因果世界-动作模型框架，核心发现是"能访问历史"不等于"会利用历史"——只有当视频基础模型以自回归方式预训练时，更长的上下文才真正带来收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时机器人控制需要足够的视觉历史来推断运动与任务进度，但处理长历史会延迟动作执行。因此需要在实时控制约束下扩展因果世界-动作模型的上下文，并解决"历史虽可得却未被有效利用"的问题。

Method: 1) 从机器人与第一人称视频中无动作标签地学习因果预测，建立历史到未来的结构；2) 在世界-动作适配阶段保持该历史-未来结构；3) 采用流式观测编码、异步执行与硬件特定加速实现实时部署。

Result: RoboCasa GR-1 上上下文从 0.0 秒增至 19.2 秒，成功率由 63.3% 提升至 78.7%，而双向预训练初始化无净增益；机器人域 AR 预训练进一步提升 GR-1 与 LIBERO-Long 的峰值成功率。在 LIBERO-Long、RoboTwin 2.0、DOMINO 上均优于对比方法；RTX 5090 上每个动作块（含未来视频潜变量预测）耗时 107.4 ms，可部署于 RTX 5090、DGX Spark、Jetson AGX Thor；Unitree G1 与 YAM 上实现动态长时程操作，动态叠杯成功率 95%，而 Pi0.5 与 Fast-WAM 在 20 次试验中均失败。

Conclusion: 历史上下文的价值取决于视频基础模型的预训练方式：自回归预训练才能让长历史转化为性能提升。Long-WAM 作为"记忆驱动的执行器"，可在实时约束下支持动态与长时程操作，并能与更高层规划互补用于复合任务。

Abstract: Real-time robot control demands enough visual history to infer motion and task progress, but processing that history can delay action. We present Long-WAM, a model-system framework for scaling the context of causal world-action models under real-time control constraints. Our central finding is that access to history is not the same as using it: longer histories pay off far more when the video foundation is pretrained autoregressively (AR). We first learn causal prediction from robot and egocentric videos without action labels, then preserve this history-to-future structure during world-action adaptation. On RoboCasa GR-1, increasing context from 0.0 to 19.2 seconds raises success from 63.3% to 78.7%, whereas a bidirectionally pretrained initialization shows no net gain; robot-domain AR pretraining further raises peak success on GR-1 and LIBERO-Long. Long-WAM also achieves the best results among compared methods on LIBERO-Long, RoboTwin 2.0, and DOMINO. Streaming observation encoding, asynchronous execution, and hardware-specific acceleration enable deployment on RTX 5090, DGX Spark, and Jetson AGX Thor without dropping future prediction; on RTX 5090, each action chunk, including future-video latent prediction, takes 107.4 ms. Real-time deployment on Unitree G1 and YAM supports dynamic and long-horizon manipulation, including 95% success on dynamic cup stacking, where Pi0.5 and Fast-WAM succeed in none of 20 trials. As a memory-informed executor, Long-WAM also complements higher-level planning in composite tasks.

</details>


### [93] [RoboPrompt: Intuitive Robot Policy Steering with Sparse Human Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10534)
*Yanwen Zou,Chenyang Shi,Guoxuan Xu,Wenye Yu,Wendi Chen,Ye Pan,Cewu Lu,Chuan Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboPrompt，一种通用轻量的机器人策略引导系统，通过将人类稀疏输入翻译为动作草稿并在噪声空间细化，实现对基础策略的引导而无需修改架构或微调，并结合DAgger实现在线策略提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端模仿学习策略受限于数据多样性，难以零样本部署；现有共享自主方法依赖专用硬件与操作培训，而将人类引导作为额外策略输入的方法通常需要架构改动与专门的引导性训练，限制其跨策略适用性。

Method: RoboPrompt将人类意图翻译与底层策略解耦：可复用模块把人类引导（绘制的轨迹、目标点、粗略方向指令）转换为动作草稿，再通过基础策略的扩散或流匹配动力学在噪声空间中对动作生成进行控制与细化，从而在人类意图与策略先验间取得平衡，无需改动基础策略架构或为其微调引导能力。

Result: 在Diffusion Policy、π_{0.5}与FastWAM上实现有效引导；使用被引导的rollout进行DAgger在线策略改进，经2-3轮迭代后，π_{0.5}在三个任务上平均成功率提升15.5%，三策略（Diffusion Policy、π_{0.5}、FastWAM）在Insert Bread任务上平均提升21.3%；平均人类干预次数分别下降44.0%（2.86→1.60）和81.9%（2.60→0.47）。

Conclusion: RoboPrompt是一种与策略无关、轻量化且无需架构修改的通用机器人策略引导框架，能够支持稀疏人类输入并借助DAgger实现高效在线策略改进，显著提升成功率并减少人工干预。

Abstract: End-to-end robot policies trained through imitation learning remain constrained by limited data diversity, making reliable zero-shot deployment in real-world settings challenging. Shared-autonomy methods enable human correction through teleoperation, but specialized hardware and operator training hinder deployment at scale. Other approaches incorporate human guidance as additional policy inputs, often requiring architectural changes and dedicated training for steerability, which limits their applicability across policies. We present RoboPrompt, a general-purpose, lightweight robot policy steering system that enables users to guide policy behavior through intuitive, sparse inputs, including drawn traces, target points, and coarse directional instructions. RoboPrompt decouples human-intention translation from the underlying policy: a reusable module converts human guidance into action drafts, which are refined through the diffusion or flow-matching dynamics of the base policy. By controlling action generation in noise space, RoboPrompt balances human intent with the policy prior without modifying the base policy architecture or fine-tuning it for steerability. Experiments demonstrate effective steering across Diffusion Policy, $π_{0.5}$, and FastWAM. We further use steered rollouts for online policy improvement through DAgger. After 2-3 rounds of iteration, average success rates increase by 15.5\% for $π_{0.5}$ across three tasks and by 21.3\% across three policies(Diffusion Policy, $π_{0.5}$, FastWAM) on the Insert Bread task, while average human intervention counts decrease by 44.0\% (2.86 to 1.60) and 81.9\% (2.60 to 0.47), respectively.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Mean Estimates for Short Polynomial Exponential Sums over Primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08886)
*Karimjon Ibrohimjonovich Mirzoabdughafurov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究素变量指数和的均值估计，给出 V_n(K,x,y) 的一致上界，并由此得到对数幂次节省的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究短区间内素数指数和随频率平均的估计问题，推广经典指数和与素数分布的结果，寻求对区间位置一致且随区间长度变化的最优型界。

Method: 使用非负 Fejér 核、逐次差分方法，结合广义除数函数的二阶与三阶矩估计；用 Brun–Titchmarsh 不等式控制区间内素数个数并获得额外的对数节省。

Result: 对 α=a/q+θ/q²，建立了 V_n(K,x,y)≪Ky[1/√(K log(2y)) + min{Δ^{1/2^n}(log 2y)^{(n²-1)/2^n}, Δ^{1/(3·2^{n-2})}(log 2y)^{(n³-1)/(3·2^{n-1})}}]，其中 Δ=1/q+1/y+q/(Ky^n)，界对区间位置一致。

Conclusion: 该估计给出了任意固定对数幂次节省的充分条件，并得到对整数求和情形的类似均值估计，推广了相关指数和的平均结果。

Abstract: Let $n\ge2$ be a fixed integer, and let $K,x,y$ be positive integers satisfying $2\le K\le y<x$. Consider a polynomial \[ f(u)=αu^n+α_{n-1}u^{n-1}+\cdots+α_1u+α_0 \] with real coefficients. We study the quantity \[ V_n(K,x,y) = \sum_{k=1}^{K} \left| \sum_{x-y<p\le x}e(kf(p)) \right|, \qquad e(t)=e^{2πit}, \] where the inner sum is over primes. For $α=\frac{a}{q}+\fracθ{q^2}$, $a\in\mathbb Z$, $q\in\mathbb N$, $(a,q)=1$, $|θ|\le1$, we establish the bound \[ \begin{aligned} V_n(K,x,y) &\ll Ky\Biggl[ \frac{1}{\sqrt{K\log(2y)}}+ \min\left\{ Δ^{\frac{1}{2^n}} \bigl(\log(2y)\bigr)^{\frac{n^2-1}{2^n}}, Δ^{\frac{1}{3\cdot 2^{n-2}}} \bigl(\log(2y)\bigr)^{\frac{n^3-1}{3\cdot 2^{n-1}}} \right\} \Biggr], \end{aligned} \] where \[ Δ= \frac{1}{q}+\frac{1}{y}+\frac{q}{Ky^n}. \] The bound is uniform in the position of the interval, and the logarithmic factors depend on its length. The proof uses the nonnegative Fejér kernel, successive differencing, and the second and third moments of the generalized divisor function. The Brun-Titchmarsh inequality accounts for the number of primes in the interval and provides an additional logarithmic saving in the term arising from averaging. We obtain sufficient conditions for a saving of any fixed power of the logarithm of the interval length, as well as an analogous mean estimate for sums over all integers.

</details>


### [95] [Consecutive Cycle Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08889)
*Jessica Marotti,Jonathan Needleman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究n-循环上连续数字之和的可表示性，证明n≥8时n-循环不完备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨将1到n连续排布在n-循环上时，哪些数k能表示为循环上若干连续数字之和这一组合数论问题，并判定n-循环的完备性。

Method: 将数字1,…,n依次排布于n-循环，考察所有可能的连续段求和集合；若1到Tn（第n个三角数）之间每个整数都能被如此表示，则称该n-循环完备。通过构造与分析连续段和的取值范围来证明其不完备性。

Result: 主要结果表明：当n≥8时，n-循环均不完备，即存在1到Tn之间无法表示为循环上连续数字之和的整数。

Conclusion: 对于n≥8，n-循环不再是完备的；该结果为循环连续和表示问题给出了明确的界。

Abstract: Consider placing the numbers $1, \ldots, n$ consecutively around an $n$-cycle. We ask for a given $n$, which numbers $k$ can be written as a sum of consecutive numbers on the $n$-cycle. If all numbers between $1$ and $T_n$, the $n$th triangular number, can be written as a sum this way we say the $n$-cycle is complete. Our main result shows that for $n\geq 8$, $n$-cycles are incomplete.

</details>


### [96] [More than 83.9% of the zeros of the Riemann zeta function are distinct and more than 67.35% are simple and on the critical line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08965)
*Kristian Muri Knausgård*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 通过Montgomery对相关定理的能量下界、大筛不等式与计算机辅助不等式，结合Lean 4形式化，改进黎曼ζ函数非平凡零点中单重零点及临界线上单重零点比例的渐近下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼ζ函数非平凡零点的简单性与临界线分布是核心问题。此前Lamzouri及他人给出的简单零点比例、临界线零点比例下界尚可改进；本文希望利用对相关定理的能量约束得到更强估计，并探索AI辅助的数学研究流程。

Method: 基于Montgomery对相关定理的无条件形式：能量是测试函数在零点对上的和，重数为d的零点贡献d^2，故对近邻零点对的下界会限制多重零点的能量。证明分三步：(1)对间距不小于平均间距固定比例的零点，用大筛不等式带重数完整计入其零点对；(2)用七或八个连续零点的计算机辅助不等式给出下界，区分简单与二重零点，其修正项作为存储函数的增量可望远镜求和；(3)选择测试函数使该贡献尽量大，代价是更多能量。并给出两种主要测试函数所能达到的上限。全部下界与上限在Lean 4中形式化，含解析输入，除Mathlib公理外使用三条公理，每条对应一个搜索程序返回true（Lean内核不校验运行本身）。

Result: 证明liminf N_d(T)/N(T) ≥ 1645064/1960733 = 0.83900...，优于先前0.83699...及未核实的0.83805...，故超过67.80%的零点为单重；liminf N_0^s(T)/N(T) ≥ 0.67353...，优于先前0.67250...及未核实的0.673492。改进Lamzouri两个估计：至少88.93%的零点为单重或位于临界线上；单重零点与临界线零点的比例平均至少83.67%。另给出两种主要测试函数下此类不等式能达到的上限。

Conclusion: 该工作显著改进了简单零点与临界线上简单零点比例的下界，并量化了主要测试函数下方法的极限；所有结果在Lean 4中形式化（含三条记录搜索返回true的公理），是一次AI辅助数学研究的实验。

Abstract: Let N(T) count the nontrivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function up to height T with multiplicity, N_d(T) the distinct ones, and N_0^s(T) those that are simple and on the critical line. We prove liminf N_d(T)/N(T) >= 1645064/1960733 = 0.83900...; earlier work proves 0.83699..., and a report we have not verified claims 0.83805.... Hence more than 67.80% are simple. Also liminf N_0^s(T)/N(T) >= 0.67353...; earlier work proves 0.67250..., and reports we have not verified claim 0.673492. Two estimates of Lamzouri are improved: at least 88.93% of the zeros are simple or on the critical line, and the proportions of simple zeros and of zeros on the line average at least 83.67%. By the unconditional form of Montgomery's pair-correlation theorem the energy, a sum of a test function over pairs of zeros, is known asymptotically. A zero of multiplicity d contributes d^2 to it and nearby zeros contribute too, so a lower bound for such pairs leaves less energy for multiple zeros. The proof has three steps. First, for zeros at least a fixed fraction of the mean spacing apart, a large sieve inequality lets their pairs be used in full, with multiplicities. Second, their contribution is bounded below by a computer-assisted inequality for seven or eight consecutive zeros which distinguishes simple and double zeros. Its correction terms telescope, as increments of a storage function. Third, the test function is chosen to make this contribution large, at the cost of more energy. We also prove upper limits for what such inequalities can give with the two main test functions. The lower bounds and these limits are proved in Lean 4, analytic inputs included. The proofs use three axioms beyond those of Mathlib, one per inequality. Each records that a search program returned true; Lean's kernel does not check the run itself. This work is an experiment in AI-assisted mathematical research.

</details>


### [97] [Automorphisms of general modular curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08998)
*Valerio Dose,Guido Maria Lido,Pietro Mercuri*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了素数水平大于311的几何连通模曲线 $X_H$ 的所有自同构均为模自同构；给出任意水平下计算模自同构的算法，并应用于 LMFDB 中的曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究模曲线 $X_H$ 的自同构群，判定其自同构是否全部来自模解释（即模自同构），并希望在大素数水平及一般水平情形下系统刻画与计算这些自同构。

Method: 证明依赖于一个一般性判别准则，该准则涉及 $X_H$ 的 gonality（射影直线覆盖的最小次数）以及 $X_H$ 的 Jacobian 的 CM 部分的维数；对任意水平，给出计算模自同构的算法，并在许多情形下证明不存在其他自同构。

Result: 对于素数水平大于 311 的几何连通模曲线 $X_H$，证明所有自同构都是模自同构；对任意水平给出计算模自同构的算法，并在许多情形下可证明无其他自同构；算法应用于 LMFDB 数据库中的曲线。

Conclusion: 建立了判定模曲线自同构模性的有效判据与算法框架，推广并验证了大素数水平下的模性结论，同时为数据库中的模曲线提供了实际计算结果。

Abstract: We prove that for a geometrically connected modular curve $X_H$ of prime level larger than $311$, all automorphisms are modular, i.e., they arise from the moduli interpretation of $X_H$. The proof relies on a general criterion involving the gonality of $X_H$ and the dimension of the CM part of the Jacobian of $X_H$. For arbitrary level, we give algorithms to compute the modular automorphisms and, in many cases, to prove that there are no other automorphisms. Finally, we apply these algorithms to the curves in the database LMFDB.

</details>


### [98] [Affine Coordinate Subspaces and Strong Khintchine Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09017)
*Zirui Jiang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了任意正维数、正余维数的仿射坐标子空间在收敛意义下均不是强Khintchine型，并结合Huang的判别准则给出一个Diophantine充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究仿射子空间上的Diophantine逼近（Khintchine型），刻画仿射坐标子空间在收敛意义下的Khintchine型性质与强Khintchine型性质之间的差异。

Method: 对任意正维数、正余维数的仿射坐标子空间进行构造性/反证式分析，证明其不满足强Khintchine型（收敛意义）；再利用Huang关于Khintchine型仿射子空间的判别准则进行结合。

Result: 每个正维数、正余维数的仿射坐标子空间在收敛意义下都不是强Khintchine型；由此得到一个Diophantine充分条件，使仿射坐标子空间为Khintchine型但非强Khintchine型（收敛意义）。

Conclusion: 仿射坐标子空间在收敛意义下的Khintchine型与强Khintchine型存在本质区别，并通过Diophantine条件刻画了这一区别。

Abstract: We prove that every affine coordinate subspace of positive dimension and positive codimension fails to be of strong Khintchine type for convergence. Combining this with Huang's criterion for Khintchine-type affine subspaces gives a sufficient Diophantine condition for an affine coordinate subspace to be of Khintchine type but not of strong Khintchine type for convergence.

</details>


### [99] [Invariant Primes in Lubin-Tate Space and Hovey-Strickland at Every Height](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10072)
*Chenglong Ma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了高度n的Honda形式群对应的环A_n中，在Morava稳定子群开子群作用下稳定的素理想恰为高度理想(u_1,...,u_j)，并给出稳定素理想分类及其在K(n)-局部谱稳定同伦论中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究环A_n与R_n中在Morava稳定子群作用下稳定的素理想结构，分类这些稳定素理想，并将其应用于Hovey-Strickland关于dualizable K(n)-局部谱的thick张量理想分类问题。

Method: 采用局部几何的特殊纤维论证：将不变素理想截断到单参数曲线，利用Cartier结构方程构造光滑形式商和特殊子群；将子群的generic fiber识别为连通-平展扩张的形变空间；构造Cartier障碍映射并与Tate向量求值比较；利用Fargues-Fontaine向量丛给出周期检测；再用Chai刚性定理将同态检测提升为形式Zariski稠密性，通过固定射流论证传递到Morava稳定子轨道；generic fiber断言则单独用Gross-Hopkins周期映射证明。

Result: 证明了对于任意高度n和素数p，A_n的稳定素理想恰为高度理想(u_1,...,u_j)；避开p的R_n稳定素理想为零。由此得到的radical理想分类通过Barthel-Heard-Naumann的正向蕴含推出Hovey-Strickland的dualizable K(n)-局部谱thick张量理想分类。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了A_n稳定素理想的完整分类，并建立了从形式群稳定素理想理论到稳定同伦论中张量理想分类的桥梁，统一了相关结果。

Abstract: Let $H_n$ be the one-dimensional Honda formal group of height $n$ over $\mathbf F_{p^n}$ and let \[ R_n=W(\mathbf F_{p^n})[[u_1,\ldots,u_{n-1}]],\qquad A_n=R_n/(p). \] We prove, for every height $n$ and every prime $p$, that the prime ideals of $A_n$ stable under an open subgroup of the Morava stabilizer group are exactly the height ideals $(u_1,\ldots,u_j)$. We also prove that a stable prime of $R_n$ avoiding $p$ is zero. The resulting radical-ideal classification implies the Hovey-Strickland classification of thick tensor ideals in the category of dualizable $K(n)$-local spectra via the forward implication of Barthel-Heard-Naumann.
  The special-fiber argument is local and geometric. After cutting an invariant prime by a one-parameter curve, we construct from the Cartier structure equation a smooth formal quotient $\mathcal{Q}$ and a distinguished subgroup $\mathcal{H}$. The generic fiber of $\mathcal{H}$ is identified with the deformation space of connected-étale extensions. A Cartier obstruction map from the full Honda endomorphism order is compared with evaluation on Tate vectors through completed universal covers. Fargues-Fontaine vector bundles give a period-detection statement. Chai's rigidity theorem then promotes detection by homomorphisms to formal Zariski density after a renormalization of the valuation. A uniform fixed-jet argument transfers this density to Morava-stabilizer orbits. The generic-fiber assertion is proved separately from the Gross-Hopkins period map.

</details>


### [100] [Idempotence in Carmichael and Giuga numbers and its application to the Agoh-Giuga conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09129)
*Miguel Ángel López*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究Giuga数与Carmichael数的幂等性，引入一个算术函数得到Giuga数的新刻画，推广Carmichael数并讨论相关性质与开放问题，最后给出Agoh-Giuga猜想反例需满足的基于幂等性的新条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Giuga数与Carmichael数均具有无平方因子性质，且与Fermat伪素数、Agoh-Giuga猜想等数论问题密切相关；作者希望借助幂等性这一视角统一刻画并推广这些数，寻找新的等价条件。

Method: 定义一个新的算术函数；在n无平方因子的前提下进行分析，并在部分情形下推广到一般情形；通过幂等性构造等价条件，并对Carmichael数进行推广以得到特定情形下的Fermat伪素数。

Result: 得到了Giuga数的一个新刻画；推广了Carmichael数，找到在特定情形下为Fermat伪素数的数；给出关于其性质的若干命题与开放问题；并给出了Agoh-Giuga猜想反例必须满足的基于幂等性的新替代条件（预期无任何数能满足）。

Conclusion: 幂等性为研究Giuga数与Carmichael数提供了有效工具，可导出新的刻画、推广及与Agoh-Giuga猜想相关的新条件，同时留下若干值得进一步研究的开放问题。

Abstract: This article analyzes the idempotence of Giuga numbers and Carmichael numbers. The first sections will lead us to define an arithmetic function through which we will find a new characterization of Giuga numbers. The following sections will lead us to generalize Carmichael numbers, finding numbers that are Fermat pseudoprimes in certain specific cases, and to formulate various statements and open questions regarding their properties. Finally, we will find a new alternative condition, based on idempotence, that a number must satisfy to be a counterexample to the Agoh-Giuga conjecture and which, therefore, presumably, no number will ever satisfy. We will almost always work under the premise that n is square-free, since both numbers always have that property, but in certain situations we will extend some of the results to the general case.

</details>


### [101] [On a Digital Signature Based on Matrix Decomposition and the MinRank Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09140)
*Miguel Ángel López*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MinRank问题变体的新型数字签名方案，涵盖密钥生成、签名与验证流程，分析其速度，并从安全性和鲁棒性角度评估其抗已知攻击能力，进而给出参数选择要求；该方案设计简单，可作为同类签名族的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基于MinRank问题变体构建数字签名方案的可行性，并为其参数选择与安全性评估建立基础。

Method: 定义密钥生成、消息签名与验证三个流程，并分析签名与验证的速度；随后针对已知攻击评估签名的安全性与鲁棒性。

Result: 通过安全性评估得出一组与变量规模及数量相关的参数选择要求。

Conclusion: 该签名方案设计简单，但可作为基于其核心思想的一整类数字签名方案的基础。

Abstract: The following article attempts to define a new digital signature scheme based on a variant of the MinRank problem under appropriate parameters. First, the key generation process will be defined, followed by the message signing and verification processes. The speed of signing and verification will be analyzed, and finally, the signature will be evaluated in terms of security and robustness against known attacks, which will lead to a set of requirements that we will consider when selecting certain parameters related to the size and number of variables. It should be noted that this signature, although simple in design, can serve as the foundation for an entire family of digital signatures based on the central idea that inspired its creation.

</details>


### [102] [Spacing statistics for a point scatterer on the cubic three-torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10031)
*Christopher Lutsko*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究立方三环面上不动点相互作用的特征值，证明归一化相邻间距收敛到与相互作用参数无关的概率律，该律由三平方同余密度的随机亚纯函数零点刻画，小间距分布为 s^5Ψ(log_2(s^{-2}))+o(s^5)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子混沌系统中特征值统计的普适性，特别是相互作用是否影响能级间距分布这一基本问题。

Method: 结合周期均方逼近、加权希尔伯特变换估计，以及保留素数间相互作用的傅里叶估计。

Result: 均值归一相邻间距收敛到与相互作用参数无关的概率律；该律由三平方同余密度构造的随机亚纯函数相邻零点刻画；小间距分布具有 s^5Ψ(log_2(s^{-2}))+o(s^5) 的形式，其中 Ψ 连续、正且单周期。

Conclusion: 特征值的相邻间距统计具有与相互作用参数无关的普适极限律，其小间距行为由含对数周期振荡的 s^5 幂律主导。

Abstract: We study the new eigenvalues of a fixed point interaction on the cubic three-torus. Their mean-one consecutive spacings converge to a probability law independent of the interaction parameter. We characterize this law by the consecutive zeros of a random meromorphic function built from three-squares congruence densities. Its small-gap distribution has the form $s^5Ψ(\log_2(s^{-2}))+o(s^5)$, where $Ψ$ is continuous, positive, and one-periodic. The proof combines periodic mean-square approximation, a weighted Hilbert-transform estimate, and Fourier estimates that retain the interactions between primes.

</details>


### [103] [On the algebraic and analytic ranks of the twin-prime elliptic curve $y^2=x(x-2)(x-p)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09370)
*Ruihan Chen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究孪生素数 $p, p-2$ 下椭圆曲线 $E_p:y^2=x(x-2)(x-p)$ 的 2-Selmer 群、Cassels-Tate 配对及 BSD 相关问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用 Qiu-Zhang 的经典 2-Selmer 计算与 Cassels-Tate 配对，刻画 $E_p$ 的 Selmer 结构，并在低余秩 BSD 假设下推导解析秩与代数秩相等、Tate-Shafarevich 群有限及 BSD 首项公式，同时厘清 $p\bmod 8$ 各剩余类中的障碍。

Method: 结合 Qiu-Zhang 的 2-Selmer 计算与 Cassels-Tate 配对，对 $p\equiv 3,5,7\pmod 8$ 分类讨论；在 October 2026 OpenAI 数学发布中宣布的低余秩 Birch-Swinnerton-Dyer 命题假设下进行推导；附录给出局部下降证明。

Result: 得到 $2^\infty$-Selmer 余秩：$p\equiv 3,5\pmod 8$ 时为 1，$p\equiv 7\pmod 8$ 时为 0。据此推出解析秩与代数秩相等、完整 Tate-Shafarevich 群有限、BSD 首项公式精确成立；2-主 Tate-Shafarevich 群平凡，剩余因子为奇的平方阶。精确识别了 $p\equiv 1\pmod 8$ 情形遗留的障碍。

Conclusion: 在低余秩 BSD 假设下，孪生素数对应的 $E_p$ 在 $p\equiv 3,5,7\pmod 8$ 时满足 BSD 主要结论；$p\equiv 1\pmod 8$ 的障碍被明确刻画，附录以所用坐标给出已知 Selmer 维数的局部下降证明。

Abstract: Let $p\ge 7$ and suppose that $p$ and $p-2$ are prime. We study $E_p:y^2=x(x-2)(x-p)$ using the classical $2$-Selmer calculation of Qiu-Zhang and the Cassels-Tate pairing. These give $2^\infty$-Selmer corank one for $p\equiv 3,5\pmod{8}$ and corank zero for $p\equiv 7\pmod{8}$. Assuming the low-corank Birch-Swinnerton-Dyer statement announced in the October 2026 OpenAI mathematics release, we deduce equality of the analytic and algebraic ranks in these cases, finiteness of the full Tate-Shafarevich group, and the exact BSD leading-term formula. The $2$-primary Tate-Shafarevich group is trivial, and the remaining factor has odd square order. We identify precisely the obstruction left in the class $p\equiv 1\pmod{8}$. An appendix gives a local descent proof of the known Selmer dimensions in the coordinates used here.

</details>


### [104] [Regular integral models of Shimura varieties and a conjecture of Pappas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09475)
*Jie Yang,Ioannis Zachos,Zhihao Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为辛型和正交型Shimura簇在奇素数p最大仿射parahoric层次上构造了正则整模型，其特纤维具有正规交叉且不可约分支重数为1或2，并证明了Pappas的等变修正猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Shimura簇构造具有良好约化性质的正则整模型是算术代数几何的核心问题；Pappas提出的等变修正猜想预言了局部模型可通过等变修正得到此类模型，但在最大parahoric层次上此前尚未解决。

Method: 对辛型和分裂偶正交similitude群对应的典范局部模型进行显式等变修正：以对应最大各向同性Grassmannian的minuscule余特征为参数，通过在特纤维中逐次爆破Schubert簇来构造修正。

Result: 构造出的整模型定义在反射域的整数环上，特纤维具有正规交叉，不可约分支重数为1或2；由此证明了上述情形下最大parahoric层次上的Pappas等变修正猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了辛型与正交型Shimura簇在最大parahoric层次的显式正则整模型，验证并推广了Pappas等变修正猜想，为研究其约化性质及相关算术问题提供了基础。

Abstract: We construct regular integral models for a class of Shimura varieties of symplectic and orthogonal type with maximal parahoric level at an odd prime $p$. These models are defined over the ring of integers of the reflex field and have special fiber with normal crossings and irreducible components of multiplicity one or two. Our construction uses explicit equivariant modifications of the corresponding canonical local models for symplectic and split even orthogonal similitude groups, with the minuscule cocharacters corresponding to maximal isotropic Grassmannians. These modifications are obtained by successively blowing up Schubert varieties in the special fiber. This proves the equivariant modification conjecture of Pappas at maximal parahoric level in the above cases.

</details>


### [105] [Higher order uniformity of the primes and cancellation of the Möbius function in shorter intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09567)
*Kaisa Matomäki,Mayank Pandey,Javier Pliego,Joni Teräväinen,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文改进素数分布中的Gowers一致性与Möbius函数相消结果：将von Mangoldt函数减去Cramér模型的Gowers一致性区间指数从5/8改进到3/5+ε，将Möbius函数相消区间指数从11/20改进到19/35+ε，并由此得到短区间内素数线性方程的局部-整体定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Gowers一致性结果仅达指数5/8、Möbius相消仅达指数11/20，短区间内素数线性方程研究受限于此。作者希望借助最新的Dirichlet多项式大值估计来突破已有指数障碍。

Method: 核心是改进短区间上的三线性求和。将其归结为三个Dirichlet多项式乘积的均值估计；在一般框架下，把在给定大值界下估计该均值的问题，转化为验证一个分段线性函数为正的问题，从而可纳入近期的大值估计。结合Guth–Maynard的最新大值估计，证明长度X^{19/35+ε}区间上type I/II和的Heath-Brown–Iwaniec型估计，以及长度X^{3/5+ε}区间上一般三线性和的Baker–Harman–Pintz平行四边形型估计。

Result: 在所有短区间[X, X+X^{3/5+ε}]上证明了von Mangoldt函数减去其Cramér模型的Gowers一致性，指数由5/8改进至3/5+ε；证明了Möbius函数在所有区间[X, X+X^{19/35+ε}]上的相消，指数由11/20改进至19/35+ε；并推出上述短区间内素数线性方程的局部-整体定理。

Conclusion: 通过将Dirichlet多项式乘积均值估计归约为分段线性函数正性判定，并将Guth–Maynard大值估计纳入框架，作者在短区间三线性求和上取得突破，从而同时改进Gowers一致性与Möbius相消的区间指数，并获得相应的素数线性方程局部-整体定理。

Abstract: We prove the Gowers uniformity of the von Mangoldt function minus its Cramér model in all short intervals $[X,X+X^{3/5+\varepsilon}]$, improving on the work of the first and fourth authors with Shao and Tao, where the exponent was $5/8$. This also implies a local-to-global theorem for linear equations in primes in such short intervals.
  We also show that the Möbius function has cancellation in all intervals $[X,X+X^{19/35+\varepsilon}]$, improving on the work of the first and fourth authors, where the exponent was $11/20$.
  Both improvements are based on improved treatment of trilinear sums over short intervals which naturally reduces to estimating mean values of products of three Dirichlet polynomials. In a general setting, we reduce the task of estimating mean values of products of Dirichlet polynomials with given large value bounds to the task of showing that a certain piecewise linear function is positive. This reduction allows recent large value estimates to be incorporated.
  Combining this general framework with the most recent large value estimates stemming from the work of Guth and Maynard, we prove a Heath-Brown--Iwaniec type estimate for type I/II sums in intervals of length $X^{19/35+\varepsilon}$ and a Baker--Harman--Pintz parallelogram type estimate for general trilinear sums in intervals of length $X^{3/5+\varepsilon}$.

</details>


### [106] [Prime points on smooth hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09735)
*Yijie Diao,Shuntaro Yamagishi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 对于次数 $d \geq 4$ 且定义 $\mathbb{P}^{n-1}_{\mathbb{C}}$ 中光滑超曲面的齐次型 $F$，当 $n \geq 24 d^4 2^d$ 且 $F$ 满足适当局部条件时，证明了方程 $F(x_1,\ldots,x_n)=0$ 素数解数目的渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高次齐次方程上的素数解分布问题，将圆法（尤其是涉及素变量与光滑超曲面约束的计数）推广到高维情形。核心困难在于维数不足时余项难以控制；本文通过提高变量个数 $n$ 相对于次数 $d$ 的下界，使得主项可被分离并给出渐近公式。

Method: 采用解析数论的圆法（Hardy–Littlewood 圆法），结合素数变量的指数和与齐次型在 $\mathbb{P}^{n-1}$ 上光滑超曲面的几何性质。主弧贡献由奇异级数（局部密度）与奇异积分给出；优弧/余弧部分借助 Weyl 求和、van der Corput 型估计或 Birch 奇异簇维数方法控制，条件 $n \geq 24 d^4 2^d$ 保证余项低于主项。

Result: 在 $n \geq 24 d^4 2^d$、$d \geq 4$ 且 $F$ 定义 $\mathbb{P}^{n-1}_{\mathbb{C}}$ 中光滑超曲面并满足适当局部条件时，得到素数解数目的渐近公式，形式为主项（奇异级数 × 奇异积分 × 主区间测度）加上可控余项。

Conclusion: 证实了高维光滑超曲面上素数解满足预期的渐近分布，推广了以往关于素数解及齐次方程渐近公式的结果。所用维数下界 $n \geq 24 d^4 2^d$ 虽较弱，但为高次情形提供了可行框架；后续工作可望降低该下界并放宽光滑性/局部条件。

Abstract: Let $F \in \mathbb{Z}[x_1, \ldots, x_n]$ be a homogeneous form of degree $d \geq 4$ which defines a smooth hypersurface in $\mathbb{P}^{n-1}_{\mathbb{C}}$. For $n \geq 24 d^4 2^d$, we prove an asymptotic formula for the number of prime solutions to the equation $F(x_1, \ldots, x_n) = 0$, provided $F$ satisfies suitable local conditions.

</details>


### [107] [Linear independence of values of polylogarithms with periodic coefficients, outside the disk of convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09774)
*Ludovic Mistiaen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文对周期函数生成的广义 Lerch/多对数级数在代数点处的线性无关性给出下界，将 Fischler 2026 年的结果推广到任意非零周期函数及 |z_0|>1 的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fischler 2026 年针对 f≡1 且 |z_0|≤1 的情形证明了多重对数取值张成空间的维数下界，本文旨在将该结果推广到任意非零周期函数 f:Z→C 以及 |z_0|>1（需通过解析延拓定义级数）的更一般情形。

Method: 采用 Siegel 引理的精细版本构造 L(f,i,z_0) 的线性组合，并应用推广的线性无关判别准则；为验证该准则的假设，利用多对数函数的积分表示和一条"Shidlovskii 引理"。

Result: 证明了由 L(f,i,z_0) 张成的 Q(z_0)-向量空间维数具有 sqrt{s/log(s)} 量级的下界，其中 f 为周期 N 的非零周期函数，z_0 为不落在半直线上的非零代数数；除复平面上有限条割线外，结论在 |z_0|>1 时仍成立。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了 Fischler 关于多重对数线性无关性的结果，建立了更一般的周期函数生成的 L-级数在代数点处线性无关的定量下界，并借助 Siegel 引理、线性无关判别准则、积分表示及 Shidlovskii 引理等工具完成证明。

Abstract: For any non-zero periodic function f\,: Z $\rightarrow$ C of period N ___ 1 and any non-zero algebraic number z\_0 that doesn't lie on a half-line [e^2i${\ell}$$π$/N , e^2i${\ell}$$π$/N $\infty$[, 0 ___ ${\ell}$ ___ N -1, we give a lower bound of order sqrt{s/log(s)} on the dimension of the Q(z\_0 )-vector space spanned by the numbers L(f, i, z\_0 )\,: sum\_{m=1}^$\infty$ f(m)z\_0^m/m^i. This generalizes a result Fischler proved in 2026, corresponding to f identically equal to 1 and |z\_0| ___ 1: in this case, the numbers L(f, i, z\_0 ) = Li\_i (z\_0 ) are polylogarithm values. Except for a finite number of cuts in the complex plane, our result still holds when |z\_0 | > 1, that is in a domain where the series definition above for the numbers L(f, i, z\_0 ) doesn't converge anymore, and we make sense of these numbers through analytic continuation. To obtain this result, we construct linear combinations of the numbers L(f, i, z\_0 ) using a refined version of Siegel's lemma, and we apply to them a linear independence criterion generalizing the one used by Fischler. To check the assumptions of this criterion, we rely on an integral representation of the polylogarithm functions and on a ''Shidlovskii lemma''.

</details>


### [108] [Maximal volume of convex bodies avoiding random points and lacunary dilates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09790)
*Yuval Peres,Bohan Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多项式傅里叶衰减的概率测度下，避开某点前N个膨胀倍数的凸体的渐近最大体积，并给出了一维反例说明亚缺项尺度上稀疏性不能替代缺项性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于凸体中避开膨胀序列点的最大体积问题，通常需要较强的序列条件。本文希望将结果推广到更一般的缺项序列及具有多项式傅里叶衰减的任意概率测度，并厘清缺项性（lacunarity）与稀疏性（sparsity）之间的本质区别。

Method: 对给定正实缺项序列与具有多项式傅里叶衰减的概率测度μ，对μ-几乎处处的x，分析避开x模Z^d的前N个膨胀倍数的凸体的渐近最大体积；分别考虑固定凸体的位似体、单位立方体内所有凸体，以及固定凸体内独立均匀点的空凸体情形；同时构造一维反例以探讨亚缺项尺度。

Result: 在固定凸体的位似体类中，最大体积渐近于(log N)/N；当允许单位立方体内所有凸体时，渐近为d(log N)/N；对Ω⊂R^d内独立均匀点，空凸体最大体积几乎必然渐近于d·vol(Ω)(log N)/N。此外，一维反例表明在亚缺项尺度上缺项性不能被稀疏性替代。

Conclusion: 结果在更一般的缺项序列与多项式傅里叶衰减测度下确定了凸体最大体积的精确渐近，并揭示了缺项性在亚缺项尺度上的关键作用。

Abstract: For a positive real lacunary sequence and any probability measure $μ$ with polynomial Fourier decay, we determine, for $μ$-almost every $x$, the asymptotic maximal volume of convex bodies avoiding the first $N$ dilates of $x$ modulo $\mathbb Z^d$. Among homothets of a fixed convex body, the maximal volume is asymptotic to $(\log N)/N$. When all convex bodies in the unit cube are allowed, it is asymptotic to $d(\log N)/N$. For independent uniform points in a fixed convex body $Ω\subset\mathbb R^d$, the maximal volume of an empty convex body is asymptotic to $d\operatorname{vol}(Ω)(\log N)/N$ almost surely. We also show, by constructing a one-dimensional counterexample, that lacunarity cannot be replaced by sparsity on sublacunary scales.

</details>


### [109] [Translation-invariant equations in $\mathbb{F}_p^n$ and groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09887)
*Katalin Gyarmati*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限群中不含线性方程 aX+bY=cZ+dU（a+b=c+d）非平凡解的子集最大规模问题，结合 slice rank 方法与构造性手段，给出了上界与突破 0.5 密度壁垒的下界构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性方程 aX+bY=cZ+dU（系数满足 a+b=c+d）是经典 Sidon 集的自然推广，理解此类"无解集"在有限群中的最大密度，对加性组合学与极值集合论具有重要意义，尤其是刻画不同群结构下的密度阈值。

Method: 一方面运用 Tao 的 slice rank 方法，对向量空间 F_p^n 中无解集建立严格上界；另一方面在循环群 Z_m 上针对非对称方程 X+5Y=3U+3Z 进行显式构造，并推广至系数按模 8 简单规律变化的一类方程。

Result: 在 F_p^n 中得到了无解集规模的严格上界；在 Z_m 中构造出关于 X+5Y=3U+3Z 无解的集合，其对数密度约为 0.5283，突破了以往普遍预期的 0.5 壁垒（对充分大的模数 m 成立）；该高密度现象可推广至模 8 下系数遵循简单规则的更广泛方程族。

Conclusion: 结果表明此类线性方程的无解集可达到超过 0.5 的渐近对数密度，揭示了系数结构（尤其模 8 规律）对密度阈值的关键影响，并拓展了 Sidon 型问题的研究框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore how large a subset of a finite group can be without containing non-trivial solutions to linear equations of the form $aX+bY=cZ+dU$ (where $a+b=c+d$). These equations naturally generalize the classic concept of Sidon sets.
