Using Tao's slice rank method, we first establish strict upper bounds for the size of such solution-free sets in the vector space $\mathbb{F}_{p}^{n}$. Next, we turn to cyclic groups $\mathbb{Z}_{m}$ and construct surprisingly large sets that have no solutions for the asymmetric equation $X+5Y=3U+3Z$. These constructions achieve a logarithmic density of roughly $0.5283$, breaking the expected $0.5$ barrier for any sufficiently large modulus $m$. Finally, we show that this high-density behavior extends to a wider family of equations whose coefficients follow simple rules modulo $8$.

</details>


### [110] [Terminal Blocks of Primes in Pisot Numeration Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10026)
*Sungkon Chang,Johann Verwee*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在Pisot整数记数系统中，固定终端块词的素数定理：出现频率为 φ^{-m}，并在Zeckendorf情形解决了一个猜想，同时证明了Tribonacci素数定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在Pisot整数记数系统及替换系统中，固定终端块词（及固定词因子）在素数位置上的分布规律，验证并推广此前Zeckendorf情形与Tribonacci情形的相关猜想。

Method: 将终端条件转化为Rauzy柱窗，再转化为紧环面上的线性轨道；对伴随替换利用Barge纯离散性定理（替换词反转后）自动获得重数为1的Rauzy几何；借助Rauzy环面作为模型。

Result: 证明总数字长度为m的终端块词在素数中出现频率为 φ^{-m}；证明Tribonacci素数定理；还得到多项式抽样律、与固定同余类的渐近独立性、固定移位相关公式及Möbius正交性；结合已有素数定理给出Chebotarev的终端细化，并证明每个固定终端素数类包含任意长、公差多项式对数有界的算术级数。

Conclusion: Rauzy环面方法为Pisot记数系统与替换固定词的素数分布提供统一框架，给出频率、独立性、相关性与正交性结果，并推出终端Chebotarev细化与算术级数结论。

Abstract: We prove a prime number theorem for fixed terminal block words in Pisot integer numeration systems. If the dominant root $\varphi$ is a Pisot number and the characteristic polynomial $P_h$ is its minimal polynomial, every terminal block word of total digit-length $m$ occurs among the primes with asymptotic frequency $\varphi^{-m}$. In the Zeckendorf case this resolves a recent conjecture. The proof converts terminal conditions into Rauzy cylinder windows and then into a linear orbit on a compact torus. For these companion substitutions, the required multiplicity-one Rauzy geometry is automatic: Barge's pure-discreteness theorem applies after reversal of the substitution words.
  The Rauzy torus also gives a prime number theorem for the substitution fixed word: every finite factor occurs at prime starting positions with its ordinary factor frequency. This proves the Tribonacci prime-number theorem suggested by Drmota--Müllner--Spiegelhofer. The toral model further yields polynomial sampling laws, asymptotic independence from fixed congruence classes, fixed-shift correlation formulas, and Möbius orthogonality. Combined with established prime theorems, it gives a terminal refinement of Chebotarev and shows that every fixed terminal prime class contains arbitrarily long arithmetic progressions with polylogarithmically bounded common difference.

</details>


### [111] [Consecutive Rankin-Cohen Bases, Full-Spark Periods, and Divisor-Tau Congruences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10052)
*Kelvin Lam*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Mellin周期泛函行列式的严格正性，得到周期在基本半区间上的满秩定理及Xue的混合奇偶周期独立性猜想；据此给出连续首Rankin–Cohen括号构成S_K的一组基，确定所有容许有序行列式的符号，并导出除数–tau卷积C_r(n)的一致精确全权公式；模素数约化得到素数次约化与稀疏Ramanujan型特殊化刻画；最后证明[E_4,E_6]生成元的括号恒等式，将Cramer系统化为一元坐标问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Mellin周期泛函的行列式非退化性，以解决周期独立性（尤其混合奇偶周期独立性猜想），并为高阶权重模形式的显式基、Eisenstein括号行列式符号及除数–tau卷积公式提供统一框架。

Method: 设K=2r+14，d=dim S_K；证明Mellin周期泛函行列式严格正，建立基本半区间上的满秩定理；利用坐标恒等式与首Rankin–Cohen括号/首Eisenstein括号；对同一坐标恒等式做模素数约化；对Q[E_4,E_6]齐次元证明括号与Jacobian行列式的恒等式，将Cramer系统归约为T=E_6^2/E_4^3的一元坐标问题。

Result: 证明周期满秩并确认Xue的混合奇偶周期独立性猜想；连续首Rankin–Cohen括号[E_{2r+10-2j},E_{2j+2}]_1（1≤j≤d）构成S_K的基；确定所有容许有序行列式的符号；给出C_r(n)=∑_{m=1}^{n-1}σ_{2r+1}(m)τ(n-m)的一致精确全权公式；对每个权重下无穷多素数得到规范素数次约化，并通过Cramer坐标消失刻画稀疏Ramanujan型特殊化；证明[f,g]_1/Δ=-3456·det((f_{E_4},f_{E_6}),(g_{E_4},g_{E_6}))。

Conclusion: Mellin周期泛函的严格正性带来满秩与周期独立性，进而给出S_K的括号显式基与行列式符号；推得除数–tau卷积的全权闭式；模约化连接素数次结构与Ramanujan型稀疏现象；Jacobian恒等式将Eisenstein括号的Cramer系统化为一元T坐标问题。

Abstract: For $r\ge1$, let $K=2r+14$ and $d=\dim S_K$. We prove strict positivity for determinants of Mellin period functionals, yielding a full-spark theorem for periods in a fundamental half-range and, in particular, the mixed odd--even period independence conjecture of Xue. As a consequence, the consecutive first Rankin--Cohen brackets $[E_{2r+10-2j},E_{2j+2}]_1$, $1\le j\le d$, form a basis of $S_K$, and we determine the signs of all admissible ordered determinants of first Eisenstein brackets. This gives a uniform exact all-weight formula for the divisor--tau convolution $C_r(n)=\sum_{m=1}^{n-1}σ_{2r+1}(m)τ(n-m)$. Reducing the same coordinate identity modulo primes, we obtain canonical prime-wise reductions for infinitely many primes in every weight and characterize sparse Ramanujan-type specializations through vanishing Cramer coordinates. Finally, for homogeneous $f,g\in\mathbf Q[E_4,E_6]$, we prove $[f,g]_1/Δ=-3456\det((f_{E_4},f_{E_6}),(g_{E_4},g_{E_6}))$, reducing the Cramer system to a one-variable coordinate problem in $T=E_6^2/E_4^3$.

</details>


### [112] [Generating sets for maximal orders in rational quaternion algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10139)
*Kirsten Eisenträger,Eyal Z. Goren,Annamaria Iezzi,Harun Kir,Eda Kırımlı,Jonathan R. Love,William E. Mahaney,Jennifer Park,Maria Sabitova*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究有理定四元数代数中极大序 $\mathfrak{O}$ 的生成问题：证明 $\mathfrak{O}$ 可由范数为 $\ell^k$（$k$ 超过显式界）的元素生成，且存在至多三个范数为 $\ell$ 的幂的元素构成的生成集，并讨论了其对超奇异同源图的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究四元数序能否由特定范数（$\ell$ 的幂）的元素作为 $\mathbb{Z}$-代数生成，这一问题与超奇异同源图的结构密切相关，需要为生成集的存在性给出定量刻画。

Method: 对不一定极大的四元数序 $\mathcal{O}$，证明 Brandt 矩阵代数的结构性质，并给出 $\mathcal{O}$ 的 theta 函数系数的显式上界；据此推导关于极大序 $\mathfrak{O}$ 生成集存在性的两个定理，并结合计算实验验证。

Result: 证明了两条定理：一是对任意大于显式界的 $k$，$\mathfrak{O}$ 可由范数为 $\ell^k$ 的元素生成；二是存在至多三个元素组成的生成集，其元素范数均为 $\ell$ 的幂。同时获得了 Brandt 矩阵代数的结构结果与 theta 函数系数的显式界。

Conclusion: 极大四元数序可由少量范数为 $\ell$ 的幂的元素生成，所得显式界与结构结果对超奇异同源图的研究具有意义，并得到计算实验支持。

Abstract: Given a maximal order $\mathfrak{O}$ in a rational definite quaternion algebra, and a prime $\ell$ coprime to the discriminant of $\mathfrak{O}$, this paper considers subsets of $\mathfrak{O}$ consisting of elements with $\ell$-power norms that together generate $\mathfrak{O}$ as a $\mathbb{Z}$-algebra. We prove two theorems about the existence of such sets: the first states that $\mathfrak{O}$ is generated by elements of norm $\ell^k$ for any $k$ larger than an explicit bound, and the second states that there is a generating set for $\mathfrak{O}$ consisting of at most three elements, each with norm a power of $\ell$. We discuss implications for the study of supersingular isogeny graphs. As steps towards these theorems, for quaternion orders $\mathcal{O}$ that are not necessarily maximal, we also prove structural results about the algebra of Brandt matrices for $\mathcal{O}$ and explicit bounds on the coefficients of the theta function of $\mathcal{O}$. Computational experiments are also discussed.

</details>


### [113] [Improved average and almost-all bounds for $G(n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10177)
*Chiara Bellotti*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究使小于等于G(n)且与n互素的整数生成(Z/nZ)^×的最小正整数G(n)，证明了其和函数的新上界并给出几乎处处的最优对数阶估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画模n的单位群需要多少个（最小的那些）互素整数作为生成元，是数论中关于乘法群生成元问题的自然量化形式；此前已知的和函数上界为 x(log x)^{97}，过于粗糙，且几乎处处的最优阶仅在GRH下可得。

Method: 通过与单位群结构（循环分解）相关的生成元判定，将G(n)的控制归约为对相关算术函数（如与模n的乘法阶、素因子分布有关的量）的估计，并结合解析数论中的筛法、大筛或均值估计等技术来处理求和与几乎处处估计。

Result: 对任意 ε>0，有 ∑_{n≤x} G(n) ≪_ε x(log x)^{8/3+ε}，将先前 ≪ x(log x)^{97} 的界显著改进；并且无条件地证明了 G(n) ≤ (log n)^2 对几乎所有 n 成立，即在几乎处处意义下达到与GRH下经典逐点界相同的对数指数2。

Conclusion: 研究表明G(n)的平均阶远小于此前估计，且其典型的逐点规模甚至在无条件下即可达到GRH所预测的对数指数2，说明GRH下的经典界在几乎处处意义上是真实且最优的。

Abstract: In this paper we study the least positive integer $G(n)$ such that the integers $a\leq G(n)$ with $(a,n)=1$ generate $(\mathbb Z/n\mathbb Z)^\times$. We prove that, for every $\varepsilon>0$, \[
  \sum_{n\leq x}G(n)\ll_\varepsilon x(\log x)^{8/3+\varepsilon}, \] improving the previously known bound $\ll x(\log x)^{97}$. We also prove that $G(n)\leq(\log n)^2$ for almost all $n$, unconditionally. Thus, for almost all $n$, we obtain the same logarithmic exponent $2$ as in the classical pointwise bound under GRH.

</details>


### [114] [Irrationality exponents of logarithms of positive rational numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10192)
*Jingwen Liu,Kai Jiang,Pingwen Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出直接分离权行列式论证，证明对任意正有理数 r≠1 有 μ(log r)=2；先证动中心插值定理，再对 r=a/b 给出全部参数、分母估计、行平移与解析行列式界，并附几何推广与显式有限次界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该稿旨在为 μ(log r)=2（r 为正有理数且 r≠1）提供一个直接、可核查的证明框架，避免依赖间接论证，并使参数与估计显式化，便于审计与复用。

Method: 方法核心为分离权行列式论证。首先证明动中心插值定理以支撑该论证；随后固定任意 r=a/b，系统给出该论证所需的参数选择、分母估计、行平移以及解析行列式上界；有理线性组合与有理仿射变换作为推论处理。补充的几何推广和显式有限次界置于附录。

Result: 得到 μ(log r)=2 对每个正有理数 r≠1 成立；并给出针对 r=a/b 的完整参数与估计配置，包括分母估计、行平移方案与解析行列式界，同时得到线性组合与仿射变换情形。

Conclusion: 该文建立了 μ(log r)=2 的直接行列式证明路线并显式化关键技术参数；但稿件仍为审核中的研究草稿，整理不构成对数学结论的独立认证。

Abstract: We present a direct separated-weight determinant argument for μ(log r) = 2 for every positive rational number r = 1. We first prove the moving-centre interpolation theorem used in the argument. We then fix an arbitrary r = a/b and give all parameter choices, denominator estimates, row translations, and analytic determinant bounds for that same argument. Rational linear combinations and rational affine changes follow as corollaries.Supplementary geometric extensions and an explicit finite-degree bound are collected in the appendices. This remains a research draft under audit; the reorganization does not constitute independent certification of its mathematical conclusions.

</details>


### [115] [Intersecting a curve in an abelian variety with multiples of another curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10212)
*Fabrizio Barroero,Gabriel A. Dill,Lars Kuehne*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Levin关于$\mathbb{G}_m^n$中两条给定曲线上点之间的整数倍数关系的轨迹问题，在阿贝尔簇情形下给出肯定答案，证明了Zilber-Pink猜想的预测，其关键是加强了Vojta和Rémond的高度不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Levin提出：对于$\mathbb{G}_m^n$（$n \geq 3$）中两条给定曲线，一条曲线上的点若其非零整数倍落在另一条曲线上，这些点的轨迹如何刻画。作者试图在阿贝尔簇的类比情形下回答该问题，并验证Zilber-Pink猜想在此情形下的预测。

Method: 作者的核心技术手段是加强对Vojta与Rémond所建立的高度不等式的估计，并以此为主要工具，结合Zilber-Pink猜想的相关框架来证明阿贝尔情形下的结论。

Result: 作者给出了Levin问题在阿贝尔簇类比情形下的明确答案，证明了Zilber-Pink猜想在该情形下所预测的结果成立。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅回答了Levin的轨迹问题在阿贝尔类比下的情形，还通过强化高度不等式为相关Zilber-Pink型问题提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Levin asked what can be said about the locus of points lying on a given curve in $\mathbb{G}_m^n$ that have a non-zero integer multiple on another given curve in $\mathbb{G}_m^n$ for $n \geq 3$. We give a definite answer to the abelian analogue of Levin's question, proving what is predicted by the Zilber--Pink conjecture in this case. An important ingredient in our proof is a strengthening of a height inequality by Vojta and Rémond.

</details>


### [116] [Local Factors in the BSD Conjecture: A Unified Statistical View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10243)
*David Kurniadi Angdinata,Kenny Lau,Ken Ono,Ashvin Swaminathan,Sameera Vemulapalli*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为短Weierstrass形式的椭圆曲线$E/\mathbb{Q}$构造多变量Euler乘积生成函数，用以编码Tamagawa乘积，并据此计算其四个统计量的极限分布、协方差、矩与尾部界，揭示约半数以上曲线Tamagawa乘积为平凡等分布规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tamagawa数$Tam(E)=\prod_p c_p(E)$是椭圆曲线算术的重要不变量，但此前缺乏对其整体分布规律的系统刻画。作者希望借助生成函数方法，理解在高度排序下Tamagawa乘积的统计行为。

Method: 基于Tate算法导出的以局部约化数据为指标的Euler乘积，对其进行特化得到多变量Euler乘积生成函数，进而用于计算极限分布、精确协方差以及矩和尾部界。结果经AxiomProver在Lean中自动形式化。

Result: 在这一高度排序下，超过一半的曲线Tamagawa乘积为平凡（等于1）；约42.2%恰有一个素数的局部Tamagawa数非平凡；仅约6.8%恰有两个这样的素数。此外给出了四个统计量的极限分布、精确协方差与矩、尾部界。

Conclusion: 多变量Euler乘积生成函数是研究Tamagawa数统计分布的有效工具，所得密度结果精确揭示了曲线Tamagawa乘积的稀疏结构。相关结果已在Lean中完成自动形式化。

Abstract: For elliptic curves $E/\mathbb{Q}$ in short Weierstrass form \[ E=E(a_4,a_6): y^2=x^3+a_4x+a_6, \] we derive a multivariable Euler product generating function which encodes the Tamagawa product $Tam(E)=\prod_p c_p(E)$. Using this generating function, we compute limiting distributions, exact covariances, and moment and tail bounds for four important statistics on the Tamagawa number; for example we show that more than half of all curves in this height ordering have trivial Tamagawa product, about $42.2\%$ have exactly one prime with nontrivial local Tamagawa number, and only about $6.8\%$ have exactly two such primes. The product is obtained by specializing an Euler product indexed by local reduction data that we derive from Tate's algorithm. The results in this paper were autoformalized in Lean by AxiomProver.

</details>


### [117] [Cocycle Relations and Elliptic Gamma Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10365)
*Teymour Gray*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用平滑椭圆 Gamma 函数满足的余循环关系，证明了 Bergeron-Charollois-García 关于复三次域椭圆单位的解析构造确实独立于挠点 h 的选取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Bergeron-Charollois-García 的工作中，复三次域的椭圆单位被猜想可由平滑椭圆 Gamma 函数之商解析地表达，其中取值依赖于导子理想、射线类群元素、平滑理想以及一个"挠点"h。若该解析函数要纳入显式类域论框架，仿照 CM 椭圆曲线挠点的情形，h 的选取应仅依赖其模某个格 L 的同余类。此猜想已有大量数值证据支持，但缺乏理论证明。

Method: 本文运用平滑椭圆 Gamma 函数所满足的余循环关系（cocycle relations），对依赖 h 的构造进行分析，证明该构造实际上不依赖于 h 的具体选取，而只依赖于其同余类。

Result: 证明了该椭圆单位构造对挠点 h 的选取是独立的，即 h 的自由度仅体现在其模格 L 的同余类上，从而为该解析构造与显式类域论框架的兼容性提供了严格的证明。

Conclusion: 该结果为 Bergeron-Charollois-García 猜想提供了理论支撑，将原先仅基于数值证据的观察提升为定理，推进了复三次域中椭圆单位的显式类域论研究。

Abstract: In the recent work of Bergeron-Charollois-García \cite{BCG23}, a conjectural analytic expression for elliptic units for complex cubic fields was proposed: namely, as the value of a smoothed quotient of elliptic Gamma functions. The complex number depended on the conductor ideal, ray class group element, smoothing ideal, and a `torsion point' $h$. If this analytic function is to fit into the framework of explicit class field theory, then, as in the case of torsion points on CM elliptic curves, our choice of $h$ should only depend on its congruence class modulo some lattice $L$. This conjecture has been supported by much numerical evidence. In this paper, we use the cocycle relations satisfied by the smoothed elliptic Gamma function to prove that the construction is indeed independent of $h$.

</details>


### [118] [Mixed moments of arithmetic functions and Kloosterman sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10372)
*Yujiao Jiang,Yuk-Kam Lau*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将短和Nair–Tenenbaum估计推广到含Kloosterman和的混合矩，得到∑_{n≤x}z^{ω(n)}|Kl(a;n)|^ν的精确阶，并给出Hecke特征值与Kloosterman和混合矩上界、大值频率、第二矩、谱计数方差及Weyl律余项积分下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Nair–Tenenbaum结果刻画非负算术函数多项式值短和，但未涉及与Kloosterman和等振荡对象的混合矩；同时Kloosterman和的矩、大值频率及谱侧方差在数论中有重要应用，需要精确阶估计。

Method: 推广Nair–Tenenbaum关于非负算术函数多项式值短和的估计到含Kloosterman和的混合矩；结合精化Fouvry–Michel方法所得下界。进一步用于Hecke特征值与Kloosterman和混合绝对矩、大Kloosterman值的对数尺度频率、对角第二矩，以及模曲面光滑谱计数方差与Weyl律余项积分。

Result: 证明对固定非零整数a及固定z,ν>0，有∑_{n≤x}z^{ω(n)}|Kl(a;n)|^ν≍_{a,z,ν}x(log x)^{z𝔰(ν)-1}，其中𝔰(ν)为Sato–Tate测度的ν阶绝对矩；并得到Hecke特征值与Kloosterman和混合绝对矩上界，确定大Kloosterman值在对数尺度上的频率，建立Li–Sarnak预测的经典Kloosterman和对角第二矩阶；在模曲面上给出对数范围内光滑谱计数方差的匹配界，以及∫_T^{2T}S(t)^2 dt≫T^2/log T。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并显著推进了Nair–Tenenbaum短和方法与Kloosterman和矩理论，提供了主项阶、上界、大值频率及谱侧方差和Weyl律余项积分下界等多方面精确结果。

Abstract: We extend the Nair--Tenenbaum estimates for short sums of nonnegative arithmetic functions at polynomial values to mixed moments involving Kloosterman sums. Combining these estimates with lower bounds obtained by refining the method of Fouvry and Michel, we prove that, for every fixed nonzero integer \(a\) and fixed \(z,ν>0\), \[ \sum_{n\leq x}z^{ω(n)} |\operatorname{Kl}(a;n)|^ν\asymp_{a,z,ν} x(\log x)^{z\mathfrak{s}(ν)-1}, \] where \(ω(n)\) counts distinct prime divisors and \(\mathfrak{s}(ν)\) is the \(ν\)-th absolute moment of the Sato--Tate measure. We also obtain upper bounds for mixed absolute moments of Hecke eigenvalues and Kloosterman sums, determine the frequency of large Kloosterman values on a logarithmic scale, and establish the order of magnitude predicted by Li and Sarnak for the diagonal second moment of classical Kloosterman sums. We obtain matching bounds for the smoothed spectral counting variance on the modular surface in a logarithmic range and the lower bound \(\int_T^{2T}S(t)^2\,dt\gg T^2/\log T\), where \(S(t)\) is the remainder in Weyl's law.

</details>


### [119] [$p$-class groups in the cyclotomic $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extension of imaginary quadratic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10452)
*Somnath Jha,Debanjana Kundu,H. Laxmi,Lawrence C. Washington*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究虚二次域分圆Z_p扩张第一层的p-类群结构，得到循环因子增长限制，并给出λ-不变量的确定与概率分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画虚二次域分圆Z_p扩张中p-类群从基础层到第一层的结构变化，并探讨Iwasawa λ-不变量的确定性与统计分布。

Method: 在p非分裂情形下分析第一层p-类群结构，推导循环因子增长限制；结合Cohen-Lenstra-Martinet思想建立概率模型。

Result: 得到循环因子增长限制，p-秩不变时每个循环因子恰好增长一个p的幂；大量情形下λ-不变量由第一层p-秩完全决定；给出基础层循环p-类群时第一层λ-不变量的概率分布。

Conclusion: 第一层p-类群结构对λ-不变量具有强约束和决定性，且其分布可用Cohen-Lenstra-Martinet框架刻画。

Abstract: We study the structure of the $p$-class group of the first layer of the cyclotomic $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extension of an imaginary quadratic field where $p$ is non-split. We obtain restrictions on the growth of the cyclic factors from the base layer to the first layer; in particular, when the $p$-rank remains unchanged, each cyclic factor grows by exactly one power of $p$. We show that in a large number of cases the Iwasawa $λ$-invariant is completely determined by the $p$-rank of the first layer itself. Finally we use a Cohen-Lenstra-Martinet type philosophy to study the probability distribution of Iwasawa lambda invariants of the first layer when the base layer has cyclic $p$-class group.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Scalable persistence pairing graphs on scalar fields with an application to porous media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09897)
*John Rick Manzanares*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展方法，从大规模立方体过滤中计算持久配对图（persistence pairing graph），并在多孔介质渗透率预测中检验其是否包含超越持久区间的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有持久同调方法通常只使用持久区间（barcode）作为摘要，尚不清楚持久类之间的图组织是否携带额外信息，且大规模立方体过滤的配对图计算在计算上极具挑战。

Method: 在全局约简前先消去等值对，以流式方式构建压缩复形，并通过投影关系与三角事件求解从一次持久约简中恢复图转移；将方法应用于DRP-372多孔介质集合的119个符号距离体，用于渗透率预测，并设置材料族分组验证、留一源项目验证及两类零模型、空间分析等对照实验。

Result: 每个256^3体在收缩前产生超过1.35亿个立方体单元，而压缩复形中位数仅含37,471个生成元；按材料族分组时，加入持久配对图描述符在Ridge回归与偏最小二乘回归下均优于仅用持久摘要；更严格的留一源项目实验中，Ridge相对仅用持久性仍有小幅提升，但超越几何与持久性的额外收益在不同项目间并不一致。两种零模型显示持久性与图的耦合及边目标组织显著非随机，但均不能单独解释预测提升。

Conclusion: 持久配对图提供了超越barcode的结构信息摘要，但效果存在领域依赖与代表性敏感性，说明需要更具不变性的关系型描述符。

Abstract: We present a scalable method for computing persistence pairing graphs from large cubical filtrations and use it to test whether graph organization among persistent classes contains information beyond persistence intervals alone. The method cancels equal-value pairs before global reduction, constructs the compressed complex in streaming form, and recovers graph transitions from one persistence reduction through projected relations and triangular event solves. We apply the method to 119 signed distance volumes from the DRP-372 porous media collection for permeability prediction. Each 256-cubed volume induces more than 135 million cubical cells before contraction, whereas the median compressed complex contains 37,471 generators. In validation grouped by material family, adding persistence pairing graph descriptors improves prediction over persistence summaries under both Ridge regression and partial least squares regression. In the stricter experiment that holds out one source project at a time, Ridge retains a modest improvement over persistence alone, although additional benefit beyond geometry and persistence is not consistent across projects. Two null models show that the observed persistence and graph coupling and edge target organization are strongly nonrandom, but neither uniquely explains the predictive improvement; the useful signal appears to reside mainly in coarser graph and event organization. Spatial analyses likewise distinguish persistence magnitude from graph prominence and reveal nonlocal organization among algebraically related persistence events. These results show that persistence pairing graphs provide structurally informative summaries beyond the barcode while exposing domain dependence and representative sensitivity that motivate more invariant relational descriptors.

</details>


### [121] [Cellular approximations for nonsymmetric homotopy operads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09945)
*Eduardo Hoefel,Grigory Solomadin*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文对任意每个元数上为CW空间的有色非对称拓扑操作元，构造其与CW同伦操作元的同伦同构（不改变对象），并通过双边bar构造将其严格化为严格CW操作元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在拓扑操作元代数中，通常只有"同伦意义上"成立的结构（高阶同伦），缺乏严格或CW意义上的操作元模型；本文旨在消除这种同伦与严格结构之间的差距，并同时将结论推广到集合上操作元的拓扑代数及有限偏序集上的有限CW复形图表。

Method: 对每个元数为CW空间的有色非对称拓扑操作元，逐元数构造CW同伦操作元（要求所有高阶同伦关于乘积结构与立方体上的CW结构是胞腔的），保持对象不变；随后利用双边bar构造（two-sided bar construction）将该同伦同构严格化为与严格CW操作元的同伦同构；该方法可推广到集合上（有色）操作元的拓扑代数；在有限偏序集上的有限CW复形图表情形，得到的图表由有限CW空间组成。

Result: 1）任意此类拓扑操作元与一个CW同伦操作元同伦同构且对象不变；2）通过双边bar构造可严格化为严格CW操作元；3）结论推广到集合上操作元的拓扑代数；4）在有限偏序集上的有限CW复形图表情景中，所得图表由有限CW空间构成，并加强了单纯集与拓扑空间之间的Quillen伴随；5）作为副产品，给出了操作元上的拓扑同伦代数到P-树范畴的分支消解的预层范畴中的嵌入，推广了拓扑操作元的已知情形。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了从同伦型拓扑操作元到CW/严格CW操作元的严格化（rectification）框架，统一并推广了若干已知结果，并改进了单纯集与拓扑空间间的Quillen伴随，同时给出了拓扑同伦代数嵌入预层范畴的一般性结论。

Abstract: In this note, for every topological colored nonsymmetric operad with CW spaces in each arity we construct a homotopy isomorphism with a CW homotopy operad (i.e. all higher homotopies are cellular with respect to the product structure and CW structure on cubes) not changing its objects. By using the two-sided bar construction, we rectify this to a homotopy isomorphism with a strict CW operad. This applies more generally to topological algebras over colored operads in sets. In the case of diagrams of finite CW complexes over finite posets, the resulting diagram consists of finite CW spaces enhancing the Quillen adjunction between simplicial sets and topological spaces. As a byproduct, we produce an embedding of topological homotopy algebras over operads into the presheaf category over the branch resolution for the category of $P$-trees generalizing the known case of topological operads.

</details>


### [122] [Exotic structures on hyperbolic manifolds via the EO-theory of Projective Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10070)
*Samik Basu,Akashdwip Biswas*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文利用复射影空间的$EO$-理论（实$K$-理论的一个版本，逐素数$p$定义）构造适当的异种球面，将复双曲流形光滑刚性失效的构造从$8n+2$维推广到更多维度，并给出关于不界定可平行化流形的异种球面上自由光滑$S^1$与$S^3$作用存在性的正面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Farrell-Jones证明维数$\geq 5$的负曲率流形在拓扑上刚性（同伦等价蕴含同胚），但光滑范畴即使对双曲流形也不成立：存在与给定双曲流形$M$同伦等价却不同胚的负曲率流形$N$。对复双曲流形，类似构造此前仅在$8n+2$维已知，作者希望将这一现象推广到更多维数。

Method: 主要方法：运用复射影空间的$EO$-理论构造合适的异种球面例子。$EO$-理论可视为实$K$-理论的某种版本，在每个素数$p$处定义；通过该理论的计算得到所需的异种球面实例。

Result: 在除$8n+2$之外的许多其他维数中证明了复双曲流形光滑刚性失效的类似构造；同时计算还给出了关于不界定可平行化流形的异种球面上存在自由光滑$S^1$和$S^3$作用的正面结果。

Conclusion: 复双曲流形中同伦等价不蕴含微分同胚的现象可推广到$8n+2$以外的众多维数；$EO$-理论是构造相关异种球面的有效工具，并附带导出异种球面上自由光滑$S^1$、$S^3$作用存在性的结论。

Abstract: Farrell and Jones showed that negatively curved manifolds in dimension $\geq 5$ are topologically rigid in the sense that homotopy equivalence implies homeomorphism. In the smooth category, the result does not hold even for hyperbolic manifolds, and there are negatively curved manifolds $N$ homotopy equivalent to a given hyperbolic manifold $M$ but not diffeomorphic. For complex hyperbolic manifolds, the analogous construction is demonstrated only in dimensions of the form $8n+2$. In this paper, we prove this result in many other dimensions. The main approach involves the use of $EO$-theory of complex projective spaces to construct suitable examples of exotic spheres. These theories may be viewed as a version of real $K$-theory, and are defined at each prime $p$. The computation also yields positive results about the existence of free smooth $S^1$ and $S^3$ actions on exotic spheres which do not bound parallelizable manifolds.

</details>


### [123] [Nullhomotopic equivariant shifts and Borsuk-Ulam-compatible compact abelian groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10111)
*Alexandru Chirvasitu,Alexander Dąbrowski*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文否定了Baum-Dąbrowski-Hajac提出的Borsuk-Ulam型猜想经典版本：对非平凡紧群自由作用不存在等变映射 X*G→X。结论是：在非平凡紧交换群中，该猜想恰在 G 为无挠投射有限群时失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Baum-Dąbrowski-Hajac 提出了一个 Borsuk-Ulam 风格的经典猜想，断言非空紧 Hausdorff 空间上非平凡紧群的自由作用不允许等变映射 X*G→X。该猜想是等变拓扑中 Borsuk-Ulam 现象的自然推广，验证或否定它有助于厘清紧群自由作用的等变映射存在性边界。

Method: 构造反例：针对非平凡紧交换群 G，分析其拓扑群结构（挠性、投射有限性），并构造相应的自由作用 X×G→X 及等变映射 X*G→X，以检验猜想在何种 G 上成立或失效。

Result: 猜想被否定。在非平凡紧交换群 G 中，猜想失效当且仅当 G 是无挠投射有限群（torsion-free profinite）；即对这类 G 存在反例，而对其他非平凡紧交换群猜想仍成立。

Conclusion: Baum-Dąbrowski-Hajac 猜想的经典版本不成立，其有效性由紧交换群的代数-拓扑性质完全刻画：失效当且仅当 G 为无挠投射有限群。这为等变 Borsuk-Ulam 型问题给出了精确的分界条件。

Abstract: We disprove the classical version of the Borsuk-Ulam-flavored conjecture, due to Baum-D{ą}browski-Hajac, that free actions $X\times \mathbb{G}\to X$ on non-empty compact Hausdorff spaces by non-trivial compact groups admit no equivariant maps $X*\mathbb{G}\to X$. Specifically, among non-trivial compact abelian $\mathbb{G}$, the conjecture fails precisely when $\mathbb{G}$ is torsion-free profinite.

</details>


### [124] [Flat principal 2-group bundles on $S^2$ and their holonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10402)
*Emily Cliff,Yves Lolelo,Sandrine St-Cyr*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文定义离散2-群的主丛，在S²上显式计算出相应的双范畴及其2-骨架，并给出其与和乐双范畴的具体等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主2-丛的刻画通常依赖和乐函子方式，但缺乏基于Čech数据的几何构造。作者希望用可微分几何的Čech方法定义离散2-群的主丛，并与已有的和乐双范畴框架建立显式的对应关系。

Method: 将离散2-群 G(G,A,α) 视为普通群 G 被阿贝尔群 A 的范畴扩张。利用好开覆盖上的Čech数据定义主 G-丛。针对流形 S² 显式计算所得双范畴并确定其2-骨架，再与和乐双范畴 Fun(*//π_{≤2}(S²), *//G) 比较，构造二者之间的具体等价。

Result: 在 S² 上显式算出了主 G-丛的双范畴及其2-骨架，并证明了该双范畴与和乐双范畴 Fun(*//π_{≤2}(S²), *//G) 之间存在具体等价。

Conclusion: Čech数据定义的主2-丛与基于和乐函子的描述在 S² 情形下是一致的，为离散2-群的丛理论提供了几何与和乐两种视角的统一。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{G}(G,A,α)$ be a discrete 2-group, given as the {categorical} extension of an ordinary group $G$ by an abelian group $A$. We define principal $\mathcal{G}$-bundles over a manifold in terms of Čech data over a good open cover. In the case of the manifold $S^2$, we calculate this bicategory explicitly, and identify its 2-skeleton. We compare with the holonomy bicategory $\mathrm{Fun}(*/\!\!/ π_{\le 2}(S^2), * /\!\!/ \mathcal{G})$, and provide a concrete equivalence between the two.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Log-concavity and unimodality of cluster monomials in fan coordinates of type $A_n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09312)
*Zhichao Chen,Zhe Sun*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 证明了A_n型簇单项式在扇初始簇的Laurent展开下都是对数凹且单峰的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在研究A_n型簇单项式的对数凹性与单峰性，部分回应Chen-Huang-Sun的对数凹猜想与Chen的单峰猜想，并补充Shen关于A_n型簇X-簇代数典范基元素乘积支撑的研究。

Method: 利用多边形模型，将系数对应于相容对角线产生的层状区间乘积，并给出系数公式；在偶数秩下将扇坐标中的对数凹性归结为沿整数直线的对数凹性；在奇数秩下利用卷积处理扇代换产生的系数和。

Result: 证明了A_n型任意簇单项式关于扇初始簇的Laurent展开是对数凹且单峰的，并得到了层状区间乘积的系数公式。

Conclusion: 该结果部分肯定地回答了Chen-Huang-Sun的对数凹猜想和Chen的单峰猜想，同时补充了Shen关于A_n型簇X-簇代数典范基元素乘积支撑的研究。

Abstract: We prove that every cluster monomial of type $A_n$ is log-concave and unimodal with respect to its Laurent expansion in a fan initial cluster. We derive a coefficient formula for laminar interval products arising from compatible diagonals in the polygon model. In even rank, we reduce log-concavity in fan coordinates to log-concavity along integer lines. In odd rank, we use convolution to handle the coefficient sums arising from the fan substitution. This gives partial affirmative answers to the log-concavity conjecture of Chen-Huang-Sun and the unimodality conjecture of Chen. Our result also complements Shen's study on the supports of products of canonical basis elements for cluster X-varieties of type $A_n$.

</details>


### [126] [Derived Picard reconstruction for graded surfaces with stops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09319)
*Wen Chang,Dongjian Wu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究带停点的分次曲面 $(S,M,\eta)$ 及其部分缠绕 Fukaya 范畴，证明映射类群与导出 Picard 群在不同不变量约定下对曲面的重构能力，并刻画纤维的基数与边界分支数的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究能否从部分缠绕 Fukaya 范畴的映射类群与导出 Picard 群重构带停点的（分次）曲面，并厘清不同不变量（移位、Serre 函子、有理/整系数）所决定的信息层次与刚性边界。

Method: 以带停点曲面 $(S,M,\eta)$ 的部分缠绕 Fukaya 范畴为对象，考察其映射类群、导出 Picard 群（分别区分移位、Serre 函子）以及有理 Serre 表示与整系数导出 Picard 表示，比较这些不变量对曲面结构的重构结果，并分析相应纤维的基数。

Result: 在亏格至少为 2 时，未分次映射类群决定带停点曲面 $(S,M)$；区分移位的导出 Picard 群决定底层分次曲面 $(S,\eta)$。但区分移位与 Serre 函子的导出 Picard 群纤维可数无限，且存在任意大的有限族两两不等价却具相同不变量与 Grothendieck 秩的范畴。对由无处消失向量场诱导的线场，有理 Serre 表示在任意亏格决定 $(S,M)$；对一般线场（亏格≥2），区分移位的整系数导出 Picard 表示的纤维基数至多为 4，当 $S$ 有 1 个或至少 5 个边界分支时完全决定 $(S,M,\eta)$，而在 2、3、4 个边界分支时出现非单点纤维。

Conclusion: 映射类群与导出 Picard 群对带停点分次曲面的重构能力取决于所保留的不变量结构；区分移位通常给出强刚性，而额外区分 Serre 函子反而导致可数无限纤维。边界分支数为 2、3、4 时刚性失效，是重构结果的关键例外情形。

Abstract: We study reconstruction of graded surfaces with stops $(S,M,η)$ from their mapping class groups and the derived Picard groups of their partially wrapped Fukaya categories. In genus at least two, we prove that the ungraded mapping class group determines the surface with stops $(S,M)$, while the derived Picard group with the shift distinguished determines the underlying graded surface $(S,η)$. Nevertheless, the derived Picard group with both the shift and the Serre functor distinguished has countably infinite fibres. We also construct arbitrarily large finite families of pairwise non-equivalent categories with the same invariant and Grothendieck rank.
  For line fields induced by nowhere-vanishing vector fields, we show that the rational Serre representation determines the surface with stops $(S,M)$ in every genus. For arbitrary line fields in genus at least two, the full integral derived Picard representation, with the shift distinguished, has fibres of cardinality at most four. It completely determines the graded surface with stops $(S,M,η)$ when $S$ has one or at least five boundary components, while non-singleton fibres occur for two, three, and four boundary components.

</details>


### [127] [On the Structure of Higher Zhu Induced Modules in Positive Characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09523)
*Haihua Huang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究整数分次顶点算子代数的高阶Zhu代数及其诱导模，确定了典范映射U→Ω_n(L_n(U))/Ω_{n-1}(L_n(U))的核，并在不可分解性与非分裂扩张方面给出构造性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Zhu代数理论刻画了顶点算子代数模范畴与结合代数模范畴之间的联系。本文将其推广到"高阶Zhu代数"，旨在厘清由高阶关系空间定义的诱导模L_n(U)的结构，特别是其与相邻低阶代数所诱导模之间的关系，从而建立弱V-模的扩张理论。

Method: 设F为特征不为2的代数闭域，V为非负整数分次顶点算子代数。对V商去包含向量D_V^{(h)}a - binom(-wt(a), h)a（a齐次，h≥1整数，D_V^{(h)}a=a_{-h-1}1）的关系空间，得到高阶Zhu代数。对n≥1及非零幺模U，考察诱导模的商L_n(U)，并通过零模展开（整数系数）与标准诱导模的分次结构确定典范映射U→Ω_n(L_n(U))/Ω_{n-1}(L_n(U))的核；同时验证该构造关于模同态的自然性，并分析不可分解性与非分裂短正合列。

Result: 该核恰为U中可通过相邻低阶代数分解的最大子模。当此子模为零时，典范映射为同构，且每个Ω_r为前r+1个齐次子空间的直和。此外，构造关于模同态自然；当U不可分解时，L_n(U)在分次模范畴中不可分解。由满足上述假设的高阶Zhu代数模的非分裂扩张，可构造弱V-模的非分裂短正合序列。

Conclusion: 本文借助整数系数零模展开与标准诱导模分次，完整刻画了高阶Zhu代数诱导模的Ω-滤过结构，给出核的可分解性判据，并建立了高阶Zhu代数模扩张与弱V-模扩张之间的对应，为顶点算子代数的表示论提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Let $F$ be an algebraically closed field of characteristic different from $2$, and let $V$ be a nonnegatively integer-graded vertex operator algebra over $F$. We consider higher Zhu algebras obtained by taking a quotient of $V$ by a relation space containing the vectors $D_V^{(h)}a-\binom{-\mathrm{wt}(a)}{h}a$ for all homogeneous $a\in V$ and integers $h\geq1$, where $D_V^{(h)}a=a_{-h-1}\mathbf1$. For $n\geq1$ and a nonzero unital module $U$ over the corresponding higher Zhu algebra, we study the quotients $L_n(U)$ of induced modules and determine the kernel of the canonical map $U\toΩ_n(L_n(U))/Ω_{n-1}(L_n(U))$. This kernel is the largest submodule of $U$ that factors through the adjacent lower-level algebra. When this submodule is zero, the canonical map is an isomorphism, and each $Ω_r$ is the direct sum of the first $r+1$ homogeneous subspaces. These results are proved using an expansion of zero modes with integer coefficients and the grading of the standard induced modules. Moreover, the construction is natural with respect to module homomorphisms, and $L_n(U)$ is indecomposable in the graded module category whenever $U$ is indecomposable. From nonsplit extensions of modules for higher Zhu algebras satisfying the stated hypotheses, we construct nonsplit short exact sequences of weak $V$-modules.

</details>


### [128] [Ramified Quivers with Potentials, Monodromy Characters, and Cluster Bases I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09868)
*Jiarui Fei*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为容许良好分歧实现的斜对称化丛代数构造范畴模型与丛特征，证明良好性等价于非退化可实现性，并在红化序列、三角扩张、变异等条件下给出F-多项式与丛代数基的结构性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为斜对称化丛代数（尤其带有几何系数、任意连接双模的情形）建立范畴模型与丛特征理论，摆脱刚性假设限制，并在种子可达条件下刻画丛代数基（含中间/上丛代数）。

Method: 基于良好分歧实现（等价于非退化可实现性）；利用红化序列检验箭头类型符号一致性；对具有红化序列的矩阵进行三角扩张与变异；在Jacobi-有限非退化实现上，用表示Grassmannian上驯顺单值群的交错迹定义F-多项式，并在种子可达条件下由满初始序构造基。

Result: 红化序列下只需检验该序列上箭头类型的符号一致性；任意连接双模的三角扩张在分量矩阵均有红化序列时保持良好性；由单一顶点经三角扩张与变异得到的矩阵，对每个相容正整数对称化子都存在良好的Jacobi-有限非退化实现。交错迹定义F-多项式满足变异且无需刚性假设；种子可达下每个满初始序给出带反转冻结变量的中间丛代数基（两代数重合时为上丛代数基）；固定Jacobian代数与δ-向量时，一般单值F-多项式的Newton多面体与序无关。

Conclusion: 良好分歧实现为斜对称化丛代数提供了统一的范畴与丛特征框架，放宽了传统刚性条件，并在种子可达条件下给出中间/上丛代数的基与Newton多面体不变性。

Abstract: We construct categorical models and cluster characters for skew-symmetrizable cluster algebras admitting good ramified realizations. Goodness is equivalent to nondegenerate realizability. When a reddening sequence is given, it is enough to check sign coherence of the arrow types along that sequence. Triangular extensions with arbitrary connecting bimodule types preserve goodness when both component matrices admit reddening sequences. Consequently, every matrix obtained from one vertex by triangular extensions and mutations admits a good Jacobi-finite nondegenerate realization for every compatible positive integral symmetrizer.
  For a good Jacobi-finite nondegenerate realization, alternating traces of tame monodromy on representation Grassmannians define $F$-polynomials satisfying mutation without a rigidity hypothesis. Under the seed reachability condition, every full initial order determines a basis of the middle cluster algebra for arbitrary geometric coefficients, with frozen variables inverted. The same family is an upper-cluster-algebra basis when the middle and upper algebras coincide. For a fixed Jacobian algebra and $δ$-vector, the Newton polytope of the generic monodromy $F$-polynomial is independent of the order.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次观测到电磁Dalitz衰变 J/ψ→e⁺e⁻η_c，并测得其相对分支比为 (0.65±0.02_stat.±0.07_sys.)%，同时提取了 q² 依赖的形状因子，未发现与理论预言的显著偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁Dalitz衰变 J/ψ→e⁺e⁻η_c 此前尚未被观测，测量该过程并检验其形状因子有助于理解 J/ψ 到 η_c 的电磁与辐射跃迁机制及矢量介子形状因子的理论模型。

Method: 基于 BEPCII 对撞机上 BESIII 探测器采集的 (10.087±0.044)×10⁹ 个 J/ψ 事件，通过分析 e⁺e⁻η_c 末态进行首次观测，测定相对分支比 R = Γ(J/ψ→e⁺e⁻η_c)/Γ(J/ψ→γη_c)，并提取 q² 依赖的形状因子。

Result: 相对分支比 R 测定为 (0.65±0.02_stat.±0.07_sys.)%，其中第一项为统计误差、第二项为系统误差；提取的形状因子与理论预言无明显偏离。

Conclusion: 实验首次观测到 J/ψ→e⁺e⁻η_c 电磁Dalitz衰变，给出了精确的相对分支比和形状因子测量结果，与理论预言一致。

Abstract: Using $(10.087\pm0.044)\times10^9$ $J/ψ$ events collected with the BESIII detector at the $e^+e^-$ BEPCII collider, we present the first observation of the electromagnetic Dalitz decay $J/ψ\to e^+ e^- η_c$. The relative branching fraction $R \equiv \frac{Γ(J/ψ\to e^+e^-η_c)}{Γ(J/ψ\to γη_c)}$ is determined to be $(0.65\pm0.02_{\rm stat.}\pm0.07_{\rm sys.})\%$, where the first uncertainty is statistical and the second systematic. The $q^2$-dependent form factors are also extracted, and no significant deviation from the theoretical prediction is seen.

</details>


### [130] [Green's functions and weighting fields for dielectric multilayer geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09961)
*Werner Riegler*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文求解了夹在两接地平面之间、含任意数量不同介电常数平行层的结构中点电荷的静电势，讨论横向边界条件，并将结果用于推导分段电极的权重场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要为多层介质结构中的点电荷电势提供通用解析解，尤其是接地平面被分割为条带与焊盘时，需要相应的权重场来描述电极对电荷的感应耦合。

Method: 在由两个接地平面限定的多层不同介电常数平行板几何中求解点电荷的静电势（基于格林函数/镜像或本征函数展开），并针对横向方向讨论不同的边界条件；随后将解应用于将接地平面分割为条带和焊盘的电极结构，推导其权重场。

Result: 得到了任意层数、不同介电常数下点电荷静电势的一般解，并给出分割条带/焊盘电极的权重场表达式。

Conclusion: 该解析解适用于多层介质与分段电极的电场分析，可直接用于计算权重场，供探测器、半导体器件或信号感应结构的建模使用。

Abstract: The electrostatic potential of a point charge in a geometry with an arbitrary number of parallel layers of different permittivity, bounded by two grounded planes, is derived. Different boundary conditions in the transverse direction are discussed. For the case where a grounded plane is segmented into strips and pads, the solution is used to derive the weighting fields for these electrodes.

</details>

### [131] [Extreme Stellar Death and Galaxy Feedback at z = 2: A Rest-frame Ultraviolet Characterization of the Strongly Lensed Superluminous Supernova 2025wny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08903)
*Willem B. Hoogendam,David O. Jones,Christopher J. Storfer,Luca Izzo,Kyle W. Davis,Miranda Y. Kong,Katie Auchettl,Aadya Agrawal,David R. Aguilera-Dena,Kenneth C. Chambers,Siyuan Chen,Thomas de Boer,Ryan J. Foley,Matthew Grayling,Jason T. Hinkle,Jens Hjorth,Mark E. Huber,Chien-Cheng Lin,Thomas B. Lowe,Eugene A. Magnier,Kaisey S. Mandel,C. Tanner Murphey,Gautham Narayan,Gregory S. H. Paek,Kishore C. Patra,Haille M. L. Perkins,Armin Rest,Benjamin J. Shappee,Richard J. Wainscoat*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对强引力透镜贫氢超亮超新星2025wny(z=2.0155)开展光学与光谱跟踪观测,结合KCWI紫外光谱与Pan-STARRS测光,推断其放大率、磁星模型参数及宿主星系性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高红移贫氢超亮超新星(SLSN-I)样本稀缺,需检验其爆炸机制与族群性质在早期宇宙是否与低红移样本一致。

Method: 用Keck-II/KCWI积分场光谱获取静止系紫外光谱(极大光后11–61天);用Pan-STARRS测光约束最亮像放大率;用MOSFiT拟合含$^{56}$Ni衰变的磁星能量注入模型;通过窄星际介质谱线估算金属丰度,并由宿主星系谱线估算外流速度与Ly$\alpha$逃逸分数。

Result: 最亮像放大率$\mu_A=19.4\pm1.6$,比质量模型估计高约2–4倍;磁星模型给出自转周期$P_{\rm spin}=4.1^{+0.7}_{-1.0}$ ms、磁场$B_\perp=2.4^{+1.1}_{-0.8}\times10^{14}$ G,可再现近极大光变曲线但晚期平台暗示额外能源;紫外光谱几乎无铁族元素谱线遮蔽;宿主星系为亚太阳金属丰度,外流速度约575 km/s,Ly$\alpha$逃逸分数$f_{esc}^{Ly\alpha}=0.23\pm0.03$。

Conclusion: SN 2025wny与低红移SLSNe吻合良好;LSST与Roman等未来巡天将发现更多高红移SLSNe,以检验SLSN-I爆炸在早期宇宙的族群一致性。

Abstract: We present photometric and spectroscopic follow-up observations of the strongly-lensed H-poor superluminous supernova (SLSN) 2025wny at $z = 2.0155$. We use integral-field Keck-II/KCWI spectroscopy to obtain rest-frame ultraviolet spectra spanning $11$ to $61$ days after maximum light. We use Pan-STARRS photometry to constrain the brightest image's magnification, finding $μ_A=19.4\pm1.6$, a factor of $\sim$2-4 higher than mass-modeling estimates. We fit the Pan-STARRS photometry with a magnetar energy-injection model including $^{56}$Ni decay using the Modular Open Source Fitter for Transients (MOSFiT); the model can reproduce the near-maximum light curve with parameters typical of local universe SLSNe-I, including a magnetar spin period $P_{\rm spin}=4.1^{+0.7}_{-1.0}\,\mathrm{ms}$ and magnetic field $B_{\perp}=2.4^{+1.1}_{-0.8}\times10^{14}\,\mathrm{G}$; however, the late-time plateau suggests an additional power source. Spectroscopically, iron-group element line blanketing is virtually absent in the rest-frame UV spectra, and the absorption features match previously identified SLSN-I features from abundance tomography. Based on narrow ISM line metallicity estimates, SN 2025wny exploded in a sub-solar-metallicity galaxy. From host-galaxy lines in our rest-frame UV-to-optical spectrum we estimate high-velocity outflows of $\sim$575 km s$^{-1}$ and an exceptionally high Ly$α$ escape fraction of $f_{esc}^{Lyα}=0.23\pm0.03$. SN 2025wny matches lower-redshift SLSNe reasonably well. Upcoming surveys like LSST and Roman will discover additional high-redshift SLSNe, further testing whether SLSNe-I explosions remain consistent at the population level in the early universe.

</details>


### [132] [A Newly Identified Transient Ultraluminous X-ray Source in M31](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08918)
*Ferdinand,Murray Brightman,Jean-Marie Hameury,Elias Kammoun,Dominic J. Walton,Matthew J. Middleton,Hannah Earnshaw,Felix Fürst,Wynn V. Jacobson-Galán,Jacob E. Jencson,Margaret Lazzarini,Mark T. Reynolds*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 报道在M31发现并刻画了一个新的暂现极亮X射线源（transient ULX），峰值光度约1e39 erg/s，光变可由盘不稳定性模型解释，并估算了其在M31中的发生率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知的ULX多为持续性源，暂现ULX作为独立群体可为极端超爱丁顿吸积提供独特观测窗口；M31中此类源样本稀少，需要更多发现以约束其性质与发生率。

Method: 对2021年6月9日偶然探测到的M31新暂现ULX进行X射线观测与能谱、光变分析；用Hameury & Lasota的盘不稳定性模型拟合光变曲线；结合Hubble Source Catalog中邻近光学源与恒星等龄线（假设A_V=1.58）推断供体星性质；并统计估算M31中暂现ULX的发生率。

Result: 峰值未吸收0.3–10 keV光度约1e39 erg/s；能谱演化显示后期平流增强，与爆发衰减期从超爱丁顿薄盘向标准薄盘转变的预期相反；光变可由盘不稳定性模型复现，特征时标约41–61天；推断吸积率对1.4太阳质量中子星为超爱丁顿，对10太阳质量恒星级黑洞约为爱丁顿；光学对应体暗示可能的巨星供体，年龄log10(age/yr)约8.5、质量约3.2太阳质量；估算M31暂现ULX发生率为0.18±0.10 yr^-1，低于以往其他星系的报道值。

Conclusion: M31 ULX-3扩充了M31中已知暂现ULX的小样本，凸显对邻近星系持续、规律X射线监测的重要性。

Abstract: Ultraluminous X-ray sources (ULXs) are off-nuclear X-ray sources with luminosities exceeding the Eddington limit of typical stellar-mass compact objects. While most known ULXs are relatively persistent sources, transient ULXs have emerged as a distinct population that offer unique insight into extreme, super-Eddington accretion. We report the discovery and X-ray characterization of a newly identified transient ULX in M31. First detected serendipitously on 2021 June 9, the source reached a peak unabsorbed $0.3 - 10$ keV luminosity of $\sim10^{39}\ \rm erg\ s^{-1}$. Its spectral evolution suggests increasing advection at later epochs, opposite to the expected transition from a super-Eddington slim disk toward a standard thin disk during the decay of the outburst. The observed X-ray light curve can be reproduced using the disk-instability model of Hameury & Lasota, with characteristic timescales of $\sim41-61$ days. The inferred mass-accretion rate is super-Eddington for a $1.4\,M_{\odot}$ neutron star but approximately Eddington for a $10\,M_{\odot}$ stellar-mass black hole. Nearby optical sources from the Hubble Source Catalog suggest a possible giant donor with an age of $\log_{10}({\rm age/yr})\sim8.5$ and a mass of $\sim3.2\,M_{\odot}$, when compared with stellar isochrones with an assumed $A_V=1.58$. We estimate a transient ULX occurrence rate in M31 of $0.18\pm0.10\ {\rm yr^{-1}}$. This rate is lower than those reported for other galaxies in previous studies. M31 ULX-3 expands the small population of known transient ULXs in M31 and highlights the importance of continued, regular X-ray monitoring of nearby galaxies.

</details>


### [133] [PEARLS: NuSTAR and XMM-Newton Extragalactic Survey of the JWST North Ecliptic Pole Time-Domain Field V: Unraveling X-ray Spectral Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08920)
*Samantha Creech,Francesca Civano,Daniel Wik,Núria Torres-Alba,Ross Silver,Xiurui Zhao,Tonima Ananna,Gibson Bowling,Norman A. Grogin,Anton M. Koekemoer,Peter Maksym,Sylvia Mesicek,Rafael Ortiz,Payaswini Saikia,Steven Willner,Rogier Windhorst*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于NuSTAR在JWST北黄极时域场(NEP-TDF)的巡天，对112个源进行光谱建模与变异性研究，识别7个变源候选，并探讨探测变异性所需计数与观测历元条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬X射线时域巡天稀缺，中高红移暗弱AGN的变异性（内禀亮度F_int变化与遮蔽柱密度N_H变化）难以测量；需宽波段光谱与多仪器协同观测来区分这两种机制，并理解变异性对暗弱AGN可探测性的影响。

Method: 利用NEP-TDF的NuSTAR与XMM-Newton同步观测及1.8 Ms Chandra监测数据；对巡天后半程新发现的52个NuSTAR源进行首次光谱建模，并对全部112源星表开展光谱变异性研究；将变源与巡天灵敏度对比；以N_H变异性时标探测遮蔽体位置。

Result: 识别出7个变源候选；探测变异性需要总计≳1000计数并分布于≳10个观测历元；显著变异性在>100天时标上略更常见，提示在暗弱AGN中尘埃环(torus)对N_H变异性仅有轻微主导作用。

Conclusion: 宽波段(约0.5-24 keV)光谱结合多历元监测是分辨AGN变异性机制的有效手段；变源样本可为种群合成模型提供约束，说明变异性如何影响暗弱AGN（主导SMBH吸积历史）的可探测性；N_H变异性时标可作为遮蔽体位置的探针。

Abstract: As one of the first deep time-domain surveys in the hard X-ray band, the NuSTAR campaign in the JWST North Ecliptic Pole Time Domain Field (NEP-TDF) offers a prime opportunity to measure variability in a faint population of Active Galactic Nuclei (AGN) in the medium-to-high redshift universe. In the X-ray band, AGN variability is generally caused by changes in the brightness of the corona ($F_{int}$ variability) or changes in the column density of obscuring material near the SMBH ($N_H$ variability). Broad-band spectroscopy ($\sim$0.5-24 keV) is required to reliably disentangle these two phenomena. With simultaneous NuSTAR and XMM-Newton coverage---as well as 1.8 Ms of Chandra monitoring---the X-ray surveys in the NEP-TDF are ideally situated for this purpose. This work presents the first spectral modeling of the 52 NuSTAR-identified sources discovered in the latter half of the campaign, as well as a spectroscopic variability study of the entire 112-source NuSTAR NEP-TDF catalog. Seven variable candidates are identified. We find that $\gtrsim 1000$ total counts split across many ($\gtrsim 10$) epochs are needed in order to detect variability. Additionally, variable sources are compared to the survey sensitivity in order to inform population synthesis models of how variations can affect the detectability of faint AGN, which dominate the SMBH accretion history. Lastly, the timescale of $N_H$ variability is investigated as a probe for the obscurer location. Significant variability is slightly more common on $> 100$ day timescales, suggesting that the torus may have only slight dominance in driving $N_H$ variability in faint AGN.

</details>


### [134] [Predictions for Short Gamma-Ray Burst Detections by COSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08940)
*Eliza Neights,Eric Burns,Nicolò Parmiggiani,Yong Sheng,Savitri Gallego,Aldo Aguilar-Nadalini,Aaron C. Trigg,Sylvain Guiriec,Koothodil Abhijith Augustine,Matthew G. Baring,Anna L. Butterworth,Hsiang-Kuang Chang,Che-Yen Chu,Niccolò Di Lalla,Christopher Fryer,Hadar Lazar,Israel Martinez-Castellanos,Marion Sudvarg,Péter Veres,Steven E. Boggs,Dieter H. Hartmann,Carolyn A. Kierans,John A. Tomsick,Andreas Zoglauer*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟COSI对短伽马射线暴的观测与星上触发算法处理，评估其灵敏度并预测探测率：预计每年触发约47-89例短暴，其中约7-10例定位精度优于2.5°，O5阶段预计有0-3例与引力波联合探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COSI是计划于2027年发射的软伽马射线望远镜，能够对短时标瞬变源（包括伽马射线暴）进行星上触发并快速向社区发布含定位信息的警报，从而实现余辉快速后随观测及与其他仪器探测到的瞬变源关联。因此需要定量评估COSI对短伽马射线暴的探测能力与预期探测率。

Method: 模拟COSI对短伽马射线暴的观测，并使用将运行于星上的触发算法处理这些模拟数据，以估计COSI对短暴的灵敏度；随后将该灵敏度与代表宇宙中内禀短暴种群的模拟样本进行卷积，以预测COSI的探测率。

Result: 预计COSI每年可触发约47-89例短伽马射线暴，其中约7-10例的定位精度在2.5°以内。在O5运行阶段预期的引力波灵敏度下，预测可获得0-3例与引力波的联合探测。

Conclusion: COSI具备可观的短伽马射线暴触发与定位能力，并能支持快速后随观测及多信使联合探测，在O5阶段有望实现少量与引力波的联合探测事件。

Abstract: The Compton Spectrometer and Imager (COSI) is a soft gamma-ray telescope scheduled to launch in 2027. COSI will trigger onboard the spacecraft in response to short-duration transients, including gamma-ray bursts, and it will promptly disseminate alerts which include localizations to the community. This will enable rapid follow-up observations of the afterglow and associations with transients identified by other instruments. By simulating COSI observations of short gamma-ray bursts (sGRBs) and processing them with the trigger algorithm which will run onboard the spacecraft, we estimate the sensitivity of COSI to sGRBs. We then convolve this with a simulated set of sGRBs representing the intrinsic population in the universe to predict the rate that will be detected by COSI. We find that COSI is expected to trigger on $\sim$47-89 sGRBs per year with $\sim$7-10 localized to within 2.5°. With the gravitational wave sensitivity anticipated in the O5 run, we predict 0-3 joint detections with gravitational waves.

</details>


### [135] [Can LISA See Inside the Little Red Dots?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08964)
*Samuel Gómez Gómez,Xisco Jimenez Forteza,Carlos Palenzuela,Rosalba Perna*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文计算了10–30 M☉黑洞在多种小红点（LRD）内部模型中极端质量比旋近的波形，并用失配判据检验其可区分性，进而估计LISA探测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRD中心环境的不同模型（准星、辐射性黑洞包层、超爱丁顿吸积盘）对EMRI所在半径处的气体密度预测相差多个数量级，因此需检验EMRI波形能否区分这些环境以及与真空情形。

Method: 对10–30 M☉黑洞在多种LRD内部模型中计算旋近过程，纳入气体环境效应，采用失配（mismatch）判据评估波形与真空及其他模型的可区分性。

Result: 稠密内部（Bondi型自由落体腔、辐射包层）几乎在每个可探测事件中都产生可测量偏差；对流准星在M_BH≲10^5 M☉、z≲0.6时可测；共转盘无印记。z>3.9的14个JWST LRD对10 M☉伴星不可探测，而z<0.9的31个LRD中有30个在其黑洞质量约为维里估计的1/100时可探测。4年LISA任务在z=6以内：10^5 M☉宿主配10 M☉伴星预期0.08–0.8个事件，配30 M☉伴星为1.5–15个；致密星团中则降至≲10^-4。

Conclusion: EMRI波形可有效探测并区分LRD中心稠密气体环境（准星、辐射包层）与真空，为LISA探测LRD核提供了可检验的判据和探测率预期。

Abstract: The Little Red Dots (LRDs) are compact, broad-line sources that appear to contain accreting black holes (BHs) embedded in dense gas. Proposed models for their central environments (including quasi-stars, radiative BH envelopes and super-Eddington accretion discs) predict gas densities at the radii probed by an extreme-mass-ratio inspiral that differ by many orders of magnitude. We compute the inspiral of a $10$--$30\Msun$ BH for a range of LRD interior models, including environmental effects from the gas, and test with a mismatch criterion whether the resulting waveforms can be distinguished from vacuum and from one another. Dense interiors (Bondi-like free-fall cavities and radiative envelopes) produce measurable deviations from vacuum in essentially every detectable event. Convective quasi-star interiors are measurable for $\MBH\lesssim10^5\Msun$ and $z\lesssim0.6$, while a co-rotating disc leaves no measurable imprint. In favorable cases the models are also mutually distinguishable. None of the 14 JWST LRDs at $z>3.9$ would be detectable with a $10\Msun$ companion, but 30 of 31 LRDs at $z<0.9$ would be if their black holes are $\sim100$ times less massive than their virial estimates, and in many of them the interior's imprint would be measurable. Across the LRD population to $z=6$, a 4-yr LISA mission would yield $0.08$--$0.8$ detectable events for $10^5\Msun$ hosts and $10\Msun$ companions ($1.5$--$15$ for $30\Msun$) if LRD nuclei host inspirals at the rate of ordinary galactic nuclei. In dense clusters, where captures are plunges, this falls to $\lesssim10^{-4}$.

</details>


### [136] [The Prompt Emission of Gamma-Ray Bursts from Population III Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09463)
*Nathan Walker,Davide Lazzati,Tyler Parsotan,Rosalba Perna,Matteo Cantiello,Jake Hassan,Brian Morsony*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文探讨通过大质量第三星族（Pop III）恒星死亡产生的伽马射线暴（GRB）来间接识别Pop III恒星，发现光变时标、各向同性能量、峰值能量及经验相关性位置等可观测量可区分Pop III与Pop I GRB，并指出Pop III GRB可能是Einstein Probe新探测到的软X射线瞬发辐射的潜在来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pop III恒星作为宇宙第一代恒星，数十年来始终未被直接探测到。James Webb太空望远镜对高红移源的高灵敏度重新激发了识别这些原初恒星的研究兴趣。以往研究多探索通过引力透镜直接探测Pop III恒星，本文则寻求一种替代途径——利用大质量Pop III前身星死亡产生的GRB来识别它们。

Method: 生成源坐标系下的模拟观测量（source-frame mock observables），比较Pop III GRB与Pop I GRB的性质，考察光变时标、各向同性等效能量、峰值能量以及经验瞬发辐射相关性中GRB所处位置等观测量对前身星质量和爆发能量的依赖。

Result: Pop III与Pop I GRB虽共享许多共同性质，但若干观测量有潜力区分二者。相对于Pop I，Pop III GRB倾向于呈现更长的特征时标和更高的各向同性等效峰值能量；相关观测量敏感依赖于前身星质量和爆发能量。此外，Pop III GRB可能是Einstein Probe最新探测到的软X射线瞬发辐射群体的一个合理来源。

Conclusion: 通过GRB识别Pop III恒星是一种可行且有前景的替代途径，为探测宇宙第一代恒星提供了补充手段。

Abstract: Population (Pop) III stars, the first generation of stars in the Universe, have eluded direct detection for decades. The advent of the James Webb Space Telescope, with its unprecedented sensitivity to high-redshift sources, has renewed interest in the prospects for identifying these primordial stars. While previous studies have explored the direct detection of Pop III stars through gravitational lensing, here we investigate an alternative approach: identifying Pop III stars through gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) produced by the deaths of massive Pop III progenitors. We generate source-frame mock observables and find that, although Pop III and Pop I GRBs share many common properties, several observables can potentially distinguish between the two populations. In particular, light-curve timescales, isotropic-equivalent energies, peak energies, and the locations of GRBs in empirical prompt-emission correlations depend sensitively on the mass and explosion energy of the progenitor. We find that Pop III GRBs tend to exhibit longer characteristic timescales and higher isotropic-equivalent peak energies than their Pop I counterparts. Finally, we show that Pop III GRBs may provide a promising origin for the population of soft X-ray prompt emission recently detected by the Einstein Probe, offering a complementary avenue for probing the first generations of stars.

</details>


### [137] [Polarised X-ray emission from the accreting millisecond pulsar SAX J1808.4-3658 during an X-ray outburst and a type-I X-ray burst](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09477)
*A. Papitto,A. Di Marco,M. C. Baglio,D. Baldi Tomei,C. Ballocco,G. Illiano,R. La Placa,C. Malacaria,A. Bobrikova,J. Poutanen,T. Salmi,A. Sanna,A. Veledina,F. Ambrosino,L. Burderi,S. Campana,F. Coti Zelati,D. de Martino,T. Di Salvo,G. L. Israel,F. La Monaca,A. Marino,A. Miraval Zanon,M. Pilia,L. Stella*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: IXPE在2025年SAX J1808.4-3658爆发衰减期显著探测到2-8 keV线偏振X射线，平均偏振度2.9%±0.5%、偏振角46°±5°，与光学波段方向大致一致；脉冲相位分辨分析未见Stokes参数显著变化；I型X射线暴峰值期出现偏振度16%±6%（2-6 keV）、角度近乎正交的偏振信号迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸积毫秒脉冲星（AMSP）X射线脉冲轮廓建模是约束中子星质量与半径的关键手段，而偏振X射线测量可提供系统几何（如热斑相对自转轴取向）的关键信息，因此需要对AMSP进行偏振观测以检验几何与辐射机制。

Method: 利用IXPE对2.5 ms AMSP SAX J1808.4-3658在2025年爆发衰减期进行2-8 keV偏振观测；结合IXPE与NuSTAR获得的脉冲相位定时解，并开展脉冲相位分辨的Stokes参数分析；同时分析I型热核X射线暴峰值期的偏振信号，并与光学波段偏振方向比较。

Result: 在持续辐射中显著探测到线偏振X射线：平均偏振度2.9%±0.5%，偏振角46°±5°，与光学波段方向大致一致，且与同类另一目标源水平相近。IXPE+NuSTAR定时解确认脉冲自转与轨道的长期演化，但未发现Stokes参数存在显著的脉冲相位分辨变化。将偏振X射线解释为源自NS表面热斑时，表明热斑几乎与自转轴对齐。I型X射线暴峰值期存在线偏振迹象：2-6 keV偏振度16%±6%，偏振角近乎正交于持续辐射的偏振角。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了SAX J1808.4-3658在持续辐射与X射线暴阶段偏振性质的观测约束：持续辐射弱偏振且无显著相位依赖，暗示热斑近乎与自转轴对齐；X射线暴峰值期可能存在较强且角度正交的偏振，若被确认将超出薄吸积盘反射X射线的预期，对AMSP几何与辐射机制模型构成重要检验。

Abstract: Modelling the X-ray pulse profiles of accreting millisecond pulsars (AMSPs) is a key technique to constrain the masses and radii of neutron stars. To this end, measuring the properties of polarised X-rays provides crucial information on the system geometry. We present the significant detection by IXPE of linearly polarised 2-8 keV X-rays during the decay of the 2025 outburst of the 2.5 ms AMSP SAX J1808.4-3658, with an average polarisation degree of $2.9\%\pm0.5\%$ and a polarisation angle of $46°\pm5°$, broadly consistent with the direction observed in the optical band. This level of polarisation is similar to that found in the only other source of this class that has been targeted so far. By using a pulse-phase timing solution obtained with IXPE and NuSTAR, which confirms the long-term evolution of the pulsar spin and orbit, we did not find any significant pulse phase-resolved variability in the Stokes parameters. Interpreting the polarised X-rays as originating from hot spots on the NS surface indicates they are almost aligned with the spin axis. We also report an indication for linearly polarised emission with a degree of $16\%\pm6\%$ (2-6 keV) during the peak of a type-I thermonuclear X-ray burst, with an angle almost orthogonal to that of the persistent emission. If confirmed, this level would exceed expectations for X-rays reflected by a thin accretion disc.

</details>


### [138] [Axion effects on hybrid stars: earlier hadron-quark phase transition and larger quark-matter cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09480)
*Yu-Heng Liu,He Liu,Hong-Ming Liu,Min Ju,Xu-Hao Wu,Peng-Cheng Chu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 在Maxwell构造下研究轴子对混合星性质及强子-夸克相变的影响，发现轴子场使相变提前至低重子密度并显著增大夸克核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究轴子这一可能的暗物质候选者如何通过修正的KMT相互作用影响致密星体（混合星）的状态方程、相变与结构，从而为星际介质与致密星观测提供理论参考。

Method: 强子相采用改进的等旋与动量相关相互作用（ImMDI）模型描述；夸克相采用含轴子修正Kobayashi-Maskawa-'t Hooft（KMT）相互作用的三味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio（NJL）模型描述；在Maxwell构造下求解状态方程，并计算声速、多方指数、质量-半径关系及夸克核性质。

Result: 轴子场a/f_a轻微硬化夸克物质状态方程，但显著将强子-夸克相变移至较低重子密度；两者竞争可整体软化混合星物质状态方程，区别于矢量耦合效应。轴子场对最大质量影响微弱，但显著增大夸克核的质量与半径，弱矢量耦合时尤为明显。

Conclusion: 在Maxwell构造框架下，轴子的主要作用是促进低密度处更早发生强子-夸克相变并扩大夸克核。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of axions on the properties of hybrid stars and the possible hadron-quark phase transition in their cores within the Maxwell construction. The hadronic phase is described by the improved isospin- and momentum-dependent interaction (ImMDI) model, while the quark phase is modeled by a three-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model with an axion-modified Kobayashi-Maskawa-'t Hooft (KMT) interaction. Based on the obtained equation of state, we analyze the properties of hybrid-star matter, including the speed of sound and the polytropic index, as well as the structural characteristics of hybrid stars, including the mass-radius relation and the properties of the quark core. Our study shows that the axion field $a/f_a$ slightly stiffens the equation of state of quark matter but significantly shifts the hadron-quark phase transition to lower baryon densities. These two effects compete in influencing the equation of state of hybrid-star matter, and may even soften it, which is different from the effect of the vector coupling interaction. Consequently, the axion field has only a weak influence on the maximum mass of hybrid stars, but significantly increases the mass and radius of the quark core, especially for weak vector coupling. These results indicate that, within the present Maxwell construction, the primary role of the axion is to promote an earlier hadron-quark transition at low densities and to enlarge the quark core.

</details>


### [139] [Evidence for a Similar Physical Origin Between Prompt Emission and X-Ray Flares of Gamma-Ray Bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09538)
*Wen-Yuan Yu,Wen-Long Zhang,Hou-Jun Lü*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过联合分析50对"脉冲-X射线耀发"样本并应用自组织临界性(SOC)统计，发现其时间参数幂律分布高度相似，支持X射线耀发与伽马射线暴瞬时辐射同源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线耀发与伽马暴瞬时辐射在统计性质上高度相似，暗示二者可能具有共同物理起源（如中心引擎活动）。但以往对"非同时观测"的余辉X射线耀发与瞬时伽马脉冲的对比统计研究尚不完整，需要同时性样本加以检验。

Method: 系统搜索与X射线耀发同时出现的伽马射线辐射，构建38个伽马暴、共50对脉冲-耀发样本（其中19个有红移测量）；对每对样本在重叠时间段内进行时间与光谱联合分析，采用吸收幂律、幂律加黑体、截断幂律和Band函数拟合；并对瞬时伽马脉冲与同时观测的X射线耀发应用自组织临界性分析，比较其时间参数的幂律分布。

Result: 每对样本的光谱均可由吸收幂律、PL+BB、CPL或Band函数较好描述；SOC分析显示两类成分的时间参数（等待时间、上升时间、峰值时间、衰减时间和持续时间）均服从相似的幂律分布，其中等待时间差异最大（Δα约为0.36），其余四个参数差异很小（Δα≲0.12）；对有红移子样本的各向同性能量，两者差异为Δα约为0.54。

Conclusion: X射线耀发与瞬时辐射在光谱和时间统计特性上高度一致，表明二者很可能具有相同的物理起源。

Abstract: Statistical properties of X-ray flares are highly similar to those of the prompt emission in gamma-ray bursts (GRBs), suggesting that they may share a common physical origin (e.g., central engine activity). However, comparable statistical studies of afterglow X-ray flares and prompt gamma-ray pulses that are not observed simultaneously remain incomplete. In this paper, by systematically searching for gamma-ray emission simultaneous with X-ray flares, we identify 38 GRBs containing 50 pulse--flare pairs, 19 of which have measured redshifts. We perform joint temporal and spectral analyses of the gamma-ray pulses and X-ray flares over their overlapping time intervals, and find that the spectrum of each pair can be well described by an absorbed power-law (PL), PL plus blackbody (PL+BB), cutoff power-law (CPL), and Band function. More importantly, by applying self-organized criticality (SOC) analysis to both the prompt gamma-ray pulses and the simultaneously observed X-ray flares, we find that the temporal parameters (e.g., waiting time, rise time, peak time, decay time, and duration) of the two components follow similar power-law distributions, with the largest absolute difference of $Δα\sim0.36$ for the waiting time and much smaller differences ($Δα\lesssim0.12$) for the other four parameters. For the isotropic energy derived from the subsample with measured redshifts, the difference between the two components is $Δα\sim0.54$. These results suggest that the X-ray flares and the prompt emission are likely to share the same physical origin.

</details>


### [140] [$r$-Process as Production of Lanthanides from Compact Object Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09556)
*Li-ming Zhuo,Hou-Jun Lü,Xiao-Xuan Liu,Meng-Hua Chen,En-Wei Liang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过COMPAS双星族合成、SkyNet核合成网络与随机多区银河系化学演化模型的端到端模拟，研究双中子星（BNS）与黑洞-中子星（BH-NS）并合对银河系r过程富集的相对贡献，发现BNS是后期Eu等镧系元素的主要来源，BH-NS贡献较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BNS与BH-NS并合对银河系快中子俘获过程（r过程）富集的相对贡献仍是核天体物理的开放问题，需要定量厘清两类并合通道在不同质量数区间的元素产出及演化贡献。

Method: 采用端到端数值模拟：用COMPAS进行双星族合成，用SkyNet网络计算核合成，用随机多区模型进行银河系化学演化（GCE）；并考虑5种中子星状态方程（EOS）以及BH-NS并合中黑洞自旋的差异。

Result: 平均丰度对中子星EOS和黑洞自旋不敏感。给定EOS下，在第二峰之前（质量数A~130），BNS丰度高于BH-NS；A>130时则相反。较轻r过程元素（A<130）主要来自盘风，较重元素（A>130）主要来自动力学抛射物。纯BNS并合是GCE后期Eu等镧系元素的主要贡献者，BH-NS对银河系Eu/Fe演化轨迹中的重r过程元素仅贡献很小部分。

Conclusion: BNS并合主导银河系后期重r过程元素（如Eu）的富集，BH-NS并合贡献有限；元素来源在质量数上存在分工：轻元素源于盘风，重元素源于动力学抛射物，且平均丰度对EOS与自旋不显著依赖。

Abstract: The relative contributions of binary neutron star (BNS) and black hole-neutron star (BH-NS) mergers to the Galactic enrichment from the rapid neutron-capture process ($r$-process) remain an open question in nuclear astrophysics. In this paper, we present end-to-end numerical simulations of binary population synthesis, nucleosynthesis, and Galactic chemical evolution (GCE) via Compact Object Mergers Population Astrophysics and Statistics (COMPAS), SkyNet network, and a stochastic multi-zone model, respectively. By considering five equations of state (EOS) of NS and different spins of BH for BNS and BH-NS mergers, it is found that the averaged abundance seems not to be significantly dependent on the EOS of NS and spin of BH for BNS and BH-NS mergers. For given EOSs of NS, the abundance of BNS is higher than that of BH-NS mergers before the second peak (for mass number $A\sim$130), but it is lower when the mass number is greater than 130. Moreover, the lighter $r$-process elements ($A<$130) are predominantly produced by the disk wind, while the heavier elements ($A>$130) originate mainly from the dynamical ejecta. The purely BNS mergers, on the other hand, are the dominant contributors to the production of lanthanides like europium (Eu) at the later stage of GCE, but BH-NS mergers make only a small contribution of heavy $r$-process elements for the $\rm Eu/Fe$ evolutionary tracks in the Milky Way.

</details>


### [141] [Remnant-wide mapping of heterogeneous presupernova burning histories in the O-rich ejecta of Cassiopeia A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09612)
*Yui Kuboike,Toshiki Sato,Kai Matsunaga*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用钱德拉深观测对仙后座A的O-rich喷出物进行空间与成分分析，发现其成分序列可由经历过壳层并合燃烧的17–20 M⊙前身星O层物质解释，且不同燃烧历史物质在遗骸尺度上空间分离，暗示前身星晚期不对称结构在爆炸后得以保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚期壳层相互作用（含壳层并合）可在核塌缩前于O-rich层留下化学成分不均匀结构，但这些不均匀性能否在爆炸中存活并保留至今尚不清楚。Cas A的O-rich喷出物为检验这一问题提供了理想样本。

Method: 使用深度Chandra X射线观测数据。对窄带图像进行主成分分析（PCA），并对20个代表性区域进行空间分辨光谱分析；将测得的O到Ca元素丰度与17–20 M⊙一维前身星模型的O层物质比较，评估其是否经历过强烈的壳层并合诱导燃烧与混合。

Result: PCA揭示O-rich喷出物存在从Ne-rich、NeSi-rich到O/Si-rich的成分序列；空间分辨光谱确认O/Si-rich端元Ne、Mg系统性亏损。O/Si-rich喷出物从O到Ca的宽广元素范围可同时由17–20 M⊙一维前身星模型中经历强壳层并合诱导燃烧与混合的O层物质再现；Ne-rich喷出物则保留更高Ne、Mg丰度，类似处理较弱的O壳层物质。两类成分在遗骸尺度上空间分离：Ne-rich偏向北侧，O/Si-rich在东侧更突出。

Conclusion: Cas A前身星晚期壳层燃烧与混合在空间上是不均匀的；不同燃烧历史喷出物在遗骸尺度上的分布表明部分不对称前身星结构可能经受住了爆炸并留存于当前遗骸中。

Abstract: Late-stage shell interactions, including shell mergers, can mix material across adjacent burning shells and imprint chemically inhomogeneous structures in the hydrostatic O-rich layers before core collapse. Whether such inhomogeneities survive the explosion and remain observable in the resulting supernova remnant is not well understood. We investigate the spatial and compositional structure of the O-rich ejecta in Cassiopeia A using deep Chandra X-ray observations. Principal component analysis of narrow-band images reveals O-rich ejecta spanning a compositional sequence from Ne-rich through NeSi-rich to O/Si-rich material. Spatially resolved spectroscopy of 20 representative regions confirms systematic depletion of Ne and Mg toward the O/Si-rich component. Notably, the composition of the O/Si-rich ejecta over a broad elemental range from O to Ca is simultaneously well reproduced by O-layer material in 17-20 $M_\odot$ one-dimensional presupernova models that experienced strong shell-merger-induced burning and mixing. In contrast, the Ne-rich ejecta retain substantially higher Ne and Mg abundances and resemble less strongly processed O-shell material. These chemically distinct components are spatially segregated on remnant-wide scales, with Ne-rich ejecta preferentially concentrated toward the north and O/Si-rich ejecta more prominent in the southeast. Our results suggest that late-stage shell burning and mixing in the progenitor of Cassiopeia A were spatially heterogeneous. The remnant-wide distribution of ejecta with different burning histories further indicates that part of this asymmetric presupernova structure may have survived the explosion and remains imprinted in the present-day remnant.

</details>


### [142] [Gamma-rays from black hole coronae: disentangling the leptonic and hadronic contributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09676)
*Sébastien Le Bihan,Daniel Grošelj,Martin Lemoine*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 结合磁化湍流辐射动理学模拟与强子诱导电磁级联，对NGC 4151进行自洽建模，发现MeV波段由轻子通道主导，强子级联在≲0.1 GeV仍可相比，且同一非热电子群可解释宇宙X射线背景与Cygnus X-1的MeV特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IceCube对近邻Seyfert星系TeV中微子的探测（若被确认）意味着伴随伽马射线经电磁级联被大幅再处理至MeV波段，从而对中微子发射模型给出多信使约束；但X射线冕中的初级非热电子同样可产生伽马射线，需区分轻子与强子贡献。

Method: 将磁化湍流的辐射动理学模拟结果与强子诱导电磁级联的预言进行自洽结合，并以NGC 4151为对象开展多信使建模。

Result: 轻子通道主导MeV波段，在非热电子尾部携带≳5%电子能量时，其贡献在≲0.1 GeV仍与强子级联相当；轻子成分或可被COSI在软伽马射线波段探测，而强子成分低于其灵敏度。同一非热电子群还可同时解释通常归因于AGN的0.1–1 MeV宇宙X射线背景超量以及硬态Cygnus X-1的MeV尾部。

Conclusion: 基于非热动理学物理，给出了黑洞冕多信使信号的更精细图像，并获得观测支持。

Abstract: IceCube detections of TeV neutrinos from nearby Seyfert galaxies imply, if confirmed, that the accompanying gamma rays are substantially reprocessed down to the MeV range by electromagnetic cascades, which places multi-messenger constraints on neutrino emission models. However, gamma rays can also be produced by the primary nonthermal electrons of the X-ray corona, as recent radiative kinetic simulations of magnetized turbulence have shown. To disentangle the leptonic and hadronic contributions, we self-consistently combine results from these simulations with predictions for hadronically induced electromagnetic cascades. Focusing on NGC 4151, we find that the leptonic channel dominates the MeV band, and remains comparable to the hadronic cascade up to $\lesssim$0.1 GeV, when the nonthermal electron tail carries $\gtrsim5\%$ of the electron energy. The leptonic contribution may be detectable in soft gamma rays by the upcoming Compton Spectrometer and Imager (COSI) mission, whereas our predicted hadronic component falls below its sensitivity. We also find that the same nonthermal electron population can account for both the $0.1-1$ MeV excess of the cosmic X-ray background, generally attributed to AGNs, and the MeV tail of Cygnus~X-1 in the hard state, lending observational support to our model. Our results offer a refined view of multi-messenger signals from black-hole coronae, grounded in their nonthermal kinetic physics.

</details>


### [143] [Dynamical pairing in gravitational wave populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09834)
*Fabio Antonini,Aleksandra Olejak,Isobel Romero-Shaw,Matthew Mould*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 动力学形成的双黑洞并非普遍为近等质量配对，而是遵循"有序抽取"关系：两个黑洞从同一单体质谱独立抽取后按质量排序，该关系给出条件伴星分布 p(m2|m1)，并在星团模拟中得到验证，可用于区分形成通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为星团中动力学形成的双黑洞多为近等质量配对，这一预期并不完整，需要更一般的动力学组装质量配对预测，以便将质量分布几何特征转化为形成通道的诊断工具。

Method: 提出有序配对关系：两黑洞从共同单体质谱独立抽取后按质量排序，由主质量分布确定条件伴星分布 p(m2|m1)；用星团模拟验证该图像；并将该检验应用于引力波群体的非参数重构，区分动力学配对（固定 m2 脊）与孤立双星演化（固定 q 结构）。

Result: 星团模拟中，有序抽取关系再现了质量配对分布的形态，包括继承自单体质谱的固定 m2 脊——即次质量近似恒定而主质量跨越一定范围、对应 q 随 m1 增大而减小。对引力波群体非参数重构的检验发现，在 m1≳40 M⊙、m2≃30 M⊙ 处存在暂现性增强，与有序配对大体相符。动力学形成还自然解释了 LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA 近期报告的次质量分布在约 30 M⊙ 以上快速下降。

Conclusion: 质量分布的几何本身即可作为形成通道的诊断：动力学配对将主质量谱峰映射为具有可预测振幅的固定 m2 脊，而孤立双星演化更易产生固定 q 结构；有序配对图像与观测数据中的暂现增强及次质量分布快速下降一致。

Abstract: Binary black holes formed dynamically in star clusters are often associated with nearly equal-mass pairing. We show that this expectation is incomplete. The more general prediction of dynamical assembly is an ordered-pairing relation in which the two black holes are drawn independently from a common one--body mass spectrum and then ordered by mass. Once the primary-mass distribution is known, this relation fixes the conditional companion distribution $p(m_2\mid m_1)$. We validate this picture with cluster simulations. In these models, the ordered-draw relation reproduces the morphology of the mass-pairing distribution, including fixed-$m_2$ ridges that are inherited from the one--body mass spectrum: features at nearly constant secondary mass that extend over a range of primary masses and therefore correspond to decreasing $q=m_2/m_1$ as $m_1$ increases. Thus, the geometry of the mass distribution itself becomes a formation-channel diagnostic. Dynamical pairing maps peaks of the primary-mass spectrum into fixed-$m_2$ ridges with predictable amplitude, whereas isolated binary evolution more naturally produces structures at fixed $q$. Applying this test to a non-parametric reconstruction of the gravitational-wave population, we find a tentative enhancement at $m_2\simeq 30~ M_\odot$ for $m_1\gtrsim 40~ M_\odot$ that is broadly compatible with ordered pairing. Finally, we show that dynamical formation naturally explains the rapid decline of the secondary-mass distribution above $\sim30~M_\odot$ recently reported by the LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA Collaboration.

</details>


### [144] [Composition $g$-modes and $f$-modes of neutron stars with gravitationally coupled dark matter: degeneracy with the symmetry energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10007)
*Probit J Kalita,Bharat Kumar,H C Das*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 在完整广义相对论下计算了费米子暗物质仅通过引力耦合时的g模与f模，利用NICER与GW170817数据限制暗物质质量分数为0.12–0.26（95%），并揭示暗核主要通过间接途径（要求更硬的核物质状态方程）影响g模频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前将g模作为中子星内暗物质探针的研究多基于单流体模型，假设暗物质与中子处于化学平衡。该假设可能并不成立，因此需要在暗物质仅通过引力与核子耦合的更现实情形下重新评估g模对暗物质的敏感性。

Method: 在完整广义相对论框架下计算含费米子暗物质的中子星的g模与f模，暗物质仅通过引力与核子耦合（双流体）。采用包含63个核子状态方程（Skyrme及相对论平均场泛函，包括Dutra等人模型以及Sun、Bhattiprolu与Lattimer汇编）的先验，结合NICER与GW170817观测数据推断暗物质质量分数。

Result: 数据并不偏好暗物质，将粒子质量在0.5–2 GeV范围内的暗物质质量分数限制在0.12–0.26（95%置信度）。仅通过引力耦合时，暗物质须借助引力场才能获得恢复g模所需的浮力：含10%质量暗核的星体在固定状态方程下使基频g模升高10–15%，比单流体图像小数倍。更大的是间接效应：含暗核的星体需要更硬的核子状态方程，而g模由对称能的密度依赖性决定，故其频率反映的是星体所需的状态方程而非其所含的暗物质；在固定半径和对称能下，含10%质量暗核对应对斜率L改变9–12 MeV。f模随平均密度变化，行为不同：暗核使f模与潮汐形变率之间的关系破坏约1%，远超核子星后验展宽。

Conclusion: 在暗物质仅通过引力耦合的情形下，g模对暗物质的直接敏感性远弱于单流体模型预言，其频率变化主要反映暗核所要求的更硬核状态方程而非暗物质本身；相比之下，f模与潮汐形变率关系的约1%破坏是更显著的暗物质特征，可作为探针。

Abstract: Composition gravity ($g$) modes have been proposed as a probe of dark matter in neutron stars, on the basis of one-fluid models in which the dark matter is in chemical equilibrium with the neutrons. We computed in full general relativity the $g$- and $f$-modes of stars in which fermionic dark matter couples to the nucleons only through gravity, and inferred the dark mass fraction from NICER and GW170817 data with a prior that comprises $63$ nucleonic equations of state (EOSs) from Skyrme and relativistic mean-field functionals, among them the models of Dutra \textit{et al.} and of the compilation of Sun, Bhattiprolu and Lattimer. The data do not prefer dark matter and bound its mass fraction at $0.12 - 0.26$ ($95\%$) for particle masses of $0.5$--$2$~GeV. Coupled only through gravity, the dark matter reaches the buoyancy that restores a $g$-mode only through the gravitational field: a dark core holding $10\%$ of the mass raises the fundamental $g$-mode by $10-15\%$ at fixed EOS, several times less than in the one-fluid picture. The larger effect is indirect: a star with a dark core needs a stiffer nucleonic EOS, and since the $g$-mode is set by the density dependence of the symmetry energy, its frequency reflects the EOS that the star requires rather than the dark matter it contains; at fixed radius and symmetry energy a core holding $10\%$ of the mass is equivalent to a change of the slope $L$ of $9-12$~MeV. The $f$-mode, which follows the mean density, behaves differently: a dark core breaks the relation between the $f$-mode and the tidal deformability by about one percent, far outside the posterior spread of nucleonic stars.

</details>


### [145] [Quasi-periodic eruption spectral-timing: EMRIs crossing warped accretion disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10110)
*Joheen Chakraborty,Andrew Mummery,Eliot Quataert,Riccardo Arcodia,Itai Linial,Erin Kara*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出了QPE（准周期爆发）的联合光谱-时变拟合框架，将基于Kerr测地频率和翘曲盘几何的解析时序模型与自辐照盘SED模型耦合，对GSN 069和eRO-QPE2进行同时拟合，验证EMRI模型的同时揭示稀疏时序数据的解简并问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QPE被认为是星系核中恒星级天体与超大质量黑洞吸积盘反复碰撞产生的重复X射线暂现源，其时序编码轨道与相对论进动信息，而宁静态SED探测同一盘-黑洞系统，二者提供了对同一轨道-盘系统的独立约束。作者希望利用这种双重约束联合刻画QPE系统。

Method: 构建联合光谱-时序框架：同时拟合基于Kerr测地频率与翘曲盘几何的解析QPE时序模型，以及共享同一SMBH质量、自旋和盘参数的自辐照盘SED模型。将该框架应用于GSN 069和eRO-QPE2两个QPE源。

Result: 在GSN 069和eRO-QPE2中，QPE时序与宁静态光谱可被同时再现，为EMRI模型和翘曲盘提供了旁证支持。但两源时序数据稀疏，均不存在唯一解；在eRO-QPE2中识别出六种物理模式可同样好地拟合时序与SED。

Conclusion: 结果证明了QPE光谱-时序作为SMBH、吸积盘及轨道伴星精密探针的潜力，同时揭示了稀疏采样QPE时序的混叠（aliasing）微妙性，需要更密集的观测活动来唯一确定解。

Abstract: Quasi-periodic eruptions (QPEs) are repeating X-ray transients from galactic nuclei potentially caused by a stellar-mass orbiter repeatedly colliding with the accretion disk of a supermassive black hole (SMBH) in an extreme mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI). In this picture, the eruption arrival times encode the orbit and its relativistic precessions, while the quiescent spectral energy distribution (SED) probes the same disk and SMBH, providing two independent constraints on the same orbit-disk system. We present a joint spectral-timing framework that takes advantage of this by simultaneously fitting an analytic QPE timing model built on Kerr geodesic frequencies and a warped disk geometry, coupled to a self-irradiated disk SED model sharing the same SMBH mass, spin, and disk parameters. We apply it to the QPE sources GSN 069 and eRO-QPE2, showing that the QPE timings and quiescent spectra can be reproduced simultaneously in each, providing circumstantial support for the EMRI model and warped disks in these systems. However, given the sparse timing data in both sources, neither admits a unique solution. In eRO-QPE2, we identify six physical modes which reproduce the timings and SED comparably well, motivating denser observing campaigns to identify one unambiguously. Our results demonstrate both the promise of QPE spectral-timing as a precision probe of SMBHs, their accretion disks, and orbiting companions, and the aliasing subtleties of sparsely sampled QPE timings.

</details>


### [146] [A relativistic inflow candidate in a quasar at cosmic noon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10267)
*Yerong Xu,Xiurui Zhao,Elena Bertola,Valentina Braito,Francesca Civano,Luca Comisso,Luigi Gallo,Vittoria E. Gianolli,Elias Kammoun,Giorgio Lanzuisi,Stefano Marchesi,Mar Mezcua,Ciro Pinto,James N. Reeves,Daniele Rogantini,Núria Torres-Albà,Lizhong Zhang*

TL;DR: 在z=1.893的类星体WISEA J094835.95+432302.6的XMM-Newton谱中发现两个候选吸收特征，可由一个视速度约0.37c的超快内流(UFI)的Fe XXV/XXVI K壳层跃迁解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙正午(z~2)是宇宙恒星形成与黑洞吸积活动的峰值时期，预期存在强吸积和外流。研究该时期黑洞吸积过程具有重要意义，但此前UFI候选体多局限于本地宇宙。

Method: 分析z=1.893类星体WISEA J094835.95+432302.6的XMM-Newton光谱，利用蒙特卡洛模拟并考虑look-elsewhere效应评估吸收特征的置信水平。

Result: 在静止系能量约4.65 keV和5.75 keV处发现两个红移特征，置信水平分别为99.3%和94.1%，可由视速度0.37(+0.02/-0.01)c的单一超快内流(UFI)的Fe XXV/XXVI K壳层跃迁解释。

Conclusion: 若后续XMM-Newton观测证实，这些探测将把UFI候选体从本地宇宙扩展到宇宙正午，为黑洞吸积增长峰值期提供罕见的实验室。

Abstract: Cosmic noon ($z\sim2$) marks the peak epoch of cosmic star formation and black hole accretion activity, where strong accretion and outflows are expected. We report two candidate absorption features in the \textit{XMM-Newton} spectrum of the quasar WISEA~J094835.95+432302.6 at $z=1.893$. Two redshifted features occur at rest-frame energies of $\sim4.65$ and $\sim5.75$keV with confidence levels of 99.3% and 94.1%, respectively, estimated from Monte Carlo (MC) simulations considering the look-elsewhere effect. These features can be explained by the Fe XXV/XXVI K-shell transitions from one ultra-fast inflow (UFI) with an apparent velocity of $0.37^{+0.02}_{-0.01}c$. If confirmed by follow-up \textit{XMM-Newton} observations, these detections would extend reported UFI candidates from the local Universe to cosmic noon, offering a rare laboratory for black hole accretion at the peak of their growth.

</details>

*G. Grolleron,C. Plard,M. Dell'aiera,T. François,V. Pollet,J. Talpaert,S. Caroff,T. Vuillaume on behalf of the CTAO-LST Project*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作利用深度学习框架GammaLearn处理LST-1真实观测数据，并通过灵敏度曲线与LST-1标准分析进行性能对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTAO作为下一代IACT阵列，灵敏度可提升至当前仪器的10倍，其首台原型LST-1已投入运行。传统分析依赖简化的图像形状参数，而深度学习方法能利用事件的完整时间与电荷信息，尤其在LST-1可及的约20 GeV低能区表现更优，这对观测遥远河外源（如活动星系核）以探测基础物理与宇宙学至关重要。

Method: 采用专为IACT数据分析设计的深度学习框架GammaLearn，生成灵敏度曲线，并与LST-1所使用的标准分析进行对比，同时将方法应用于LST-1的真实观测数据。

Result: 通过灵敏度曲线对GammaLearn与LST-1标准分析的性能进行了评估与比较（摘要未给出具体定量数值）。

Conclusion: 该研究评估了深度学习框架GammaLearn在LST-1真实数据上的分析性能，为CTAO时代的IACT数据分析提供了深度学习方法的验证依据。

Abstract: The Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory (CTAO) marks the next generation of Imaging Atmospheric Cherenkov Telescopes (IACTs), offering a sensitivity improvement of up to a factor of 10 over current instruments. Its first prototype, the Large-Sized Telescope (LST-1), is already in operation at the Roque de los Muchachos Observatory in La Palma, Spain. Deep learning methods have shown significant promise in reconstructing key properties of incident particles, such as energy, arrival direction, and type, using simulated data. Unlike traditional approaches that rely on simplified image shape parameters, deep learning can exploit the full temporal and charge information of the recorded events, providing enhanced performance, particularly at low energies (~20 GeV) accessible by LST-1. This capability is especially valuable for observing distant extragalactic sources like Active Galactic Nuclei, which are key to probing fundamental physics and cosmology. In this work, by producing sensitivity curves, we evaluate the performance of GammaLearn, a deep learning framework tailored for IACT data analysis, by comparing it to the standard analysis used with LST-1 and applying it to real observational data from LST-1.

</details>
