# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [FlexGrad: a root-free approach to adaptive step sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09203)
*Pablo Barros,Aaron Defazio,Vincent Guigues*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出FlexGrad优化器，一种自适应梯度方法，利用累积梯度与累积平方梯度同时缩放步长，解决经典AdaGrad类方法无法利用梯度相关性的问题，并在凸Lipschitz目标下证明其达到最优渐进收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典AdaGrad类方法仅依赖累积平方梯度进行步长缩放，无法利用梯度之间的相关性（即梯度方向的持续性）。当梯度方向持续一致时，此类方法仍采用较小步长，导致收敛速度受限。

Method: FlexGrad同时使用累积梯度和累积平方梯度来缩放步长：在噪声较大的区域保持AdaGrad式的行为，而在梯度方向持续时允许显著更大的步长。该方法完全在线、无需预先知道时间界（horizon-free），且在优化过程中不需要任何辅助搜索过程，并提供全局与逐坐标两种变体。

Result: 在凸Lipschitz目标上，理论证明FlexGrad对非光滑凸优化达到最优渐进收敛率，且全局与逐坐标变体均有保证。实验表明，在一系列凸与非凸问题上，FlexGrad在多种优化设置中表现具有竞争力。

Conclusion: FlexGrad实现简单，与实用的梯度归一化方法联系紧密，兼具理论最优性与实践竞争力，是对AdaGrad类方法在利用梯度相关性方面的重要改进。

Abstract: We introduce the FlexGrad optimizer, an adaptive gradient method that addresses a key limitation of classical AdaGrad-type methods: their inability to exploit correlations between gradients. By scaling steps using both cumulative gradients and cumulative squared gradients, FlexGrad preserves AdaGrad-like behavior in noisy regimes while allowing substantially larger steps when gradient directions are persistent. The method is fully online, horizon-free, and avoids any auxiliary search procedure during optimization. For convex Lipschitz objectives, we prove that FlexGrad attains the optimal asymptotic convergence rate for nonsmooth convex optimization, with guarantees for both global and coordinate-wise variants. FlexGrad is straightforward to implement and is closely connected to practical gradient-normalized methods. Experiments on a range of convex and nonconvex problems show that FlexGrad performs competitively across a range of optimization settings.

</details>


### [151] [Regret-Optimal Linear-Quadratic Control under Distributional Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09220)
*Lukas-Benedikt Fiechtner,Jose Blanchet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究扰动分布随时间漂移的有限时域LQR，提出事前分布鲁棒遗憾优化（DRRO），用含Gelbrich动能预算与矩偏差-累积失配预算的时间耦合矩模糊集刻画允许律序列，并将其精确化为半定规划，实验验证其遗憾显著低于常见律DRRO与名义控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限时域LQR中扰动律可能随时间漂移，经典假设律已知或时不变的控制与DRO方法难以刻画这种演化不确定性，需要一个能够约束律序列演化、同时保持可计算性的鲁棒优化框架。

Method: 提出事前分布鲁棒遗憾优化（DRRO），最小化相对于已知律序列的最优因果控制器的期望超额成本；构造时间耦合矩模糊集：一个Gelbrich动能预算，加第二个预算限制相对参考矩的偏离与持续累积失配；在仿射扰动反馈综合下将问题精确化简为半定规划（SDP），并从SDP解提取最坏情形矩路径与对应律。

Result: 尽管矩在时间上耦合，仿射扰动反馈综合仍精确等价于SDP；最优策略用历史扰动预测由当前与未来扰动均值决定的LQR修正量；SDP同时给出最坏情形矩路径与律；作为对比，标准DRO的类似SDP松弛可能存在对偶间隙；组合清算实验中，该方法在最坏情形遗憾上显著低于常见律DRRO与名义控制。

Conclusion: 时间耦合矩模糊集下的DRRO为分布漂移的LQR提供了可精确求解为SDP的鲁棒控制方法，兼顾演化建模与计算可行性，并优于标准DRO类方法。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon LQR with independent disturbances whose laws may drift over time. We address uncertainty in this evolution through ex-ante distributionally robust regret optimization (DRRO), which minimizes worst-case excess expected cost relative to the optimal causal controller that knows the law sequence. To specify the admissible law sequences, we introduce a temporally coupled moment ambiguity set with a Gelbrich kinetic-energy budget and a second budget limiting departures from reference moments and persistent cumulative mismatch. Despite the coupling of moments across time, affine disturbance-feedback synthesis reduces exactly to a semidefinite program. An optimal policy uses past disturbances to predict the correction to nominal LQR determined by current and future disturbance means. The SDP solution also yields worst-case moment paths and corresponding laws. For standard distributionally robust optimization (DRO), the analogous SDP relaxation can have a duality gap. Portfolio liquidation experiments show substantially lower worst-case regret over the drifting ambiguity set than common-law DRRO and nominal control.

</details>


### [152] [Fluid Mixing and Incompressible Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09235)
*Max Emerick,John Igraszek,Bassam Bamieh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将流体混合问题重构为被动标量输运的最优控制问题，建立类比经典最优传输的几何框架，指出动能目标导致不适定问题，而enstrophy目标更合理，导出EPDiff测地线方程并给出数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可压缩流体混合问题虽研究已久，但"高效混合流场是什么样、如何演化"这一基本问题仍待回答。作者希望借助最优质量传输的动力学与几何方法，为混合问题提供一个统一的几何变分框架。

Method: 将混合问题表述为被动标量输运的最优控制问题，动力学由连续性方程加不可压缩约束给出；构建类Wasserstein的度量及其形式黎曼结构，分解为若干基本几何问题，推导最优性必要条件、测地线方程与梯度流。将控制目标分别取为动能与enstrophy进行特化；过程中推广Helmholtz分解、重导Clebsch表示，并发展Otto微积分的平行理论。

Result: 动能目标恢复不可压缩Euler方程，但问题呈现不适定性，说明动能不是自然的混合代价度量；enstrophy目标则导出体积保持微分同胚群上右不变齐次H^1度量的Euler-Poincaré（EPDiff）测地线方程，并给出测地线与梯度流的数值模拟。

Conclusion: enstrophy比动能更适合刻画混合努力，EPDiff框架为高效混合流场的几何刻画提供了合理工具，并将混合问题与Arnold几何流体力学、Otto最优传输微积分统一起来。

Abstract: The problem of incompressible fluid mixing is long-studied, yet many questions remain open. This paper aims to address the question ``what do efficient flow fields for mixing look like, and how do they behave?'' Inspired by the dynamic and geometric approach to optimal mass transport, we formulate a version of the fluid mixing problem as an optimal control problem for transport of a passive scalar, in which the dynamics are given by the continuity equation together with an incompressibility constraint. We then develop a geometric framework around this problem which parallels that of classical optimal transport: the optimal control problem induces a metric on the set of reachable states (analogous to the Wasserstein metric), and this metric carries a formal Riemannian structure when the optimal control objective is induced by an inner product. Using this structure, we decompose our problem into a series of more fundamental geometric problems and derive (formal) necessary conditions for optimality, geodesic equations, and gradient flows for mixing. Specializing the control objective to the kinetic energy recovers the incompressible Euler equation, connecting our framework to the geometric hydrodynamics of Arnold. The resulting problem appears to be ill-posed, however, suggesting that the kinetic energy is an unnatural measure of mixing effort. Specializing instead to the enstrophy, which we argue is better suited to mixing, yields the Euler-Poincaré (EPDiff) geodesic equation for the right-invariant homogeneous $\dot{H}^1$ metric on the group of volume-preserving diffeomorphisms, and we present numerical simulations of both geodesics and gradient flows in this case. Along the way, we generalize the classical Helmholtz decomposition of vector fields, rederive the Clebsch representation of incompressible flows, and develop a parallel to the Otto calculus of optimal transport.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Nominal Equilibria: Risk-Averse Multi-Population Mean-Field Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09244)
*Bhavini Jeloka,Siddhartha Ganguly,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出风险规避多群体平均场博弈新范式，各群体在其它群体平均场流的动态可行模糊集上优化最坏情况期望回报，建立了理论性质、收缩性结果与学习算法，并通过实验验证收敛性与风险规避行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多群体平均场博弈方法通过代表智能体及其平均场分布建模大规模异构多智能体系统，但未显式考虑其它群体行为的不确定性。

Method: 采用占用测度（occupation-measure）建模与集值分析工具，定义风险规避多群体平均场均衡；在熵正则化下推导不动点算子的收缩性，并提出风险规避虚拟博弈（fictitious-play）方案用于学习均衡。

Result: 在温和假设下证明了模糊集的几何性质与新型风险规避多群体平均场均衡的存在性；给出不动点算子收缩性并用于均衡学习；风险规避虚拟博弈的利用度（exploitability）收敛至零，数值实验展示了收敛性与风险规避行为。

Conclusion: 该工作将风险规避引入多群体平均场博弈，为存在跨群体行为不确定性的大规模异构系统提供了理论框架与可学习算法。

Abstract: Recent advances in mean-field games and its multi-population variants enable large-scale heterogeneous multi-agent systems to be modeled through representative agents and their associated mean-field distributions. However, existing approaches do not explicitly account for uncertainty in the behavior of other populations. To this end, we introduce a new paradigm: risk-averse multi-population mean-field games, where each population optimizes a worst-case expected reward over dynamically feasible ambiguity sets of mean-field flows of a subset of the other populations. Employing an occupation-measure formulation along with tools from set-valued analysis, we establish, under mild assumptions, several theoretical properties of the multi-population game, including the geometric properties of the ambiguity sets and the existence of a novel risk-averse multi-population mean-field equilibrium. Further, we derive contractivity results of the fixed-point operator under entropy regularization and show that it can be utilized to learn the equilibrium. Finally, we propose a risk-averse fictitious-play scheme and show that exploitability decays to zero, despite the additional nonlinearity introduced by the worst-case objective. We report several numerical experiments to illustrate convergence and risk-averse behavior.

</details>


### [154] [Consumption and Investment When Welfare Floors Change over Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09263)
*Gugyum Ha,Junkee Jeon,Takwon Kim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究有限期限下带时变福利下限的消费-投资问题，将问题化为抛物型障碍问题，刻画干预边界的出现条件与正则性，并通过强对偶确定可行财富下确界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限期限内，投资者需保证延续效用始终不低于随时间变化的福利下限（期末下限即终端财富下限）。这类约束下的最优消费投资策略、约束何时真正起作用的边界，以及可行财富的最低水平尚缺乏系统刻画；同时该框架可统一处理完全市场中随机收入下的外部选择权与内生借贷能力问题。

Method: 利用累积乘子（cumulative multiplier）把原问题约化为抛物型障碍问题（parabolic obstacle problem）。基于障碍问题的解构造对偶价值，验证最优累积乘子，并将其表示为running supremum以控制其矩。用强对偶性确定可行财富的精确下端点。对有随机收入的完全市场情形，将福利下限解释为外部选择权价值。

Result: 当相对风险厌恶系数大于1时，干预边界在每个内点日期都存在；当小于1时，边界仅在折现后下限变换局部递增且创下严格running record的日期存在，因此边界可以消失后再度出现。证明了边界的局部正则性，并给出最优累积乘子与可行财富下确界的精确刻画。

Conclusion: 通过障碍问题与对偶方法完整刻画了时变福利下限约束下的最优消费投资和干预边界，将其与有限承诺及内生借贷能力联系起来，拓展了约束效用最大化问题的理论框架。

Abstract: We study consumption and investment over a finite horizon when continuation utility must stay above a welfare floor that changes over time, and the floor at the horizon acts as a terminal wealth floor. A cumulative multiplier reduces the problem to a parabolic obstacle problem. When relative risk aversion exceeds one, the intervention boundary exists at every interior date. When it is below one, the boundary exists exactly at the dates where a discounted transformation of the floor is locally increasing and sets a strict running record, so it can disappear and later reenter. We prove local regularity of the boundary, construct the dual value from the obstacle solution, and verify the optimal cumulative multiplier, whose moments are controlled through its representation as a running supremum. Strong duality identifies the exact lower endpoint of feasible wealth. With stochastic income in a complete market, the floor becomes the value of an outside option, which links the boundary to limited commitment and endogenous borrowing capacity.

</details>


### [155] [The Geometry of Anisotropic Dilation for Optimal Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09310)
*Carson Newman,Oscar Leong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文提出各向异性膨胀（anisotropic dilation）变换，证明其可在Gibbs类正则化子空间上传递作用，给出最优适配闭式解与泛化界，并在二维族与MNIST去噪上验证联合学习有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动逆问题的核心难题是构造能自适应数据分布几何的正则化子。已有最优正则化理论表明，Gibbs类中与分布P最匹配的正则化子由单一方向相关的径向汇总统计量ρ_P决定，这提示可通过变换数据来控制正则化子几何。因此需研究哪些变换能简单显式地作用于ρ_P，并能否改善变分问题的优化性质或将固定基正则化子适配到数据。

Method: 引入各向异性膨胀：一种方向保持映射，沿欧氏射线按球面上的正剖面函数对每个点重新缩放。刻画由此在分布上诱导的轨道结构，推导将固定基正则化子最优适配到数据的剖面闭式解，并证明其相对各向同性缩放的改进由Jensen间隙支配。进一步建立联合学习基正则化子与各向异性剖面的有限样本泛化界，在log剖面由线性特征模型或稀疏ReLU网络参数化时给出显式速率。基于该理论，将基正则化子与剖面参数化并联合从样本学习，得到数据自适应正则化子。

Result: 理论上，该变换族在径向汇总统计量空间上传递作用，任何Gibbs类目标正则化子均可由任一源分布经单一显式剖面达到；Jensen间隙在若干自然族上可证为正。实验上，在受控二维族与MNIST去噪任务中，学习剖面进一步提升了性能。

Conclusion: 各向异性膨胀为控制正则化子几何提供了简洁而通用的工具，既能闭式最优适配数据，又具备可证的泛化保证，使学习到的数据自适应正则化子在变分逆问题中优于各向同性缩放。

Abstract: A central question in data-driven inverse problems is how to construct a regularizer that adapts to the geometry of the data distribution. Recent work in optimal regularization shows that, within a broad Gibbs class, the regularizer best matched to a distribution $P$ is determined by a single, direction-dependent radial summary statistic $ρ_P$. This suggests a way to control regularizer geometry by transforming the data. In particular, which transformations act on $ρ_P$ in a simple, explicit way? Can they improve the optimization properties of the resulting variational problems or adapt a fixed base regularizer to data? To address these questions, we introduce anisotropic dilation, a direction-preserving map that rescales each point along its Euclidean ray by a positive profile on the sphere. Despite its simplicity, this family acts transitively on the space of radial summary statistics: any target regularizer in the Gibbs class can be reached from any source distribution by a single explicit profile. We characterize the resulting orbit structure on distributions, derive in closed form the profile that optimally adapts a fixed base regularizer to the data, and show that its improvement over isotropic rescaling is governed by a Jensen gap that is provably positive for several natural families. We also establish finite-sample generalization bounds for jointly learning the base regularizer and anisotropic profile, with explicit rates when the logarithm of the profile is parameterized by linear feature models or sparse ReLU networks. Building on this theory, we parameterize the base regularizer and anisotropic profile and learn them jointly from samples, yielding a data-adaptive regularizer for variational inverse problems. Experiments on a controlled two-dimensional family and MNIST denoising show that learning the profile further improves performance.

</details>


### [156] [Random projection with linear equalities and inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09324)
*Santanu S. Dey,Jingye Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文用随机过程的上确界与泛型链（generic chaining）工具，重新解释随机投影的精度现象，并将随机投影从线性等式约束推广到线性不等式约束，证明在一般（非多面体）设定下仍能近似保持对偶界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机投影通过将线性约束替换为随机线性组合来降维，但已有理论多依赖 Johnson–Lindenstrauss 引理与多面体设定，适用范围有限；作者希望用更一般的概率工具给出更简洁、更锐利的保证，并覆盖线性不等式约束这一更具挑战的情形。

Method: 结合拉格朗日对偶与泛型链（generic chaining），以随机过程上确界刻画随机投影的近似保持性；针对不等式约束，构造一种新的随机投影对偶证书的方式，保持对偶可行性。

Result: 得到比现有结果更短的证明和更锐的保证，且不限于基于 Johnson–Lindenstrauss 引理的多面体情形；随机投影后的不等式约束通常既不构成原可行域的松弛也不构成其限制，但可近似保持对偶界；新的对偶证书投影构造保持对偶可行性，且区别于既有基于 Johnson–Lindenstrauss 的构造。

Conclusion: 随机过程上确界与泛型链为随机投影提供了统一而一般的分析框架，可超越多面体设定并扩展到不等式约束；随机投影虽不保持问题结构，却能在对偶层面近似保持最优界。

Abstract: Random projection is a preprocessing technique for optimization that reduces the number of linear equality constraints by replacing them with random linear combinations while approximately preserving the optimal value. In this paper, we emphasize the role of suprema of random processes, and in particular generic chaining, in explaining this phenomenon. By combining Lagrangian duality with generic chaining, we obtain shorter proofs and sharper guarantees that apply beyond the typical polyhedral settings based on the Johnson--Lindenstrauss lemma. More importantly, this perspective allows us to extend random projection to problems with linear inequality constraints. Although randomly projected inequality constraints generally define neither a relaxation nor a restriction of the original feasible region, we show that random projection can nevertheless approximately preserve the dual bound. The key is a new construction for randomly projecting dual certificates that preserves dual feasibility and differs from existing constructions motivated by the Johnson--Lindenstrauss lemma.

</details>


### [157] [Nonmonotone Coderivative-Based Newton Methods for Nonsmooth Optimization with Machine Learning Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09364)
*Sina Kazemdehbashi,Yanchao Liu,Boris S. Mordukhovich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于coderivative的牛顿框架，用于求解无约束与约束非光滑优化问题，采用广义Hessian与自适应正则化，配备HAN线搜索，具全局收敛与局部超线性收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿型算法因局部收敛快而高效，但难以扩展到非光滑优化，核心困难在于二阶信息的引入。作者希望借助变分分析与广义微分工具，构建统一的非光滑牛顿框架。

Method: 方法使用广义Hessian（子梯度映射的coderivative），适用于C^{1,1}函数及含扩展实值分量的凸复合优化问题。引入自适应正则化以处理广义Hessian半正定的情形，并设计混合自适应非单调（HAN）线搜索实现全局化。提出精确与非精确版本，并通过前向-后向包络扩展到凸复合优化，可处理光滑分量强凸与否的情形。

Result: 在标准假设下，精确与非精确版本均全局收敛；当子梯度映射满足semismooth*性质时实现局部超线性收敛。在Lasso、logistic Lasso与SVM上的数值实验表明算法高效稳健，与多种一阶、二阶非光滑优化方法相比具有计算竞争力，同时保持强收敛性质。

Conclusion: 该coderivative牛顿框架有效统一了非光滑优化的二阶方法，兼顾全局收敛与局部快速收敛，并通过包络扩展与自适应机制增强了适用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Newton-type algorithms are among the most effective methods for solving optimization problems because of their rapid local convergence. However, extending these methods to nonsmooth optimization is challenging due to the difficulty of incorporating second-order information. To address this issue, we propose a coderivative-based Newton framework for solving both unconstrained and constrained nonsmooth optimization problems using tools from variational analysis and generalized differentiation. The proposed method employs generalized Hessians, defined as coderivatives of the subgradient mapping, and is applicable to both $C ^{1,1}$ functions and convex composite optimization problems with extended-real-valued components. To enhance robustness, the algorithm incorporates an adaptive regularization strategy that enables it to handle problems whose generalized Hessians are positive semidefinite. In addition, a Hybrid Adaptive Nonmonotone (HAN) line search scheme is developed as a globalization technique to improve global convergence and practical performance. Under standard assumptions, both the exact and inexact versions of the algorithm are globally convergent and achieve local superlinear convergence when the associated subgradient mapping satisfies the semismooth* property. The proposed framework is further extended to convex composite optimization through the forward-backward envelope, allowing it to handle problems with or without strong convexity in the smooth component of the objective function. Numerical experiments on Lasso, logistic Lasso, and support vector machine (SVM) problems demonstrate the efficiency and robustness of the proposed algorithm. Comparisons with several well-established first-order and second-order methods for nonsmooth optimization show that the proposed approach is computationally competitive while maintaining strong convergence properties.

</details>


### [158] [Exact and Efficient Methods for Identifying Carcinogenic Multi-Hit Gene Combinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09420)
*Rick S. H. Willemsen,Teresa L. F. Ho,Tenindra Abeywickrama*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对多打击癌症驱动组合识别中的定价子问题，提出一种利用对偶信息剪枝的深度优先搜索算法，在单CPU上数秒内求解至最优，从而首次给出MHCDSCP的精确求解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症由约2至9个基因突变（多打击组合）驱动，但由于基因数量庞大，枚举全部组合在计算上极具挑战，此前需要超级计算机才能完成。近期将任务建模为多打击癌症驱动集覆盖问题（MHCDSCP），并用单CPU的列生成法求解，但其中需反复求解的定价问题成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 针对定价问题提出一种专门的深度优先搜索方法，枚举所有可能的基因组合，并利用对偶信息对搜索空间进行大规模剪枝，从而在单CPU上快速求解至最优。

Result: 尽管以往在约15万CPU核心的超级计算机上才进行此类深度优先枚举，本文算法在单CPU上数秒内即可将定价问题求解至最优，消除了该瓶颈。

Conclusion: 通过消除定价瓶颈，本文方法成为MHCDSCP的首个精确算法，可保证找到全局最优解。

Abstract: Cancer is driven by an estimated two to nine gene mutations, known as multi-hit combinations. Due to the large number of genes, identifying these multi-hit combinations presents a computationally challenging problem, which has previously required supercomputers to enumerate all or most gene combinations. Recent work formulated this classification task as the Multi-Hit Cancer Driver Set Cover Problem (MHCDSCP) and solved it via column generation on a single commodity CPU, where a pricing problem identifies promising gene combinations (arXiv:2602.22551). The main bottleneck is the pricing problem that has to be solved repeatedly. To solve the pricing problem to optimality, we propose a dedicated depth first search method that enumerates all possible gene combinations. Although enumerating gene combinations with depth first search have been performed on supercomputers with around 150,000 CPU cores (arXiv:2603.16721), our depth first search algorithm solves the pricing problem to optimality within seconds on a single CPU by leveraging dual information to prune large parts of the search space. By eliminating this bottleneck, our approach yields the first exact method for the MHCDSCP, which is guaranteed to find a global optimal solution.

</details>


### [159] [Boundary-aware Reinforcement Learning for Hypercube State Spaces via Deterministic Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09712)
*Lijun Bo,Yijie Huang,Chenhao Lu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文为反射状态动力学建立连续时间确定性策略梯度框架，推导Neumann Bellman方程、优势率函数与策略梯度公式及鞅刻画定理，并提出带软惩罚或硬架构约束的连续时间深度确定性策略梯度算法，量化离散化误差，在油藏控制实验中验证边界感知方法能降低Neumann边界残差并提升学习稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间强化学习理论多针对无约束或非反射状态动力学，而许多实际系统（如油藏控制）的状态被限制在超立方体内并受反射随机微分方程支配，其边界（Neumann型）条件使值函数刻画与策略梯度推导面临额外困难。作者希望填补反射随机系统下连续时间确定性策略梯度理论的空白，并解决实践中离散采样与边界条件施加的问题。

Method: 在超立方体上构造由受控反射随机微分方程支配的状态过程；在适当正则性假设下建立值函数与Neumann Bellman方程的联系；引入优势率（advantage-rate）函数，推导确定性策略梯度公式，并证明鞅刻画定理；据此设计面向反射随机系统的连续时间深度确定性策略梯度算法，通过软惩罚或硬架构约束两种方式施加Neumann边界条件；进一步量化理想连续时间动力学与实际离散采样探索动力学之间的偏差。

Result: 理论上给出值函数与Neumann Bellman方程的对应关系、确定性策略梯度公式和鞅刻画定理；误差分析表明离散化偏差随时间网格加密与探索噪声消失而衰减；油藏控制问题的实验显示该强化学习框架有效，边界感知方法显著降低Neumann边界残差并提升学习稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作将连续时间确定性策略梯度理论推广到反射状态动力学，提供可实现的深度确定性策略梯度算法及边界条件处理方案，并从理论与实验两方面证明边界感知设计对反射随机系统强化学习的重要性。

Abstract: We develop a continuous-time deterministic policy gradient framework for reinforcement learning with reflected state dynamics, where the state process is governed by a controlled reflected stochastic differential equation on a hypercube. Under suitable regularity assumptions, we establish the connection between the value function and the Neumann Bellman equation, introduce an advantage-rate function that yields a deterministic policy gradient formula, and prove the martingale characterization theorem. Motivated by these theoretical results, we propose a continuous-time deep deterministic policy gradient algorithm for reflected stochastic systems, in which the Neumann boundary condition is imposed via either soft penalization or hard architectural constraint. We further quantify the discrepancy between the ideal continuous-time dynamics and the discretely sampled exploratory dynamics executed in practice, showing that the error decays as the time grid is refined and exploration noise vanishes. Our experiments on reservoir control problems illustrate the effectiveness of the RL framework, highlighting that boundary-aware methods substantially reduce Neumann boundary residuals and enhance learning stability.

</details>


### [160] [Curvature bounds for the cost of coherence in conditional optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09738)
*Xiaozhen Wang,Xiang Zhou,Zihan Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了方向性动能极小值与联合粒子实现的期望Dirichlet能量之间的差距，建立了曲率下界并证明非零曲率处最小超出量呈r^4阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一族条件概率测度的联合粒子实现中，方向性动能极小值与期望Dirichlet能量之间的差距，并理解其与参数域上曲率的关系。

Method: 结合局部最优传输速度的加权Poisson刻画、精确能量-残差恒等式、弱Stokes公式以及显式传输构造；在正则余面积假设、紧连通纤维和星形参数域下推导曲率下界。

Result: 最小超出量恰好为零当且仅当Lie括号曲率在整个参数域上消失；在非零曲率中心，于半边长r的正方形上（未归一化面积测度）最小超出量为r^4阶。在两相环面电力需求模型数值实验中验证了该四阶规律，径向传输在小正方形上约减半有序构造的超出量。

Conclusion: 方向性动能极小值与期望Dirichlet能量之间的差距由Lie括号曲率刻画，非零曲率导致r^4阶最小超出量，数值实验验证理论并表明径向传输可显著降低超出量。

Abstract: We study the gap between the integrated direction-wise kinetic minima and the minimum expected Dirichlet energy of a joint particle realization of a prescribed two-parameter family of conditional probability measures. Under regular coarea assumptions with compact connected fibres and a star-shaped parameter domain, we derive a curvature lower bound valid for every admissible law of Sobolev maps realizing the prescribed smooth positive conditional measures. Our method combines the weighted-Poisson characterization of local optimal-transport velocities with an exact energy-residual identity, a weak Stokes formula, and explicit transport constructions. The minimum excess vanishes exactly when the Lie-bracket curvature vanishes throughout the parameter domain, whereas at a nonzero-curvature centre it is of order $r^4$ as $r\to0$ on squares of half-side length $r$ with unnormalized area measure. In a two-phase torus model fitted to electricity-demand data, numerical tests illustrate this quartic order for explicit couplings, with radial transport approximately halving the ordered construction's excess on the tested small squares.

</details>


### [161] [Necessary Conditions for Free End-Time Sweeping Process Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09750)
*Piernicola Bettiol,Jérémy Rouot,Paulin Bruneau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究由sweeping process动力学支配的自由终止时间最优控制问题，推导出必要条件。针对法锥项引入的状态不连续性和非Lipschitz性，分别在时间Lipschitz与可测两种情形下给出了横截条件，并结合扰动技术与惩罚方法进行证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优控制理论依赖状态方程的Lipschitz正则性，而sweeping process中法锥项导致状态方程对状态变量不连续，破坏了标准假设，使得轨迹分析与最优性条件构造需采用更精细的工具。作者旨在将自由终止时间最优控制的最优性条件推广到这一非Lipschitz、不连续框架。

Method: 结合适当的扰动技术与针对sweeping process定制的惩罚方法。移动集建模为有限个不等式定义集合的交集，满足约束资格条件，包括正线性无关条件及关联Gramian矩阵的对角占优性质，并引入约束资格的局部版本。分别处理数据在时间上Lipschitz连续与仅可测两种情形。

Result: 在Lipschitz情形下，自由终止时间相关的横截条件用与最大化Hamiltonian几乎处处一致的有界变差函数表示；在可测情形下，通过最大化Hamiltonian的子/超本质值概念获得合适解释，得到强化的横截条件，并包含反映协态弧与最优端点处移动集法锥相互作用的附加项。结果适用于包括多面体等无界移动集的广泛问题类，并通过两个简单例子说明其相关性。

Conclusion: 所得到的最优性系统将近期关于经典自由终止时间问题的结果扩展并精细化到sweeping process的非Lipschitz、不连续框架，凸显了自由终止时间横截条件的重要性。

Abstract: This paper derives necessary optimality conditions for free end-time optimal control problems governed by sweeping process dynamics. In contrast to classical optimal control problems, the presence of the normal cone term in the state equation introduces discontinuities with respect to the state variable and prevents the standard Lipschitz regularity assumptions typically used in optimal control theory. As a result, both the analysis of trajectories and the formulation of optimality conditions require refined tools. We address problems with general endpoint constraints and time-dependent data, considering both the case in which the dynamics are Lipschitz continuous in time and the more challenging setting of merely measurable time dependence. Special attention is devoted to the additional transversality conditions associated with the free end-time variable. In the Lipschitz case, these conditions are expressed in terms of a function of bounded variation that coincides almost everywhere with the maximized Hamiltonian. In the measurable case, we show that a suitable interpretation can be obtained via the notions of sub- and super-essential values of the maximized Hamiltonian, recently introduced in the literature, leading to strengthened transversality conditions. These conditions also involve an additional term reflecting the interaction between the costate arc and the normal cone to the moving set at the optimal endpoints. Our approach combines suitable perturbation techniques with a penalization method tailored to sweeping processes. The moving set is modelled as a finite intersection of inequality-defined sets satisfying suitable constraint qualifications, including a positive linear independence condition and a diagonal dominance property of the associated Gramian matrix. We also introduce a local version of the constraint qualification condition. Our results apply to a broad class of problems, including those involving unbounded moving sets such as polyhedra. The resulting optimality system extends and sharpens recent results for classical free end-time problems to the non-Lipschitz, discontinuous framework of sweeping processes. The relevance of the transversality conditions obtained in this article is illustrated through two simple examples.

</details>


### [162] [Sparsity in control problems: Recent trends and open challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09787)
*Luca Ballotta,Geethu Josephb,Waseem Abbas,Sophie M. Fosson,Chandra R. Murthy,Masaaki Nagahara,Alex Olshevsky,Kyle Poe,René Vidal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于控制领域中稀疏性问题处理方法的综述，统一梳理了执行器调度、最小能量可控性、最大免手动控制与在线凸优化等研究方向，并给出理论、算法与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制工程师始终面临高性能与感知、执行、控制资源有限之间的矛盾，该矛盾常被形式化为带稀疏约束的设计问题。尽管这一问题贯穿众多方法与应用，但控制文献分散于各特定场景，缺乏统一的综述梳理。

Method: 采用综述方式，将多个处理控制稀疏性的研究方向汇聚起来，包括执行器调度、最小能量可控性、最大免手动控制与在线凸优化，并梳理其理论基础与算法，构建连贯的叙述主线。

Result: 建立了关于控制场景中稀疏性问题与解决方法的连贯、结构化的叙述框架，覆盖多个原本分散的研究线索及其理论与算法基础。

Conclusion: 作者讨论了开放问题与未来研究方向，动因来自控制系统日益增长的复杂性及其与普适计算、分布式学习、网络物理安全、生态足迹等关键领域的关联。

Abstract: As in all fields of engineering, control engineers constantly face a dichotomy between achieving high performance and the limited availability of resources for sensing, actuation, and control. This tension often translates into formalizing design problems with sparsity constraints; that is, the design involves decision variables whose number, spatial reach, temporal spread, or other physical expressions are much smaller than in the ideal scenario of unconstrained optimization of a task-related performance metric. Even though this fundamental limitation underlines a plethora of methodological approaches and application domains, the control literature is fragmented into several lines of work tailored to specific scenarios. This survey fills the gap by bringing together several lines of work on handling sparsity in relevant control problems such as actuator scheduling, minimal-energy controllability, maximum hands-off control, and online convex optimization, and weaving a red thread through theoretical foundations and algorithms. Beyond establishing a coherent, structured narrative of problems and solutions for handling sparsity in control scenarios, we discuss open areas and relevant directions for future research, motivated by the growing complexity of control systems and their relations with key domains such as pervasive computing, distributed learning, cyberphysical security, and ecological footprint.

</details>


### [163] [Stochastic Optimal Control of Decoupled Reflected FBSDEs: A Variational Approach with Residual Reflection Terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09867)
*Hanane Ben-Gherbal,Omar Kebiri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文研究带反射约束的解耦正倒向随机微分方程最优控制问题，通过惩罚方法、尖峰变分与对偶论证推导出Pontryagin型反射变分不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受控系统由解耦前向-倒向随机微分方程描述，且倒向分量上带有反射约束；代价泛函通过一个下障碍依赖于前向状态的反射BSDE初值定义。反射项的非光滑性使得经典变分方法无法直接应用，因此需要建立新的必要条件。

Method: 采用惩罚方法、尖峰变分（spike variations）与对偶论证，先导出惩罚变分关系；核心技巧是把反射贡献隔离为一个残差项，从而避免对非光滑惩罚算子求导，同时保留与经典随机最大值原理类似的Hamiltonian伴随结构；最后取极限。

Result: 得到Pontryagin型反射变分不等式形式的必要最优性条件，其中障碍效应通过一个与反射项相关的残差贡献显式表示，并保持了Hamiltonian伴随结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为含反射约束的随机控制问题提供了可用的必要最优性条件框架，克服了反射项非光滑性带来的困难。

Abstract: This paper studies necessary optimality conditions for stochastic control problems governed by decoupled forward--backward stochastic differential equations with reflection constraints on the backward component. The cost functional is defined through the initial value of a reflected BSDE whose lower obstacle depends on the forward state. The nonsmooth nature of the reflection term prevents the direct application of classical variational methods.
  Using a penalization approach, spike variations, and duality arguments, we derive a penalized variational relation. A key feature of our method is to isolate the reflection contribution into a residual term, avoiding the differentiation of the nonsmooth penalization operator while preserving a Hamiltonian adjoint structure analogous to the classical stochastic maximum principle. Passing to the limit yields a reflected variational inequality of Pontryagin type, where the obstacle effect is explicitly represented through a residual contribution associated with the reflection term.

</details>


### [164] [Two Answers on Heavy-Ball Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09912)
*Dmitry Pasechnyuk-Vilensky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究重球法在光滑强凸函数上的收敛性：一是确定Polyak调参下维数一致最坏情形指数率的精确增长因子并给出四族代数分类，得到收敛阈值κ<9+4√5；二是刻画任意步长与动量参数的相图，以特征多项式夹角Θ(ω)判别收敛（Θ(ω)>ω/2）与周期轨道（Θ(2πj/k)≤π/k），剩余区域为由Farey分数组织的旋转数间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重球法在光滑强凸函数上的最坏情形收敛率长期缺乏精确刻画：Polyak调参下的维数一致指数率未确定其精确增长因子与收敛阈值；而在任意步长和动量参数下，Lyapunov区域与周期区域之间的参数平面区域此前未被描述，存在未解决的开区域。

Method: 对Polyak调参，将最坏情形指数率刻画为可插值旋转几何序列混合的最大增长因子，结合精确多项式证书、有理参数化、临界三循环处五次数域上的精确解与验证区间延拓，完成全部条件数下的分类。对任意参数，通过两个极值二次特征多项式夹角Θ(ω)构建相图，用Farey分数组织旋转数间隙，并利用单个严格证书在阈值指数处界定收敛率。

Result: Polyak调参下最优混合构成四个代数族，两个频率即足够，五维中单个函数在每个时域达到该率，上下界相差常数因子，且方法在全类上收敛当且仅当κ<9+4√5。任意参数下，当Θ(ω)>ω/2时收敛，当Θ(2πj/k)≤π/k时存在k周期轨道；第一层间隙中Farey分数扇在共振端点累积并携带多谐波周期轨道，显式扇滞不等式可认证收敛，两类行为均出现在此前未决区域。

Conclusion: 本文精确解决了重球法在光滑强凸函数上的两个核心问题：给出了Polyak调参最坏情形速率的完整代数分类与精确收敛阈值κ<9+4√5，并通过夹角Θ(ω)的相图统一刻画了任意步长与动量下的收敛、周期轨道及Farey间隙结构，同时为此前开放的区域提供了收敛与周期行为的认证方法。

Abstract: We answer two questions about the heavy-ball method on smooth strongly convex functions. The first concerns Polyak's tuning. Its dimension-uniform worst-case exponential rate equals the largest growth factor of an interpolable mixture of rotating geometric sequences, and we determine this factor for every condition number. The optimal mixtures form four algebraic families; the classification combines exact polynomial certificates, a rational parametrization, an exact solution in a quintic number field at the critical three-cycle, and verified interval continuation. Two frequencies suffice, one function in dimension five attains the rate at every horizon, the upper and lower bounds differ by constant factors, and the method converges on the whole class if and only if $κ<9+4\sqrt5$, the threshold located by Badithela and Seiler. The second question concerns arbitrary step sizes and momenta, in particular the regions of the parameter plane between the computed Lyapunov and cycle regions. We describe the phase diagram through the angle $Θ(ω)$ between the characteristic polynomials of the two extreme quadratics. The method converges when $Θ(ω)>ω/2$, has a $k$-periodic orbit when $Θ(2πj/k)\leπ/k$, and the remaining regions are rotation-number gaps organized by Farey fractions. A single strict certificate at one growth exponent bounds the rate, so convergence can be certified at the threshold exponent. In the first level of the gaps, fans of Farey fractions accumulating at a resonant endpoint carry periodic orbits with several harmonics, explicit fan-lag inequalities certify convergence, and both behaviors occur in the regions left open before.

</details>


### [165] [Distances on Finite Constraint Systems and LICQ Radii in Nonlinear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09991)
*Kohei Hatano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个度量框架，用于量化数学规划建模表述中依赖表述性质的鲁棒性，并以LICQ为例给出点态LICQ半径的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学规划表述常通过代数改写、约束函数扰动及约束增删被修改，这些修改或保持可行集不变，或改变优化问题本身。现有研究缺乏统一度量来量化"表述依赖性质"对这类修改的鲁棒性，因此作者希望建立一套系统的度量框架。

Method: 将表述建模为带类型的约束函数集合；在每个固定类型-基数类内，通过最优匹配同类型约束，并在底层函数空间范数下度量匹配后的差异，从而定义表述间距离。作为具体应用，引入点态LICQ半径，即从可行表述到"在指定点仍可行但在该点不满足LICQ"的表述集合的距离；在适当假设下推导该半径的显式公式，并刻画增删单个等式或不等式约束的影响。

Result: 在适当假设下得到了点态LICQ半径的显式公式，并刻画了添加单个等式或不等式约束对该半径的影响。

Conclusion: 该度量框架为分析表述依赖性质（如LICQ）在表述修改下的鲁棒性提供了统一工具，LICQ半径的显式结果为约束增删的定量影响提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Mathematical programming formulations are routinely modified through algebraic reformulations, perturbations of constraint functions, and the addition or removal of constraints. Some of these modifications preserve the feasible set, whereas others reflect newly imposed modeling requirements and therefore change the optimization problem itself. This paper develops a metric framework for quantifying the robustness of formulation-dependent properties. Formulations are modeled as typed collections of constraint functions. Within each fixed typed-cardinality class, their distance is defined by optimally matching constraints of the same type and measuring the resulting discrepancies in a norm on the underlying function space.
  As a concrete demonstration of the framework, we study the Linear Independence Constraint Qualification (LICQ). We introduce the pointwise LICQ radius, defined as the distance from a feasible formulation to the set of formulations that remain feasible at a prescribed point but fail LICQ there. Under suitable assumptions, we derive an explicit formula for this radius and characterize the effect of adding a single equality or inequality constraint.

</details>


### [166] [A Nonlocal Stochastic Optimal Control with Elliptic-Type Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10029)
*Stefana-Lucia Anita,Luca Di Persio*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究一类非局部随机最优控制问题，控制通过椭圆光滑算子 $\mathcal{S}=(I-\Delta)^{-1}$ 作用于扩散过程的分布（等价于受控 Fokker-Planck 方程），证明最优控制存在性并建立 Pontryagin 型最大值原理，得到逐点极小化规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制的非局部性（通过 $\mathcal{S}$ 作用于分布）使经典随机最优控制与 Fokker-Planck 控制理论的标准工具不再直接适用，需要新的存在性论证与变分方法来刻画最优控制。

Method: 以扩散过程的分布（受控 Fokker-Planck 方程，系数依赖 $\mathcal{S}u$）为状态；用变分法中的直接方法证明最优控制存在性；通过 spike 变分并结合算子 $\mathcal{S}$ 的精细性质推导 Pontryagin 型最大值原理。

Result: 证明了最优控制的存在性，并建立了 Pontryagin 型最大值原理，由此给出最优控制的逐点极小化规则。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于具有非局部作用的最优控制问题，并在种群动力学情形下讨论了模型与控制问题的实际含义。

Abstract: We study a nonlocal stochastic optimal control problem in which the control acts through the elliptic smoothing operator $\mathcal{S}=(I-Δ)^{-1}$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$. The state is described by the law of a controlled diffusion, equivalently by a controlled Fokker-Planck equation with coefficients depending on $\mathcal{S}u$. We prove the existence of an optimal control by the direct method in the calculus of variations. We then derive a Pontryagin-type maximum principle by spike variations, relying on sharp properties of the operator $\mathcal{S}$. The result yields a pointwise minimisation rule for the optimal control. We discuss the meaning of the optimal control problem in the case of population dynamics.

</details>


### [167] [Breaking the Odd Cycle: Regularization and First-Order Optimization for the Maximum $s$-Plex Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10205)
*Eleonora Bellesso,Immanuel M. Bomze,Francesco Rinaldi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文基于一种新的正则化连续形式，规避最大s-plex问题的伪解，提出块坐标一阶方法，将标准单纯形上的away-step Frank-Wolfe更新与分数b-匹配多面体上的线性优化相结合，并给出收敛性与有限支撑识别等理论保证，实验验证了正则化有效性和算法高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始三次连续形式在求解最大s-plex问题时存在伪解，导致支撑识别失败，影响解的质量与算法可靠性。因此需要一个能避免伪解的连续形式及配套的高效求解方法。

Method: 提出新的正则化连续形式，并设计定制的块坐标一阶方法：在标准单纯形上执行away-step Frank-Wolfe更新，同时在分数b-匹配多面体上进行线性优化。

Result: 证明了辅助变量的有限稳定化、收敛到驻点，以及合适条件下支撑的有限识别；在标准基准图上的数值实验表明，正则化在实践中避免了原三次形式的支撑识别失败，并能快速找到高质量的s-plex。

Conclusion: 正则化连续形式结合块坐标一阶方法，在理论与实践中均有效解决最大s-plex问题，兼顾收敛保证与计算效率。

Abstract: Building on a new regularized continuous formulation which avoids spurious solutions of the maximum $s$-plex problem, we develop a tailored block-coordinate first-order method that combines away-step Frank-Wolfe updates on the standard simplex with linear optimization over the fractional $b$-matching polytope. We establish finite stabilization of the auxiliary variables, convergence to stationary points, and finite support identification under suitable conditions. Numerical experiments on standard benchmark graphs show that, also in practice, regularization avoids the support-identification failures observed with the original cubic formulation and enables the proposed method to quickly find high-quality $s$-plexes.

</details>


### [168] [First-order $Γ$-expansion for entropic optimal transport with non-degenerate costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10257)
*Luca Nenna,Brendan Pass,Paul Pegon,Louis Tocquec*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将熵正则化最优传输（EOT）重写为关于Gibbs测度的相对熵最小化问题，利用Laplace方法的推广证明了Gibbs测度的收敛性，并在非退化ground cost下给出了EOT关于正则参数ε消失时的一阶Γ-展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EOT在理论与计算（Sinkhorn算法）中都很重要，但其正则参数ε趋于零时的渐近行为（一阶展开）尚待更一般的刻画，已有结果仅覆盖较窄的ground cost类别。

Method: 把EOT改写为相对于与对偶间隙能量相关的Gibbs测度的相对熵最小化；采用Laplace方法的温和推广，证明Gibbs测度的收敛性；在对偶间隙、接触集与边缘分布施加适当假设下，针对非退化ground cost推导一阶Γ-展开。

Result: 证明了Gibbs测度的收敛，并给出非退化ground cost下EOT的一阶Γ-展开，同时得到熵选择原理以及EOT代价精确到o(ε)的展开。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了已有工作，统一刻画了EOT的一阶渐近行为，为熵选择原理提供了框架。

Abstract: Entropy-regularized optimal transport (EOT) has become a key tool in optimal transport, providing both theoretical insight and efficient computation via the Sinkhorn algorithm. We study the first-order \(Γ\)-expansion of EOT as the regularization parameter \(\varepsilon\) vanishes, by rewriting EOT as the minimization of a relative entropy with respect to a Gibbs measure \(\gibbs\) associated with the duality gap energy. Using a mild extension of the Laplace method, we prove the convergence of the Gibbs measures and derive this \(Γ\)-expansion for non-degenerate ground costs, under suitable assumptions on the duality gap, the contact set and the marginals. This yields at the same time an entropic selection principle and an expansion of the EOT cost up to \(o(\varepsilon)\) for this class of ground costs, generalizing existing results.

</details>


### [169] [Sharp Initialization Guarantees for the Heavy-Ball Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10369)
*Kang Liu,Quanen Wu,Zhan Sun,Mengxiao Chen,Suyan Li,Bohao Qu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究重球法(heavy-ball)的初始化问题：刻画了仅依赖曲率与正则性界时可保证收敛的初始化邻域，该邻域随动量趋近1而收缩；对Lipschitz连续Hessian，其半径与阻尼同阶。上下界匹配表明该初始化要求无法在函数类上一致放松。作者提出基于当前梯度的启动规则——有限步梯度下降预热进入保证区域后再切换重球法，在强凸二次型最优参数下，后续迭代满足加速的局部渐近收敛率界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重球法在极小点附近能加速收敛，但在一般强凸目标上可能进入持续振荡；如何在初始化阶段可靠地实现这一局部加速优势尚不明确。现有仅依赖曲率与光滑性界的结果缺乏对可保证收敛初始化邻域的精确刻画，因此需要研究该邻域的大小、其随动量的变化规律，并设计可行的启动策略。

Method: 1) 定义并刻画"保证收敛初始化邻域"：在仅使用曲率界与正则性(Hessian光滑性)界的条件下给出收敛保证，分析该邻域半径与动量、阻尼的关系；2) 针对Lipschitz连续Hessian证明半径与阻尼同阶，并通过匹配的上下界证明该初始化要求无法在函数类上一致放松；3) 提出基于当前梯度的启动规则：先用有限步梯度下降预热，使迭代进入保证区域，再切换至重球法；4) 采用对强凸二次型最优的参数，分析切换后迭代的局部渐近收敛率。

Result: 保证收敛的初始化邻域随动量趋于1而收缩；对Lipschitz连续Hessian，其半径与阻尼同阶。匹配的上下界说明初始化要求无法在函数类上一致放松。基于梯度的有限步梯度下降预热可进入保证区域，随后切换重球法，在强凸二次型最优参数下后续迭代满足加速的局部渐近速率界。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 重球法的局部加速优势依赖于足够好的初始化；本文给出了可保证收敛邻域的定量刻画及其不可放松性，并提供有限步梯度下降预热再切换的实用启动规则，从而在强凸二次型上实现加速的局部渐近收敛率。

Abstract: The heavy-ball method can accelerate convergence near the minimizer, but it may also enter persistent oscillations on general strongly convex objectives. How should it be initialized to reliably realize this local advantage? We study the initialization neighborhood in which convergence can be guaranteed using only curvature and regularity bounds. This neighborhood shrinks as momentum approaches one; for Lipschitz-continuous Hessians, its radius is of the same order as damping. Matching upper and lower bounds show that this initialization requirement cannot be relaxed uniformly over the function class. We then derive a startup rule based on the current gradient: a finite gradient-descent warm-up enters the guaranteed region before switching to heavy-ball. With parameters optimal for strongly convex quadratics, the subsequent iterates satisfy an accelerated local asymptotic rate bound. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/HeavyBall-811D/.

</details>


### [170] [An Optimal Second-Order Algorithm for Finite-Sum Optimization under Average Smoothness and Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10495)
*Artem Tsedenov,Dmitry Kovalev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Hessian满足均方Lipschitz的有限和凸优化问题，提出达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n + n^{6/7}/\epsilon^{2/7})$二阶oracle复杂度的随机算法，并证明在随机二阶线性张成假设下该复杂度为最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限和凸优化问题中，二阶随机算法的计算复杂度是否为最优尚属公开问题，本文旨在建立该问题类的二阶oracle最优复杂度上下界。

Method: 在目标函数凸、n个分量函数二阶连续可微且Hessian满足均方Lipschitz的条件下：(1) 设计随机二阶算法，达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n + n^{6/7}/\epsilon^{2/7})$的期望二阶oracle调用数；(2) 在随机二阶版本的线性张成假设下，构造下界证明，导出$\Omega(n + n^{6/7}/\epsilon^{2/7})$的复杂度下界。

Result: 所提算法的上界复杂度与所证下界在忽略对数因子意义下相匹配，均为$\Theta(n + n^{6/7}/\epsilon^{2/7})$量级。

Conclusion: 上下复杂度界的匹配解决了该类有限和凸问题最优二阶随机复杂度的公开问题，确立了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n + n^{6/7}/\epsilon^{2/7})$的紧凑复杂度刻画。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a finite-sum optimization problem, where the objective function is convex, the $n$ component functions are twice continuously differentiable and their Hessians are mean-square Lipschitz. Our contribution is twofold. First, we develop a stochastic algorithm, which requires $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n + n^{6/7}/ε^{2/7})$ second-order oracle calls in expectation to find an approximate solution to the problem with the expected accuracy $ε$. Second, we establish a lower complexity bound of $Ω(n + n^{6/7}/ε^{2/7})$ for any stochastic second-order algorithm satisfying a stochastic second-order version of the first-order linear span assumption, which is widely adopted in the optimization literature. Our lower and upper complexity bounds match up to logarithmic factors and thus resolve an important open question of establishing the optimal complexity of this problem class.

</details>


### [171] [Decentralized SGD under Heavy-Tailed Noise: Optimal Convergence Rates and the Role of Gradient Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10527)
*Aleksandar Armacki,Haoyuan Cai,Ali H. Sayed*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明在重尾噪声下，采用裁剪（clipping）的去中心化SGD（DSGD）能达到阶最优收敛率，并实现智能体数量的线性加速，同时揭示裁剪相比归一化在去中心化场景中的关键优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾噪声在机器学习中普遍存在，梯度裁剪与归一化在集中式设置下已有较好理解，但在去中心化设置下，局部梯度上的非线性同时影响优化与一致性，认识不足。已有工作显示裁剪率次优、归一化需局部动量或小批量才能收敛，因此本文追问：使用非线性的基线去中心化方法能否在重尾噪声下达到最优收敛率。

Method: 针对光滑非凸代价函数，在有限p阶矩噪声（p∈(1,2]）下提出并分析裁剪去中心化SGD（clipped DSGD）。关键技术是对一致性间隙（consensus gap）进行精细分析，利用裁剪的结构特性，将网络效应归入高阶项。

Result: clipped DSGD同时在高概率和期望意义下达到阶最优收敛率，并获得智能体数量的线性加速（此前裁剪类去中心化方法未见此类结果）；数值实验验证了理论。

Conclusion: 裁剪与归一化在去中心化设置中存在重要区别：归一化DSGD可能不收敛，而裁剪保留了幅度信息，使DSGD收敛且阶最优。这为去中心化重尾优化提供了简洁有效的基线方法。

Abstract: Heavy-tailed noise has been widely observed in modern machine learning, motivating the use of methods like gradient clipping and normalization. While these methods are well understood in centralized settings, much less is known in decentralized ones, where applying a nonlinearity to local gradients affects both optimization and consensus. Recent works on decentralized non-convex optimization have studied both clipping and normalization under heavy-tailed noise, with clipping yielding suboptimal rates and normalization needing local momentum or mini-batches to converge. This raises the question: can a baseline decentralized method using a nonlinearity achieve optimal convergence rates under heavy-tailed noise? We answer affirmatively with clipped decentralized SGD ($\mathtt{DSGD}$). For smooth non-convex costs under bounded $p$-th moment noise, $p \in (1,2]$, we show that clipped $\mathtt{DSGD}$ achieves order-optimal rates both with high probability and in expectation. Moreover, we establish a linear speed-up in the number of agents, which, to our knowledge, has not been shown for decentralized methods with clipping. The key technical ingredient is a sharp analysis of the consensus gap that exploits the structure of clipping, relegating network effects to higher-order terms. Our results highlight an important distinction between clipping and normalization in decentralized settings: while normalized $\mathtt{DSGD}$ can fail to converge, clipping retains magnitude information, enabling $\mathtt{DSGD}$ to be convergent and order-optimal. Numerical experiments validate our theory.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Zero Mode Operators and Charge-Conjugation Defects in Non-Abelian Fractional Quantum Hall-Superconductor Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08907)
*Junyi Cao,Eduardo Fradkin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究分数量子霍尔-超导异质结中束缚于电荷共轭缺陷的零模，推广了体-边构造方法至非阿贝尔母拓扑序情形，导出了广义零模代数并给出多种FQH-SC体系的零模结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: G-交叉编织张量范畴虽能描述FQH-SC异质结中的电荷共轭缺陷，但其零模的局域算符描述一般未知，缺乏将体威尔逊算符映射到一维局域零模算符的一般框架。

Method: 将局域零模算符的体-边构造推广到具有非阿贝尔母拓扑序的FQH-SC，把折叠理论中的体威尔逊算符映射为一维FQH-SC理论中的局域零模算符，区分零模任意子与作用于缺陷希尔伯特空间的局域零模算符，并建立广义零模代数。

Result: 区分了隧穿中的融合道依赖：零模算符隧穿探测体单绕（monodromy），而直接零模任意子转移探测G-交叉结合数据；对填充ν=1/m的费米子Laughlin FQH-SC恢复Z_{2m} parafermion零模结构，对广义Moore-Read态得到Z_2^{(n)}×Z_N结构（费米子N=2m，玻色子N=m），对ν=3/(3M+2)的k=3 Read-Rezayi态得到Fib×Z_N结构（费米子N=2(3M+2)，玻色子N=(3M+2)/2）。

Conclusion: 在Read-Rezayi情形下，零模算符隧穿与直接零模任意子转移给出中性融合道间的不同普适振幅比，可作为底层Fibonacci缺陷结构的特征信号。

Abstract: We study zero modes bound to charge-conjugation defects in fractional quantum Hall-superconductor (FQH-SC) heterostructures built from Abelian and non-Abelian FQH states. Although the associated defects are well described by $G$-crossed braided tensor categories, a local operator description of their zero modes is not generally known. We generalize the bulk-to-boundary construction of localized zero mode operators to FQH-SCs with non-Abelian parent topological orders, mapping bulk Wilson operators in the folded theory to localized zero mode operators in the one-dimensional FQH-SC theory. This construction distinguishes zero mode anyons from the local zero mode operators that act on the defect Hilbert space and yields a generalized zero mode algebra. It also determines the fusion-channel dependence of tunneling: tunneling of zero mode operators probes bulk monodromy, while direct zero mode anyon transfer probes $G$-crossed associativity data. Applying this framework, we recover the $\mathbb{Z}_{2m}$ parafermion zero mode structure of fermionic Laughlin FQH-SCs at filling $ν=1/m$, obtain a $\mathbb{Z}_2^{(n)}\times\mathbb{Z}_N$ structure for generalized Moore-Read states at filling $ν=1/m$, with $N=2m$ for fermionic states and $N=m$ for bosonic states, and find a $\mathrm{Fib}\times\mathbb{Z}_N$ structure for the $k=3$ Read-Rezayi state at filling $ν=3/(3M+2)$, with $N=2(3M+2)$ for fermionic states and $N=(3M+2)/2$ for bosonic states. In the Read-Rezayi case, zero mode operator tunneling and direct zero mode anyon transfer yield distinct universal amplitude ratios between the neutral fusion channels, providing a characteristic signature of the underlying Fibonacci defect structure.

</details>


### [173] [Exact solutions for kinematics across interaction-deformed Fermi surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08921)
*Alexander Kruchkov,F. Duncan M. Haldane*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文推导了相互作用开启且固定密度调谐时，相互作用费米面内部占据亏损与外部占据盈余的精确变化率，将其分解为形变费米面扫过的权重与固定动量下Luttinger被积函数的变化，并证明表面项之差等于非相干权重1−Z_k的流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Luttinger定理只固定费米液体费米海体积，却不能说明相互作用如何在其表面附近重新分配占据。作者希望精确刻画这种占据重分布。

Method: 在固定密度下，将相互作用缓慢开启并调谐，利用Luttinger积分表示与费米面形变分析，推导占据亏损和盈余对相互作用参数的变化率，并区分表面贡献与固定动量贡献。

Result: 占据亏损与盈余的变化率各由两部分组成：一是形变费米面扫过的权重，二是固定动量下Luttinger被积函数的变化；两者中表面项之差为1−Z_k的非相干权重流量。

Conclusion: 这些恒等式在Luttinger定理成立或不成立时均适用，因此超出仅依赖Luttinger定理的体积约束，给出了费米面附近占据重分布的精确关系。

Abstract: Luttinger's theorem fixes the volume of the Fermi sea of a Fermi liquid, but not how interactions redistribute occupation around its surface. For an interaction switched on and tuned at fixed density, we derive the exact rates of change of the occupation deficit inside the interacting Fermi surface and of the surplus outside it. Each is the weight swept by the deforming surface plus a change of Luttinger's integrand at fixed momentum; the difference of the surface terms is flux of incoherent weight $1 - Z_{\vec k}$. These identities hold with or without the Luttinger theorem.

</details>


### [174] [From continuum neural quantum states to effective lattice models: Fluctuating Wigner molecules in moiré superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08955)
*Ahmed Khalifa,Shubhayu Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"波函数读出"方法，将连续空间神经量子态(NQS)映射为格点分辨的电荷与自旋统计量，从而在微观解中直接识别有效自由度。应用于ν=3的莫尔过渡金属硫族化合物，发现低自旋基态，并揭示电荷与自旋涨落相互纠缠，导出自旋双轨道Hubbard模型的有效描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料的有效描述依赖能量尺度分离以确定低能自由度。但当动能、晶格势与相互作用相互竞争时，这一层级被打破，微观状态及其有效描述均未知。连续NQS可绕过预设能带、轨道或自旋直接求解基态，但其微观波函数难以用有效变量解释，因此需要一种从连续波函数提取有效自由度的途径。

Method: 引入一种波函数读出(wavefunction readout)技术，将连续NQS映射到格点分辨的电荷与自旋统计量，使有效自由度从微观解中自然涌现。以填充ν=3的莫尔过渡金属硫族化合物为研究对象，通过该读出方法分析其基态电荷密度、doublon-hole对、分子电荷态的电子结构与自旋关联。

Result: 在ν=3莫尔TMD中，NQS揭示出位于自旋极化Wigner分子铁磁晶体之下的低自旋基态。电荷密度保持三角分子构型，但读出显示存在显著的电荷涨落，源于束缚的最近邻doublon-hole对。每个分子电荷态具有惰性的自旋单态核心与承载自旋的rim电子，因而电荷与自旋涨落相互纠缠，格点间呈反铁磁自旋关联。

Conclusion: 结果导出一个自旋双轨道Hubbard模型的有效描述。更一般地，本文展示了当竞争尺度使受控归约无法进行时，从连续波函数通往有效格点模型的一条路径。

Abstract: Effective descriptions of quantum materials rely on a separation of energy scales that identifies their low-energy degrees of freedom. When kinetic energy, lattice potential, and interactions compete, this hierarchy breaks down, and both the microscopic state and its effective description are unknown. Continuum neural quantum states (NQS) can find the ground state directly without projecting onto prescribed bands, orbitals, or spins, but their microscopic wavefunctions are difficult to interpret in terms of effective variables. Here, we introduce a wavefunction readout that maps the continuum NQS onto site-resolved charge and spin statistics, allowing effective degrees of freedom to emerge from the microscopic solution. Focusing on moiré transition metal dichalcogenides at filling $ν= 3$, the NQS reveals a low-spin ground state below the ferromagnetic crystal of spin-polarized Wigner molecules. Although the charge density retains triangular molecular motifs, our readout reveals significant charge fluctuations through bound nearest-neighbor doublon--hole pairs. Each molecular charge state has an inert spin-singlet core and rim electrons that carry the spin. Charge and spin fluctuations are therefore intertwined, with antiferromagnetic spin-correlations between sites, motivating an effective description by a spinful two-orbital Hubbard model. More generally, our results demonstrate a route from continuum wavefunctions to effective lattice models when competing scales preclude a controlled reduction.

</details>


### [175] [Ambipolar metal-insulator transition in bilayer MoSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09036)
*Ha-Leem Kim,Hyungbin Lim,Terry Wu,Amogh Y. Waghmare,Garima Gupta,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Archana Raja,Eric Y. Ma,Feng Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用无接触射频反射测量技术，在双层MoSe₂器件中同时观测到电子与空穴的金属-绝缘体转变，证明其由库仑相互作用主导，并揭示了维格纳固体在磁场下的Pomeranchuk式扩张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当库仑相互作用主导动能时，稀薄二维载流子形成维格纳固体，其熔化与无序驱动的局域化均可导致密度调控的金属-绝缘体转变，但二者相对作用长期存在争议。原子级薄半导体可同时用输运与扫描隧道显微镜探测同一材料，然而在关联最强处接触质量差，阻碍了输运测量。

Method: 引入无接触射频反射测量（RF reflectometry），通过电容栅耦合感测方块电阻，从而在接触受限体系中实现定量输运测量。在同一样品上对比电子与空穴转变（二者共享无序但质量不同），并结合扫描隧道显微镜与磁阻测量。

Result: 在同一双层MoSe₂器件中分辨出电子转变以及此前无法探测的空穴转变；两者临界密度相差四倍，但对应相近的库仑能与动能之比，隧道显微镜在该处观测到固-液共存。磁阻在每个转变处出现尖锐峰值，反映维格纳固体在外场下对关联液体的Pomeranchuk式扩张。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了高质量二维半导体中的相互作用驱动转变，并将无接触射频反射测量确立为在接触受限体系中开展定量输运研究的有效手段。

Abstract: When Coulomb interactions dominate kinetic energy, dilute two-dimensional carriers form a Wigner solid. Both its melting and disorder-driven localization can drive density-tuned metal-insulator transitions, but their relative roles and interplay have been debated for decades. Atomically thin semiconductors offer a new test because transport and scanning tunneling microscopy can probe the same material, yet poor contacts impede transport where correlations are strongest. Here we introduce contact-free radio-frequency reflectometry that senses sheet resistance through capacitive gate coupling. It resolves the electron and previously inaccessible hole transitions in the same bilayer MoSe2 device, whose carriers share disorder but not mass. Their critical densities differ fourfold yet correspond to comparable Coulomb-to-kinetic-energy ratios, where tunneling microscopy finds solid-liquid coexistence. Magnetoresistance peaks sharply at each transition, reflecting a Pomeranchuk-like, field-driven expansion of the Wigner solid against the correlated liquid. Our results demonstrate interaction-driven transitions in high-quality two-dimensional semiconductors, opening quantitative transport in contact-limited systems.

</details>


### [176] [Multiorbital Anisotropy and Magnetic Excitations in Trilayer Nickelate Pr$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09210)
*Ziqiang Guan,Sophia F. R. TenHuisen,Xuefei Guo,Wei He,Aulden K. Jones,Marli dos Reis Cantarino,Xinglong Chen,Hong Zheng,J. F. Mitchell,Nicholas B. Brookes,Mark P. M. Dean,Matteo Mitrano*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合XAS与RIXS解析Pr₄Ni₃O₁₀的多轨道电子结构与磁激发，发现层间交换耦合显著且对稀土替代稳健，多层结构而非稀土替代是调控磁性相互作用的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土替代会强烈改变RP镍酸盐的结构、金属性和超导行为，但其对磁性相互作用的影响尚不明确；需要分辨多轨道电子结构与磁激发来厘清稀土替代与结构层数各自的角色。

Method: 采用X射线吸收谱(XAS)与共振非弹性X射线散射(RIXS)，结合Ni L₃、Ni L边与O K边的偏振依赖测量，区分面内与面外的Ni-O杂化通道，并进行动量分辨RIXS与有效自旋波分析。

Result: 无重叠的稀土共振使Ni L₃边可直接测量；偏振依赖测量分辨出不同的面内与面外Ni-O杂化通道；动量分辨RIXS显示磁激发有色散并向非公度自旋密度波波矢软化；有效自旋波分析给出可观的层内（三层内）交换耦合，与主导面内相互作用相当，且对稀土替代稳健。

Conclusion: 多层结构（而非单一稀土替代）是这些材料中磁性相互作用的关键调控参量。

Abstract: Rare-earth substitution strongly modifies the structure, metallicity, and superconducting behavior of Ruddlesden-Popper (RP) nickelates, but its impact on their magnetic interactions remains unclear. Here, we combine x-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS) and resonant inelastic x-ray scattering (RIXS) to resolve the multiorbital electronic structure and magnetic excitations of Pr$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$. The absence of an overlapping rare-earth resonance provides direct access to the Ni $L_3$ edge, while polarization-dependent measurements at the Ni $L$- and O $K$-edges resolve distinct planar and out-of-plane Ni-O hybridization channels. Momentum-resolved RIXS reveals dispersive magnetic excitations that soften toward the incommensurate spin-density-wave wave vector. An effective spin-wave analysis yields a sizable intra-trilayer exchange coupling, comparable to the leading in-plane interactions and robust against rare-earth substitution. Our results identify the multilayer architecture, rather than rare-earth substitution alone, as a key control knob of the magnetic interactions in these materials.

</details>


### [177] [Polarized neutron diffraction with ex-situ $^3$He neutron spin filter and two-dimensional detector for noncollinear incommensurate magnetic order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09345)
*Shingo Takahashi,Yuta Osawa,Ryuju Kobayashi,Takashi Ino,Hiraku Saito,Takuro Kawasaki,Takayuki Oku,Tatsuya Nakamura,Yoichi Ikeda,Masaki Fujita,Daisuke Koto,Yusuke Tokunaga,Taka-hisa Arima,Taro Nakajima*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文开发了将³He中子自旋过滤器与二维探测器相结合的实验装置及数据处理流程，实现对水平散射面之外的中子偏振分析，并在多铁材料Mn₃WO₆上成功观测到面外非公度磁反射的偏振依赖强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统极化中子散射多采用铁磁单晶单色器与分析器的三轴谱仪，仅适用于水平散射面内的逐点测量。而多铁体、磁斯格明子及拓扑非平庸磁序等新兴研究对象常具有沿倒空间多个方向延伸的非公度磁调制矢量，水平面内测量无法捕获，因而亟需突破该几何限制的偏振分析手段。

Method: 搭建了³He中子自旋过滤器（³He-NSF）与二维探测器相结合的实验装置，可在水平散射面之外分析散射中子偏振；同时建立了数据处理流程，将二维强度图转换为倒空间三维强度分布，并对³He极化不完善效应进行修正。将该方法应用于多铁材料Mn₃WO₆的单晶衍射测量。

Result: 成功观测到面外非公度磁反射的偏振依赖强度，验证了该方法在分析复杂磁结构方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该³He-NSF结合二维探测器的方案突破了传统三轴谱仪的水平面测量限制，配合考虑³He极化修正的二维到三维数据转换流程，可有效用于复杂非公度磁序的极化中子散射研究，具有推广价值。

Abstract: Polarized neutron scattering has been widely used for studying magnetic orders in condensed matter. A typical setup for this technique is a triple-axis spectrometer with a ferromagnetic single-crystal monochromator and analyzer, which is suited for point-by-point measurements within the horizontal scattering plane. However, this setup cannot fully meet the demands of recent studies on complex magnetic orders with emergent cross-correlated phenomena. For instance, multiferroics, magnetic skyrmions, and other topologically nontrivial magnetic orders tend to have incommensurate magnetic modulation vectors running along various directions in reciprocal space, which cannot be captured by measurements restricted to the horizontal scattering plane. To overcome these limitations, we have developed an experimental setup combining a $^3$He neutron spin filter ($^3$He-NSF) and a two-dimensional (2D) detector, which enables us to analyze the polarization of neutrons scattered away from the horizontal plane. We also establish a data reduction procedure to convert the 2D intensity maps into three-dimensional intensity distributions in reciprocal space, correcting for the effect of imperfect $^3$He polarization. This method is applied to single-crystal diffraction measurements on the multiferroic material Mn$_3$WO$_6$, which exhibits complex incommensurate magnetic orders. We have successfully observed polarization-dependent intensities of out-of-plane incommensurate magnetic reflections, demonstrating the effectiveness of the present method for analyzing complex magnetic structures.

</details>


### [178] [Inhomogeneous cluster DMFT study on p-n junction Mott gap closing in Hubbard lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09453)
*Florian Brette,Eunjae Jeong,Hongchul Choi,Ji Hoon Shim,Geunsik Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过动力学平均场理论（DMFT）研究方形晶格Hubbard模型中p-n结（由空穴掺杂与电子掺杂畴构成）在外加电势梯度下的Mott能隙闭合行为，比较电势降落沿方形边与对角方向两种情形。发现电势降与开放2×2簇基态—第一激发态能量差相当时金属相优先，临界激发能约0.35t（U=5.8t），且方向依赖性微弱；但对角方向下零能相干峰更强，源于两个分子轨道简并保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上已观测到Mott能隙闭合阈值，但外场（电势梯度）驱动的绝缘体—金属转变机制尚不清楚，尤其是电势降落方向（沿方形边或对角）对关联效应的影响缺乏理论刻画。作者希望借助非均匀DMFT框架，在p-n结几何下澄清Mott能隙闭合的临界条件与方向依赖性，从而与实验阈值对照。

Method: 在方形晶格Hubbard模型上构建由空穴掺杂畴和电子掺杂畴组成的p-n结，界面处局域在位势平滑变化。采用动力学平均场理论（DMFT）处理非均匀关联效应，将界面区域用2×2面砖状（plaquette）杂质簇描述，分别考虑电势沿方形边和沿对角方向两种降落情形。以开放2×2簇的基态与第一激发态能量本征值之差作为临界尺度，考察电势降与该能量差相当时的相变行为，并分析关联金属相零能相干峰的强度与分子轨道简并/劈裂的关系。

Result: 当电势降可与开放2×2簇的基态与第一激发态能量本征值差相比拟时，金属相相对于Mott绝缘体被优先选择。在相同电场强度下，激发能几乎不依赖电势降落方向，U=5.8t时约为0.35t（t为跃迁参数）。绝缘体—金属转变后，关联金属在零能处呈现相干峰；沿方形对角降落时峰值强度大于沿边降落。主要原因是前者保持两个分子轨道的简并，而后者能级发生劈裂。结果与实验测量的Mott能隙闭合阈值进行了对照讨论。

Conclusion: 外加电势梯度可诱导Mott能隙闭合，临界条件由2×2 plaquette的基态—第一激发态能量差标定（约0.35t，U=5.8t），且对电势降落方向不敏感；但相干峰的强弱强烈依赖方向，对角情形因分子轨道简并保持而更强。该机制为理解实验中Mott能隙闭合阈值提供了理论依据，并凸显几何/轨道对称性在电场调控关联绝缘体相变中的关键作用。

Abstract: Mott gap closing induced by an external potential gradient is studied by considering the Hubbard model on a square lattice. Our model consists of hole- and electron-doped domains forming the p-n junction where the on-site potential varies smoothly across the interface. The effect of the potential drop direction, either along square edge or diagonal, is studied by describing the correlation effects of inhomogeneous 2x2 plaquette impurity clusters within a dynamical mean-field theory framework. It is found that the metallic phase is favored over the Mott insulators when the potential drop becomes comparable to the energy eigenvalue difference of an open 2x2 plaquette between the ground state and the first excited state. Given the same associated electric-field strength, the excitation energy is almost independent of the drop direction, which amounts ~0.35t at U=5.8t with the hopping parameter t. Upon insulator-to-metal transition, the correlated metal exhibits a coherent peak at zero energy whose intensity is greater for the drop direction along the square diagonal than along the edge. The main origin is attributed to the preserved degeneracy of two molecular orbitals for the former case, while it undergoes splitting in the energy levels for the latter. Our result is discussed regarding the experimentally measured Mott gap closing thresholds.

</details>


### [179] [Unified treatment of local dynamical interactions in correlated metals using Eliashberg theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09656)
*Jan Berges,Samuel Poncé,Mario Caserta,Nicola Marzari,Tommaso Chiarotti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作揭示GW自能与Fan-Migdal自能在拓扑上是同一费曼图，可用单一有效相互作用统一处理电子-电子与电子-声子相互作用，进而导出正常态Eliashberg方程（作为Hedin-Baym方程的子集）。在局域近似下求解，应用于Sr$_2$RuO$_4$，准粒子能带重整化与实验及动力学平均场理论定性一致，但低能线宽与电阻率被低估，提示需引入顶点修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联材料的从头计算本就因电子关联而理论复杂、代价高昂，若再考虑晶格自由度则难度进一步增加。现有方法往往割裂处理电子-电子相互作用与电子-声子相互作用，缺乏对二者一致、统一的第一性原理框架，因此需要一个可扩展的动力学方案将电子激发与振动激发置于同等地位处理。

Method: 关键观察是GW自能与Fan-Migdal自能拓扑上为同一费曼图，故可用二者之和构成的单一有效相互作用统一描述直接与声子介导的电子-电子相互作用。由电子格林函数的Dyson方程得到正常态Eliashberg方程，其可视为耦合电子-声子Hedin-Baym方程的子集。在局域近似下求解该动力学方程，从头计算温度依赖的准粒子谱，重点关注决定输运的低能窗口，将此"扩展动力学Hubbard方法"应用于关联金属Sr$_2$RuO$_4$，并用气泡近似下的Green-Kubo形式计算电阻率。

Result: 计算得到的准粒子能带重整化与实验及动力学平均场理论结果定性一致；但低能线宽及对应的电阻率在气泡近似下被低估，表明需要引入顶点修正。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，简洁的动力学表述可为研究关联材料提供可扩展的框架，将电子激发与振动激发一致地、平等地处理；同时指出低能输运量的精确描述仍需顶点修正。

Abstract: First-principles studies of materials with strong electronic correlations introduce additional theoretical complexities and computational costs, even more so if lattice degrees of freedom also play a role. To address these challenges, we show how direct and phonon-mediated electron-electron interactions can be treated consistently. The key observation is that the GW and Fan-Migdal self-energies are topologically the same diagram, so that they can be unified using a single effective interaction, which is the sum of the two interactions. The Dyson equation for the electron Green function then yields normal-state Eliashberg equations, which can be viewed as a subset of the Hedin-Baym equations for coupled electrons and phonons [Phys. Rev. X 13, 031026 (2023)]. We solve these dynamical equations in a local approximation to calculate from first principles the temperature-dependent quasiparticle spectrum, with close attention to the low-energy window determining transport. We apply this extended dynamical Hubbard approach to the correlated metal Sr$_2$RuO$_4$, which has long served as a benchmark system for advanced electronic-structure methods. The resulting renormalization of quasiparticle bands is in qualitative agreement with experiments and results from dynamical mean-field theory. However, here the low-energy linewidth and associated resistivity within the Green-Kubo formalism in the bubble approximation are underestimated, suggesting the need for vertex corrections. This work highlights how simple dynamical formulations can provide an extensible framework for studying correlated materials, which treats electronic and vibrational excitations on equal footing.

</details>


### [180] [Efficient Optimization of Tensor Rings with Low-Rank Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10027)
*Matthieu Jeannin,Alessandro Chessari,Jan Von Delft*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出高效的双位点 Ring-DMRG 算法，通过规范变换与广义 Davidson 求解器提升数值稳定性，将周期性张量环环境的截断在可分离时化为普通 SVD、不可分离时用交替优化保留完整环境，并论证临界系统中环境秩不随系统尺寸增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量环（TR）分解天然适合表示周期系统，但闭合几何使其缺乏全局正则形式，导致环境计算昂贵且病态，优化困难。已有周期 DMRG 通过将长环境压缩为低秩表示来缓解该问题，本文希望在此基础上进一步扩展出高效且数值稳健的双位点算法。

Method: 将周期 DMRG 的长环境压缩为低秩表示；扩展为双位点 Ring-DMRG 算法；通过适当的规范变换和广义 Davidson 求解器提升数值稳健性；针对双位点公式中的截断步骤，在环境充分可分离时用合适的坐标系变换将其归结为普通 SVD，在不可分离时改用保留完整环境的交替优化。

Result: 双位点公式的局部操作降至 O(pχ^3)（p 为保留的环境秩）；临界系统中发散关联长度使长程观测量在热力学极限下仍对周期几何敏感，此时达到固定观测精度所需的环境秩不随系统尺寸增长；Ring-DMRG 与标准 DMRG 保持相同的对键维度的三次标度，但所需键维度显著更小；低秩环境构造给出信念传播（BP）的系统性推广，标准 BP 在秩一极限下被恢复。

Conclusion: 低秩环境压缩是优化周期张量环表示的有效途径：双位点 Ring-DMRG 在保持立方标度的同时显著降低所需键维度，并在临界体系中具备尺寸无关的环境秩；该方法还统一并推广了信念传播框架。

Abstract: Tensor-ring (TR) decompositions provide a natural representation of periodic systems but are difficult to optimize because the closed geometry prevents a global canonical form and leads to costly, ill conditioned environments. Existing periodic DMRG methods alleviate this difficulty by compressing long environments to a low-rank representation, reducing local operations to $\mathcal{O}(pχ^3)$, where $p$ is the retained environment rank. Here, we extend this approach to an efficient two-site Ring-DMRG algorithm and improve its numerical robustness through appropriate gauge transformations and a generalized Davidson solver. A central challenge in the two-site formulation is the truncation step, which must account for the surrounding environment. When this environment is sufficiently separable, a suitable change of frame reduces the truncation to an ordinary SVD. When it is not separable, we instead use an alternating optimization that retains the full environment.
  The computational advantage of tensor rings over tensor trains depends on the scaling of the required environment rank $p$ with bond dimension and system size. For critical systems, the divergent correlation length makes long-range observables remain sensitive to the periodic geometry as the thermodynamic limit is approached. In this regime, the environment rank required to reach a fixed accuracy in the observable does not grow with the system size. Ring-DMRG therefore retains the same cubic scaling with bond dimension as standard DMRG, but at a substantially smaller bond dimension. The low-rank environment construction also provides a systematic generalization of belief propagation (BP), with standard BP recovered in the rank-one limit.

</details>


### [181] [Fourth-order perturbation theory for the Frohlich polaron: Analytic structure of the weak-coupling series and the crossover to strong coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10030)
*Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文计算了三维Fröhlich极化子基态能量与有效质量的Rayleigh-Schrödinger微扰级数至耦合常数α的四阶，给出三、四阶有效质量系数（新结果）。利用比值法与Padé方法分析该收敛级数，发现系数具有显著规律性，可由α≈8.5处的幂律奇异性（指数约1.4）描述；弱耦合Padé近似在α≤5范围内与图示蒙特卡罗结果吻合优于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fröhlich极化子问题中，传统微扰级数在α≈6处失效，但该级数实际具有非零收敛半径。作者希望刻画这一收敛结构，澄清弱耦合展开的适用范围，并确定其向强耦合过渡的交叉点位置。

Method: 将Fröhlich相互作用视为固定总动量下孤立非简并基态的形状有界微扰，从而保证级数具有非零收敛半径；计算至四阶的基态能量与有效质量系数；采用适用于收敛级数的比值法与Padé近似进行分析，并构造仅基于弱耦合系数的Padé近似。

Result: 三阶与四阶有效质量系数为全新结果；系数异常规则，表现为α≈8.5附近的幂律奇异性，指数约1.4。逆质量各阶截断的零点（首个为教科书值α=6）缓慢收敛于该半径。仅用弱耦合系数构建的Padé近似在α≤5时与图示蒙特卡罗质量结果偏差小于1%。

Conclusion: 基态解析性与强耦合渐近的精确结果要求系数不能保持同号，而现有系数却全为同号，故表观奇异性必为靠近正实轴的复共轭对。该奇异性标志着向强耦合的交叉：弱耦合级数能准确定位交叉点，但无法穿越该点继续延拓。

Abstract: We calculate the Rayleigh-Schrodinger perturbation series for the ground-state energy and the effective mass of the three-dimensional Frohlich polaron through fourth order in the coupling constant alpha. The third- and fourth-order coefficients of the effective mass are new. Because the Frohlich interaction is a form-bounded perturbation of an isolated nondegenerate ground state at fixed total momentum, the series has a nonzero radius of convergence, and we analyze it with the ratio and Pade methods appropriate to convergent series. The coefficients are strikingly regular and are described by a simple power-law singularity at alpha near 8.5 with an exponent near 1.4. The couplings at which successive truncations of the inverse mass vanish, the first of which is the textbook breakdown value alpha = 6, are slowly converging estimates of this radius. Pade approximants built from the weak-coupling coefficients alone agree with diagrammatic Monte Carlo results for the mass to better than one percent for alpha up to 5. Exact results on the analyticity of the ground state and on the strong-coupling asymptotics imply that the coefficients cannot keep a constant sign, although all known coefficients do, so that the apparent singularity must be a complex-conjugate pair close to the positive real axis. It marks the crossover to strong coupling: the weak-coupling series locates this crossover accurately but cannot be continued through it.

</details>


### [182] [Mapping Strain and Spatial-Modulation Phase from Local Wave Vectors in Scanning Tunneling Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10255)
*Xinze Yang,Sandra Sajan,Maria N. Gastiasoro,Miguel M. Ugeda,Eduardo H. da Silva Neto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出局域波矢映射（LWM）算法，直接在实空间测量局域波矢以提取STM图像中的应变，避免了Lawler--Fujita方法所需且易失效的二维相位解缠，对涡旋及拓扑缺陷附近的相位梯度纹理成像尤为稳健，并在4Ha-NbSe2与2H-NbSe2实验数据中解析出CDW非公度性、晶格畸变与CDW相位涡旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间变化的晶格畸变会引起局域电子性质改变，但区域平均测量会将其掩盖；STM具有原子级空间分辨能力，原则上可局域量化此类畸变。常用的Lawler--Fujita（LF）算法由晶格调制相位求位移场、再微分得应变，但依赖二维相位解缠，在相位定义不良的区域（尤其刃位错等拓扑缺陷附近）容易失效，因此需要一种规避该限制的新方法。

Method: 提出局域波矢映射（LWM）算法：不依赖相位解缠，而是直接在实空间测量局域波矢，并推导局域波矢、应变与相位梯度之间的关系；利用含已知晶格畸变及人工生成涡旋的模拟数据进行基准测试；随后将方法应用于实验STM数据，分析4Ha-NbSe2与2H-NbSe2中的CDW。

Result: LWM在相位本身定义不良的情形下依然稳健，特别适合成像与涡旋及其他拓扑缺陷相关的相位梯度纹理；模拟数据基准测试验证了算法在已知畸变与人工涡旋下的可靠性；应用于实验STM数据后，成功解析出4Ha-NbSe2与2H-NbSe2中的CDW非公度性、晶格畸变以及CDW相位涡旋。

Conclusion: LWM为从STM图像中提取局域应变与相位梯度提供了一种规避相位解缠难题的替代途径，在拓扑缺陷与涡旋相关研究场景中具有优势；相关物理意义的讨论已另文报道，程序包及示例用法已在GitHub公开。

Abstract: Spatially varying lattice distortions can produce local changes in electronic properties that are obscured by measurements averaging over extended regions. With its atomic-scale spatial resolution, scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) provides, in principle, a means of quantifying such distortions locally. A widely used approach for extracting local strain from STM images is the Lawler--Fujita (LF) algorithm [Lawler et al., Nature 466, 347 (2010)], which determines the displacement field from the phase of the lattice modulation and subsequently obtains the strain by differentiation. However, this approach requires two-dimensional phase unwrapping, which can fail in regions where the phase is ill-defined, particularly near topological defects such as edge dislocations. Here, we introduce an alternative approach that circumvents these limitations by directly measuring the local wave vector in real space. We derive the relationship between the local wave vector, strain, and the phase gradient, and show that the local wave vector mapping (LWM) algorithm remains robust even when the phase itself is ill-defined. This makes the method particularly well suited for imaging the phase-gradient textures associated with vortices and other topological defects. We benchmark the algorithm using simulated data containing known lattice distortions and artificially generated vortices. Finally, we apply the method to experimental STM data and resolve the charge density wave (CDW) incommensurabilities, lattice distortions, and CDW phase vortices in 4Ha-NbSe2 and 2H-NbSe2. The physical implications of these observations were recently reported by S. Sajan, X. Yang, et al. [arXiv:2607.20335 (2026)]. The program package and the example usage is publicly available at the GitHub: https://github.com/jsntxyang/LocalWavevectorMeasuremnt.

</details>


### [183] [Exact Excitation Spectra and Spectral Gaps of the Frustrated Spin-1/2 $J_1$-$J_2$ Model via Dual Spin-Sector Variational Quantum Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10454)
*Sk Saniur Rahaman*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出双自旋扇区VQE框架，解决阻挫自旋系统中态序反转问题，精确分离激发单态并验证于1D链和2D格子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在阻挫自旋-1/2 J1-J2海森堡模型中，当J2/J1<0.25时，Sz=0扇区最低激发为三重态(S=1)，其能量低于激发单态(S=0)。标准单扇区算法如VQD因deflation机制会投影掉基态单态并错误收敛到三重态，将其误判为激发单态，导致态序反转下的变分模糊。

Method: 提出双自旋扇区VQE框架：先在Sz=1扇区（单态无法存在处）优化基态三重态；通过总自旋降算符S-=Σ_i S_i^-将其映射至Sz=0；结合双扇区惩罚代价函数，从而无变分歧义地隔离激发单态E_S。

Result: 态矢量数值模拟显示收敛到机器精度且重叠保真度为单位值。方法捕捉关键物理特征：无能隙相、J2/J1≈0.2411的BKT临界点、Majumdar-Ghosh二聚点(J2/J1=0.50)以及2D非磁性阻挫区(J2/J1≈0.40-0.60)。有限采样模拟与门数标度分析（至N=100量子比特）证明资源高效性与抗采样噪声能力。

Conclusion: 双自旋扇区VQE有效解决态序反转，精确获得激发单态及其物理特征，适用于近期量子硬件上的阻挫自旋系统激发态计算。

Abstract: Finding excited states and excitation gaps in frustrated spin systems using Variational Quantum Eigensolvers (VQE) is complicated by state-ordering inversions. In the frustrated spin-1/2 $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg model, standard single-sector algorithms like Variational Quantum Deflation (VQD) in $S_z=0$ fail when $J_2/J_1 < 0.25$. In this regime, the lowest-lying excitation in $S_z=0$ is a Triplet ($S=1$), which lies below the Excited Singlet ($S=0$). Standard deflation projects out the ground singlet and converges onto the Triplet state, misidentifying it as the Excited Singlet. Here, we present a Dual Spin-Sector VQE framework that resolves these state-ordering inversions across 1D spin chains and 2D rectangular lattices. By first optimizing the ground Triplet state in the $S_z=1$ sector (where singlet states cannot exist), mapping it into $S_z=0$ via the total spin-lowering operator $S^- = \sum_i S_i^-$, and evaluating a dual-sector penalty cost function, we isolate the Excited Singlet ($E_S$) without variational ambiguity. Numerical state-vector simulations confirm exact convergence to machine precision with unit overlap fidelity. The method captures key physical features, including the gapless phase, the BKT critical point at $J_2/J_1 \approx 0.2411$, the Majumdar-Ghosh dimer point ($J_2/J_1 = 0.50$), and the 2D non-magnetic frustrated regime ($J_2/J_1 \approx 0.40 - 0.60$). Finite-shot sampling simulations and gate-scaling analytics up to $N=100$ qubits demonstrate the resource efficiency and shot resilience of the algorithm for near-term quantum execution.

</details>


### [184] [Landau Theory for Non-relativistic Magnetism Beyond Altermagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10523)
*Hana Schiff,Judit Romhányi,Paul McClarty,Jeffrey G. Rau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 将共线磁序的唯象朗道理论推广到共面与非共面磁序，构建零波矢单不可约表示下所有多维有序通道的四次自由能，并建立朗道方法与自旋空间群方法的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有朗道理论工作主要针对共线磁序，而共面与非共面磁序（如自旋向列、类比³He的A/B相）缺乏系统的对称性分类与自由能构造；同时朗道方法与自旋空间群方法之间缺少明确关联。

Method: 针对由晶体点群单一不可约表示描述、波矢k=0的磁序，构造至四次项的对称允许自由能，覆盖所有可能的多维不可约有序通道，并分tetragonal、trigonal/hexagonal、cubic对称性讨论；计算净磁化、自旋电导、多极矩、压磁等观测量并与自旋空间群约束对照。

Result: 得到tetragonal、trigonal/hexagonal、cubic三类不同的朗道理论；发现两种典型结果：塌缩为低对称共线相，或形成自旋空间中相互正交的共面/非共面序（类比³He的A、B相）。证明该对称破缺相实现的正是与序参量空间表示对应、且与顺磁相共享母空间群的自旋空间群。给出各情形共面/非共面磁序的具体例子及关键观测量。

Conclusion: 建立了朗道理论与自旋空间群方法之间的直接联系，讨论了低对称共线相与常规共线自旋群、原子altermagnetism、超流³He及电子向列相之间的关系。

Abstract: In this work we study phenomenological Landau theories of non-relativistic magnetic systems, generalizing work on the collinear case to coplanar and non-coplanar orders. Focusing on zero-wavevector ($\boldsymbol{k}=0$) magnetic orders described by a single irreducible representation of the crystallographic point group, we construct the symmetry-allowed free energies to quartic order for all possible multi-dimensional irreducible ordering channels. We find distinct Landau theories for tetragonal, trigonal/hexagonal, and cubic symmetry. Similar to the case of spin-nematic fluids, we find two natural outcomes: a collapse to a lower-symmetry collinear phase or a genuinely coplanar or non-coplanar order where the order parameters form a mutually orthogonal set in spin space -- reminiscent of the $A$ and $B$ phases in ${}^3$He. We show in general that this symmetry-broken phase realizes the spin space group associated with the spatial representation of the order parameter sharing the same parent space group as the paramagnetic phase. This provides a direct link between the Landau and spin space group approaches. We calculate key observables using this Landau approach, including net magnetization, spin conductivity, multipolar moments, and piezomagnetism, connecting our results to constraints from the underlying spin space group. Examples of coplanar and non-coplanar magnetic orders for each case are worked out in detail. Finally, we discuss the relationship of the lower-symmetry collinear phases to the usual collinear spin groups, as well as connections to atomic altermagnetism, superfluid ${}^3$He and electronic nematic phases.

</details>
