### [185] [Dynamical Insights into the Current Control Strategies for Chikungunya](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08856)
*Bo Zheng,Xingzhi Chen,Jianglong Xiao,Kai Wang,Jianshe Yu,Huaiping Zhu*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该论文针对基孔肯雅热在现行蚊媒防控政策下疫情仍可反复上升的问题，构建含无症状感染与筛查率的媒介-宿主动力学模型，解析推导疫情峰值与峰值时间的近似表达式，揭示短期瞬态与长期结果的内在联系，并提出升级版"十二字"防控策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在中国现行基孔肯雅热防控政策（清积水、灭成蚊、防叮咬）下，疫情仍可反复出现并持续上升，说明现有策略存在未覆盖的传播机制，亟需从动力学角度揭示其内在原因并加以改进。

Method: 构建纳入无症状感染与筛查率的媒介-宿主动力学模型；推导疫情峰值与峰值时间的近似解析表达式；在人类初始感染、蚊媒初始感染及人蚊共同初始感染三种情景下进行数值模拟，分析筛查率对传播动态的影响。

Result: （i）即使媒介种群被有效压制，无症状携带者仍可维持传播；（ii）筛查率对峰值时间的影响呈非单调性，存在使疫情延迟最大化的临界阈值；（iii）适度筛查既能抑制第一波疫情，又可避免留下过大的易感人群池而放大后续波次。

Conclusion: 将原政策升级为"十二字"策略——清积水、灭成蚊、防叮咬、早筛查——以实现预防、缓解与控制，为基孔肯雅热及其他蚊媒传染病的防控提供理论支持。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge that, under China's current mosquito-control policy for chikungunya---\textit{clearing stagnant water, killing adult mosquitoes, and preventing bites}---epidemics can still recur and continue to rise. By constructing a vector--host dynamical model that incorporates asymptomatic infections and a screening rate, we derive approximate analytical expressions for the epidemic peak value and peak time, and reveal the intrinsic link between short-term transients and long-term outcomes. Simulations across three scenarios---human-seeded, mosquito-seeded, and joint human--mosquito-seeded---show that (i) even when the vector population is effectively suppressed, asymptomatic carriers can still sustain transmission; (ii) the effect of the screening rate on the peak time is non-monotonic, and there exists a critical threshold that maximizes the delay of the epidemic; and (iii) moderate screening can both suppress the first wave and avoid leaving an excessively large susceptible pool that would amplify subsequent waves. Accordingly, we upgrade the original policy to a \textit{Twelve-character} strategy for prevention, mitigation, and control---\textit{clearing stagnant water, killing adult mosquitoes, preventing bites, and screening cases early}---providing theoretical support for chikungunya and other mosquito-borne diseases.

</details>


### [186] [Mathematical statistics of wild mammal biomass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09744)
*Klaus M. Frahm,Dima L. Shepelyansky,José Lages*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该论文对全球4795种野生陆生哺乳动物生物量分布做数理统计分析，发现其洛伦兹曲线吉尼系数高达0.944，超过世界国家财富不平等，并可用Rayleigh-Jeans扩展谱模型（财富热化假说WTH）在四个数量级上复现洛伦兹与帕累托曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借用经济学中刻画财富不平等的工具（洛伦兹曲线、吉尼系数、帕累托分布），考察野生陆生哺乳动物生物量在物种间的极端集中现象，并检验此前仅在人经济分布上验证过的财富热化假说（WTH）能否推广到受生态与演化约束的生物分布。

Method: 基于新发布的全球野生陆生哺乳动物生物量普查数据（N_s=4795个物种），计算生物量分布的洛伦兹曲线与吉尼系数；将物种生物量视为能量能级，按Rayleigh-Jeans（RJ）稳态分布填充，构建RJ扩展谱模型，并与实际洛伦兹曲线和帕累托曲线进行拟合比较。

Result: 生物量分布的吉尼系数G=0.944，超过世界国家间财富分布的报道不平等程度。RJ扩展谱模型在四个数量级的生物量变化范围内重现了真实洛伦兹与帕累托曲线，刻画了生物量在少数重型物种中的强烈凝聚，以及在绝大多数轻型物种中的近乎缺失。

Conclusion: 结果将此前在人经济分布上验证的WTH框架推广到生物分布，把大型哺乳动物的极端稀有性与人类社会的寡头财富集中纳入同一统计数学描述；并讨论了可能促成该分布的生态机制，包括种间相互作用、食物可获得性与竞争。

Abstract: Using the recently published global census of the biomass of wild terrestrial mammals, we perform a detailed mathematical statistical analysis of its distribution over $N_s=4795$ species, drawing on tools developed in economics to characterize wealth inequality. We show that the Lorenz curve of the mammal biomass distribution is characterized by a large Gini coefficient $G=0.944$ exceeding the inequality reported for wealth distribution among world countries. This distribution is compared to the predictions of the Wealth Thermalization Hypothesis (WTH), in which species biomass values are treated as energy levels populated according to a Rayleigh-Jeans (RJ) steady-state distribution. We show that an RJ extended spectral model reproduces the real Lorenz and Pareto curves over four orders of magnitude of biomass variation, capturing the strong condensation of biomass among the rare, heavy-bodied species and its near-absence among the vast majority of light-bodied ones. These results extend the WTH framework, previously validated on distributions of human economic origin, to a biological distribution shaped by ecological and evolutionary constraints, and place the extreme rarity of large-bodied mammal species within the same statistical mathematical description as the oligarchic concentration of wealth in human societies. We finally discuss possible ecological mechanisms that may contribute to the observed distribution, including interspecific interactions, food availability, and competition.

</details>


### [187] [Stochasticity and Environmental Switching Shape Quorum Sensing Evolution in Bacterial Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10492)
*Uttam Kumar,Hong-Yan Shih*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 研究用双自诱导物拨动开关网络，在波动环境中通过基于个体的空间种群模型和双性状自适应动力学模型，揭示膜通透性经由集体机制演化，环境频率主导演化结果，噪声影响非单调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典群体感应（QS）被理解为细菌感知自身密度的机制，但环境波动使单细胞估计不可靠；另一种观点认为种群可汇总个体估计以实现可靠的集体决策，本文旨在探究双自诱导物拨动开关网络如何在波动环境中实现这种集体适应。

Method: 采用空间结构种群的基于个体模型（individual-based model），以膜通透性为演化性状（控制自诱导物分泌）；并建立双性状自适应动力学模型（two-trait adaptive-dynamics model）统一不同噪声机制，在对称情形下约化为闭式一维方程。

Result: 环境频率是演化结果的主要决定因素：强不对称时主导性状达到高平台，稀有环境相关性状保持低水平并随其频率上升而平滑升高；总通透性接近固定预算，环境频率主要决定两性状间的分配。两环境等频时无单一性状胜出，二者共存，两感知通道的噪声不对称性仅偏置共存水平而非选择胜者，且共存是最慢达到的结果。噪声越高适应越快，但演化通透性对噪声非单调，在中间值处达峰。自适应动力学模型在远离对称处约化为闭式一维方程，能捕捉个体模型结果；在对称处弱交叉抑制给出单一共存吸引子。

Conclusion: 双自诱导物拨动开关网络通过集体机制实现通透性的演化适应，环境频率主导性状分配，噪声影响兼具促进适应与非单调性；双性状自适应动力学模型统一了先前关于噪声有利于集体感知的结论，并能复现个体模型与重复种群的共存现象。

Abstract: Quorum sensing (QS) is classically understood as a mechanism by which bacterial cells sense their own density, but environmental fluctuations make any single cell's estimate unreliable. An alternative view holds that populations pool individual estimates to reach reliable collective decisions. We investigate how a two-autoinducer toggle switch network enables this collective adaptation in fluctuating environments. At the single-cell level, the toggle switch is bistable. Membrane permeability controls autoinducer secretion and is the evolving trait. Using an individual-based model of a spatially structured population, we show that permeability evolves through a collective mechanism. Environmental frequency is the primary determinant of evolutionary outcome. Under strong asymmetry, the dominant trait reaches a high plateau. The trait linked to the rare environment stays low. It rises smoothly as its environment becomes more frequent. Total permeability stays close to a fixed budget; environmental frequency mainly sets how it is split between the two traits. When the two environments are equally frequent, no single trait wins. Both traits coexist, and noise asymmetry between the two sensing channels biases the levels at which they coexist rather than selecting a winner. Coexistence is also the slowest outcome to reach. Higher noise speeds adaptation, but the evolved permeability is non-monotonic in noise, peaking at an intermediate value. Earlier work found that more noise favors collective sensing as long as cells are correct on average. We develop a two-trait adaptive-dynamics model that unifies these regimes. Away from symmetry, it reduces to a closed one-dimensional equation that captures the individual-based results. At symmetry, weak cross-repression gives a single coexistence attractor, consistent with replicate populations maintaining both traits.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

### [188] [Numerical Bogoliubov approximation of bosonic many-body quantum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08956)
*Yoann Le Hénaff,Christian Lubich,Peter Pickl*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种求解平均场标度下N≫1玻色子含时薛定谔方程的算法，复杂度与粒子数N无关，基于Bogoliubov拟设，结合Dirac-Frenkel变分原理与动态低秩近似（BUG积分器）实现自适应秩演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有量子动力学数值算法计算量随粒子数N增长，难以处理N≫1的多体玻色子系统，需要一种复杂度独立于N的数值方法。

Method: 基于Bogoliubov理论的波函数拟设：第一部分用Hartree方程演化凝聚体函数，以Dirac-Frenkel含时变分原理推导激发函数运动方程；利用玻色对称性证明（除正比于N的时变全局相位外）近似波函数导数有界且与N无关。第二部分用复合算法近似波函数（模去相位），对运动方程恰当分裂，并对基本双粒子激发函数作动态低秩近似（单粒子函数乘积的线性组合），采用对系数矩阵小奇异值稳健且可秩自适应的basis update and Galerkin (BUG)积分器演化。

Result: 推导出的近似波函数（除去频率正比于N的全局相位）导数与N无关；低秩激发函数由BUG积分器稳定演化，支持秩自适应；给出数值结果评估算法性能。

Conclusion: 该算法为大规模玻色子量子动力学提供了计算复杂度独立于粒子数N的可行途径，验证了Bogoliubov拟设与动态低秩方法的结合有效性。

Abstract: We propose an algorithm for the numerical solution of the time-dependent Schrödinger equation for N >> 1bosons interacting via a two-body interaction potential in the mean-field scaling regime. Unlike existing numerical algorithms for quantum dynamics, the proposed algorithm has a computational complexity that is independent of the number N of particles. It is based on an ansatz for the wave function inspired by the Bogoliubov theory, which is an asymptotic theory for large N.
  In a first part, we follow Bogoliubov theory in evolving the condensate function by the Hartree equation, but we use the Dirac--Frenkel time-dependent variational principle to derive nonlinear equations of motion for the excitation functions in the Bogoliubov ansatz. By carefully exploiting the bosonic symmetry, the so obtained approximate wave functions are shown to have derivatives bounded independently of N apart from a time-dependent global phase factor which rotates with a frequency proportional to N.
  In the second part, we numerically approximate the wave function (modulo the phase) by a composite algorithm, which employs an appropriate splitting of the equations of motion and uses a dynamical low-rank approximation of the fundamental two-particle excitation function by linear combinations of products of single-particle functions. The low-rank excitation function is evolved in time by a basis update and Galerkin (BUG) integrator that is robust to small singular values of the coefficient matrix and allows for rank adaptivity. Numerical results are provided to assess the performance of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [189] [Convergence of isoparametric BGN methods for mean curvature flow of closed surfaces with high-order finite elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08972)
*Yifei Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了等参数BGN方法在闭合曲面平均曲率流中的收敛性，并给出最优的h²阶误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BGN方法是求解平均曲率流的常用数值方法，但其在闭合曲面情形下的严格收敛性证明此前尚不完善，需要建立完整的收敛性理论。

Method: 证明采用两个关键工具：一是通过时间展开构造近似流，使其插值满足带更优缺陷项的BGN格式；二是利用切向检验函数的代数恒等式导出切向误差的精细估计。

Result: 对于次数k≥6的有限元与时间步长τ=O(h²)，数值曲面在流形距离意义下以h²阶收敛到精确曲面。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了BGN方法求解闭合曲面平均曲率流的收敛性证明及最优阶误差估计。

Abstract: We prove the convergence of the isoparametric Barrett--Garcke--Nürnberg (BGN) method for mean curvature flow of closed surfaces. For finite elements of degree $k\ge6$ and time steps $τ=O(h^2)$, the numerical surfaces converge to the exact surfaces in the manifold distance with order $h^2$. The proof relies on two ingredients. First, we construct approximation flows by an expansion in time, whose interpolations satisfy the BGN method with an improved defect. Second, we derive a refined estimate for the tangential error from an algebraic identity for tangential test functions.

</details>


### [190] [Polynomial Difference Schemes with Energy and Momentum Multipliers on Orthogonal Uniform Meshes: Stencil Restrictions and Monge--Ampère-Type Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08979)
*E. I. Kaptsov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究一维流体力学型方程守恒型不变有限差分格式，证明9点模板上任意次多项式无法同时保持能量与动量守恒律，并借助直接法的差分模拟给出更大模板上的存在性结果，得到一个具五守恒律与D4不变性的Monge-Ampère格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有经验表明，正交均匀网格上采用9点模板的非线性多项式格式，在标准u_t、u_x差分离散给出的乘子下，难以同时保持能量与动量守恒律的差分对应物；作者希望严格界定这一现象并推广到更大模板与更一般的方程类。

Method: 对9点模板，针对任意次数多项式给出不可能性证明；对更大模板，采用直接法的差分模拟，分析有界次数多项式的可能方案，并刻画其所逼近的二阶方程类型；进一步构造并数值验证所得Monge-Ampère型格式。

Result: 在9点模板上证明了任意次多项式不能同时保持能量与动量守恒；在足够大的模板上，有界次数的此类多项式格式可存在，但其所逼近的二阶方程为Monge-Ampère型；作为副产物得到齐次Monge-Ampère方程的一个格式，具五个守恒律、保持微分方程多数对称性、且具离散二面体群D4不变性。数值试验在精确解上确认了二阶逼近精度，并显示守恒律以同阶精度满足。

Conclusion: 9点模板上的"能量—动量守恒不可兼得"障碍具有一般性（对任意次多项式成立）；突破该障碍需扩大模板，且所获格式自然地联系到Monge-Ampère型方程；所构造的D4不变Monge-Ampère格式兼顾高阶逼近、多守恒律与对称性，具备实际数值可用性。

Abstract: Experience with invariant finite-difference schemes for one-dimensional hydro\-dynamic-type equations for a function $u(t,x)$ suggests that nonlinear polynomial schemes on orthogonal uniform meshes with the 9-point stencil cannot simultaneously preserve difference analogues of the energy and momentum conservation laws, with the multipliers given by the standard difference approximations of $u_t$ and $u_x$. For the 9-point stencil this hypothesis is proved for polynomials of arbitrary degree. On larger stencils it is investigated by means of the difference analogue of the direct method: for polynomials of bounded degree, such schemes are found to exist only on sufficiently large stencils, and the second-order equations they approximate are of Monge--Ampère type. As a by-product, a scheme for the homogeneous Monge--Ampère equation is obtained that has five conservation laws, admits most of the symmetries of the differential equation, and is invariant under the discrete dihedral group $D_4$. Numerical tests on exact solutions confirm its second order of approximation and show that the conservation laws are satisfied on these solutions with the same order of accuracy.

</details>


### [191] [Sum of Squares and Non Sum of Squares Regimes for the Toeplitz Bottcher Wenzel Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08980)
*Wenqi Zhu,Ping Nie*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文否定了 László 关于 Toeplitz 矩阵对 Böttcher--Wenzel 形式为平方和的猜想，给出一类角支撑 Toeplitz 对在大阶数下非 SoS 的反例，并在特定对称/反对称情形及低阶数情形下证明了 SoS 可实现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Toeplitz 矩阵是平移不变性最简单的结构模型，出现在卷积、平稳协方差模型以及平移不变算子离散化中。Böttcher--Wenzel 形式是衡量交换子不等式间隙的非负四次型。László 猜想当两个矩阵均为 Toeplitz 时，该四次型对任意矩阵阶数都是平方和。作者旨在检验该猜想的正确性。

Method: 构造一族显式的角支撑 Toeplitz 对，仅保留两侧最外侧对角线；将全部 Gram 表示归约为一个有限复测试，其中 Gram 不变量部分出现严格负的极限方向，而剩余依赖 Gram 的贡献趋于零；并给出矩阵阶数的显式充分阈值。此外，对对称或反对称 Toeplitz 因子的情形证明任意阶数下的 SoS 性，对一般 Toeplitz 对则通过计算验证并辅以 Lean 4 形式化验证。

Result: 证明 László 猜想不成立：所构造的角支撑 Toeplitz 对在足够大阶数下 BW 形式不是平方和，并给出显式的阶数阈值。正面结果包括：若任一 Toeplitz 因子是对称或反对称的，则对应 BW 形式对任意阶数均为 SoS，另一因子可为任意实 Toeplitz 矩阵；对无限制 Toeplitz 对，证明所有 2≤N≤50 阶均具有 SoS 表示，其中阶数至 20 通过 Lean 4 形式化验证。

Conclusion: László 的 Toeplitz BW 形式 SoS 猜想在一般情况下为假，但在对称/反对称因子的子情形以及低阶数范围内成立，说明该问题存在清晰的正面与负面分界。

Abstract: Toeplitz matrices are among the simplest structured models of translation invariance, arising naturally in convolution, stationary covariance models, and discretizations of translation-invariant operators. For a pair of real matrices, the Böttcher--Wenzel form (BW form) is a nonnegative quartic measuring the gap in the corresponding commutator inequality. László conjectured that, when both matrices are Toeplitz, this quartic is a sum of squares for every matrix order. We show that the conjecture is false. More precisely, we identify an explicit family of corner-supported Toeplitz pairs, retaining only the outermost diagonals on the two sides, for which the associated BW form is not a sum of squares at all sufficiently large orders. The proof reduces the complete family of Gram representations to a finite complex test: its Gram-invariant part develops a strictly negative limiting direction, while the remaining Gram-dependent contribution vanishes. We also give an explicit sufficient threshold for the matrix order. The negative result is complemented by two positive regimes. If either Toeplitz factor is symmetric or skew-symmetric, then the corresponding BW form is SoS for every order, with the other factor allowed to be an arbitrary real Toeplitz matrix. For unrestricted Toeplitz pairs, we further prove SoS representability for every $2\le N\le50$, with formal Lean 4 verification through order 20.

</details>


### [192] [JAXIGA - A software framework for GPU-enabled and differentiable isogeometric analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09083)
*Cosmin Anitescu,Yongjie Jessica Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: JAXIGA：基于JAX的开源等几何分析库，支持JIT编译、反向模式自动微分与GPU加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为将等几何分析的网格构造与可微计算解耦，便于几何评估、装配与求解器统一编译并支持灵敏度/伴随分析，弥补现有IGA工具在自动微分与GPU性能上的不足。

Method: 将网格构造与计算分离，计算部分被追踪以便编译与微分；在同一逐点能量密度下实现Galerkin法、能量最小化法与配点法；通过求解一次伴随问题获得收敛解的灵敏度；引入层次抽取用于变分方法的局部细化，并使用光滑样条空间处理四阶问题。

Result: 结果与解析解及独立的FEniCSx、JAX-FEM计算对比一致；GPU基准（36,448个未知量）上装配比主机CPU快24倍；CG求解器也在GPU上运行时，总求解时间由31.8 s降至1.38 s，结果与直接解吻合至5×10⁻¹¹。

Conclusion: 求解器选择与编译复用对整体计算成本的降低至关重要。

Abstract: In this paper, we present JAXIGA, an open-source library for isogeometric analysis based on JAX. The library separates the construction of the mesh from the computations which are traced for compilation and differentiation. This allows geometry evaluation, assembly and the supported solvers to be combined in a just-in-time compiled program, for which first-order reverse-mode derivatives can be computed. We consider three numerical methods obtained from a single pointwise energy density. The Galerkin and energy-minimization methods use the same discrete potential and have the same stationary points, while collocation enforces the corresponding strong form. The sensitivities of a converged solution are obtained by solving one adjoint problem, and their accuracy depends on the tolerances of both the primal and adjoint problems. We also describe hierarchical extraction for local refinement of the variational methods and the use of smooth spline spaces for fourth-order problems. The numerical results are compared with analytical solutions and independent FEniCSx and JAX-FEM computations. For the reported GPU benchmark with 36,448 unknowns, assembly is twenty-four times faster than on the host CPU. When the conjugate-gradient solver is also run on the GPU, the total solution time is reduced from 31.8 to 1.38 seconds, and the result agrees with the direct solution to $5\times10^{-11}$. These results show that the choice of solver and the reuse of compilation are important for obtaining an overall reduction in computational cost.

</details>


### [193] [Improving Learning of Green's Functions Through Kernel Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09101)
*Beiji Chen,Qiang Du,Kui Ren,Tian-Yi Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出通过减去Green函数的奇异部分，将自伴二阶椭圆算子逆的学习转化为边界上的调和延拓算子学习，并证明该方法能将输入代数收敛率提高两阶、输出收敛率在解析正则性下提升为指数率，且可推广至半线性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习自伴二阶椭圆算子 $\mathcal{L}$ 的解算子（或Green函数） $\mathcal{L}^{-1}$ 是算子学习的核心难题。直接学习 $\mathcal{L}^{-1}$ 面临Green函数本身的奇异性，导致收敛率受限、精度低、训练困难，因此需要一种能够规避奇异性的有效学习框架。

Method: 将Green函数分解为奇异部分与正则部分：奇异部分由解析形式或显式Laplace parametrix给出，从而把 $\mathcal{L}^{-1}$ 的学习重写为学习边界上的 $\mathcal{L}$-调和延拓算子。将该过程形式化为再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）之间的算子学习问题，并进一步通过迭代分解将该框架推广至半线性问题，其中唯一需要学习的对象仍为线性算子。

Result: 核分解策略将输入的代数收敛率提高两个数量级；在诱导边界数据具有一致解析正则性且输出空间相容的条件下，输出收敛率由代数率提升为指数率。数值实验验证了理论分析结果。

Conclusion: 通过分离Green函数的奇异部分，可将椭圆算子逆的学习归结为边界调和延拓的RKHS算子学习，显著提升收敛率；该线性改进率可直接传递到半线性迭代中，为高效求解椭圆问题提供了理论与数值支撑。

Abstract: This work analyzes a framework for learning the solution operator, or equivalently, the Green's function, of a general self-adjoint second-order elliptic operator $\cL$. Subtracting the singular part of the Green's function, either known analytically or supplied by an explicit Laplace parametrix, recasts the learning of $\cL^{-1}$ as learning an $\cL$-harmonic extension operator on the boundary. We formulate the process as operator learning between reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHSs) to show that the kernel decomposition strategy raises the algebraic input rate by two orders and, under uniform analytic regularity of the induced boundary data together with compatible output spaces, upgrades the output rate from algebraic to exponential. We extend the framework further to semilinear problems via an iterative decomposition in which the only learned object remains a linear operator, ensuring that the improved linear rates translate directly to the nonlinear iteration. Numerical experiments are provided to validate the theoretical development.

</details>


### [194] [High-order entropy stable isothermal wall boundary condition for the compressible Navier--Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09249)
*Mohammed Sayyari,Nail K. Yamaleev*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的等温无滑移壁面边界条件，基于SBP-SAT算子，能在保持设计精度的同时确保熵稳定性，并正确捕捉壁面熵产生符号及离散熵平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵稳定边界条件对保证数值格式满足离散熵不等式至关重要，可离散地模拟可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的热力学第二定律。已有工作提出了可证明熵稳定的绝热壁面边界条件（Parsani 2014），并将其推广至移动绝热固壁或指定热通量壁面（Dalcin 2019）。然而，在SBP-SAT算子框架下，目前文献中尚缺乏针对等温壁面边界条件的熵稳定格式，本文正是为填补这一空白。

Method: 基于分部求和（SBP）与同步近似项（SAT）算子，构造新的等温无滑移壁面边界条件，使其强制满足壁面温度与速度条件，同时保持设计阶精度和稳定性。

Result: 所提出的边界条件满足三项特性：1）以设计精度强制满足温度与速度壁面条件并保持稳定；2）提供壁面处熵产生的正确符号；3）在离散层面模拟Navier-Stokes方程的熵平衡。其保结构性与设计阶特性在可压缩流动的标准基准问题上得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了SBP-SAT框架下等温壁面熵稳定边界条件的空白，所提方法兼具设计精度、稳定性和离散熵平衡特性，并通过标准基准问题验证了有效性。

Abstract: Entropy stable boundary conditions are critical for ensuring that the corresponding numerical scheme satisfies the discrete entropy inequality, thus, mimicking the second law of thermodynamics for the compressible Navier-Stokes equations discretely. Provably entropy-stable adiabatic wall boundary conditions were introduced in Parsani 2014~\cite{parsani2014entropy}. These discrete wall boundary conditions were further generalized in Dalcin 2019~\cite{dalcin2019conservative} to a moving adiabatic solid wall or a wall with a prescribed heat flux for the compressible Navier--Stokes equations discretized by using summation-by-parts (SBP) and simultaneous-approximation-term (SAT) operators. Currently no entropy-stable formulation using SBP-SAT operators for isothermal wall boundary conditions is available in the literature. This paper presents a new isothermal no-slip wall boundary conditions that: 1) enforces the $T|_{y=0}=T^{wall}$ and $\vec{v}|_{y=0}=\vec{v}^{wall}$ conditions with the design order of accuracy while maintaining stability, 2) provides the correct sign of entropy production at the wall, and 3) mimics the entropy balance of the Navier-Stokes equations at the discrete level. The structure-preserving and design-order properties of the proposed methodology are demonstrated and verified on standard benchmark problems for compressible flows.

</details>


### [195] [Convergence of repeated-pole rational approximation of the exponential on infinite sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09251)
*Fei Xue,Tianqi Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究用带单个有限极点的有理函数在扇形区域上逼近指数函数的最优一致误差,给出线性配比下的精确双边渐近阶估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画以单极点、有限重数有理函数逼近 e^{-hz} 的扇形一致误差,并确定最优极点位置配比,是理解投影型 Krylov/Arnoldi 方法收敛速率的关键。

Method: 利用 Möbius 变换将问题化为紧致透镜上的多项式逼近;通过精确边界 Faber 积分、整体分数幂围道变形与双鞍点展开进行分析。定义 γ_β=2-β/π 与鞍点合并阈值 α_c,并引入实鞍点作用量指数 H_β(α)。

Result: 在 0<α<α_c 的非合并区间上,得到锐利双边阶 E_{m,β}(αm)≍m^{-1/2}H_β(α)^m,且对紧子集一致;指数因子 H_β(α) 的唯一极小点为 α_β^*=1/[γ_β sin(π/(2γ_β))],极小值为 tan(π/(4γ_β)),在 β=0 时退化为经典半直线因子 tan(π/8)=√2-1。数值域估计给出与矩阵维度无关的投影 shift-and-invert Arnoldi 上界;有限次极点搜索与非正规实验显示了扇形一致律与单个矩阵计算之间的差异。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了单极点有理逼近指数函数的统一扇形渐近理论,明确了最优极点配比与收敛指数,并将扇域一致误差律与具体非正规矩阵迭代行为区分开。

Abstract: For $h>0$, degree $m\ge0$, $0\leβ<π$, and $q>0$, let $Σ_β=\{re^{iθ}:r\ge0,\ |θ|\leβ/2\}$ and let $E_{m,β}(q)$ denote the best uniform error for approximating $e^{-hz}$ on $Σ_β$ by rational functions whose only possible finite pole is $s=-q/h$, with multiplicity at most $m$. For the linear scaling $q=αm$ with a fixed $α>0$, a Möbius map reduces the problem to polynomial approximation on a compact lens. Write $γ_β=2-β/π$ and let $α_c(γ_β)$ denote the saddle-coalescence threshold. For $0<α<α_c(γ_β)$, let $H_β(α)\in(0,1)$ be the exponential of the real saddle action. An exact boundary Faber integral, a global fractional-power contour deformation, and a two-saddle expansion yield the sharp two-sided order $E_{m,β}(αm)\asymp m^{-1/2}H_β(α)^m$, uniformly on compact subsets of $0<α<α_c(γ_β)$. On this noncoalescing interval, the exponential factor $H_β(α)$ has the unique minimizer $α_β^*=1/[γ_β\sin(π/(2γ_β))]$ and minimum $\tan(π/(4γ_β))$, recovering the classical half-line factor $\tan(π/8)=\sqrt{2}-1$ at $β=0$. Numerical-range estimates give matrix dimension-independent upper bounds for projected shift-and-invert Arnoldi, while finite-degree pole searches and nonnormal experiments illustrate the distinction between the sector-uniform law and individual matrix computations.

</details>


### [196] [Shifted tapered-pole approximation on finite sectors: an exact asymptotic and dimension control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09256)
*Fei Xue,Tianqi Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究闭单位半径扇形区域上 $g(z)z^\alpha(\log z)^m$ 的显式 lightning-plus-polynomial (LP) 逼近，在已知基本扇形 root-exponential 速率的基础上，通过 Abel--Poisson 公式、顶点尺度重整与表示极点带构造三项精细化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本扇形 root-exponential 逼近速率已确立，但尚缺乏更精细的刻画：分支误差界对网格平移的一致性与对数幂次的普适性、稠密与平衡机制下的精确主常数、以及显式的表示维数分配。本文旨在回答这些更细的问题。

Method: 采用单侧 Abel--Poisson 公式与无极点缺口围道导出分支误差界；对 $z^\alpha$ 在稠密与平衡机制下进行精确的顶点尺度重整以确定指定平移序列的正有限主常数；使用速率匹配的附加表示极点带并结合多项式压缩构造表示。

Result: 得到对紧子集 $(0,1)$ 内网格平移一致、且对任意固定对数幂次成立的分支误差界；在稠密与平衡机制下识别出指定平移序列的正有限主常数；给出显式表示维数 $N+2\sqrt{2\alpha\lambda_\sigma N}+O(1)$，同时保持前 $N$ 个锥形极点不变，并证明略过分辨的混合序列亦保持精确主常数。

Conclusion: 匹配维数实验（含重入扇形）验证了上述极点与多项式维数的分配策略。文中精确渐近与维数结论仅针对所述构造，不涉及扇形 minimax 逼近或整体计算复杂度。

Abstract: We study explicit lightning-plus-polynomial (LP) approximation of $g(z)z^α(\log z)^m$ on a closed unit-radius sector, where $0<α<1$, $m$ is fixed, and $g$ is analytic nearby. Since the basic sector root-exponential rate is already known, we address three finer questions. First, a one-sided Abel--Poisson formula and a pole-free notched contour give a branchwise error bound that is uniform for grid shifts in compact subsets of $(0,1)$ and valid for every fixed logarithmic power. Second, for $z^α$ in the dense and balanced regimes, an exact vertex-scale rescaling identifies a positive finite leading constant for a specified shifted sequence. Third, a rate-matched band of additional representation poles followed by polynomial compression gives the explicit representation dimension $N+2\sqrt{2αλ_σN}+O(1)$ while leaving the first $N$ tapered poles unchanged. A slightly overresolved hybrid sequence also preserves the exact leading constant. Matched-dimension experiments, including reentrant sectors, illustrate the allocation. These exact-asymptotic and dimension statements concern the stated constructions, not sector minimax approximation or total computational complexity.

</details>


### [197] [Convergence of kernel and neural-network methods for Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equations on unbounded domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09299)
*Yumiharu Nakano*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文从经验平方残差出发，为无界区域上HJB方程的两种非单调无网格近似（Wendland核配点法与PINN）推导了误差界，将经验损失转化为逐点残差控制，进而给出粘性解误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman方程在无界空间域上的数值求解，需要从经验（可计算）残差得到严格的粘性解误差界；而现有粘性解误差估计通常依赖逐点（sup范数）残差控制，无法直接由经验损失获得，故需建立经验到总体的转换工具，并量化离散/采样误差与区域截断误差。

Method: 研究两类非单调、无网格近似：(1) 在native space范数约束下的Wendland核配点法；(2) 在Sobolev范数约束下、使用光滑激活函数的PINN。核心技术手段包括：经验到总体的估计、将L2残差转为sup范数界的内插不等式，以及控制域截断所致误差的扩散估计。核方法方面，在经典与native space正则性下证明紧集上一致收敛，并给出唯一有界连续粘性解的后验定量误差界；神经网络方面，在经典Sobolev正则性下证明紧集上依概率一致收敛，给出高概率定量误差界。

Result: 核方法的误差界由fill distance控制，神经网络方法的误差界由网络规模与样本量控制。两种方法均实现了从经验平方残差到粘性解误差的定量估计，并在一维与二维数值实验中验证了方法及误差界。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了基于经验残差的HJB方程无网格近似的严格误差分析框架，统一处理核方法与PINN，并明确区分粘性适定性与为获得收敛所需的额外正则性假设。

Abstract: We derive error bounds from empirical squared residuals for two non-monotone, mesh-free approximations of Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equations on unbounded spatial domains: collocation with Wendland kernels under a native space norm constraint, and physics-informed neural networks with smooth activations under a Sobolev-norm constraint. The main step is to recover the pointwise residual control needed for viscosity-solution error estimates from the empirical loss, while quantifying the discretization or sampling error. For the kernel method, we prove uniform convergence on compact sets under classical and native space regularity and derive an a posteriori quantitative error bound for the unique bounded continuous viscosity solution. For the neural-network method, we prove uniform convergence on compact sets in probability and give a quantitative high-probability error bound under classical Sobolev regularity. The bounds are governed by the fill distance for kernels and by the network and sample sizes for neural networks. The proofs combine empirical-to-population estimates, an interpolation inequality that turns an $L^2$ residual into a sup norm bound, and a controlled diffusion estimate for the error caused by truncating the domain. The regularity assumptions used to obtain convergence from approximation properties are additional to viscosity wellposedness. Numerical experiments in one and two space dimensions illustrate the methods and the estimates.

</details>


### [198] [An energy--stable ALE--FEM with compensated tangential velocity for Navier--Stokes free--boundary flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09301)
*Nuo Lei,Weiying Zheng,Na Zhu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种能量稳定的ALE有限元格式，用于含表面张力驱动自由边界的不可压Navier-Stokes流动。核心是无乘子的补偿切向速度格式，通过离散切向曲率残差重分布边界节点，松弛参数取α=γ₀/μ。证明全离散格式适定、能量耗散且时间一致，数值验证鲁棒性与网格质量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含表面张力的自由边界不可压流动在ALE框架下模拟时，边界节点易发生畸变，传统方法（如BGN）在小时间步下网格质量下降明显；同时现有切向速度重分布方法常需引入乘子，增加复杂度。因此需要一种无需乘子、且与粘性-毛细物理尺度匹配的能量稳定格式。

Method: 构建ALE有限元格式：采用无乘子的补偿切向速度公式，利用离散切向曲率残差重分布边界节点；将控制人工切向速度的松弛参数按粘性-毛细平衡进行标定，自然导出α=γ₀/μ（γ₀为表面张力系数，μ为动力粘度）。并对全离散格式的适定性、能量耗散性与时间一致性进行理论分析。

Result: 理论上证明全离散格式适定、能量耗散，且与半离散格式时间一致；数值实验表明方法在较大范围的μ与γ₀取值下均保持鲁棒，并在网格质量保持方面优于BGN方法，尤其在小时间步情形下优势明显。

Conclusion: 所提出的无乘子补偿切向速度ALE有限元格式兼具能量稳定性与良好网格质量保持能力，松弛参数α=γ₀/μ由粘性-毛细平衡自然确定，为表面张力驱动自由边界流动提供了稳健高效的数值方案。

Abstract: We propose an energy-stable arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian (ALE) finite element formulation for incompressible Navier-Stokes flows with surface-tension-driven free boundaries. The key ingredient is a multiplier-free compensated tangential velocity formulation, which redistributes boundary nodes via the discrete tangential curvature residual. The relaxation parameter governing the artificial tangential velocity is scaled according to the viscous-capillary balance, yielding the natural choice $α= γ_0/ μ$, where $γ_0$ is the surface tension coefficient and $μ$ is the dynamic viscosity. We prove that the fully discrete scheme is well-posed, energy-dissipative, and temporally consistent with its semi-discrete counterpart. Numerical results confirm the robustness of the proposed method across a range of $μ$ and $γ_0$ values. Furthermore, the method exhibits superior mesh quality preservation compared with the BGN method, particularly in the small-time-step regime.

</details>


### [199] [Fully Deterministic Shallow Neural Networks with Localized Activations. Part I: Construction and Optimal Sobolev Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09338)
*Ran Bi,Weibing Deng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 在有界Lipschitz区域上构造固定Gaussian与tanh差激活的浅层神经网络，确定性地选取隐藏参数，得到维度无关的最优H^s到H^m逼近速率N^{-(s-m)/d}，并给出外偏移截断裕度条件及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对目标函数逼近，希望网络隐参数不依赖目标u，且逼近速率在维数d下保持最优，同时简化外偏移节点的处理。Sobolev宽度下界表明该速率在维度≤N的线性空间中最优，因此动机在于构造显式、确定性、目标无关且速率最优的浅层网络逼近方案。

Method: 在Ω⊂R^d（d≥2）上，对每个s>0用显式确定性规则固定Gaussian和tanh差激活的隐藏参数，独立于目标u∈H^s(Ω)。频率分辨率M下，N_M≍M^d个预设特征张成线性空间V_M；取M≍N^{1/d}使N_M≤N，构造有界线性算子A_N:H^s(Ω)→V_M。方向用显式球面求积节点，偏移用Gauss-Legendre节点或等距中点。通过截断脊积分、移除外部中点节点，并分析外偏移裕度（Gaussian为M^{-1}logM量级、tanh差为M^{-1}(logM)^2量级）来保证逼近率。

Result: 对任意u∈H^s(Ω)及整数0≤m<s，有‖u-A_Nu‖_{H^m(Ω)}≤C N^{-(s-m)/d}‖u‖_{H^s(Ω)}，C与u、N、m无关。Sobolev宽度下界证明该速率在维度至多N、独立于目标选择的线性空间中是最优的。外偏移裕度可随M→∞收缩至零而保持逼近率；超过sup|x|的裕度达M^{-1}logM（Gaussian）或M^{-1}(logM)^2（tanh差）即可截断脊积分并移除外部中点节点。二维和三维数值实验考察了L^2与H^1逼近。

Conclusion: 该工作给出显式、目标无关的浅层Gaussian/tanh差网络构造，实现维数无关的最优Sobolev逼近速率，并提供可验证的外偏移截断裕度条件，数值实验支持理论结果。

Abstract: We construct shallow neural networks with fixed Gaussian and tanh-difference activations on bounded Lipschitz domains $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^d$, $d\ge2$. For each $s>0$, explicit deterministic rules prescribe all hidden parameters independently of the target $u\in H^s(Ω)$. At frequency resolution $M$, the $N_M\asymp M^d$ prescribed features span a linear space $V_M$. For every sufficiently large integer $N$, we choose $M\asymp N^{1/d}$ with $N_M\le N$ and construct a bounded linear operator $A_N:H^s(Ω)\to V_M$ such that \[
  \|u-A_Nu\|_{H^m(Ω)}
  \le C N^{-(s-m)/d}\|u\|_{H^s(Ω)} \] for every $u\in H^s(Ω)$ and every integer $0\le m<s$, with $C$ independent of $u$, $N$, and $m$. Sobolev width lower bounds show that this rate is optimal among linear spaces of dimension at most $N$ chosen independently of the target. We use explicit spherical cubature nodes for the directions and Gauss-Legendre nodes or equispaced midpoints for the offsets. We show that the exterior offset margin can shrink to zero as $M\to\infty$ while preserving the approximation rate. A margin beyond $\sup_{x\inΩ}|x|$ of order $M^{-1}\log M$ for the Gaussian and $M^{-1}(\log M)^2$ for the tanh difference suffices to truncate the ridge integral and remove exterior midpoint nodes. Numerical experiments in two and three dimensions examine $L^2$ and $H^1$ approximation.

</details>


### [200] [Fully Deterministic Shallow Neural Networks with Localized Activations. Part II: Condition Number Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09357)
*Ran Bi,Weibing Deng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对确定性浅层网络中Gaussian与tanh差激活的连续Sobolev Gram矩阵证明了正定性与条件数量化上界，其上界为exp(CN^{2/d}/log(N+2))，并通过匹配下界与二维实验验证指数尖锐性与归一化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Sobolev空间中的网络特征Gram矩阵是否正定及其条件数如何随特征数增长，为基于Sobolev构造的浅层网络逼近理论提供定量稳定性保证，并厘清特征族与偏移、归一化对条件数的影响。

Method: 针对在R^d（d≥2）中固定非空有界Lipschitz域上、固定整数m≥0的情形，考察完整H^m内积下的原始与按列归一化Gram矩阵；特征族采用Part I的最优Sobolev逼近构造，激活为Gaussian和tanh差；偏移采用中点与Gauss-Legendre格式。证明结合齐次微分算子（分离方向组）与一维系数恢复估计。

Result: 对充分大特征数N，原始与列归一化Gram矩阵的谱条件数上界为exp(CN^{2/d}/log(N+2))，C不依赖N；上界对中点与Gauss-Legendre偏移均成立。对中点偏移与适当的角序列，在固定椭球上的匹配下界表明该指数在所述域类上对原始Gaussian矩阵以及原始和归一化的tanh差矩阵是尖锐的。二维L^2方形实验展示了条件数增长与归一化效应。

Conclusion: Sobolev Gram矩阵正定且其条件数呈超多项式但亚指数级增长，指数2/d在给定域类下不可改进；归一化可改变条件数行为；分析框架可用于刻画浅层网络Sobolev特征的数值稳定性。

Abstract: We prove positive definiteness and quantitative condition-number bounds for continuous Sobolev Gram matrices of deterministic shallow networks with Gaussian and tanh-difference activations. The prescribed feature families include the optimal Sobolev approximation constructions of Part I. On every fixed nonempty bounded Lipschitz domain in $\R^d$, $d\ge2$, and for every fixed integer $m\ge0$, the raw and column-normalized Gram matrices in the full $H^m$ inner product have spectral condition numbers bounded by $\exp(CN^{2/d}/\log(N+2))$ for sufficiently large $N$, where $N$ is the feature count and $C>0$ is independent of $N$. The upper bounds hold for midpoint and Gauss-Legendre offsets. For midpoint offsets and a suitable angular sequence, matching lower bounds on fixed ellipsoids establish sharpness of this exponent over the stated domain class for raw Gaussian matrices and both raw and normalized tanh-difference matrices. The proof combines homogeneous differential operators that isolate direction groups with one-dimensional coefficient-recovery estimates. Two-dimensional $L^2$ experiments on a square illustrate condition-number growth and normalization effects.

</details>


### [201] [Structure-preserving full- and low-rank exponential methods for the optimal control of Lindblad equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09403)
*Hao Chen,Alfio Borzi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为Lindblad方程最优控制提出统一框架：由Pontryagin极大值原理导出连续最优性系统并用SQH方法求解，基于二阶指数中点传播子构造保结构的全秩与低秩格式，并给出严格误差估计与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统的最优控制需要反复求解前向与伴随演化方程；在大Hilbert空间中，这些计算要求数值方法保结构、高精度、高效，同时兼容非光滑优化技术，现有方法难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: 从Pontryagin极大值原理导出连续最优性系统，采用序列二次Hamiltonian（SQH）方法求解；数值实现基于含时Hamiltonian的前向与伴随Lindblad方程的二阶指数中点传播子。提出保持密度矩阵Hermitian与半正定结构的全秩格式，以及显著降低存储与计算成本的低秩格式，并对全秩和低秩的前向、伴随传播子建立严格误差估计。

Result: 数值实验验证了理论预测的收敛阶，证实了低秩近似的有效性，并展示了所提FREM-SQH与LREM-SQH算法在光滑与非光滑控制代价的最优控制问题中的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架与全秩/低秩保结构二阶指数中点传播子方案，配合SQH方法，能够高效且高精度地求解Lindblad方程最优控制问题，并兼容光滑与非光滑控制代价，理论误差估计与数值结果一致。

Abstract: Optimal control of open quantum systems governed by the Lindblad master equation requires the repeated solution of forward and adjoint evolution equations. For large Hilbert spaces, these computations demand numerical methods that are structure-preserving, accurate, and computationally efficient, while remaining compatible with nonsmooth optimization techniques. In this work, a unified framework for optimal control of Lindblad equations is presented. The continuous optimality system is derived from the Pontryagin maximum principle and solved by a sequential quadratic Hamiltonian (SQH) method. Its numerical realization is based on second-order exponential midpoint propagators for the forward and adjoint Lindblad equations with time-dependent Hamiltonians. Full-rank schemes preserving the Hermitian and positive-semidefinite structure of the density matrix are developed together with low-rank formulations that substantially reduce storage requirements and computational cost. Rigorous error estimates are established for the full- and low-rank forward and adjoint propagators. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted convergence rates, demonstrate the effectiveness of the low-rank approximations, and illustrate the performance of the proposed FREM-SQH and LREM-SQH algorithms for optimal control problems with smooth and nonsmooth control costs.

</details>


### [202] [Leapfrog Crank--Nicolson decoupling of wave--heat-type problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09603)
*Benjamin Dörich,Julian Dörner*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出并分析耦合波-热系统的数值格式：波动部分用蛙跳、热部分用Crank–Nicolson，以Strang分裂方式耦合，整体显式耦合且仅需双曲型CFL条件。引入抽象空间离散，通过误差分解导出全离散误差估计，数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波问题常用显式蛙跳格式，热方程却受抛物型时间步限制；直接耦合会导致过于严格的时间步约束。需要设计既保持二阶精度、又只受双曲型CFL条件限制的耦合格式。

Method: 构建二阶交错时间积分格式：波部分采用leapfrog，热部分采用Crank–Nicolson，并以Strang分裂方式组织耦合，使耦合项保持显式。引入可涵盖协调与稳定化非协调离散的抽象空间离散框架，利用一般误差分解推导全离散误差估计。

Result: 在所要求的双曲型CFL条件下，建立了全离散格式的误差估计；数值实验针对所建模型应用验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该分裂耦合策略避免了抛物型时间步限制，保持二阶精度，并适用于粘弹性、热弹波及Maxwell方程耦合色散材料等广泛的波-热系统。

Abstract: This paper proposes and analyzes a numerical method for coupled wave-heat systems that arise, for example, in visco-elasticity with memory, thermo-elastic wave propagation with finite thermal speed, and Maxwell's equations coupled to dispersive material laws. Motivated by the widespread use of explicit leapfrog schemes for wave problems and the parabolic time-step restriction for heat equations, we design a second-order scheme that combines leapfrog time integration for the wave part with a Crank--Nicolson step for the heat part. The coupling is arranged in a Strang-splitting--type fashion so that the overall scheme remains explicit in the coupling and is subject only to a CFL condition of hyperbolic type, rather than the more restrictive parabolic constraint.
  We introduce an abstract space discretization that covers a broad class of wave--heat systems and accommodates both conforming and stabilized non-conforming discretizations. Using a general error decomposition, we derive error estimates for the fully discrete scheme under the desired CFL condition. Numerical experiments for our model applications confirm the theoretical results.

</details>


### [203] [A locking-free virtual element method for the three-dimensional quasi-static electroporoelasticity equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09646)
*Jianguo Huang,Wenxuan Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于三维准静态电孔隙弹性方程的无锁定虚拟元方法，采用最低阶节点、棱、面和类Stokes虚拟元空间离散各物理场，配合后向欧拉时间离散，可在一般多面体网格上工作，并通过细化网格构造插值算子消除锁定现象，将四场系统降为三场系统以降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电孔隙弹性方程涉及多物理场耦合，且传统方法在近不可压缩情形下易出现锁定现象（误差估计依赖Lamé常数λ），同时多场耦合系统计算成本高。因此需要一种能在一般多面体网格上工作、无锁定且计算高效的数值方法。

Method: 采用虚拟元方法：压力、电场、磁场、位移场分别用最低阶节点、棱、面、类Stokes虚拟元空间离散；时间上用后向欧拉格式；在细化网格$\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_h$上定义类Stokes空间，构造满足$÷(\mathrm{I}_s\bm v)|_K=Π^K_0÷\bm v$的插值算子以克服锁定；利用精确序列$\curl\bm V^e_h=\bm V^f_{h,0}$显式更新磁场，将四场系统降为三场系统。

Result: 所提方法的误差估计不依赖于Lamé常数λ，从而克服了锁定现象；磁场可显式更新，降低了计算成本；数值实验验证了该方法的性能。

Conclusion: 该无锁定虚拟元方法适用于一般多面体网格上的三维准静态电孔隙弹性问题，兼具无锁定特性和计算效率优势，数值实验证实了其有效性。

Abstract: We develop and analyze a locking-free virtual element method for the three-dimensional quasi-static electroporoelasticity equations. The pressure, electric, magnetic, and displacement fields are discretized by the lowest-order nodal, edge, face, and Stokes-like virtual element spaces, respectively, so that the scheme works on general polyhedral meshes, while the temporal discretization is carried out by a backward Euler scheme. The locking phenomenon is overcome by defining the Stokes-like space on a refined mesh $\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}_h$, on which we can construct an interpolation operator $\mathrm{I}_s$ satisfying $÷(\mathrm{I}_s\bm v)|_K=Π^K_0÷\bm v$, which renders the error estimates independent of the Lamé constant $λ$. Moreover, since the exact sequence $\curl\bm V^e_h=\bm V^f_{h,0}$ holds, the magnetic field is updated explicitly, reducing the four-field system to a three-field one and lowering the computational cost. Numerical experiments are provided to demonstrate the performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [204] [Anisotropic Lagrange interpolation on exact spherical triangles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09686)
*Hiroki Ishizaka*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究球面精确测地三角形上线性Lagrange插值的各向异性误差估计，通过将单元映射分解为仿射映射与径向修正，把弦向各向异性与径向畸变分离，并导出锐利的局部估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将平面各向异性插值估计严谨地移植到球面，同时保留弦三角形方向长度尺度并显式保留几何因子，弥补现有估计中用直径替代方向尺度、且最大角依赖关系不锐利的缺陷。

Method: 将精确测地三角形单元映射分解为仿射映射与径向修正；推导关于曲面测度、Dirichlet能量与方向导数的精确恒等式；借助这些恒等式把平面各向异性插值估计迁移到球面上；构造四个显式三角形族进行定量分析与最优性验证。

Result: 所得局部误差估计保留两个弦向方向长度尺度且几何因子显式，常数与单元尺寸、形状及球半径无关；梯度估计中最大角因子仅乘以较长的方向尺度。四个三角形族量化了用直径替代方向尺度带来的损失，证明了最大角依赖的锐利性，并表明最大角因子不可移至较短尺度、指数不可低于1；第四族证明平方根径向迁移因子的最优性。局部估计可推广到相容球面三角剖分。

Conclusion: 该估计推广到相容球面三角剖分，并作为应用给出零均值Laplace–Beltrami问题保形逼近在精确积分下的各向异性能量误差估计。

Abstract: We study linear Lagrange interpolation on exact geodesic spherical triangles obtained by radial projection of chordal triangles. Factorising the element map into an affine map and a radial correction separates chordal anisotropy from radial distortion. We derive exact identities for the surface measure, Dirichlet energy, and directional derivatives, and use them to transfer planar anisotropic interpolation estimates to the sphere. The resulting local error estimates retain the two chordal directional length scales and keep the geometric factors explicit, with constants independent of the element size, shape, and sphere radius. In the gradient estimate, the maximum-angle factor multiplies only the longer directional scale. Four explicit triangle families quantify the loss caused by replacing these scales with the diameter and establish the sharp maximum-angle dependence. They also show that, within the same two-term directional bound with a constant independent of the element, the maximum-angle factor cannot be moved to the shorter scale or have its exponent reduced below one. The fourth family proves the optimality of the square-root radial-transfer factor while retaining the same transported directional scale. The local estimates extend to compatible spherical triangulations. As an application, they yield an anisotropic energy-error estimate for the conforming approximation of the mean-zero Laplace--Beltrami problem with exact integration.

</details>


### [205] [Operator preconditioning of the Costabel FEM-BEM coupling for the time-harmonic Maxwell transmission problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09765)
*Quang Huy Nguyen,Van Chien Le,Kristof Cools*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文给出时谐Maxwell透射问题Costabel FEM-BEM耦合的等价新表述，并构造分块对角预条件子，证明预处理矩阵谱条件数一致有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Costabel FEM-BEM耦合的离散系统条件数较差，直接求解效率低；需要寻找便于高效迭代求解的等价表述与有效预条件子。

Method: 引入迹提升算子与Steklov-Poincaré算子，将耦合重新表述在迹空间乘积上；代数上相当于消去内部FEM未知量，得到含内部FEM块Schur补的离散系统；在频率避开共振集的假设下，证明新表述与Costabel耦合在连续与离散层面等价；基于算子预条件框架，用Maxwell单层边界积分算子诱导的Galerkin矩阵构造预条件子。

Result: 证明了重表述与Costabel耦合在连续与离散层面等价；证明预处理矩阵的谱条件数一致有界；数值实验验证了方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该等价表述与基于单层算子的预条件子为Lipschitz界面时谐Maxwell透射问题提供了理论完备且实用的高效求解方案。

Abstract: This paper derives an equivalent formulation of the Costabel FEM-BEM coupling for the time-harmonic Maxwell transmission problem with a Lipschitz interface, and constructs a block-diagonal preconditioner for the resulting discrete system. The reformulated coupling is posed on a product of trace spaces and is obtained by introducing a trace lifting operator and the Steklov--Poincar{\' e} operator. At the algebraic level, it corresponds to eliminating the interior FEM unknowns, resulting in a discrete system that involves the Schur complement of the interior FEM block. Assuming that the frequency lies outside the resonance sets, we prove that the reformulated coupling is equivalent to the Costabel coupling at both the continuous and discrete levels. Using the operator preconditioning framework, we build a preconditioner from the Galerkin matrix induced by the Maxwell single layer boundary integral operator. The spectral condition number of the preconditioned matrix is shown to be uniformly bounded. Numerical experiments illustrate the practical effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [206] [An arbitrarily high-order accurate and locally energy/entropy-stable space-time method for a class of nonlinear wave systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09797)
*Michael Dumbser,Ilaria Perugia,Enrico Zampa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一类保结构、任意高阶精度的时空格式，用于非线性声波传播系统，基于非结构单纯形网格，在离散层面同时保证局部守恒、局部能量/熵平衡及速度场旋度对合的弱保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性声波传播系统中非线性体现在状态变量与对偶变量之间的本构关系上，而微分算子仍保持标准一阶声波结构。此类问题需要数值格式在任意高阶精度下同时保持守恒性、能量/熵稳定性以及旋度约束，传统格式难以兼顾这些性质。

Method: 方案由三部分构成：i）空间上采用间断Galerkin（DG）离散，其中对偶变量由能量对守恒变量的导数在DG空间上的L2投影计算得到；ii）数值通量以及基于对偶变量的、气泡加权的单元局部粘性正则化；iii）时间上采用连续-间断Petrov-Galerkin方法。

Result: 在完全离散层面，格式满足：I）状态变量的局部守恒；II）每个时空单元上的局部能量/熵平衡，具有一致、单值的数值能量/熵通量与非负局部能量/熵耗散，且当省略稳定项时精确守恒能量；III）速度场旋度对合的弱保持，即使在存在间断且激波捕捉项激活时仍成立。上述性质先经数学证明，再通过数值试验验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为非线性声波传播系统提供了一类兼具任意高阶精度与多重保结构性质的时空离散框架，DG空间离散、对偶变量投影、气泡加权粘性正则化与时空Petrov-Galerkin时间离散的组合是实现这些性质的关键。

Abstract: We present a new class of structure-preserving, arbitrarily high-order accurate space-time schemes using unstructured simplicial meshes in space for a class of nonlinear acoustic wave propagation systems, in which the nonlinearity is encoded in the constitutive relations between the state and dual variables, while the differential operators retain the standard first-order acoustic wave structure. At the fully discrete level, the schemes satisfy the following properties: I) local conservation of the state variables; II) a local energy/entropy balance on each space-time element, with a consistent, single-valued numerical energy/entropy flux and nonnegative local energy/entropy dissipation, and exact energy conservation when the stabilization terms are omitted; III) weak preservation of the curl involution of the velocity field, even in the presence of discontinuities and with the shock-capturing terms activated. The key ingredients of the new schemes are: i) a discontinuous Galerkin (DG) discretization in space, in which the dual variables are computed as the L2 projections onto the DG space of the energy derivatives with respect to the conserved variables; ii) numerical fluxes and a bubble-weighted, element-local viscous regularization expressed in terms of the dual variables; iii) a continuous-discontinuous Petrov-Galerkin method in time. Properties I)-III) are first proved mathematically and are then verified by suitable numerical tests.

</details>


### [207] [UnCut FEM with PHT-splines on unfitted T-meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09909)
*Falai Chen,Nuo Lei,Buyang Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出基于PHT样条的非贴体有限元法，在背景网格上最小化扩展域内L²残差求解隐式边界PDE，建立适定性与H¹最优先验估计，并配合后验误差指示子实现自适应细分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式边界（水平集描述）区域上的椭圆PDE，传统贴体/切割单元方法需构造弯曲切割子区域并做复杂数值积分，实现困难且成本高。

Method: 利用PHT样条（层次T网格上的多项式样条）的高光滑性，将方程改写为在物理域扩展邻域上的L²残差最小化，全程在背景网格上用标准逐单元积分实现，避免切割几何与专门积分；并采用基于残差的后验误差指示子驱动层次T网格局部自适应细分。

Result: 离散系统为对称正定；证明了数值格式的适定性，并导出H¹范数下的最优先验误差估计；数值实验验证理论结果，显示方法的精度与效率。

Conclusion: 该PHT样条非贴体有限元框架实现简单、理论完备，兼具最优收敛性与自适应能力，适用于隐式边界描述的椭圆问题。

Abstract: We propose a novel unfitted finite element method based on polynomial splines over hierarchical T-meshes (PHT-splines) for elliptic partial differential equations (PDEs) posed on domains whose boundaries are described implicitly by a level-set function. Exploiting the enhanced smoothness of the spline space, we formulate the method by minimizing the $L^2$ residual of the PDE over an extended neighborhood of the physical domain. This formulation allows the method to be implemented entirely on the background mesh using standard element-wise quadrature. It yields a symmetric positive-definite linear system and avoids the geometric construction of curved cut subregions in boundary elements, as well as the associated nontrivial numerical integration. We establish well-posedness of the numerical scheme and derive optimal \emph{a priori} error estimates in the $H^1$ norm. In addition, we employ a residual-based \emph{a posteriori} error indicator to guide local adaptive refinement of the hierarchical T-meshes. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical results and demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [208] [Neural Sampling with Reweighted Normalizing Flows via the Wasserstein--Fisher--Rao JKO Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10278)
*Chenguang Duan,Johannes Hertrich,Gabriele Steidl*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出基于WFR JKO格式的神经采样算法，理论上证明精确迭代指数收敛且无需结构假设，并通过重加权归一化流实现，在多峰分布上表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非归一化Boltzmann密度指定的分布中采样是一个核心难题，尤其在多峰、缺乏log-concavity或对数Sobolev不等式等结构假设的目标上，现有方法收敛性难以保证。

Method: 基于Wasserstein--Fisher--Rao几何下KL散度的Jordan--Kinderlehrer--Otto（WFR JKO）格式；证明对任意固定步长，精确WFR JKO迭代随迭代次数指数收敛到目标；用重加权归一化流参数化其传输（transport）与反应（reaction）分量，构建神经实现。

Result: 理论上：对任意固定步长，精确WFR JKO迭代指数收敛，且不要求log-concavity或对数Sobolev不等式等结构假设。实验上：在具有挑战性的多峰目标上，数值实验展示了该方法有前景的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作将WFR JKO格式的收敛理论与神经实现结合，为无结构假设的玻尔兹曼密度采样提供了兼具理论保证与实用性能的新途径。

Abstract: We propose a neural algorithm for sampling from distributions specified by unnormalized Boltzmann densities. Our approach is based on the Jordan--Kinderlehrer--Otto scheme for the Kullback--Leibler divergence in the Wasserstein--Fisher--Rao geometry (WFR JKO scheme). Our contributions are twofold. First, we prove that, for any fixed step size, the exact WFR JKO iterates converge exponentially fast to the target as the number of iterations tends to infinity. Notably, this result requires no structural assumptions on the target, such as log-concavity or a logarithmic Sobolev inequality. Second, we develop a neural implementation of the WFR JKO scheme that parametrizes its transport and reaction components using reweighted normalizing flows. Numerical experiments on challenging multimodal targets demonstrate the promising performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [209] [BDF2-type integrator for Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation in micromagnetics: IMEX time-stepping and full effective field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10291)
*Michele Aldé,Dirk Praetorius,Agus L. Soenjaya*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文将BDF2型有限元积分器从原始LLG方程推广到含低阶有效场项与电流驱动非保守力矩的LLG方程，采用IMEX时间分裂实现每步线性求解，证明了全离散解无条件弱收敛到弱解，并去除了前人工作中的CFL型限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始BDF2型积分器仅针对基本LLG方程，而实际微磁应用需要处理低阶有效场贡献与电流驱动非保守力矩；同时已有工作（含DMI情形）存在人为的CFL型限制，需去除这些限制以提升方法的适用范围与稳健性。

Method: 将BDF2型有限元积分器推广至含附加项的LLG方程；采用隐式-显式（IMEX）时间分裂处理新增项，从而保持每时间步仅需线性求解。

Result: 主定理证明了全离散近似无条件弱收敛到LLG方程的弱解；分析去除了原工作中首末时间步的CFL型限制；在DMI情形下收敛同样无条件，无需相关工作中强加的CFL限制；数值实验支持理论结论。

Conclusion: 该推广的BDF2-IMEX有限元方案在保持计算高效（线性求解）的同时，实现了含附加项与DMI情形下的无条件弱收敛，消除了既有方法的CFL型约束。

Abstract: We extend the BDF2-type finite-element integrator proposed in [M. Aldé, M. Feischl, D. Praetorius; BDF2-type integrator for Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation in micromagnetics: unconditional weak convergence to weak solutions. (Math. Comp., 2026, doi:10.1090/mcom/4210)] to the Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG) equation with lower-order effective-field contributions and current-induced non-conservative torques arising in micromagnetic applications. To incorporate these additional terms while retaining a linear solve at each time step, we employ an implicit-explicit (IMEX) time splitting. Our main theorem proves unconditional weak convergence of the fully discrete approximations towards weak solutions of the LLG equation. The analysis removes the mild but artificial CFL-type restrictions required at the first and final time steps in [Aldé M., Feischl M., Praetorius D.; Math. Comp., 2026]. Furthermore, in the presence of the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI), the convergence is likewise unconditional and does not require the CFL-type restriction imposed in [G. Hrkac, C.-M. Pfeiler, D. Praetorius, M. Ruggeri, A. Segatti, and B. Stiftner. Convergent tangent plane integrators for the simulation of chiral magnetic skyrmion dynamics. (Adv. Comput. Math., 2019. doi: 10.1007/s10444- 019-09667-z)]. Numerical experiments support the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [210] [A hat function based positive contact discontinuity capturing Boltzmann scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10419)
*Balwinder Singh,S. V. Raghurama Rao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出基于peculiar velocity与紧支撑hat function的接触间断捕获Boltzmann格式，简化数值分析并证明正性，扩展至二阶精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maxwell速度分布函数能恢复Euler方程的守恒变量与通量向量，但其在速度空间具有无限支撑，给基于该分布的动理学格式数值分析带来困难。虽然Maxwellian是自然选择，却并非唯一保持这些关键矩的分布函数。Perthame(1990)引入紧支撑hat function作为替代方案。

Method: 基于peculiar velocity构造接触间断捕获的Boltzmann格式，采用hat function作为速度空间的分布函数。利用hat function在速度空间中的紧支撑特性简化数值分析，直接证明格式的正性；并在不破坏一阶格式保正性的前提下将格式扩展至二阶精度。

Result: 由于hat function的紧支撑，格式的数值分析显著简化，实现了正性的直接证明；二阶扩展格式保持保正性，并在一维和二维多个基准无粘流动问题上进行了测试。

Conclusion: 基于hat function构造的Boltzmann格式借助紧支撑特性简化了数值分析并保证正性，可扩展至二阶精度且保持保正，适用于接触间断捕获与无粘流动数值模拟。

Abstract: The suitable moments of the Maxwell velocity distribution function recover the conserved variable and flux vectors of the Euler equations of gas dynamics. However, the Maxwellian has infinite support in velocity space, which poses challenges for the numerical analysis of kinetic schemes based on it. Although Maxwellian is a natural choice, it is not the only distribution function that preserves these key moments. In his seminal paper, Perthame (1990) introduced a compactly supported hat function as an alternative. This paper presents the formulation and analysis of a contact discontinuity capturing Boltzmann scheme based on peculiar velocity, constructed using the hat function. Owing to the compact support of the hat function in velocity space, the numerical analysis of the proposed scheme is considerably simplified, allowing for a direct proof of its positivity. The scheme, extended to second-order accuracy without compromising the positivity-preserving property of its first-order counterpart, is tested on several benchmark inviscid flow problems in one- and two-dimensions.

</details>


### [211] [A finite element exterior-to-interior reconstruction in the fractional conductivity inverse problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10484)
*Mukul Dwivedi,Jesse Railo,Andreas Rupp*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种正则化有限元方法求解分数阶电导率方程，分两步从含噪Dirichlet-to-Neumann数据重构外部与内部电导率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶电导率方程的逆问题需从边界测量恢复系数，且需在噪声与离散化条件下保证稳定性。作者在Covi--Railo--Zimmermann（2026）外部确定原理基础上，构建可数值实现的正则化格式，并解决从外部重构到内部电导率恢复的完整流程。

Method: 采用网格局部化输入与质量集中Tikhonov正则化的有限元离散，实现外部确定原理；证明外部L^2与L^∞收敛及临界Hölder指数处的对数修正；通过Liouville约化将问题转为分数阶Schrödinger方程的Calderón问题；用分离测量恢复内部势，重叠测量确定外部势尾；结合恢复的外部电导率，为外部Cauchy数据做Tikhonov正则化有限元最小二乘延拓。

Result: 证明变换后电导率的H^s收敛与内部电导率的L^2收敛，给出完全观测空间下的对数状态偏差与电导率误差估计；可行重构保持一致正性与有界性；一维和二维数值实验展示了外部与内部重构精度及其对测量噪声和离散化的敏感性。

Conclusion: 该正则化有限元框架将分数阶外部确定原理与内部Calderón型重构有效结合，兼顾理论收敛性与数值稳健性，为分数阶电导率逆问题的噪声与离散化处理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We develop a regularized finite element method for the fractional conductivity equation, recovering the conductivity on an exterior observation region from noisy Dirichlet-to-Neumann measurements and using this reconstruction to determine the interior conductivity. Mesh-localized inputs and mass-lumped Tikhonov regularization discretize the exterior determination principle of Covi--Railo--Zimmermann (2026, Calc. Var. PDE). We prove exterior \(L^2\)- and \(L^\infty\)-convergence with error estimates, including the logarithmic correction at the critical Hölder exponent. The exterior reconstruction enables the Liouville reduction to the Calderón problem for the fractional Schrödinger equation. Separated measurements recover the interior potential, after which overlapping measurements determine the exterior potential tail. Together with the recovered exterior conductivity, the tail supplies the exterior Cauchy data for a Tikhonov-regularized finite element least-squares continuation. We prove \(H^s\)-convergence of the transformed conductivity and \(L^2\)-convergence of the interior conductivity, together with logarithmic state-bias and conductivity error estimates for full observation spaces. The admissible reconstructions remain uniformly positive and bounded. We present one- and two-dimensional numerical experiments illustrating the accuracy of the exterior and interior reconstructions and their sensitivity to measurement noise and discretization.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Matrix Product State Representatives of Symmetry-Protected Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09501)
*Thomas Jackson*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种构造整数自旋 MPS 的简洁拟设，可代表由 Tasaki 与 Ogata 研究的每三个 Z2 拓扑指数组合所对应的相，给出自旋-1 的精确例子及 Fortran 程序，结果与 Chen-Gu-Wen 分类及 Tasaki 最新结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tasaki 与 Ogata 曾为时间反演、反射等对称性定义了多个 Z2 拓扑指数（含隐藏序指数与 D2 对称性对应指数），但这些指数组合是否都有可显式构造的、满足 SO(3)×Z2^T×Z2^R 对称性的整数自旋矩阵乘积态（MPS）代表，此前缺乏系统构造。

Method: 作者为整数自旋系统提出一种简洁的 MPS 拟设，直接以 SO(3)×Z2^T×Z2^R 不变性为约束，设法为每种平凡/非平凡指数三元组构造代表态；对自旋-1 给出精确例子，并编写 Fortran 程序在任意整数自旋下搜索所需代表，同时用小自旋做随机抽样数据。

Result: 每种平凡与非平凡指数组合都存在自旋-1 的 SU(2)、时间反演与格点反射对称的 MPS 代表，其单射性长度 l≤8；给出了自旋-1 精确实例、可对任意整数自旋查找三元组代表的 Fortran 程序以及小自旋随机抽样数据。

Conclusion: 此类 MPS 态的存在性与 Chen-Gu-Wen 的对称保护拓扑分类一致，也印证了 Tasaki 近期关于隐藏序与 Ogata 指数（对应二面体 D2 对称性的那个指数）相等的结果。

Abstract: We provide a simple ansatz for finding integer spin $SO(3)\times\Z_2^T\times\Z_2^R$-invariant matrix product states representing a phase for each triple of $\Z_2$ topological indices studied in the literature by Tasaki \cite{tasakiheistop}\cite{tasakitop} and Ogata (corresponding to time-reversal and reflection symmetries) \cite{ogatatime}\cite{ogatareflection}. Every combination of trivial and non-trivial indices has a spin-$1$ $SU(2)$, time-reversal, and site-reflection symmetric MPS representative with injectivity length $l\leq 8$. We provide some exact examples for spin-$1$ and a Fortran program which will find representatives of any triple for any integer spin, as well as some data from random sampling for small spin. The existence of such states agrees with the classification of Chen, Gu, and Wen \cite{chen}, and with the recent results of Tasaki \cite{tasakinew} equating the hidden-order and one of Ogata's indices, wherein the Ogata index is that associated to the dihedral $D_2$ symmetry.

</details>


### [213] [Absence of Absolutely Continuous Spectrum for One-Dimensional Capacitance Operators with Critical Long-Range Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09582)
*Habib Ammari,Jiayu Qiu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类具有乘性无序与临界长程跳跃的一维标量Laurent算子不存在绝对连续谱，并将其应用于亚波长物理中的电容算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论要求非对角项至少具有四次衰减率，此限制过于严格；作者希望放宽该条件，以覆盖仅以 |n|^{-1}log^{-β}|n|（β>1）速率衰减的临界长程相互作用。

Method: 利用卷积核的有限差分界（finite-difference bound）绕开四次非对角衰减要求，建立标量一维Laurent算子在乘性无序与临界长程跳跃下无绝对连续谱的抽象结果；随后验证三维谐振子链的电容算子满足所需的衰减与有限差分估计。

Result: 成功去除了现有理论中至少四次非对角衰减率的限制，在仅 |n|^{-1}log^{-β}|n|（β>1）的临界衰减下证明了无绝对连续谱；并将抽象结论应用于每周期一个谐振子的三维谐振子链电容算子，验证其满足所需条件并得出无绝对连续谱的结论。

Conclusion: 本文为具有临界长程相互作用的波系统进行Anderson局域化证明提供了关键一步，但纯点谱与奇异连续谱的区分仍留待未来研究。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the absence of absolutely continuous spectrum for scalar one-dimensional Laurent operators with multiplicative disorder and critical long-range hoppings. In particular, the requirement of at least quartic off-diagonal decay rate in the existing theory is bypassed by exploiting the finite-difference bound of the convolution kernel, which is available even when the interaction decays only as $|n|^{-1}\log^{-β}|n|$ with $β>1$. We then apply the abstract main result to a concrete physical model, the capacitance operator in subwavelength physics. For a chain of three-dimensional resonators with one resonator per period, we verify the required decay and finite-difference estimates and conclude the absence of absolutely continuous spectrum. These results leave open the distinction between pure point and singular continuous spectrum, but constitute a key step in proving Anderson localization in wave systems with critical long-range interactions.

</details>


### [214] [Operator Schmidt Rank and the Geometry of Schmidt Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09794)
*Mohsen Kian,Mohammad Sal Moslehian*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文确定了在算子Schmidt秩受限的幺正变换下，向量Schmidt秩可达到的最小算子Schmidt秩，并给出统一的Schmidt谱累积分布不等式、优序关系及Schatten范数与Rényi熵的精确失真界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究幺正算子（视为量子信道/变换）对复合系统量子态Schmidt谱的影响，刻画在给定算子Schmidt秩预算下能够实现的Schmidt秩调控范围，并将秩可达性推广到对整个Schmidt谱累积分布的定量控制。

Method: 引入算子Schmidt秩（OSR）与向量Schmidt秩（SR），通过构造性极小化与匹配下界证明主导结果：对任意输入向量v（SR(v)=p），求所有满足SR(Uv)=q的幺正U的最小OSR，得到其精确值为⌈max{p/q, q/p}⌉；进一步证明OSR(U)≤k时对每个向量x有累积Schmidt系数不等式F_{min{kr,d}}(Ux)≥F_r(x)及其对称形式，并由此建立优序关系。

Result: 证明 min{OSR(U): U幺正, SR(Uv)=q} = ⌈max{p/q, q/p}⌉（d=min{m,n}）；并证明若OSR(U)≤k，则对任意单位向量x及任意r，F_{min{kr,d}}(Ux)≥F_r(x)且F_{min{kr,d}}(x)≥F_r(Ux)。该结论强于秩可达性，可控制整个累积Schmidt剖面；据此得到Schatten范数失真与Rényi熵变化的尖锐（可达）上下界，并给出了相应的优序关系。

Conclusion: 算子Schmidt秩受限的幺正变换对Schmidt谱的作用可由一条精确的累积不等式及优序关系完全刻画，从而统一给出Schmidt秩调控的最优代价以及Schatten范数与Rényi熵失真问题的紧界。

Abstract: The main result of the paper states that if $0\neq v\in\mathbb C^m\otimes\mathbb C^n$, $d=\min\{m,n\}$, and $p=SR(v)$, then for every $1\le q\le d$, \begin{equation*}
  \min\left\{OSR(\mathbf U):
  \begin{array}{l}
  \mathbf U\in\mathbb M_m\otimes\mathbb M_n\text{ is unitary},
  SR(\mathbf Uv)=q
  \end{array}\right\}
  =
  \left\lceil\max\left\{\frac pq,\frac qp\right\}\right\rceil . \end{equation*} Here $OSR(\mathbf U)$ denotes the operator Schmidt rank of $\mathbf U$. Furthermore, if $\mathbf U\in\mathbb M_m\otimes\mathbb M_n$ is unitary and $OSR(\mathbf U)\le k$, then, for every unit vector $x\in\mathbb C^m\otimes\mathbb C^n$ and every $1\le r\le d$, \begin{equation*}
  F_{\min\{kr,d\}}(\mathbf Ux)\ge F_r(x)
  \quad\text{and}\quad
  F_{\min\{kr,d\}}(x)\ge F_r(\mathbf Ux), \end{equation*} where $F_r(x)$ denotes the sum of the $r$ largest squared Schmidt coefficients of $x$. This result is stronger than rank reachability as it controls the entire cumulative Schmidt profile. We also establish a majorization relation and use it to obtain sharp bounds on Schatten-norm distortion and on the change of Rényi entropies under unitaries with bounded operator Schmidt rank.

</details>


### [215] [Asymptotic Neutrality for the Ionization Conjecture in the Two-Center Thomas-Fermi-Dirac-von Weizsäcker Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09997)
*Lu Chen,Yong Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在双中心情形下推进了Thomas-Fermi-Dirac-von Weizsäcker模型的电离猜想，证明束缚电子数满足 N ≤ Z + C Z^{153/154}(1+log Z)^{3/8}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thomas-Fermi-Dirac-von Weizsäcker模型的电离猜想关心给定核构型能束缚多少电子。此前仅单中心情形由Frank、Nam和Van Den Bosch解决，原始分子（多中心）情形仍悬而未决，本文旨在推进双中心情形。

Method: 证明依赖于与中性Thomas-Fermi密度的比较，以及通过屏蔽势耦合的局域化与外部L^1估计。

Result: 对总电荷为Z的两个不同原子核，证明束缚电子数N满足 N ≤ Z + C Z^{153/154}(1+log Z)^{3/8}，其中常数与核位置和核电荷无关。

Conclusion: 本文在双中心情形下推进了电离猜想，所得结果可推广到具有任意固定数目K个原子核的分子。

Abstract: The ionization conjecture for the Thomas-Fermi-Dirac-von Weizsäcker model asks how many electrons a given nuclear configuration can bind. So far, only the single-center case has been settled by Frank, Nam, and Van Den Bosch, while the original molecular case has still remained open. We advance this conjecture in the two-center case. We prove that for two distinct nuclei of total charge $Z$, the number $N$ of bound electrons satisfies $N\le Z+C Z^{153/154}(1+\log Z)^{3/8}$, with constants independent of the nuclear positions and the nuclear charges. The proof relies on a comparison with the neutral Thomas-Fermi density and on localization and exterior $L^1$-estimates coupled through the screened potential. Our results could be extended to molecules with any fixed number $K$ of nuclei.

</details>


### [216] [Iterated Graph Systems (II): Bernoulli percolation and critical-exponent universality classes on hierarchical lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10469)
*Ziyu Neroli*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究边迭代图系统(EIGS)生成的分层格上的Bernoulli渗流及其普适类，证明任何有限个替代-乘性维度族都不足以完全决定普适性，从而严格证明了维度型普适性的失效，并进一步否证了临界指数普适性需要可公度离散重整化尺度的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在严格检验"维度是否决定普适类"这一长期讨论的问题，回应Hauser与Saxena提出的维度型普适性失效现象，并澄清重整化尺度可公度性与临界指数普适性之间的关系。

Method: 在EIGS生成的分层格上研究Bernoulli渗流，考察八个临界指数，分析替代-乘性维度与普适类的关系；借助六指数定理给出的数论判据分析尺度可公度性。

Result: 证明不存在任何有限个替代-乘性维度族能够完全决定普适性，给出维度型普适性失效的严格证明；尽管六指数定理提供了尺度可公度的数论判据，仍否证了临界指数普适性要求重整化尺度可公度的猜想。

Conclusion: 维度不足以决定渗流的普适类；临界指数普适性并不依赖于重整化尺度的可公度性，从而否定了相关猜想。

Abstract: We study Bernoulli percolation and its universality classes on hierarchical lattices generated by edge iterated graph systems (EIGS). By examining eight critical exponents, we prove that no finite family of substitution-multiplicative dimensions completely determines universality, providing a rigorous proof of the failure of dimension-based universality discussed by Hauser and Saxena [Phys. Rev. B 34 (1986), 8193-8195]. Although the six exponentials theorem provides number-theoretic criteria for scale commensurability, we further disprove the conjecture that critical-exponent universality requires commensurate discrete renormalisation scales.

</details>
